## 连续视觉 Tokenizer 与表征自编码器 [[Back]](#toc)

面向理解与生成统一的连续视觉 token、VAE/ViT、RAE、DINO/SigLIP/MAE latent 与视觉表征重建。

### [1] [ScaleAware-JEPA: Latent Representation for Discovery in Multiscale Physical Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29723)
*Guang-Xing Li*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, astro-ph.IM, physics.comp-ph

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续视觉 Tokenizer 与表征自编码器

Importance: 高优先级 (92.7) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 ScaleAware-JEPA，一种面向多尺度连续物理场的自监督潜表征学习框架。通过约束扩散分解（CDD）将物理场分解为像素级尺度分量，并基于这些尺度坐标定义 JEPA 的掩码几何结构，使预测任务与场的尺度层级对齐，从而在无标签条件下学习稠密的结构潜坐标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续物理场（如湍流、星际气体、城市灯光）具有多尺度结构，但标准自监督方法在固定图像坐标中定义上下文和目标，预测任务与场的连续尺度层级不匹配，且有用的坐标先验未知。需要一种能自动发现场的内在尺度结构并学习对应潜表征的方法。

Method: 提出 ScaleAware-JEPA 框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Constrained Diffusion Decomposition (CDD)，将每个物理场分解为像素对齐的尺度分量，提供定义掩码几何的尺度坐标；(2) 基于 JEPA 的自监督目标，其中上下文足迹由每个分量的扩散尺度决定而非固定 patch 大小，从而在潜空间中预测隐藏结构。

Result: 在 MHD 湍流、星际分子气体和城市夜间灯光三个多尺度物理场上，学习到的潜坐标几何能够映射回连贯的形态结构，形成稠密的结构图谱，无需标签或预定义分割规则。

Conclusion: ScaleAware-JEPA 通过将潜预测与场的尺度层级绑定，构建了可用于检查复杂物理模式的潜坐标，在相关结构被预先规定之前即可实现物理场的结构发现。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ScaleAware-JEPA，通过 Constrained Diffusion Decomposition (CDD) 将连续物理场分解为尺度分量，并在连续潜空间中学习稠密、无标签的潜在坐标表征。这属于连续视觉/物理场表征学习，与 [CANONICAL] 连续视觉 Tokenizer 与表征自编码器 方向高度吻合，该 subtopic 涵盖连续视觉 token、VAE/ViT latent 与表征重建，本论文的潜空间尺度分解表征正是其延伸。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous visual tokenizer and representation autoencoder for multiscale physical fields, aligning with top priority subtopic. Signals: continuous visual tokenizer, representation autoencoder, self-supervised latent representation, multiscale physical fields, JEPA framework

Abstract: Continuous physical fields represent a large fraction of data under scientific investigation. Their multiscale structures are central to discovery, yet useful coordinates are not known in advance. Standard self-supervised methods define context and targets in fixed image coordinates, posing a predictive task misaligned with fields organized across a continuous scale hierarchy. We introduce ScaleAware-JEPA, a framework that constructs dense, label-free latent coordinates for continuous scalar fields. Constrained Diffusion Decomposition (CDD) separates each field into pixel-registered scale components and provides the scale coordinates that define the masking geometry. The resulting JEPA objective predicts hidden structure with a context footprint tied to the diffusion scale of each component rather than to an arbitrary patch size. Across MHD turbulence, interstellar molecular gas and urban nighttime-light structure, the learned geometry maps back to coherent morphology, forming dense structural atlases without labels or predefined segmentation rules. By tying latent prediction to the scale hierarchy of a field, ScaleAware-JEPA constructs latent coordinates through which complex physical patterns can be inspected before their relevant structures have been prescribed. Code is available at .

</details>

### [2] [Depth-Staggered Fibonacci Spacing for Sparse Attention: Static Schedules Beat Learned Dilation and Extrapolate Where Dense Attention Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28560)
*Chad A. Capps*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (93.17) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究稀疏自注意力中Fibonacci间隔的深度交错调度，发现静态逐层交错优于固定和学习型alpha，且所有稀疏变体可外推到4倍训练长度，而稠密基线在4倍长度时困惑度上升201%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索稀疏注意力中如何跨深度层调度注意力间隔模式，以提升长序列外推能力并降低推理延迟。

Method: 提出Depth-Staggered Fibonacci Spacing方法，每层使用一个标量alpha压缩或扩展Fibonacci间隔，比较固定、逐层学习、静态线性交错和互质重分配四种调度策略，在60M参数、16层、426M token的21个语言模型上进行对比实验。

Result: 静态逐层交错优于固定和学习型alpha；学习型alpha效果不优于静态调度且推理延迟高5倍；所有稀疏变体可外推至4倍训练长度，而稠密基线在4倍长度时困惑度上升201%。

Conclusion: 静态深度交错调度是稀疏注意力的有效策略，能显著提升长序列外推能力，但训练长度下稀疏模型困惑度仍比稠密基线高约26%。

Classification reason: 该论文研究稀疏自注意力机制中的Fibonacci间隔静态调度，属于连续语言模型中的注意力机制优化，与[连续语言模型与语言流]方向相关。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model efficiency via static sparse attention schedules that extrapolate far beyond training length, aligning with top-priority subtopic. Signals: Fibonacci sparse attention, static depth-staggered schedule beats learned, 4x length extrapolation without degradation, dense baseline collapses at 4x, 26% perplexity gap at training length

Abstract: We study sparse self-attention in which each query attends to a dense local window plus a set of Fibonacci-spaced offsets, with a per-layer scalar alpha that compresses or expands the spacing. Across 21 language models trained under one matched recipe (60M parameters, 512 hidden, 16 layers, 426M tokens), we compare four ways of setting alpha across depth: fixed, per-layer learned, a static linear stagger, and a coprime (anti-gridding) reassignment of that stagger, together with a reach-matched power-of-2 control. Three results stand out. First, a static per-layer stagger improves perplexity over both fixed and learned alpha, and the gain is base-agnostic: applying the same stagger to a power-of-2 base lifts it above fixed Fibonacci and to parity with learned Fibonacci attention. Second, learning per layer is inert: it does not beat the static schedule and costs roughly five times the inference latency. Third, and most consequential, all sparse variants extrapolate to four times their training length with little or no degradation, whereas a recipe-matched dense baseline collapses (perplexity rises by 201% at 4x length); we attribute this to fixed-offset attention only ever querying relative positions seen during training. We also report two honest negatives: at training length the best sparse model has about 26% higher perplexity than the dense baseline, and the staggering gain is uniform across context positions rather than concentrated at long range.

</details>


### [3] [Flow Reasoning Models: Scaling Reasoning Through Iterative Self-Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29150)
*Alec Helbling, Andrey Bryutkin, Mauro Martino, Nima Dehmamy, Hendrik Strobelt*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (94.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Flow Reasoning Models (FRMs)，利用离散流模型的结构化推理能力，通过自验证（动态稳定性）实现测试时缩放，并结合自条件化训练和偏好优化提升效率，在数独和Zebra谜题上达到接近100%的求解率，且泛化到分布外更难谜题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散流模型在少步文本生成上表现良好，但直接应用于结构化推理任务（如数独、Zebra谜题）时，模型会自信地收敛到错误答案（仅解出约36%的数独）。作者希望利用流模型的去噪动力学特性，设计一种训练和测试时缩放框架，提升流模型在结构化推理上的准确率和效率。

Method: 1) 观察到流模型可作为自身的验证器：正确答案是去噪动力学的稳定不动点，重新加噪再求解后仍回到自身。2) 测试时缩放：生成多个候选解，保留动态稳定的解，在Sudoku-Shah上达到~100%，Zebra上95.9%。3) 训练改进：引入自条件化通道（self-conditioning），推理时关闭该通道让模型自我修正；使用直接偏好优化（DPO）训练模型避免自身失败生成。这些改进使模型仅需7次前向传播即可达到99.2%的数独准确率。

Result: 在Sudoku-Shah上达到~100%求解率，Zebra谜题95.9%，且泛化到分布外的Sudoku-Extreme（96.1%）。训练改进后，仅需7次前向传播达到99.2%准确率，比最强掩码扩散基线快8倍以上。结合测试时缩放，能更高效地解决分布外困难谜题。

Conclusion: 流模型通过自验证的动态稳定性机制可以有效解决结构化推理任务，结合自条件化训练和偏好优化能大幅提升效率。该框架为流模型在推理任务中的应用提供了新的范式，展示了测试时缩放和训练改进的协同效果。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Flow Reasoning Models (FRMs)，在离散流模型（flow models）框架下进行结构化推理（数独、Zebra 谜题），属于连续语言模型与语言流（continuous_language_flow）范畴。论文核心是流模型的推理缩放与自验证，与 canonical 子主题"连续语言模型与语言流"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Introduces a novel test-time scaling and self-verification method for discrete flow models on structured reasoning, directly advancing continuous language model reasoning. Signals: flow reasoning, self-verification via dynamic stability, test-time scaling, self-conditioning training, direct preference optimization

Abstract: Discrete flow models have recently shown promising performance on few-step text generation; however, when naively applied to structured reasoning tasks such as Sudoku and Zebra puzzles, they converge confidently to incorrect answers (solving only $\sim$36% of Sudoku puzzles). We introduce Flow Reasoning Models (FRMs), a training and test-time-scaling framework for structured reasoning with flow models. We make the observation that, despite their poor solve rate, flow models can act as their own verifiers. A correct answer is a stable fixed point of the denoising dynamics, returning to itself when re-noised and re-solved. This enables a test-time-scaling paradigm: propose many candidate solutions and keep those that are dynamically stable, which alone reaches high solve rates on Sudoku-Shah (~$100\%$) and Zebra ($95.9\%$). This even generalizes to harder out-of-distribution puzzles like Sudoku-Extreme ($96.1\%$), without ever training on that distribution. This pure search, however, wastes a great deal of computation generating incorrect candidate solutions. We therefore design a training recipe to improve the base model's efficiency. First, we train flow models with a self-conditioning channel and close it at inference, letting them refine their own past predictions. Second, we train models to avoid their own failed generations using direct preference optimization. These changes substantially improve the base model's efficiency, letting it reach $99.2\%$ on Sudoku in just $7$ forward passes, over $8\times$ fewer than the strongest matched masked-diffusion baseline we compare needs for the same accuracy. When combined with test-time scaling, this lets flow models solve hard out-of-distribution puzzles (e.g. Sudoku-Extreme) far more efficiently.

</details>


### [4] [Invariant Reasoning Directions in Latent Trajectories of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29164)
*Arun Vignesh Malarkkan, Manan Roy Choudhury, Utkarsh Byahut, Yash Ravindra Charde, Vivek Gupta, Yanjie Fu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CG

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 值得关注 (74.8) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现语言模型的隐空间推理轨迹中存在稳定的不变方向（低秩结构），并提出无需训练的 TILR 框架来识别和操控这些方向，从而提升推理一致性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐空间推理模型在隐藏状态空间中执行多步推理，但这些推理轨迹的结构尚不清晰。作者希望理解哪些隐空间方向承载了可迁移的推理能力，以及如何利用这些方向提升推理的稳定性和一致性。

Method: 提出 Trajectory-Invariant Latent Refinement (TILR)，一种无需训练的干预框架。首先从对比性轨迹差异（强推理 vs 弱推理）中学习低秩不变子空间，然后通过自适应对齐门控将隐空间干预约束在该子空间内，抑制对齐不良的更新方向。

Result: 在六个推理基准上，少量隐空间方向即可解释强弱推理轨迹间的大部分差异。TILR 将同义改写下的答案一致性提升约10%，隐轨迹方差降低高达50%，同时保持推理准确率。

Conclusion: 隐空间推理中存在稳定的低维几何结构，可迁移的推理行为源于这些不变方向。TILR 验证了通过操控这些方向可以因果性地改善推理一致性和鲁棒性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究语言模型在隐空间中的推理轨迹结构，发现对比性轨迹差异呈现低秩结构，并提出 TILR 方法在连续隐空间中识别和操控稳定推理方向。这属于连续语言模型在隐空间中的推理行为分析，与 [CANONICAL] 连续语言模型与语言流 主题高度相关。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous latent reasoning with a training-free intervention framework, aligning with top-priority continuous language model subtopic. Signals: continuous language model, latent reasoning, invariant directions, training-free intervention, low-rank structure

Abstract: Latent reasoning models perform multi-step inference directly in hidden-state space, yet the structure of these latent reasoning trajectories remains poorly understood. We show that contrastive refinement signals between stronger and weaker reasoning trajectories exhibit a highly concentrated low-rank structure, while unconstrained latent updates remain sensitive to paraphrases, checkpoint choice, and trajectory perturbations. These observations suggest that latent reasoning trajectories contain stable invariant directions mixed with unstable instance-specific variation. We introduce \textbf{Trajectory-Invariant Latent Refinement (TILR)}, a training-free intervention framework for identifying and manipulating stable reasoning directions in latent space. TILR first learns a low-rank invariant subspace from contrastive trajectory differences across inputs, then constrains latent interventions to this subspace while suppressing poorly aligned updates through an adaptive alignment gate. Across six reasoning benchmarks, we find that a small number of latent directions explain most variation between strong and weak reasoning trajectories. Interventions on these directions causally improve reasoning consistency and reduce trajectory instability under paraphrases and perturbations. TILR improves answer consistency under paraphrase by ~10% and reduces latent trajectory variance by up to $50\%$ while preserving reasoning accuracy. These results support a geometric view of latent reasoning in which transferable reasoning behavior emerges from stable low-dimensional structure within hidden-state trajectories.

</details>


### [5] [Multi-Block Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29215)
*Yijie Jin, Jiajun Xu, Yuxuan Liu, Chenkai Xu, Yi Tu, Jiajun Li, Dandan Tu, Xiaohui Yan, Kai Yu, Pengfei Liu, Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CL

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - Top 3 精读候选

TL;DR: 本文提出多块扩散语言模型（MBD-LM），通过多块教师强制（MultiTF）训练策略和块缓冲区（Block Buffer）解码机制，将单块扩散语言模型扩展为多块并行解码，显著提升推理吞吐量和生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有块扩散语言模型（BD-LM）在推理时仅支持单块解码，无法利用块间并行性；而扩散强制策略的训练状态与多块推理存在差异。作者希望弥合训练与推理之间的差距，实现多块并行解码以加速文本生成。

Method: 提出多块教师强制（MultiTF）训练策略，在干净前缀条件下对 bounded noise-groups 进行训练，并引入随机化噪声调度器以匹配多块推理状态。同时提出基于块缓冲区（Block Buffer）的优化解码算法，保持前缀缓存复用和输入形状静态，将并行度提升转化为实际加速。

Result: MBD-LLaDA2-Mini 将平均每前向传递 token 数（TPF）从 3.47 提升至 6.19，平均准确率从 79.95% 提升至 81.03%；结合 DMax 后，MBD-LLaDA2-Mini-DMax 达到平均 TPF 9.34，在数学和代码基准上仅下降 1.02% 准确率。

Conclusion: 多块扩散语言模型通过训练-推理对齐和优化的解码机制，有效提升了扩散语言模型的并行解码能力和生成质量，为扩散式文本生成的高效推理提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多块扩散语言模型（MBD-LM），属于连续潜空间中的语言建模方法，与 [CANONICAL] 连续语言模型与语言流 方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model decoding efficiency and quality, a top priority subtopic. Signals: Multi-block diffusion language model, Multi-block teacher forcing, Block buffer decoding, Tokens per forward pass improvement, Continuous language model acceleration

Abstract: Block Diffusion Language Models (BD-LMs) improve diffusion-based text generation with KV caching and flexible-length generation. A natural next step is to extend them from Single-Block Diffusion (SingleBD) to Multi-Block Diffusion (MultiBD), where a \textit{running-set} of consecutive blocks is decoded concurrently for inter-block parallelism. However, existing BD-LMs are mostly trained under teacher forcing, where the model observes only one noisy block conditioned on a clean prefix. While the recent diffusion forcing strategy introduces visibility among multiple noisy blocks, its training states still differ from MultiBD inference, where decoding operates on a bounded \textit{running-set} with heterogeneous slot-wise noise patterns. To bridge this gap, we propose \textit{Multi-Block Diffusion Language Models} (MBD-LMs), obtained by post-training BD-LMs with \textit{Multi-block Teacher Forcing} (MultiTF). MultiTF integrates teacher forcing and diffusion forcing by training on bounded \textit{noise-groups} conditioned on clean prefixes, with randomized \textit{noise-schedulers} that better match MultiBD inference states. To make MultiBD practically executable, we further introduce an optimized decoding algorithm based on the \textit{Block Buffer} mechanism that preserves prefix-cache reuse, keeps input shapes static, and translates increased decoding parallelism into wall-clock acceleration. Empirically, MBD-LLaDA2-Mini increases average Tokens Per Forward pass (TPF) from 3.47 to \textbf{6.19} and improves average accuracy from 79.95\% to \textbf{81.03\%}; when combined with DMax, MBD-LLaDA2-Mini-DMax reaches an average TPF of \textbf{9.34} with only a 1.02\% accuracy drop on math and code benchmarks.

</details>


### [6] [Adaptive Block Diffusion: Resolving Training-Inference Mismatch in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29275)
*Gagan Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应块扩散（ABD），通过将去噪配置视为随机变量，在完整配置空间上训练单一扩散语言模型，解决了固定上下文结构训练与任意配置推理之间的不匹配问题，避免了推理时的性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型在固定上下文结构下训练（如仅对特定位置的 token 子集去噪），但推理时模型需要处理任意配置，导致训练-推理不匹配，模型在训练网格外出现性能退化。

Method: 将去噪配置（如 prefix-window 结构）视为随机变量，在完整配置空间上优化去噪风险，训练单一模型覆盖所有可能的解码策略配置，无需修改架构。

Result: ABD 在不同解码尺度下表现出结构不变性，避免了离网格退化，恢复了块大小与困惑度之间的单调关系，在目标尺度上匹配或超越固定块专家模型。

Conclusion: 训练分布的支持集决定了跨解码策略的泛化能力，ABD 保证任何推理策略只要其配置在训练中被覆盖即可达到最优去噪效果，有效解决了扩散语言模型的训练-推理不匹配问题。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散语言模型（DLM）的训练-推理不匹配问题，属于连续语言模型范畴。现有 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "连续语言模型与语言流" 明确包含扩散语言模型（diffusion language model），因此直接复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Directly addresses training-inference mismatch in diffusion language models, a core challenge for continuous language model development. Signals: diffusion language model, training-inference mismatch, adaptive block diffusion, continuous language model, denoising optimality

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) are typically trained under fixed context structures, restricting denoising to predetermined token subsets. This creates a mismatch between training and inference, where models must operate over arbitrary configurations, leading to degradation off the training grid. We propose Adaptive Block Diffusion (ABD), which resolves this mismatch by optimizing denoising risk over a distribution of prefix-window configurations. By treating the configuration as a stochastic variable, ABD trains a single model over the full configuration space without architectural changes. We show that generalization across decoding strategies is governed by the support of the training distribution, and that ABD guarantees denoising optimality for any inference policy whose configurations are covered during training. Empirically, ABD exhibits structural invariance across decoding scales, avoiding off-grid collapse and recovering a monotonic relationship between block size and perplexity, while matching or outperforming fixed-block specialists at their target scales.

</details>

### [7] [FADA: Few-Shot Domain Adaptation via Dynamics Alignment for Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28476)
*Angchen Xie, Nikhil Sobanbabu, Ishayu Shikhare, Alan Wang, Max Simchowitz, Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: FADA 提出一种 Planner-IDM（规划器-逆动力学模型）三阶段框架，通过少量目标域 rollout 数据微调逆动力学模型，实现人形机器人在动力学偏移下的高效少样本域自适应控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人控制面临目标域动力学不匹配问题（如地形、负载、执行器响应变化），现有零样本迁移或上下文适应方法缺乏目标域特化，而传统自适应方法需要大量目标域数据或最优示范，难以实用。

Method: FADA 采用三阶段框架：1）使用特权信息训练 oracle 策略；2）通过 DAgger 将 oracle 行为蒸馏为可部署的 Planner-IDM 学生模型；3）部署时冻结规划器，仅用约2分钟的目标域 rollout 数据（动作-观测对）通过标准监督学习微调 IDM，使其动作生成与目标域动力学对齐。

Result: 实验表明 FADA 在动力学偏移下优于上下文适应和端到端适应基线，并在真实人形机器人上成功执行多种高精度全身任务。

Conclusion: FADA 通过解耦规划与逆动力学建模，仅需少量无奖励/无最优示范的目标域数据即可实现高效域自适应，为人形机器人在真实环境中的实用化部署提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究人形机器人的少样本域自适应控制，属于具身智能中的机器人控制与规划问题，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on humanoid control domain adaptation, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Few-shot domain adaptation, Humanoid control, Planner-IDM framework, Dynamics alignment, Robotics

Abstract: High-precision humanoid control is limited by target-domain dynamics mismatch, where the same control objective can induce different realized motions under changes in terrain, payload, or actuator response. Existing methods either pursue zero-shot transfer through domain randomization or in-context adaptation without target-domain specialization, or require heavy adaptation pipelines that leverage target-domain data, such as model calibration, residual learning, or policy retraining. In this paper, we present FADA (Few-Shot Domain Adaptation via Dynamics Alignment), a three-stage Planner-Inverse Dynamics Model (Planner-IDM) framework for few-shot adaptation in humanoid control. FADA first trains an oracle policy with privileged information and then distills the oracle behavior into a deployable Planner-IDM student through DAgger. At deployment, FADA freezes the planner and finetunes only the IDM using approximately 2 minutes of target-domain rollouts with standard supervised learning. Rather than requiring optimal demonstrations or rewards, FADA uses the paired actions and observations that are observed during these rollouts as supervision, aligning the IDM's action generation with target-domain dynamics. Experiments show that FADA outperforms both in-context and end-to-end adaptation baselines, improving task performance under dynamics shifts and enabling real humanoid robots to execute diverse high-precision whole-body tasks. Implementation details and qualitative hardware rollout videos are available at .

</details>


### [8] [The Speedup Paradox: Rethinking Inference Speed-Quality Trade-off in Embodied Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28529)
*Yujin Wang, Junli Chen, Yixuan Li, Shunan Dong, Huazhong Yang, Yongpan Liu, Hongyang Jia*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (68.67) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出TISED分析框架，系统研究具身任务中推理加速技术（量化、剪枝、异步推理）对任务级性能的影响，揭示了三个悖论性现象：静态任务中加速可能反而增加总完成时间；动态任务中适度有损优化可提升成功率；硬件配置会影响最优加速点的位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效推理研究主要关注静态ML任务中的单步计算成本，但具身任务涉及与环境反复交互的闭环过程，任务级性能受闭环效应影响，目前缺乏对这类效应的系统刻画。

Method: 提出TISED（Task-level Inference Speedup Effect Decomposition）分析框架，统一量化、剪枝、异步推理等多种有损加速技术，将其对静态任务和动态任务的影响进行分解分析，并通过实验验证三个悖论性效应。

Result: 发现三个悖论：(1)静态任务中加速有时反而增加端到端完成时间；(2)动态任务中适度有损优化可提升任务成功率；(3)两个效应的单调性和最优加速点随硬件配置变化。

Conclusion: 为将推理优化技术适配到具身任务提供了新视角，表明具身场景中需要重新审视传统加速-质量权衡的假设。

Classification reason: 该论文研究具身基础模型中的推理加速技术（量化、剪枝、异步推理）对任务级性能的影响，属于具身智能/机器人领域的高效推理分析，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度相关。

Importance reason: Reveals counterintuitive trade-offs in inference speed vs. task performance for embodied world models, directly relevant to your world_model and efficient inference priorities. Signals: world model, efficient inference, embodied tasks, speed-quality paradox, closed-loop effects

Abstract: Embodied foundation models have recently been widely used to improve robot generalization and task success rates. Previous works apply lossy efficient-inference techniques such as quantization, pruning, and asynchronous inference, accepting small action quality degradation in exchange for lower per-step computation cost and inter-action latency. However, unlike traditional static ML tasks, embodied tasks involve repeated interaction with the environment, and task-level performance is determined not only by per-step cost, but also by closed-loop effects unique to embodied execution, which remain insufficiently characterized in current efficient-inference studies. In this work, we propose TISED (\underline{T}ask-level \underline{I}nference \underline{S}peedup \underline{E}ffect \underline{D}ecomposition), an analytical framework that unifies diverse lossy inference optimization techniques and decomposes their effects on static and dynamic tasks, and uncovers some paradoxical effects on task-level performance: (1) on \textit{static tasks}, optimization sometimes can lengthen end-to-end per-task completion time even as per-step latency drops; (2) on \textit{dynamic tasks}, moderate lossy optimization can raise task success rate even above the baseline; and (3) the monotonicity and sweet-spot location of both effects can shift with hardware configuration. Together, our findings provide a new perspective on adapting inference optimization techniques to embodied tasks.

</details>


### [9] [J-LAW: Joint Localization and Actionable World Modeling via Coupled Latent Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28712)
*Guanqun Cao, Liang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (78.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: J-LAW 提出一种耦合因子图方法，将经典 SLAM 的度量定位与可行动世界模型的潜空间预测统一为联合优化问题，通过位姿-潜变量耦合因子实现定位与预测的相互增强，在 PushT 和 WildGS 数据集上有效降低了潜预测误差和端点漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 SLAM 仅估计度量位姿和几何地图，无法为规划提供可预测的世界模型；而动作条件世界模型学习紧凑潜动力学用于规划，但忽略全局度量一致性且开环滚动时累积漂移。作者认为这两者是同一估计问题的两个视角，应联合优化。

Method: 提出 J-LAW，构建一个耦合因子图，将度量物体位姿、潜世界状态和潜地标嵌入作为联合优化变量。引入位姿条件潜编码器和位姿-潜耦合因子作为桥梁，将观测、动作条件预测、度量里程计、位姿-潜耦合、潜闭环检测和潜地标观测统一为单个最大后验（MAP）目标中的概率因子。

Result: 在 PushT 和 WildGS 真实数据实验上，耦合图校正相比开环滚动显著降低了潜预测 RMSE 和端点漂移，潜闭环检测提升了全局轨迹一致性。J-LAW 同时输出了度量地图（位姿）和可行动地图（用于规划的潜地标）。

Conclusion: J-LAW 成功将定位与可行动世界建模统一为耦合因子图框架，证明了两个任务可以相互增强，为机器人同时获得度量一致性和可预测潜动力学提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出联合定位与可行动世界模型（J-LAW），通过耦合因子图联合优化度量物体位姿、潜世界状态和潜地标嵌入，面向机器人规划与控制。这属于具身世界模型与规划控制的典型工作，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 "具身世界模型与规划控制"。

Importance reason: Combines SLAM and world models for robotics, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: world model, SLAM, latent dynamics, factor graph, robotics

Abstract: Classical SLAM estimates metric poses and a geometric map but produces no actionable predictive model for planning. Action-conditioned world models learn compact latent dynamics for planning but ignore global metric consistency and accumulate drift under open-loop rollout. We argue these are two views of the same estimation problem and propose J-LAW (Joint Localization and Actionable World Modeling) in this letter: a coupled factor graph that jointly optimizes metric object poses, latent world states, and latent landmark embeddings. The bridge is a pose-conditioned latent encoder and a learned pose--latent coupling factor, so that better localization improves the world model and vice versa. We cast observation, action-conditioned prediction, metric odometry, pose--latent coupling, latent loop closure, and latent landmark observation as probabilistic factors in a single MAP objective. Real-data experiments on PushT and WildGS show that coupled graph correction substantially reduces latent prediction RMSE and endpoint drift relative to open-loop rollout, while latent loop closure improves global trajectory consistency. J-LAW yields a map that is simultaneously metric (poses) and actionable (latent landmarks for planning).

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 仿真实验表明 He3-Seeker 能够实现快速且高保真的氦-3分布建图，为资源勘探任务提供了可靠方案。

Conclusion: He3-Seeker 验证了机器人信息规划在月球资源勘探中的有效性，为未来深空探测与空间采矿中的自主勘探提供了可行技术路径。

Classification reason: 该论文研究月球氦-3资源勘探中的机器人主动探索与信息规划（RIP），涉及自主导航、主动感知与资源分布建图，属于具身智能中机器人规划与控制的范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on lunar robotics and resource mapping, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or efficient inference. Signals: world model, robotic information planning, lunar helium-3, active exploration, remote sensing

Abstract: Lunar helium-3 is a highly valuable strategic resource, pivotal to the advancement of both deep-space exploration and space mining. Existing lunar helium-3 exploration methodologies rely primarily on indirect measurements via remote sensing, which are often characterized by limited precision, low reliability, and insufficient spatial resolution. In this paper, we introduce He3-Seeker, an active robotic exploration method for helium-3 distribution mapping. First, we provide a formal definition of the active helium-3 exploration problem. Subsequently, we developed the He3-Seeker framework, which is conceptually based on multi-point drilling, sampling, and in situ analysis. In particular, we use robotic information planning (RIP) to guide autonomous robot navigation and active sensing. Additionally, to thoroughly evaluate the proposed algorithm, we introduce a reliable method for generating reference data of lunar helium-3 distribution based on low-resolution orbital remote sensing measurements. Simulation experiments verify that He3-Seeker achieves both rapid and high-fidelity mapping of helium-3 distribution, providing a reliable solution for resource exploration tasks. Our code and simulation environment will be publicly accessible at .

</details>


### [11] [Vision-Language Models for Deployable Social Robot Navigation: Bridging Semantic Reasoning and Low-Level Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28760)
*Runji Cai, Toshihiko Yamasaki, Ling Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (59.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文综述了视觉语言模型（VLM）在社交机器人导航（SRN）中的应用，提出了一个统一框架，将VLM的高层语义推理、低层规划控制以及连接两者的中间机制整合在一起，并讨论了数据集、评估平台和开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人导航不仅需要几何路径规划，还需要理解人类意图、社会规范和上下文线索。传统导航方法缺乏语义推理能力，而VLM虽然提供了高层理解能力，但如何将其集成到实时、安全关键的导航系统中并可靠地将高层推理转化为具体的导航动作仍是一个根本性挑战。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，系统梳理了基于VLM的社交机器人导航研究，将现有方法组织为三个相互关联的组件：高层VLM推理、低层规划与控制、以及连接推理与动作的中间机制。基于此视角，提出了一个结构化路线图，涵盖语义推理、评估器、空间定位、中间表示和控制模块。

Result: 提出了一个统一的VLM-SRN框架和结构化路线图，总结了代表性数据集和评估平台，识别了关键开放挑战（如实时性、安全性、泛化性等），并强调了混合架构对于实际部署的必要性。

Conclusion: VLM为社交机器人导航带来了语义理解和常识推理的新能力，但将其可靠地集成到实时导航系统中仍面临诸多挑战。混合架构——结合VLM的高层推理与传统控制方法的低层可靠性——是实现可部署的、社会合规的导航系统的关键方向。

Classification reason: 该论文是关于社交机器人导航中视觉语言模型应用的综述，核心关注如何将VLM的高层语义推理与低层导航控制相结合，属于机器人/具身智能中的规划控制问题，因此映射到[具身世界模型与规划控制]（CANONICAL）。同时论文涉及VLM在导航中的理解与推理，也匹配多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: Survey on VLM for social robot navigation is tangentially related to world models but lacks direct focus on user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: VLM survey, social robot navigation, semantic reasoning, low-level control, hybrid architectures

Abstract: Social robot navigation (SRN) requires more than geometric path planning; it demands understanding human intentions, social norms, and contextual cues to generate socially compliant behaviors. Although classical navigation methods provide reliable metric planning and collision avoidance, they often lack the semantic reasoning capabilities necessary for operation in complex human-centered environments. Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have opened new opportunities for SRN by enabling high-level VLM understanding, commonsense reasoning, and natural language interaction. However, a fundamental challenge remains: how to integrate VLMs into real-time, safety-critical navigation systems and reliably translate their high-level reasoning into grounded navigation actions. In this survey, we present a unified perspective of VLM-based SRN and organize existing approaches into three interconnected components: high-level VLM reasoning, low-level planning and control, and intermediate mechanisms that bridge reasoning and action. Based on this perspective, we propose a structured roadmap for coupling VLMs with navigation systems, covering semantic reasoning, evaluators, spatial grounding, intermediate representations, and control modules. The roadmap highlights both the strengths of VLMs and the necessity of hybrid architectures for practical deployment. We further review representative datasets and evaluation platforms developed for SRN. Finally, we discuss key open challenges. This survey aims to provide a foundation for building reliable, socially compliant, and deployable VLM-enabled navigation systems.

</details>

*Shuo Cheng, Chuye Zhang, Alfred Cueva, Caelan Garrett, Ajay Mandlekar, Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (56.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Human2Any 提出从人类视频中学习物体-物体交互运动先验，通过约束感知的组合规划将人类示范迁移到不同机器人上，无需目标场景的真实机器人数据即可实现鲁棒的操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频是丰富的机器人操控监督源，但直接迁移面临本体差异、场景变化和机器人可行性约束等挑战。现有方法通常需要大量目标场景的机器人数据，限制了泛化能力。

Method: Human2Any 将操控表示为物体-物体交互运动（object-object interaction motion），抽象掉本体细节，保留任务相关的场景变化。学习可复用的交互先验后，结合机器人侧的可行性推理（feasibility reasoning）和运动规划（motion planning）进行组合式规划，使同一人类知识适应不同本体、场景几何和任务上下文。

Result: 在 Franka 桌面平台和 RBY-1 人形移动机器人上进行了真实世界实验，展示了无需真实机器人训练数据的鲁棒交互中心操控。

Conclusion: Human2Any 验证了从人类视频中学习物体交互先验并迁移到不同机器人本体的可行性，为减少机器人数据需求提供了有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从人类视频中学习可复用的物体交互先验，并将其迁移到不同机器人（Franka桌面机械臂、RBY-1人形机器人）上，结合机器人侧可行性推理与运动规划实现操控。这属于具身智能中从人类示范到机器人策略迁移的经典问题，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robot transfer from human video, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: world model, robot manipulation, human-to-robot transfer, constraint-aware planning, object-centric interaction priors

Abstract: Human videos are a scalable source of supervision for robot manipulation, as they are abundant and naturally capture rich object interactions. However, transferring human demonstrations to robots remains challenging due to embodiment mismatch, scene variation, and robot-specific feasibility constraints. We present Human2Any, a framework for learning reusable object-centric interaction priors from human videos without requiring real-world robot demonstrations in the target task contexts. Human2Any represents manipulation through object-object interaction motion, capturing task-relevant scene changes while abstracting away embodiment-specific details. It composes learned interaction priors with robot-side feasibility reasoning and motion planning, allowing the same human-derived knowledge to adapt to different embodiments, scene geometries, and task contexts. We validate Human2Any across diverse manipulation settings, including real-world experiments on a Franka tabletop setup and an RBY-1 humanoid mobile robot, demonstrating robust interaction-centric manipulation without real-world robot training data. Project website: .

*Shuning Li, Sikai Li, Jiachen Li, Mingyu Ding*

TL;DR: AnyBody 提出一种统一全身人形控制器，通过教师-学生蒸馏学习单一潜空间运动表征，并训练基于掩码自注意力的关键点编码器，支持部署时任意关键点子集引导的全身控制，无需全身动捕或轨迹重定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于物理的人形跟踪方法要么依赖昂贵的全身动捕和易出错的轨迹重定向，限制了数据采集和策略学习的可扩展性；要么将上下半身控制分解为分离的层次化表示，牺牲了全身协调运动。本文旨在弥合这一差距，实现任意关键点子集即可驱动的统一全身控制。

Method: 首先在大规模无结构运动语料上训练特权教师跟踪器，然后在线蒸馏为确定性编码器-解码器学生网络，其潜空间为单位球面。训练一个基于掩码自注意力的Transformer关键点编码器，接受任意身体关键点子集，并将其对齐到特权潜空间。将冻结的解码器作为运动先验，在潜空间中使用轻量级残差校正器专门化下游任务。

Result: AnyBody 能够从任意关键点子集跟踪大规模人体运动，支持自由形式控制、灵活遥操作，并学习包括行走、空中书写和障碍物到达在内的下游行为。

Conclusion: AnyBody 通过统一的潜空间运动表征和任意关键点引导机制，有效解决了全身人形控制中数据瓶颈和协调性问题，为可扩展的人形机器人控制提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出全身人形机器人控制器，通过潜空间运动表征实现任意关键点子集引导的全身控制，属于具身智能中的机器人控制与策略学习，与[CANONICAL]具身世界模型与规划控制高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and embodied control with a novel unified whole-body humanoid controller using latent motion representation and arbitrary keypoint guidance. Signals: world model, embodied control, unified whole-body humanoid, latent motion representation, arbitrary keypoint guidance

Abstract: We present AnyBody, a unified whole-body humanoid controller driven by an arbitrary subset of body keypoints chosen at deploy time. Prior physics-based trackers either rely on expensive full-body motion capture and error-prone trajectory retargeting, which bottleneck scalable data collection and policy learning, or decompose upper- and lower-body control into separate hierarchical representations, sacrificing the coordinated whole-body motions that loco-manipulation requires. We close this gap by learning a single latent motion representation that any keypoint subset can address. To achieve this, we first train a privileged teacher tracker on a large unstructured motion corpus and distill it online into a deterministic encoder-decoder student whose latent space is a unit sphere. We then train a transformer keypoint encoder that admits any subset of body keypoints through masked self-attention, aligning it to the privileged latent. Additionally, we treat the frozen decoder as a motor prior and specialize downstream tasks with a lightweight residual corrector in the latent space. We demonstrate the effectiveness of AnyBody by tracking large-scale human motions from arbitrary keypoint subsets, free-form control, flexibly teleoperating, and learning downstream behaviors including locomotion, in-air writing, and obstacle-reach.

</details>


### [14] [ReGuide: From Test-Time Guidance to Self-Improving Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28939)
*Tzu-Hsiang Lin, Srinivas Shakkottai, Dileep Kalathil, P. R. Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: ReGuide 是一个自改进框架，通过阶段条件引导（PCG）在测试时生成纠正性 rollout，并将成功的纠正轨迹作为可复用的 on-policy 数据回注到扩散策略中进行微调或重训练，从而缓解行为克隆扩散策略的协变量偏移问题。在 Robomimic 多个任务上，成功率提升 1.3-7.7 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆的扩散策略虽然表达能力强大，但容易受到协变量偏移的影响——微小的状态偏差会累积导致任务失败。现有方法要么需要昂贵的专家纠正或数据增强来扩展训练分布，要么在测试时用引导来修正但丢弃了纠正后的轨迹。作者希望将测试时引导产生的纠正轨迹作为可复用的数据来持续改进策略。

Method: ReGuide 包含两个核心组件：(1) Phase-Conditioned Guidance (PCG)——构建阶段特定的潜空间目标，仅在"漂移但可恢复"的阶段施加引导，通过估计的干净动作来匹配动力学模型的训练分布，生成纠正性 rollout；(2) ReGuide-FT（微调当前 checkpoint）和 ReGuide-FS（在增强数据集上从头训练）两种方式将成功的引导 rollout 吸收回策略中，两者可组合迭代。

Result: 在 Robomimic 的 Can、Square、Transport 和 Tool Hang 四个任务上，ReGuide 将基础策略的成功率提升 1.3-7.7 倍，在仅测试时引导的设置下优于 LPB 基线。匹配数据量的消融实验表明，性能提升来自引导产生的纠正数据，而非单纯的额外 rollout。

Conclusion: ReGuide 证明了测试时引导产生的纠正轨迹可以有效地回注到扩散策略中实现自我改进，为缓解行为克隆策略的协变量偏移提供了一种无需额外专家数据的高效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ReGuide 框架，通过测试时引导（PCG）生成纠正性轨迹并回注到扩散策略中微调，以缓解行为克隆中的协变量偏移问题。论文聚焦机器人操作任务的扩散策略改进与自我提升，属于具身智能中的动作规划与控制，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Focuses on diffusion policy for robotics, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: diffusion policy, self-improving framework, test-time guidance, covariate shift, robotics

Abstract: Behavior-cloned diffusion policies are expressive but remain vulnerable to covariate shift: small deviations from demonstrated states can compound into task failure. Existing methods address this either by expanding the training distribution through expert corrections or synthetic augmentation, or by steering a frozen policy at test time with guidance from a learned model. The former can be expensive or assumption-dependent, while the latter discards the corrected trajectories after execution. We introduce ReGuide, a self-improving framework that treats guided rollouts as reusable on-policy recovery data. ReGuide first uses Phase-Conditioned Guidance (PCG) to generate corrective rollouts: it constructs phase-specific latent targets, applies guidance only in the drifted-but-recoverable regime, and guides through the estimated clean action to match the dynamics model's training distribution. Successful guided rollouts are then absorbed back into the policy through ReGuide-FT, which fine-tunes the current checkpoint, or ReGuide-FS, which retrains from scratch on the augmented dataset; the two can also be composed and iterated. On Robomimic Can, Square, Transport, and Tool Hang, ReGuide improves base-policy success by $1.3$--$7.7\times$, outperforms LPB in the test-time-only setting, and matched-data ablations show that the gains come from guided recovery data rather than additional rollouts alone.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习中的世界模型通常针对特定任务或场景训练，缺乏可迁移的动力学先验。本文旨在通过大规模预训练学习通用的、可迁移的动作-视觉动力学先验，使世界模型能够跨任务、跨场景泛化，同时服务于模拟器评估和策略学习两个互补目标。

Method: 提出 A2World，一个多视角交互式扩散世界模型。在大规模机器人操作数据（含真实动作标注）上预训练，模型学习预测动作如何驱动视觉场景演化。预训练后，模型可沿两条路径适配：(1) A2World-sim：微调为任务/场景专用模拟器，支持长程 rollout 用于策略评估；(2) A2World-policy：微调为视频-动作联合预测模型，在视觉和指令条件下预测动作。

Result: 在仿真基准和真实机器人设置上的实验表明，动作条件世界模型预训练能够学习到可迁移的动力学先验，在模拟器评估和策略学习两个任务上均取得良好效果，验证了预训练动力学先验的迁移能力。

Conclusion: 动作条件世界模型预训练是一种可扩展的学习可迁移动力学先验的方法，能够同时服务于模拟器评估和策略学习，为机器人学习提供了一种统一的世界模型预训练范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究动作条件的世界模型预训练，使用大规模机器人操作数据训练多视角交互式扩散世界模型 A2World，并验证其在模拟器策略评估和策略学习中的迁移能力。这完全符合 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 的定义，即面向机器人、具身智能、动作规划和控制的世界模型。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and video generation priorities, with transferable dynamics priors for robotics. Signals: world model, video diffusion, action-conditioned, transferable dynamics, multi-view

Abstract: We study action-conditioned world modeling as a scalable way to learn transferable dynamics priors for robot learning. By pretraining a model to predict how actions drive visual scene evolution, the resulting world model captures reusable interaction dynamics beyond appearance-level video generation. Concretely, we pretrain a multi-view interactive base diffusion world model, A2World, on large-scale robot manipulation data with real action annotations. We validate the learned dynamics priors from two complementary perspectives. First, we adapt A2World into a task- or scene-specialized real-world simulator, A2World-sim, whose long-horizon rollouts support simulator-based policy evaluation and scalable what-if analysis by replacing real-robot rollouts with world model rollouts. Second, starting from the same pretrained weights, we adapt A2World into a video-action joint prediction model, A2World-policy, that predicts actions under visual and instruction conditioning. Experiments across simulation benchmarks and real-robot settings demonstrate that action-conditioned world model pretraining yields transferable dynamics priors that benefit both simulator-centric and policy-centric robot learning.

</details>


### [16] [Cognitive World Models for Process-Level Social Influence Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29495)

TL;DR: 本文提出CogWM（认知世界模型），一个基于LLM的用户模型，通过预测用户信念、欲望、意图和情绪（BDI/E）等内部认知状态的变化来评估社交影响力对话。CogWM同时作为用户模拟器和评估平台，采用三级评估框架（轮级忠实度、轨迹级状态动态、任务级综合评分），在15万样本上训练，情感准确率达77.6%，并能有效区分不同商业对话代理的认知影响力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交影响力对话评估方法（如BLEU/ROUGE或单次LLM评分）只能评估表面文本质量或给出单一分数，无法捕捉对话过程中用户内部认知状态（信念、欲望、意图、情绪）的渐进变化。作者认为，社交影响力的核心在于用户认知状态的改变，需要一种过程导向的评估方法。

Method: 提出CogWM框架：1）基于LLM的用户模型，联合预测BDI/E认知状态和用户话语；2）三级评估框架：轮级忠实度（turn-level fidelity）、轨迹级状态动态（trajectory-level state dynamics）、任务级综合评分（task-level composite scoring）；3）Summarize-and-Allocate (SaA) 标注流程，在四个社交影响力场景上构建150,454条用户轮次样本进行训练。

Result: CogWM在情感准确率上达到77.6%，是GPT-5.5的2.1倍。在3600次多智能体判别试验中，能有效区分六个商业对话代理的认知影响力，其中Llama-4-Scout排名第一（CTS +0.233）。

Conclusion: CogWM将社交影响力对话评估从终端判断（terminal judgment）转向过程追踪（process tracking），通过建模用户内部认知状态的动态演变，提供了比传统表面指标更深入的评估视角。该方法可作为用户模拟器和评估平台，为社交影响力对话系统的开发提供支持。

Classification reason: 该论文提出CogWM，一个基于LLM的认知世界模型，用于模拟和评估社交影响力对话中用户内部认知状态（信念、欲望、意图、情绪）的演变。虽然不涉及物理世界或机器人，但论文明确自称为"世界模型"（Cognitive World Model），且属于"用户模拟器+评估平台"的广义世界模型范畴。最接近的[CANONICAL]子主题是"具身世界模型与规划控制"，但该子主题主要面向机器人/具身智能。考虑到world_model方向下没有更合适的子主题（video_world_model、interactive_physical_world、spatial_3d_4d_world_model均不匹配），而embodied_world_model作为最宽泛的[CANONICAL]子主题可涵盖广义世界模型，故选择复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on social influence dialogue evaluation, not on core continuous multimodal or video generation priorities. Signals: world model, LLM-based user model, cognitive state prediction, social influence dialogue, process-level evaluation

Abstract: Social influence dialogue changes user behavior by altering internal cognitive states. The central evaluation question is whether the user's beliefs, desires, intentions, and emotions measurably change over the course of conversation, a process-oriented criterion that neither surface-level text metrics (BLEU/ROUGE) nor single-score LLM judgments can capture. We propose the \textbf{Cog}nitive \textbf{W}orld \textbf{M}odel \textbf{(CogWM)}, an LLM-based user model that reframes multi-turn dialogue evaluation from ``what did the user say'' to ``how did the user's internal cognitive state evolves.'' CogWM jointly predicts BDI/E cognitive states and user utterances and serves as both a user simulator and an evaluation platform, using a three-tier evaluation framework that covers turn-level fidelity, trajectory-level state dynamics, and task-level composite scoring. Trained via our \textbf{S}ummarize-\textbf{a}nd-\textbf{A}llocate \textbf{(SaA)} annotation pipeline on 150,454 user-turn samples across four social influence scenarios, CogWM achieves 77.6\% emotion accuracy (2.1$\times$ over GPT-5.5). In 3600 multi-agent discrimination trials, it distinguishes six commercial agents by their cognitive influence, with Llama-4-Scout ranking first (CTS +0.233). CogWM moves social influence dialogue evaluation from terminal judgment to process tracking. We have released our code\footnote{\scriptsize Code: } and models\footnote{Model: }.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文发现机器人动作序列在DCT频谱域中存在语义分解：低频分量对应全局运动意图，高频分量对应精确时序与接触行为。基于此提出Causal Spectral Policy (CSP)，将动作生成建模为因果粗到细过程：先预测粗粒度运动轨迹，再条件式生成细粒度修正。在仿真和真实实验中，CSP在精度敏感操作任务上显著优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作任务中，动作序列包含不同层次的语义信息（任务级运动意图 vs. 执行级精确调整），但现有策略学习方法往往将两者混为一谈，难以同时兼顾全局规划与局部精度。作者希望通过频谱分析揭示这种层次结构，并据此设计更有效的分层策略学习方法。

Method: 1) 使用离散余弦变换(DCT)对机器人动作序列进行频谱分析，发现低频分量编码全局运动轨迹，高频分量编码精确时序/对齐/接触行为。2) 提出Causal Spectral Policy (CSP)：先根据观测和语言指令预测粗粒度运动（低频DCT系数），再基于已实现的轨迹条件式生成细粒度修正（高频DCT系数），形成因果粗到细的生成过程。3) 提出类人遥操作噪声注入作为数据增强方法，提升对噪声演示的鲁棒性。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人操作任务上，CSP在精度敏感任务（如精密装配、对齐等）上一致优于多种强基线方法。在噪声演示数据下，CSP展现出较强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 频谱分解揭示了机器人动作序列的层次语义结构，基于此设计的因果粗到细策略学习范式能够有效解耦全局运动规划与局部精确执行，在精度敏感操作任务上取得显著提升，且对噪声演示具有鲁棒性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于频谱分解的分层策略学习（CSP），将机器人动作生成建模为从粗到细的因果过程，属于机器人动作规划与控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong robotics manipulation paper but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: DCT spectral decomposition for robot actions, causal coarse-to-fine policy, precision-sensitive manipulation, teleoperation noise injection, world model adjacent but not core

Abstract: In this paper, we identify a semantic decomposition in robot action sequences, separating task-level motion intent from execution-level refinements. By analyzing actions in the spectral domain using the discrete cosine transform (DCT), we observe that low-frequency components capture global motion trajectories, while high-frequency components encode precise timing, alignment, and contact behaviors. Motivated by this structure, we propose Causal Spectral Policy (CSP), which models action generation as a causal coarse-to-fine process: coarse motion is predicted from observation and language, and fine corrections are generated conditionally on the realized trajectory. Across simulation and real-world evaluations, CSP consistently outperforms strong baselines on precision-sensitive manipulation tasks. Additionally, we propose human-inspired teleoperation noise injection as a data augmentation method, under which our approach demonstrates strong robustness to noisy demonstrations.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出 GPC（Generative Pretrained Controllers），通过端到端强化学习联合优化有限标量量化（FSQ）的"运动词汇表"和对应的控制策略，再用 GPT 风格的自回归 Transformer 建模词汇表结构，实现基于 next-token 预测的物理仿真角色通用控制器，在大量运动片段上达到 99.98% 的成功率，并展现出抗扰动、跌倒恢复等涌现行为。


<details>

Method: 提出 GPC 框架：1）使用端到端强化学习联合优化 FSQ（Finite Scalar Quantization）编码的离散"运动词汇表"和对应的控制策略；2）学习到码本后，用 GPT 风格的自回归 Transformer 对词汇表结构进行建模，通过 next-token 预测生成物理控制信号；3）提出一套适应技术（finetuning）将预训练控制器迁移到新的下游任务。

Result: 在大量运动片段上达到 99.98% 的重现成功率；控制器展现出自然的涌现行为，如对扰动的响应和跌倒恢复行为；在多种下游任务中表现出高度的鲁棒性和通用性。

Importance reason: Strong world model and autoregressive token prediction, but focuses on motor control rather than user's priority subtopics. Signals: world model, autoregressive transformer, next-token prediction, physics-based character animation, emergent behaviors

Abstract: Developing controllers capable of completing a wide range of tasks in a natural and life-like manner is a key challenge in enabling practical applications of physics-based character animation. In this work, we introduce Generative Pretrained Controllers (GPC), which leverage tokenization and next-token modeling to create general-purpose, reusable generative controllers from large-scale motion datasets. Our framework utilizes end-to-end reinforcement learning to jointly optimize a "motion vocabulary", modeled via Finite Scalar Quantization (FSQ), along with a corresponding control policy that can map the discrete codes to physics-based controls. After the "codebook" has been learned, the underlying structure of this large vocabulary is modeled by training a GPT-style autoregressive transformer, leading to a powerful generative controller that generates controls for a physically simulated character by performing next-token prediction. Once the generative controller has been trained, we propose a suite of adaptation techniques for finetuning the controller for new downstream tasks. Our proposed framework greatly simplifies the training process compared to previous tokenized methods, and achieves a 99.98% success rate in reproducing a vast corpus of motion clips. The generative controller exhibits a variety of natural emergent behaviors, such as responsive behaviors to perturbations and recovery behaviors after falling. This results in highly robust general purpose controllers for a variety of downstream applications.

</details>

TL;DR: STEAM 提出一种自监督时序集成优势建模方法，从专家演示中学习帧级优势信号，无需人工标注即可区分机器人轨迹中的有效进展与失败/回退行为，在多种真实世界机器人任务中显著提升策略成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界机器人学习依赖异构数据，但演示和 rollout 数据中常混有停滞、修正和次优行为。现有方法缺乏帧级优势信号来区分可靠的局部进展与失败/回退，而人工标注成本高昂。因此需要一种无标签的方法来自动学习这种优势信号。

Method: STEAM 训练一个时序偏移预测器集成（ensemble），在专家轨迹的帧对上使用归一化时序偏移作为自监督信号。每个预测器将帧对映射到时序偏移分布，再转换为标量优势值。集成中取最小优势值对混合质量的 rollout 数据进行保守评分。该优势信号可与 CFGRL 等策略学习方法结合使用。

Result: 在真实世界的双臂毛巾折叠、芯片结账、可乐补货和单臂拾取放置任务中，STEAM 能有效识别停滞、失败和恢复行为。结合 CFGRL 后，策略成功率分别提升 59%、54.3%、23% 和 16.2%。

Conclusion: STEAM 提供了一种无需人工标注的帧级优势建模方法，能够有效提升真实世界机器人策略学习的性能，具有较好的通用性和实用性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究真实世界机器人学习中的策略学习与优势建模，属于具身智能/机器人领域，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robot learning, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Self-supervised learning, Robot learning, Real-world tasks, Policy improvement, No manual annotation

Abstract: Real-world robot learning increasingly relies on heterogeneous data, but demonstrations and rollouts often mix useful progress with stalls, corrections, and suboptimal behavior. Effective policy learning therefore requires frame-level advantages that distinguish reliable local progress from failures and regressions. We propose Self-supervised Temporal Ensemble Advantage Modeling (STEAM), a label-free method that learns such advantages from expert demonstrations. STEAM trains an ensemble of temporal-offset predictors on frame pairs within expert trajectories, using the normalized temporal offset between two frames as a self-supervised signal. Each predictor maps a frame pair to a distribution over temporal offsets, which is converted into a scalar advantage. STEAM then takes the minimum advantage across the ensemble to score mixed-quality rollout data conservatively. Across real-world bimanual towel folding, chip checkout, cola restocking, and single-arm pick-and-place tasks, STEAM identifies stalls, failures, and recoveries. When combined with CFGRL, STEAM further improves policy success rate by 59%, 54.3%, 23% and 16.2% over baselines, respectively.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA强化学习方法通常依赖外部环境反馈（如预定义的成功信号）来指导策略更新，这在实际部署中难以获取。作者发现VLA模型自身具备内部评估能力——高置信度生成的轨迹更可能成功，因此希望利用这一内在信号实现无需外部奖励的自我提升。

Method: 提出T²VLA框架，核心包括：(1) 将轨迹级生成置信度与高置信度专家演示的相似度作为内在奖励信号；(2) 置信度驱动的双专家引导机制（Confidence-Driven Dual Expert Bootstrapping），动态平衡局部伪专家（Local Pseudo-Expert）用于探索和全局专家池（Global Expert Pool）用于训练稳定性；(3) 架构无关的测试时RL框架，适用于不同VLA范式。

Result: 在LIBERO和RoboTwin基准上，T²VLA一致优于监督基线，并接近使用真实奖励的oracle RL性能，且适用于OpenVLA-OFT和pi系列等多种VLA架构。

Conclusion: VLA模型内部的高置信度信号可以作为有效的内在奖励，驱动无需外部反馈的测试时强化学习，为机器人策略的自适应提升提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉-语言-动作模型(VLA)在机器人具身任务中的测试时强化学习，属于具身智能与机器人规划控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]这一规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel test-time RL for VLAs using internal confidence, but focuses on robotics, not user's priority subtopics. Signals: test-time RL, VLA, confidence-driven, self-bootstrapping, robotics

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become indispensable for pushing Vision-Language-Action Models (VLAs) beyond static imitation learning. However, existing RL methods typically require external environmental feedback, relying on predefined success signals to guide policy updates. In this work, we show that VLA models possess useful internal evaluative capabilities: in discrete-action VLAs, trajectories with higher generation confidence are significantly more likely to succeed. Based on this observation, we introduce T^2VLA (Test-time VLA), an architecture-agnostic test-time RL framework that enables VLA models to achieve self-bootstrapping policy improvement. Instead of relying on external rewards, T^2VLA leverages trajectory-level similarity to high-confidence expert demonstrations as an intrinsic reward signal. In addition, we propose a Confidence-Driven Dual Expert Bootstrapping mechanism, which dynamically balances a Local Pseudo-Expert for exploration and a Global Expert Pool for training stability. Extensive experiments on the LIBERO and RoboTwin benchmarks show that T^2VLA consistently outperforms supervised baselines and approaches oracle RL performance with ground-truth rewards, achieving effective improvement without external reward feedback. Furthermore, T^2VLA adapts to distinct VLA paradigms, including both OpenVLA-OFT and the pi series.

</details>

<details>

Result: 在多个导航基准上，SWAM在成功率、轨迹精度和推理效率上显著超越最先进的两阶段规划器，并展现出对未见环境的鲁棒零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: SWAM通过任务中心的联合生成范式有效解决了传统两阶段方法的候选依赖和计算开销问题，验证了联合观察-动作生成在具身导航中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出SWAM，一个面向具身导航的观察-动作联合生成框架，属于具身智能中的世界模型规划与控制，与[CANONICAL]具身世界模型与规划控制高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and embodied navigation priorities, with novel joint observation-action generation. Signals: world model, embodied navigation, joint observation-action generation, spatial priors, zero-shot generalization

Abstract: Existing world model-based planners for visual navigation typically follow a verification-centric paradigm, decoupling goal intent from trajectory synthesis. This approach suffers from candidate dependence, heavy computational overhead, and inconsistencies between sampled actions and predicted visuals. To address these issues, we propose SWAM (Spatial-perceiving World Action Model), a task-centric joint observation-action generation framework. Given start and goal RGB observations, SWAM performs single-pass inference to simultaneously generate intermediate RGB-D sequences and corresponding action trajectories, promoting goal-consistent trajectory generation and improved spatial feasibility. While SWAM leverages depth pseudo-labels during training to internalize spatial priors, it requires only monocular RGB input at inference time. We further introduce a visual-guided action refinement module and a trajectory-scale regularization loss to enforce fine-grained alignment between motion and visual cues while stabilizing predictions across varying distances. Extensive experiments show that SWAM significantly outperforms state-of-the-art two-stage planners in success rate, trajectory accuracy, and inference efficiency, while demonstrating robust zero-shot generalization to unseen environments.

</details>

<details>

Importance reason: Focuses on quadrotor navigation, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: InstanceImageNav, quadrotor navigation, cross-graph memory, viewpoint planning, hierarchical framework

Abstract: Instance-Specific Image-Goal Navigation (InstanceImageNav) requires a robot to navigate toward the exact object instance depicted in a query image. Extending this task to quadrotors is challenging due to continuous 3D control, limited field of view (FOV), and safety constraints, which make successful navigation highly dependent on selecting informative viewpoints. We propose a hierarchical navigation framework for quadrotor InstanceImageNav that separates high-level decision making from low-level motion execution. Instead of navigating directly to spatial locations, the system generates viewpoint-aware action nodes around frontier regions and potential target objects, enabling the robot to explore while maintaining informative viewpoints for detecting the target instance. A lightweight semantic memory maintains object-level and observation-level context, allowing semantic cues to propagate to candidate action nodes for decision making. A learning-based policy selects the most promising action node, and a trajectory planner generates dynamically feasible 3D flight paths for safe execution. Experiments in simulation demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines, and real-world quadrotor flights validate the practicality and robustness of the proposed framework.

</details>

TL;DR: SAGA是一个面向长时域策略游戏的LLM多智能体框架，通过地图语义场景图、工具增强规划器和双视野反馈循环三个机制，分别解决空间盲视、上下文溢出/领域耦合和跨回合浅层学习问题，在FreeCiv上取得最高文明得分并降低27%输出token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在复杂策略游戏中存在三个系统性失败：原始坐标导致的场景盲视、整体状态转储导致的上下文溢出与领域耦合、以及将每回合孤立对待导致的跨回合浅层学习。需要一种能同时解决这些问题的框架。

Method: SAGA包含三个核心机制：(1) 地图语义场景图（MSSG），将实体间的类型化空间关系编码为每个单位的自然语言上下文，解决空间盲视而不增加全局token；(2) 工具增强规划器（TAP），按需拉取细粒度领域状态并分派指令给专用控制器，消除上下文溢出和领域耦合；(3) 双视野反馈循环（DHFL），结合回合内目标生成与跨回合因果事后分析，实现无需人工奖励工程的策略演化。

Result: 在FreeCiv上，SAGA取得最高平均文明得分且方差低于两个最强基线；是唯一在基础设施建设上显著超越所有基线的方法；在多数直接对战中胜出两个最强基线；输出token减少27%；跨回合演化模块使连续五回合的最终得分达到最高。

Conclusion: SAGA通过三个针对性机制有效解决了LLM智能体在复杂策略游戏中的系统性问题，消融实验证实每个组件独立贡献优势，表明该框架在长时域战略规划任务中具有显著效果和效率优势。

Classification reason: 该论文提出SAGA多智能体框架，用于复杂策略游戏（FreeCiv）中的长时域战略规划，涉及场景理解、工具增强规划、跨回合反馈循环等机制，属于具身/游戏世界模型中的规划与控制方向，与[CANONICAL]具身世界模型与规划控制（embodied_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong LLM multi-agent planning framework for strategy games, but outside user's core priority subtopics. Signals: LLM multi-agent, long-horizon planning, strategy game, scene graph, tool-augmented planner

Abstract: Long-horizon strategic planning in complex strategy games demands concurrent reasoning across multiple decision domains under imperfect information and sparse reward. Existing LLM-based agents suffer from three systematic failures: scene blindness from raw tile coordinates, context overflow and domain coupling from monolithic state dumps, and shallow cross-game learning that treats each episode in isolation. We present SAGA, an LLM multi-agent framework with three mechanisms each directly targeting one class of failure: (i) a Map-Semantic Scene Graph that encodes typed spatial relations among game entities into per-unit natural-language context, resolving spatial blindness without global token inflation; (ii) a Tool-Augmented Planner that pulls fine-grained domain state on demand and dispatches per-domain directives to dedicated specialist controllers, eliminating context overflow, domain coupling, and mechanical constraint violations; and (iii) a Dual-Horizon Feedback Loop that combines periodic within-game goal generation with structured cross-game causal post-mortem, enabling principled strategic evolution without manual reward engineering. Evaluated on FreeCiv, SAGA attains the highest mean civilization score -- the environment's sole sparse objective reward -- with lower variance than the two strongest baselines, and is the only method that significantly surpasses every baseline on infrastructure construction, the resource axis most readily sacrificed under multi-objective conflict. It outscores the two strongest baselines in most head-to-head games while cutting output tokens (the dominant decoding cost) by 27%. Equipped with the cross-game evolution module, SAGA reaches the highest end-of-chain score across five successive episodes. Ablation studies confirm that each architectural component contributes independently to this advantage.

</details>

*Giuliano Pioldi, Yashika Batra, Arman Ibrayeva, Yuanchen Bai, Purnjay Maruur, Promise Ekpo, Angelique Taylor*

TL;DR: REPAIR-Bench 是一个面向人机交互中机器人故障感知与交互恢复的基准，包含 214 次交互试验、四种故障类型、同步面部动作单元/头部姿态/语音转录/情感报告等数据，以及故障检测、故障类型分类、用户偏好恢复预测三项评测任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常将故障视为独立事件、仅关注二分类故障检测、并使用基于规则的恢复建模，缺乏对用户感知和恢复偏好的系统性研究。

Method: 构建包含 41 名参与者、214 次交互试验的基准数据集，涵盖四种诱导故障类型，提供同步多模态数据（面部动作单元、头部姿态、语音转录、情感与恢复报告）。设计三项评测任务：跨会话故障检测（层次循环建模）、视觉故障类型分类、基于上下文的用户偏好恢复预测（QLoRA 微调 Mistral-7B）。

Result: 层次循环模型在故障检测上严格 F1=0.80（优于单会话模型 0.68），故障定位平均符号误差 -0.51s、中位绝对误差 2.97s，恢复预测 Hit@5=0.76、F1@5=0.32。

Conclusion: REPAIR-Bench 为 HRI 和医疗 HRI 社区提供了标准化框架，用于评估机器人故障并构建透明、自适应、可信赖的恢复系统。

Classification reason: 该论文研究人机交互中机器人故障的感知与恢复，属于机器人具身智能中的世界模型与规划控制范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on robot failure recovery in HRI, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: robot failure detection, human-robot interaction, recovery prediction, benchmark, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Understanding how users perceive and respond to robot failures is essential for building robust and trustworthy robot systems. Prior work, however, (i) often treats failures as independent events, (ii) emphasizes binary failure detection, (iii) with rule-based recovery modeling. We present REPAIR-Bench, built on 214 interaction trials from 41 participants, the benchmark spans four induced failure types and provides synchronized facial action units, head pose, speech transcripts, and post-interaction affect and recovery reports. The benchmark spans three novel evaluation tasks that jointly capture the lifecycle of failure in human-robot interaction (HRI): (i) failure detection over inter-dependent interaction sessions, modeling longitudinal user adaptation across repeated failures; (ii) visual failure-type classification beyond binary success/failure formulations; and (iii) user-centered recovery prediction, inferring users' preferred recovery strategies from interaction context rather than relying on manually designed or rule-based strategies. In baseline experiments, hierarchical recurrent modeling improved failure detection over a single-session model (strict F1: 0.80 vs. 0.68), achieved a failure localization mean signed error of -0.51 s, median absolute error of 2.97 s and, for recovery prediction, a QLoRA-tuned Mistral-7B reached Hit@5=0.76 and F1@5=0.32. REPAIR-Bench provides both the HRI and Medical HRI communities with a standardized framework for (1) evaluating robot failures and (2) building transparent, adaptive, and trustworthy recovery systems.

</details>

TL;DR: TacEvo 提出一种基于 LLM 驱动的质量-多样性搜索框架，用于自动发现机器人触觉感知的神经网络架构。它利用 LLM 生成代码级变异和交叉，结合 MAP-Elites 算法保持架构多样性，在 ViTacTip 力回归和光栅分类任务上显著提升性能，并匹配或超越专家基线。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉图像具有传感器和物理特异性，有效的架构通常依赖专家直觉和大量手动迭代。现有神经架构搜索方法计算成本高且搜索空间受限，缺乏架构新颖性和多样性。

Method: TacEvo 使用 LLM 生成代码级变异和交叉操作，通过 MAP-Elites 质量-多样性循环保留多样化的精英架构，并优先重用能持续带来改进的提示。探索由两个行为描述符（架构多样性和效率比）引导，覆盖结构变化和计算-规模权衡。

Result: 在 ViTacTip 力回归和光栅分类任务上，TacEvo 实现了 96.0%/94.5% 的高自主生成可靠性，20 代后最佳验证适应度分别提升 56.1%/96.1%。20 次种子后搜索高保真评估中，TacEvo 在力预测上匹配专家基线，在细粒度光栅分类上超越专家基线。

Conclusion: LLM 驱动的自进化搜索为专业机器人感知中的 AI 辅助科学发现提供了一种实用范式，能够自动发现高效且多样化的触觉感知架构。

Classification reason: 该论文关注机器人触觉感知的神经架构搜索，属于具身智能中感知与模型设计范畴，与 embodied_world_model（具身世界模型与规划控制）方向相关，且该 canonical 条目涵盖机器人感知、规划与控制。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotic tactile perception, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: LLM-driven architecture search, quality-diversity algorithm, robotic tactile sensing, ViTacTip sensor, MAP-Elites

Abstract: Vision-based tactile sensing converts contact-induced surface deformation into images, enabling robots to infer contact forces and fine surface textures that are not accessible through conventional vision alone. However, tactile images are sensor- and physics-specific, so effective architectures often require expert intuition and extensive manual iteration. Existing neural architecture search (NAS) pipelines can reduce this burden, but they are often computationally expensive and restricted to hand-designed search spaces, which limits architectural novelty and diversity. We introduce TacEvo, a self-evolving architecture discovery framework that improves network designs from downstream feedback. TacEvo uses an LLM to generate code-level mutations and crossovers, and a MAP-Elites quality-diversity loop that preserves diverse elite architectures while preferentially reusing prompts that consistently yield improvements. Exploration is guided by two behavioural descriptors, Architectural Diversity and Efficiency Ratio, which encourage coverage across structural variations and compute-size trade-offs. On ViTacTip force regression and grating classification, TacEvo achieves high autonomous generation reliability (96.0%/94.5% trainable) and improves best validation fitness over 20 generations by 56.1%/96.1%. In a 20-seed post-search high-fidelity evaluation, TacEvo matches the expert baseline on force prediction and outperforms it on fine-grained grating classification. These results suggest that LLM-driven self-evolving search constitutes a practical paradigm for AI-assisted scientific discovery in specialised robotic sensing.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前具身智能体通常依赖人工设计的感知、记忆、规划、动作模块组合，架构设计空间巨大但缺乏自动化方法。虽然文本域已有Agent Architecture Search（AAS），但尚未在感知型具身智能体上通过模拟器回滚进行系统评估。本文旨在研究AAS向具身场景的迁移。

Method: 提出AgentCanvas——一种类型化图运行时，将具身执行器表示为可编辑的节点-连线程序，支持模拟器感知执行和回合级日志记录；提出KDLoop——一种编码智能体搜索流程，循环执行提议、批评、实验和蒸馏四个阶段，并在停滞时触发反思。在四个具身执行器（视觉语言导航、具身问答、语言条件操作）上评估三种AAS变体。

Result: 3×4实验矩阵表明，架构级搜索可以在具身任务上产生可部署的成功率提升，但一个高分候选被识别为存在信息泄露。同时发现文本域AAS中不明显的约束：优化信号可能被回滚噪声掩盖、搜索可能陷入局部编辑盆地、回合级信用分配即使有详细日志也只能部分实现。

Conclusion: 自动化架构搜索在具身智能体领域有潜力但面临独特挑战，包括回滚噪声、局部最优和信用分配问题。这些发现刻画了AAS在具身场景中的前景与当前局限。

Classification reason: 该论文研究具身智能体的架构自动化搜索（Agent Architecture Search），涉及感知、记忆、规划、动作模块的设计，属于具身智能体与规划控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]这一规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Bridges text-domain agent architecture search to embodied agents with systematic evaluation, aligning with world model and efficient inference priorities. Signals: Embodied agent architecture search, typed-graph runtime, simulator-aware evaluation, world model connection, rollout noise analysis

Abstract: Embodied agents are typically built as hand-designed compositions of perception, memory, planning, and action modules. This modularity exposes a large architectural design space, but current systems still rely on researcher intuition to choose where information is stored, how observations are processed, and how model calls are connected. Agent Architecture Search (AAS) automates such design for text-domain agents, but has not been systematically evaluated on perceptual embodied agents through simulator rollouts. We study this transfer. We introduce AgentCanvas, a typed-graph runtime that hosts embodied executors as editable node-and-wire programs with simulator-aware execution and episode-level logs, and KDLoop, a coding-agent search procedure that cycles through proposal, critique, experiment, and distillation, with triggered reflection after stalls. We evaluate three AAS variants across four embodied executors spanning vision-language navigation, embodied question answering, and language-conditioned manipulation. The resulting 3x4 matrix shows that architecture-level search can produce deployable and directional success-rate gains on embodied tasks, while one apparent high-scoring candidate is rejected as leak-bearing. At the same time, the experiments expose constraints that are muted in text-domain AAS: optimization signals can be masked by rollout noise, search can become trapped in local edit basins, and episode-level credit assignment only partially emerges even when detailed logs are available. These results characterize both the promise and the current limits of automated architecture search for embodied agents.

</details>

TL;DR: Event-VLA 提出利用事件相机（event camera）作为光照鲁棒的互补观测，通过动作查询路由（action-query routing）机制将事件信息注入 VLA 模型，提升机器人在低光照、近黑暗等退化条件下的操作成功率，同时保持正常光照下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型通常假设光照良好的稳定室内环境，而真实世界的具身操作常面临光照变化（如低光照、黑暗），导致 RGB 观测严重退化。现有方法缺乏对这类退化场景的鲁棒性研究，因此需要引入光照不敏感、运动敏感的互补观测模态来增强 VLA 模型的泛化能力。

Method: Event-VLA 以预训练的 VLA 模型为基础，引入事件流（event stream）作为补充输入。核心设计是动作查询路由（action-query routing）：使用可学习的动作查询（action queries）从 VLA 推理过程中提取任务相关语义，并通过门控交叉注意力（gated cross-attention）选择性聚合事件 token，构建事件感知的动作表征。这种设计在保留预训练 RGB-语言语义先验的同时，有效利用事件信息进行鲁棒动作预测。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，Event-VLA 在正常光照下保持强操作性能，在低光照退化和近黑暗的真实场景中显著提升了任务成功率，验证了事件流作为互补模态对 VLA 鲁棒性的有效性。

Conclusion: Event-VLA 通过事件增强和动作查询路由机制，有效解决了 VLA 模型在光照退化条件下的鲁棒性问题，为具身智能在复杂真实环境中的部署提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Event-VLA，一个事件增强的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）框架，用于在光照变化条件下实现鲁棒的具身操作。这属于具身智能中的动作条件策略与鲁棒控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel event camera fusion for robust VLA, but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords apply. Signals: event camera, VLA, robust manipulation, low-light, action-query routing

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become an important paradigm of embodied AI. However, existing VLA models typically assume well-lit and stable indoor settings, while real-world embodied manipulation may involve degraded RGB observations caused by illumination shifts, posing critical challenges for robust robotic manipulation. To address this gap, we propose \textbf{Event-VLA}, an event-enhanced VLA framework for generalizable manipulation across varying illumination conditions. We formulate VLA-based manipulation under degraded visibility as a practical robustness problem for RGB-centric policies, and introduce event streams as an illumination-robust, motion-sensitive complementary observation to improve robustness across visibility levels. Specifically, unlike conventional multimodal fusion that directly merges event features into the global semantic token space, Event-VLA injects event information through an action-query routing pathway. It uses learnable action queries to extract task-relevant semantics from the VLA reasoning process, and selectively aggregates event tokens via gated cross-attention to construct event-aware action representations. This design preserves the pretrained RGB-language semantic priors while effectively leveraging event information for robust action prediction. Experiments in simulation and real-world deployment show that Event-VLA maintains strong manipulation performance under normal lighting and improves success rates under low-light degradation and near-dark real-world settings.

</details>


### [28] [SA-VLA: State-aware tokenizer for improving Vision-Language-Action Models' performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30113)
*Tengyue Jiang, Chunpu Xu, Jiayue Kang, Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: SA-VLA提出了一种状态感知的动作分词器，通过将机器人当前本体感知状态（proprioceptive state）引入离散动作编码/解码过程，使每个离散动作token能表示一族状态依赖的连续动作，从而缩小离散VLA策略中的压缩差距。在12个RoboTwin操作任务上，平均成功率从0.29提升至0.56。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散动作分词器将每个离散编码映射到固定的连续动作原型，忽略了机器人当前的本体感知状态（如关节配置、物体位姿、接触条件）。在操作任务中，相同的动作token在不同状态下需要不同的连续控制，导致离散化压缩损失严重，限制了VLA策略的性能。

Method: 提出SA-VLA状态感知动作分词器，在VQ-based动作分词基础上引入两种状态注入机制：(1) 状态与动作特征之间的交叉注意力；(2) 轻量级状态适配器（state adapter），预测动作维度的调制因子，实现状态条件化的动作调制与重建。该适配器使每个离散token能表示一族状态依赖的连续动作，同时保持离散动作建模的效率和兼容性。集成到LLM-based VLA策略中，支持自回归和平行动作token解码。

Result: 在12个RoboTwin操作任务上，SA-VLA将平均成功率从最强基线（0.29）提升至0.56。在零样本sim-to-real实验中，三个真实世界任务的平均成功率从0.15提升至0.33。

Conclusion: 状态条件化的动作解码是一种简单有效的机制，能够显著缩小离散VLA策略中的压缩差距，提升机器人操作任务的成功率。

Classification reason: 该论文提出SA-VLA，一种状态感知的动作分词器，用于改进基于LLM的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，属于具身智能中机器人动作规划与控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world_model and continuous multimodal tokenization for embodied VLA, aligning with top priority subtopics. Signals: state-aware action tokenizer, discrete VLA policy improvement, world model for robotics, continuous action decoding, zero-shot sim-to-real transfer

Abstract: Discrete action tokenization provides a compact interface for autoregressive VLA policies, but accurately recovering continuous robot actions from discrete codes remains challenging. Existing tokenizers typically map each discrete code to a fixed continuous action prototype, ignoring the robot's current proprioceptive state. This limitation is particularly pronounced in manipulation, where the same action token may require different continuous controls under different joint configurations, object poses, and contact conditions. We therefore propose SA-VLA, a state-aware action tokenizer that conditions action decoding on robot state. We study two state-injection mechanisms for VQ-based action tokenization: cross-attention between state and action features, and a lightweight state adapter that predicts action-wise modulation factors for state-conditioned action modulation and reconstruction. The adapter formulation expands the effective support of a finite codebook by allowing each discrete token to represent a family of state-dependent continuous actions, while preserving the efficiency and compatibility of discrete action modeling. Integrated into an LLM-based VLA policy, SA-VLA supports both autoregressive and parallel action-token decoding with minimal changes to the model interface. On 12 RoboTwin manipulation tasks, SA-VLA improves the average success rate from 0.29 to 0.56 over the strongest tokenizer baseline. In zero-shot sim-to-real experiments on three real-world tasks, it further improves average success from 0.15 to 0.33 over the strongest tokenizer baseline. These results demonstrate that state-conditioned action decoding is a simple and effective mechanism for reducing the compression gap in discrete VLA policies.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人受益于丰富的人体运动数据，但非人形腿式机器人（如四足、六足机器人）缺乏类似的运动数据资源。直接重定向人体运动到不同形态的机器人上往往产生视觉上合理但物理不一致或难以跟踪的运动。因此，需要一种方法能够将丰富的人体运动数据转化为适用于多种非人形形态机器人的可部署行为策略。

Method: X-Morph 包含三个阶段：(1) 跨形态运动重定向——将人体运动转换为运动学上合理且保留意图的机器人参考运动；(2) 特权强化学习策略——利用重定向后的参考运动训练一个拥有完整状态信息的教师策略来跟踪这些运动；(3) 因果学生策略蒸馏——将教师策略蒸馏为仅依赖本体感知的因果学生策略，实现零样本部署。

Result: 在四足机器人、六足机器人和四足+机械臂三种形态平台上，X-Morph 训练的策略能够跟踪多样化的重定向运动，泛化到未见的人体运动，并支持视频遥操作、行为先验控制和文本条件运动生成等下游任务。

Conclusion: 大规模人体运动数据可以作为学习超越人形机器人的广泛、可复用行为先验的基础，为跨形态机器人行为学习提供了一种可扩展的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文将人体运动重定向到多种非人形腿式机器人（四足、六足、四足+机械臂）上，通过强化学习训练可部署的运动策略，属于具身智能中跨形态机器人行为学习与策略控制，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robot morphology transfer, not on the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or efficient inference. Signals: Human motion retargeting, Legged robot learning, Privileged RL, Cross-morphology, Not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Recent progress in humanoid behavior models has been driven in large part by abundant human motion data, but comparable motion data is scarce for non-humanoid legged robots such as quadrupeds, hexapods, and quadruped manipulators. A promising alternative is to repurpose human motion across embodiments; however, direct retargeting often produces motions that are visually plausible yet physically inconsistent or difficult to track under robot dynamics. We present X-Morph, a human-motion-to-robot-behavior pipeline that converts human motion into deployable locomotion and loco-manipulation policies for diverse non-humanoid legged morphologies. A cross-morphology retargeting stage converts human motions into kinematically plausible, intent-preserving robot references, which are then tracked by a privileged RL policy and distilled into a causal student policy. We evaluate X-Morph on three morphologically distinct platforms: a quadruped, a hexapod, and a quadruped equipped with a manipulator. The resulting policies track diverse retargeted motions, generalize to unseen human motions, and support downstream use cases including video-based teleoperation, behavior-prior control, and text-conditioned motion generation. These results suggest that large-scale human motion can serve as a substrate for learning broad, reusable behavior priors beyond humanoid robots. Project page:

TL;DR: Chronos 是一个受物理启发的全历史框架，用于非马尔可夫长时程机器人操作。它将观测历史从辅助上下文提升为策略动力学的潜状态，通过选择性状态空间模型传播因果历史状态，结合 IMLE 多模态粗动作先验和二阶薛定谔桥加速度预测，在记忆依赖型操作任务上显著超越马尔可夫 VLA 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 和生成式模仿策略大多依赖当前观测或短时间窗口（马尔可夫假设），但在记忆依赖型操作任务中，相同的观测在不同历史下需要不同的动作。这种马尔可夫捷径导致策略在需要记住任务阶段、历史上下文的长时程操作中失败。

Method: Chronos 在每个物理控制步将观测与本体感知融合为一个状态表征 token，使 token 序列与物理时间一一对齐。使用选择性状态空间模型（SSM）传播因果历史状态，通过隐式最大似然估计（IMLE）条件化多模态粗动作先验，再通过二阶薛定谔启发桥（Schrödinger bridge）预测加速度场，对动作进行精炼，产生更平滑、物理更合理的机器人运动。

Result: 在 16 个模拟任务和 4 个真实世界实验中评估。在 RMBench 上，Chronos 平均成功率 73.6%，比马尔可夫 VLA 基线 pi0.5 高 62.4 个百分点（6.6 倍相对提升），参数量少 10 倍；比记忆 VLA Mem-0 高 22.8 个百分点，参数量少 30 倍以上。真实世界双臂实验中，Chronos 四任务平均成功率 78%，记忆依赖型任务 72%，而 pi0.5 仅 7% 和 0%。

Conclusion: 历史不应被视为辅助上下文，而应作为操作策略的潜状态。Chronos 通过全历史状态建模显著提升了记忆依赖型长时程操作的成功率，且参数量远小于现有方法，验证了非马尔可夫策略在机器人操作中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Chronos 框架，将观测历史作为策略动力学潜状态，通过状态空间模型传播因果历史状态，并生成机器人操作动作。这属于面向机器人操作的世界模型与规划控制方法，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong physics-informed world model for non-Markovian manipulation, but outside core priority subtopics. Signals: world model, non-Markovian policy, selective state space model, IMLE coarse action prior, second-order Schrodinger bridge

Abstract: General-purpose robot policies should be modeled as dynamical systems, yet many VLA and generative imitation policies still rely on present observations or short windows. This Markovian shortcut fails in memory-dependent manipulation: identical observations can demand different actions after different histories. We present Chronos, a physics-informed full-history framework for non-Markovian long-horizon manipulation. The key idea is to elevate observation history from auxiliary context to the latent state of the policy dynamics. At each physical control step, Chronos forms one state-representative token by fusing observation and proprioception, so the token sequence is aligned one-to-one with physical time. A selective state space model propagates this causal historical state, which conditions a multimodal coarse action prior through implicit maximum likelihood estimation (IMLE). This prior is then refined by a second-order Schrodinger-inspired bridge that predicts acceleration fields, yielding smoother and more physically grounded robot motion. Across 16 simulated tasks and 4 real-world experiments, Chronos is evaluated on precision insertion, general manipulation, and memory-dependent long-horizon control. On RMBench, where success requires remembering task phase, Chronos achieves 73.6% average success, outperforming Markovian VLA baseline pi0.5 by +62.4 percentage points, a 6.6x relative gain, while using 10x fewer parameters. It also surpasses the memory VLA Mem-0 by 22.8 points while using over 30x fewer parameters. In real-world dual-arm experiments using a single RGB camera, Chronos achieves 78% average success over four tasks, including 72% on the three memory-dependent tasks, whereas pi0.5 achieves 7% overall and 0% on the memory-dependent subset. These results suggest that history should not be treated as auxiliary context, but as the latent state of the manipulation policy.

</details>

*Xiao Chen, Weishuai Zeng, Xiaojie Niu, Zirui Wang, Jianan Li, Huayi Wang, Furui Xu, Jiahe Chen, Weixiang Zhong, Lihe Ding, Kailin Li, Jiangmiao Pang, Tai Wang, Tianfan Xue, Jingbo Wang*
