TL;DR: ReactiveBFM 是一个面向人形机器人的实时闭环规划-控制框架，通过调度前缀采样课程缓解生成式规划器的暴露偏差，并引入异步重规划机制解决自回归规划与高频跟踪之间的延迟不匹配问题，在 Unitree G1 人形机器人上实现了零样本移动目标追踪和 93.1% 的抗扰动成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行为基础模型（BFM）仅能执行预定义的参考动作，对环境变化脆弱且无法实现反应式全身协调。简单级联生成式运动规划器由于跟踪误差导致的累积暴露偏差，无法实现真正的反应式控制。

Method: 提出 ReactiveBFM 框架，核心包括：(1) 调度前缀采样课程（scheduled prefix sampling curriculum），让生成式规划器从非完美物理状态中主动学习错误恢复行为，而非仅从真实轨迹学习；(2) 异步重规划机制，解决自回归规划与高频跟踪之间的延迟不匹配；(3) 轨迹分块（trajectory chunking）对空间参考进行时间集成，保证时空流畅执行。

Result: 在 Unitree G1 人形机器人上实现零样本移动目标追踪和全身协调重规划。在 sim-to-sim 基准测试中，面对严重扰动达到 93.1% 的成功率，比级联开环基线高出 28.6%。

Conclusion: ReactiveBFM 通过闭环规划-控制框架有效弥合了生成式规划器与物理执行之间的差距，为人形机器人实现反应式全身控制提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ReactiveBFM，一个面向人形机器人的实时闭环规划-控制框架，涉及具身智能中的动作规划、闭环控制与物理世界交互，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong robotics planning contribution but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: humanoid whole-body control, closed-loop motion planning, exposure bias mitigation, asynchronous replanning, zero-shot moving target reaching

Abstract: While current Behavior Foundation Models (BFMs) provide robust control priors for humanoids, they only execute pre-defined reference motions. As a result, they are vulnerable to environmental shifts and incapable of reactive whole-body coordination. Naively cascading them with generative motion planners fails to achieve true reactivity, as inevitable tracking discrepancies induce fatal cumulative exposure bias. To bridge this gap, we propose ReactiveBFM, a real-time closed-loop planning-control framework. At its core, we effectively mitigate exposure bias via a scheduled prefix sampling curriculum, forcing the generative planner to actively learn error-recovery behaviors from imperfect physical states rather than ground-truth trajectories. Systematically, to reconcile the severe latency mismatch between auto-regressive planning and high-frequency tracking, we introduce an asynchronous replanning mechanism. Combined with trajectory chunking to temporally ensemble spatial references, our system guarantees spatio-temporally fluid execution without physical jitter. Deployed on the Unitree G1 humanoid, ReactiveBFM demonstrates unprecedented physical agility across a vast repertoire of text-conditioned closed-loop motions. Notably, ReactiveBFM achieves zero-shot moving target reaching, showcasing intricate whole-body coordination and on-the-fly replanning. In sim-to-sim benchmarking under severe perturbations, ReactiveBFM achieves a 93.1% success rate, significantly outperforming cascaded open-loop baselines by 28.6%.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型在仿真基准上表现良好，但迁移到真实机器人平台时面临闭环不稳定等问题。本文旨在系统性地探究这一差距的根源，并建立可复现的真实机器人评估框架。

Method: 在UR5e机械臂上构建完整的数据采集管线，将数据转换为RLDS格式，微调OpenVLA和OpenVLA-OFT模型，系统验证动作表示（如末端执行器位姿、关节角度）和控制接口，进行真实机器人闭环实验并对比离线指标。

Result: 建立了完整的数据采集、数据集转换和VLA模型微调部署基础设施。实验揭示了离线指标与真实闭环行为之间的系统性差距，识别出影响部署成功的关键因素：动作语义、坐标系约定、模态时间对齐、图像预处理一致性和数据集覆盖质量。

Conclusion: VLA系统在真实场景中的成功部署更多取决于对整个数据-模型-控制管线的精确控制，而非模型容量的增量改进。应将VLA机器人问题重新定义为系统级工程挑战。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLA模型在真实UR5e机械臂上的部署与评估，涉及机器人动作表示、控制接口、数据采集与闭环控制，属于具身智能与机器人规划控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Provides practical system-level insights for deploying VLA models on real robots, but lacks novelty in core model or world model advances. Signals: VLA deployment, real-world gap, system-level engineering, UR5e, action semantics

Abstract: This project investigates whether recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can be transferred from controlled research benchmarks to a real-world robotic platform, specifically a UR5e manipulator, in a reproducible and operationally meaningful manner. The work integrates real-robot data acquisition, dataset engineering (compatible with the RLDS format), and the fine-tuning and deployment of OpenVLA and OpenVLA-OFT models, with systematic validation of action representations and control interfaces. The project resulted in several foundational assets: (i) a complete real-robot data acquisition pipeline, (ii) a dataset conversion workflow aligned with RLDS standards, (iii) an initial fine-tuning and inference infrastructure for VLA models, and (iv) a structured set of experimental observations grounded in real-robot trials. These elements collectively establish a reproducible framework for evaluating learning-based manipulation systems beyond simulation. Empirically, the experiments reveal a consistent gap between promising offline indicators and unstable closed-loop behavior on the physical system: this gap cannot be attributed solely to model limitations, it is strongly influenced by action semantics, coordinate frame conventions, temporal alignment between modalities, image preprocessing consistency, and dataset coverage and quality. These observations lead to a key interpretation: the successful deployment of VLA systems in real-world settings depends less on incremental improvements in model capacity and more on precise control of the entire data-model-control pipeline. The project reframes VLA-based robotics from a primarily model-centric challenge to a system-level problem; it highlights the difficulty of running robust task execution on the real robot and provides a clear, experimentally grounded understanding of the conditions required for reliable deployment.

</details>


### [33] [Learning from Mistakes: Rollout-Retrieval Lifelong Policy Learning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30537)
*Cheng Gong, Haoyang Wang, Chao Lu, Zirui Li, Jianwei Gong*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO, cs.LG, cs.CV

Importance: 普通相关 (59.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 R²LPL（Rollout-Retrieval Lifelong Policy Learning），一种面向自动驾驶的终身策略学习框架。该方法通过从策略自身在闭环仿真中产生的可恢复错误中检索纠正目标，将稀疏的失败证据转化为紧凑的监督知识，实现稳定且样本高效的策略持续改进。在 nuPlan 基准上，仅经过少量 rollout 和持续学习循环，即可将中等初始性能的学习型规划器提升至 SOTA 水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶策略大多依赖专家演示进行训练或微调，在部署中遇到长尾场景时缺乏显式的错误纠正与知识保留机制。闭环仿真中的错误虽然暴露了策略的薄弱环节，但并未直接告诉策略应该学习什么。因此，需要一种能从自身错误中持续学习并保留已有能力的终身学习框架。

Method: R²LPL 包含三个关键步骤：(1) 在闭环仿真中 rollout 当前策略，收集失败轨迹；(2) 过滤可恢复的错误状态，并通过检索机制获取可行的纠正目标（如专家轨迹或规划器输出）；(3) 将这些纠正目标作为监督信号，对策略进行终身学习更新，同时通过经验回放防止灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在 nuPlan 大规模闭环基准上，R²LPL 仅经过少量 rollout 和持续学习循环，即将中等初始性能的学习型规划器提升至 SOTA 水平，尤其在具有挑战性的长尾 Test14-hard 分割上表现突出。

Conclusion: R²LPL 证明了将闭环仿真中的可恢复错误转化为纠正知识用于持续策略改进的有效性，为自动驾驶策略的终身学习提供了一种实用且高效的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究自动驾驶策略的终身学习，通过闭环仿真中的错误回滚与检索来持续改进策略，属于具身智能中的规划控制与策略学习，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong in world model and autonomous driving, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: lifelong policy learning, autonomous driving, closed-loop mistake correction, nuPlan benchmark, rollout-retrieval

Abstract: Autonomous driving policies should be able to improve continually as deployment exposes them to increasingly diverse and long-tail traffic situations. However, most learning-based policies are trained or fine-tuned on expert demonstrations and then rely largely on generalization to handle challenging closed-loop scenarios, lacking an explicit mechanism to correct and retain the mistakes exposed in these scenarios. This paper studies autonomous driving policy improvement from a lifelong learning perspective: Can a pretrained policy improve continually by accumulating corrective knowledge derived from its own mistakes, while retaining previously acquired driving competence? To answer this question, we propose Rollout-Retrieval Lifelong Policy Learning (R$^2$LPL), a policy learning framework that retrieves corrective targets from recoverable policy-induced mistakes and retains the resulting knowledge through lifelong policy learning. R^2LPL addresses a key bottleneck in continual policy improvement: closed-loop mistakes reveal where the policy is weak, but do not directly specify what the policy should learn. By filtering recoverable mistake-related states and retrieving feasible corrective targets, R$^2$LPL turns sparse failure evidence into compact supervised knowledge for stable and sample-efficient policy improvement. We evaluate R$^2$LPL on large-scale closed-loop nuPlan benchmarks. With only a few rollout and continual-learning cycles, R$^2$LPL elevates a learning-based planner with moderate initial performance to state-of-the-art performance across the evaluated benchmarks, especially on the challenging and long-tail Test14-hard split. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of R$^2$LPL in converting recoverable closed-loop mistakes into corrective knowledge for sustained policy improvement.

</details>


### [34] [Training Vision-Language-Action Models with Dense Embodied Chain-of-Thought Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30552)
*Haoyang Li, Guanlin Li, Youhe Feng, Chen Zhao, Zhuoran Wang, Yang Li, Qizhe Wei, Shifeng Bao, Haitao Shen, Yihan Zhao, Tong Yang, Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: ZR-0是一个26亿参数的VLA模型，采用双流架构：预训练VLM（System 2）生成密集的具身思维链（ECoT）推理，Diffusion Transformer动作专家（System 1）通过flow matching生成连续动作。两者通过交叉注意力耦合，推理时可完全跳过ECoT生成而不损失性能。在ProcCorpus-60M（约6000万帧、400K+轨迹）上预训练，在单臂、双臂和人形机器人三个仿真基准及真实xArm平台上均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨本体迁移是VLA模型的核心挑战，因为不同机器人平台的低层状态和动作空间存在根本差异。作者观察到操作背后的高层认知过程（场景感知、物体识别、任务规划、子任务分解）在不同本体间是共享的，因此希望通过密集的具身思维链监督来对齐跨本体表征，实现有效的跨本体迁移。

Method: ZR-0采用双流架构：(1) System 2：预训练VLM，训练时生成结构化的ECoT推理（包括场景描述、物体识别、任务规划、子任务分解）；(2) System 1：基于Diffusion Transformer的动作专家，通过flow matching生成连续动作块。两个组件通过交叉注意力耦合，并设计注意力掩码限制动作专家仅关注输入提示特征，使得推理时可完全跳过ECoT生成。模型在ProcCorpus-60M数据集上预训练，该数据集包含约6000万帧、40万+轨迹，其中96.8%的帧具有密集ECoT标注。

Result: 在LIBERO（单臂）、RoboTwin 2.0（双臂）和RoboCasa GR-1 Tabletop（人形）三个仿真基准以及真实xArm平台上均取得强性能，展示了有效的跨本体迁移能力。

Conclusion: 通过密集的具身思维链监督，VLA模型能够有效对齐跨本体表征，实现从仿真到真实、从单臂到人形等多种机器人平台的泛化。ECoT在训练时提供丰富的语义监督，而推理时可完全跳过，不影响效率。

Classification reason: 该论文提出ZR-0，一个面向机器人操作的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，使用密集的具身思维链（ECoT）监督进行跨本体迁移。论文涉及具身智能、动作规划与控制，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]（embodied_world_model）高度匹配。同时，其使用VLM进行推理、Diffusion Transformer生成动作的设计也与多模态理解与生成方向相关。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and continuous multimodal priorities via embodied chain-of-thought and flow matching action generation. Signals: world model, embodied chain-of-thought, flow matching, continuous action generation, dual-stream VLA architecture

Abstract: Cross-embodiment transfer in vision-language-action (VLA) models remains challenging because low-level state and action spaces differ fundamentally across robot platforms. We observe that the high-level cognitive process underlying manipulation, including scene perception, object identification, task planning, and sub-task decomposition, is largely shared across embodiments. Based on this observation, we present ZR-0, a 2.6 billion parameter end-to-end VLA model that uses dense Embodied Chain-of-Thought (ECoT) supervision to align cross-embodiment representations within the vision-language model (VLM). ZR-0 adopts a dual-stream architecture: a pre-trained VLM (System 2) generates structured ECoT reasoning during training, while a Diffusion Transformer-based action expert (System 1) produces continuous action chunks via flow matching. The two components are coupled through cross-attention, with an attention mask that restricts the action expert to input prompt features only, enabling ECoT generation to be entirely skipped at inference without any performance loss. ZR-0 is pre-trained on ProcCorpus-60M, a large-scale dataset comprising approximately 60 million frames (approximately 1,000 hours) from over 400K trajectories, with dense ECoT annotations covering 96.8% of all frames. We evaluate ZR-0 on three simulation benchmarks spanning single-arm (LIBERO), bimanual (RoboTwin 2.0), and humanoid (RoboCasa GR-1 Tabletop) embodiments, as well as real-world experiments on the xArm platform, demonstrating strong performance across all settings. Code and model checkpoints are available at .

</details>


### [35] [Sequential Planning via Anchored Robotic Keypoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30613)
*Bryce Grant, Aryeh Rothenberg, Logan Senning, Zonghe Chua, Zach Patterson, Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (59.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SPARK 是一个免训练的神经符号操作系统，通过单个 Gemini 调用将规划编码为类型化行为树，将计算预算集中在感知层（SAM3 多提示检测 + 恢复循环），在 LIBERO-PRO 上达到 43.7%（超越 VLA 基线两倍以上），并在三个机器人家族九种任务上平均 68% 成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 和代码生成智能体（如 CaP-Agent0）在面对位置和任务变化时，感知层是最薄弱的环节，且每次失败都需要重新查询 LLM 从头生成方案，成本高昂。作者希望设计一个将规划与感知解耦、免训练且可独立替换模块的操作系统。

Method: SPARK 的核心是类型化行为树（typed BT），由单个 Gemini 调用生成，包含可组合的基元（运动、抓取、深度几何等），每个基元已封装底层控制逻辑。感知层使用第二个 Gemini 调用为每个物体生成三个候选文本提示，SAM3 评估后保留置信度最高的提示→标签对；失败时通过恢复循环重新检测物体，无需新 LLM 调用。检测器、规划器和控制器模块可独立替换。

Result: 在 LIBERO-PRO 六个位置和任务单元上达到 43.7%，是 CaP-Agent0（18.2%）的两倍以上。替代提示在空间套件上提升 +27.7 点，物体套件上提升 +10.0 点，恢复循环整体提升 +5.0 点。在三个机器人家族（UR10e、Franka FR3、双臂 Franka）九种任务各 20 次试验中平均成功率为 68%。

Conclusion: SPARK 证明了一个免训练的神经符号规划器可以在多种机器人平台上超越 VLA 基线，同时生成带标签的验证轨迹，为后续策略学习提供数据，无需遥操作。

Abstract: We present Sequential Planning via Anchored Robotic Keypoints, SPARK, a training-free neurosymbolic manipulation system that reaches 43.7% on six LIBERO-PRO position \& task cells, more than doubling CaP-Agent0 and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) baselines. CaP-Agent0, a multi-turn code-generation agent, achieves 18.2% by re-querying an LLM at every turn, but its restart-from-scratch solution proves costly against minor policy failures. Perception is the layer that fails most under position and task changes so SPARK spends its computation there. A single Gemini call composes the plan as a typed behavior tree (BT) of composable primitives, each already containing the low-level control (motion, grasping, depth geometry) a code-generation agent would otherwise regenerate on every trial. The rest of the budget goes to perception: a second Gemini call proposes three alternative text prompts per object, SAM3 evaluates each, and we keep the prompt$\to$label pair with the most confident detection and a recovery loop then retries a failed primitive against freshly detected objects, with no new LLM call. The alternative prompts add +27.7 points on the spatial suite and +10.0 on the object suite, with the recovery loop adding +5.0 overall. SPARK runs the same primitives on three robot families (UR10e, Franka FR3, bimanual Franka) across nine unique tasks at twenty trials each, averaging 68%. Since the detector, planner, and controller modules sit behind the typed plan, they swap independently without training, and each primitive's checkable post-condition traces a failure to the corresponding module or a kinematic limit. Every trial logs a verified, labeled trajectory, so a training-free planner that already beats VLAs can supply the data those policies need without teleoperation. Project page:

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型为 LLM Agent 提供行动后果预测以辅助规划，但不可靠的预测可能被忽略、误用甚至损害决策质量。如何在不更新模型参数的情况下，让世界模型在部署时自我改进预测质量，是一个关键问题。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 和 ScienceWorld 上，WorldEvolver 在三个骨干模型上均取得最高预测准确率，并在下游 Agent 成功率上领先其他世界模型基线方法。

Conclusion: 测试时的记忆修订机制能够有效提升世界模型的预测保真度和 Agent 的规划性能，为 LLM Agent 的长期规划提供了一种轻量级、无需重新训练的自进化方案。

Abstract: World models offer a principled way to equip long-horizon LLM agents with foresight: predictions of action consequences before execution. However, unreliable foresight can be ignored, misused, or even degrade downstream decision-making. In this paper, we introduce WorldEvolver, a self-evolving world model framework that revises its deployment-time context while keeping the downstream agent and all model parameters frozen. WorldEvolver integrates three modules: (i) Episodic Memory, which exploits real action transitions through retrieval-based simulation; (ii) Semantic Memory, which extracts persistent heuristic rules from prediction-observation mismatches; and (iii) Selective Foresight, which filters low-confidence predictions before integrating them into agent reasoning context. We evaluate WorldEvolver on ALFWorld and ScienceWorld, measuring world model prediction accuracy on Word2World and downstream agent success rate on AgentBoard. Extensive experiments show that WorldEvolver achieves the highest prediction accuracy across three backbones and leads other world model baselines on downstream agent success rate, demonstrating that test-time memory revision enhances both predictive fidelity and planning performance.

</details>

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents can solve complex procedural tasks by interacting with environments over multiple turns, but this ability typically depends on large models, long contexts, and repeated inference calls. This makes advanced memory-augmented agents difficult to deploy on resource-constrained devices. We introduce DuoMem, a dual-space distillation framework that transfers procedural problem-solving ability from a large teacher model to compact student models. DuoMem distils in two complementary spaces: (1)context-space distillation, which replaces student-generated memories with higher-quality teacher-generated procedural memories prepended to the student's input, and (2)parameter-space distillation, which fine-tunes lightweight LoRA adapters on successful teacher trajectories. Evaluated on ALFWorld, a challenging embodied decision-making benchmark, DuoMem boosts a 4B-parameter model from 4.3% to 77.9% task success rate, closing most of the gap to a 72B teacher model (87.1%), while adding fewer than 10M trainable parameters and only a few megabytes of pre-computed teacher memories. Moreover, the DuoMem-enhanced 4B model completes tasks over 3x faster than the 72B teacher in wall-clock time, making it viable for real-time edge deployment, which would be challenging for the ablations across eight models spanning 2B-72B parameters reveal that both distillation axes contribute complementary

  <summary>Details</summary>

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world_model and efficient inference priorities, using world-model supervision for trajectory refinement in autonomous driving. Signals: world model, VLM planning, layer-wise refinement, future-frame supervision, autonomous driving

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) provide powerful semantic understanding and commonsense reasoning for End-to-End Autonomous Driving (E2E-AD) planning. However, trajectories directly generated by VLMs often encode only coarse driving intentions and remain insufficient for geometrically accurate, future-aware, and multi-view-grounded planning. To address these limitations, we develop the Layer-Wise World-Model-Guided Driving framework (LWDrive). LWDrive is a VLM planning framework that refines coarse trajectories through layer-wise world-model guidance. Instead of treating the VLM output as the final trajectory, LWDrive uses it as an intent-aware coarse plan, expands a diverse candidate space around it, and progressively refines the candidates through a Foresight Cascade Planner (FCP). Specifically, we introduce future-frame generation supervision to encourage the VLM to learn forward-looking scene representations, thereby injecting planning-relevant predictive dynamics into its internal hidden states. Built upon these world-model-supervised representations, FCP exploits VLM features across multiple layers and integrates historical temporal states, Action-Query representations, and current-frame multi-view Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) features to refine candidate trajectories in a coarse-to-fine manner. This design enables progressive correction of spatial positions and motion trends while grounding trajectory refinement with multi-view scene cues and preserving the high-level driving intention produced by the large model. Finally, a score head evaluates the refined candidates and selects the best trajectory as the final planning output. Experiments show that LWDrive achieves a score of 92.0 on the NAVSIM benchmark and 89.6 on NAVSIM-v2. Code and models will be made publicly available.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型研究主要关注预测精度，但对其潜动力学内部如何组织和利用物理信息缺乏理解。作者希望揭示世界模型的隐状态是否以及如何编码事件相关的物理结构。

Method: 设计受控诊断协议：(1) 构建平衡的受控生成数据集，包含自由运动、碰撞、遮挡三类事件；(2) 评估循环模型、注意力模型和潜状态空间模型在固定时域预测下的表现；(3) 进行事件状态读出分析、物理场加权分析、时间对齐与方向一致性分析；(4) 提出固定时域投影因果场效应（CFE）方法，通过抑制场对齐方向来检验其功能重要性。

Result: 隐状态支持可靠的事件状态读出；不同事件上下文系统性地重新加权运动学、接触和物体恒存性场：自由运动以运动学为主，碰撞结合运动学和接触，遮挡结合运动和物体恒存性；时间对齐分析显示场强调存在相位相关偏移；CFE分析表明抑制场对齐方向会降低事件相关预测性能，接触场在碰撞窗口中最显著，物体恒存性场在遮挡窗口中有较有限的证据。

Conclusion: 世界模型的潜空间存在事件条件化的物理场组织，且这些场对齐的表征成分对预测具有功能敏感性。但结果并不暗示存在显式的物理模块、孤立的因果回路或上下文不变的滑动窗口泛化能力。

Classification reason: 论文研究被动物体状态世界模型中潜动力学的物理结构诊断，涉及运动、碰撞、遮挡等物理事件条件下的动力学建模与表征分析，属于物理世界建模范畴，与 canonical subtopic "可交互与物理世界建模" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on world model diagnostics rather than generative or continuous multimodal modeling, which are the user's top priorities. Signals: world model diagnostics, latent physical structure, event-conditioned analysis, not generative, no continuous token or flow matching

Abstract: World models can predict future physical states, but prediction accuracy alone does not explain how physical information is organized and used inside their latent dynamics. We introduce a controlled diagnostic protocol for studying event-conditioned latent physical structure in passive object-state world models. The protocol tests whether hidden representations encode event-regime information, whether event contexts reweight non-exclusive physical field readouts, and whether field-aligned representational components have functional consequences for prediction. Using a balanced controlled-generator dataset with free-motion, collision, and occlusion events, we evaluate recurrent, attention-based, and latent state-space transition models under a fixed-horizon forecasting setup. The models learn useful predictive dynamics and their hidden states support reliable event-regime readout. Event contexts systematically reweight kinematic, contact, and object-permanence field readouts: free motion is kinematic-dominant, collision combines kinematic and contact structure, and occlusion combines motion-related and object-permanence structure. Time-aligned and directional-consistency analyses further show phase-related shifts in field emphasis. Finally, fixed-horizon projection causal field effect (CFE) shows that suppressing field-aligned directions can degrade event-relevant prediction, with strongest evidence for contact-aligned structure in collision-contact windows and more qualified evidence for object-permanence-aligned structure in hard-occlusion hidden windows. These results support event-conditioned organization and fixed-horizon functional sensitivity of latent physical fields, while not implying explicit physical modules, isolated causal circuits, or context-invariant sliding-window generalization.

</details>

*Christian Conti, Bilan Yang, Alexander Sigrist, Lorenzo Miele, Yamen Saraiji, Peter Dürr, Naoya Takahashi*

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向专业级机器人乒乓球的物理仿真模型，涵盖空气动力学（基于雷诺数和旋转比的阻力与马格努斯力）、球台碰撞（考虑球体屈曲效应的恢复系数）和球拍碰撞（残差神经网络修正恢复系数与旋转阻尼），首次实现了能与职业选手对战的真实机器人乒乓球AI智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在竞技级乒乓球中，球的高速旋转会产生复杂且反直觉的轨迹，机器人需要在极短时间内精确回击。真实环境中训练强化学习策略成本高昂且危险，因此高保真仿真至关重要。现有机器人乒乓球仿真仅覆盖有限的速度和旋转范围，无法捕捉职业比赛中的丰富球行为，且仿真与现实的任何偏差都会成为对手可利用的弱点。

Method: 1) 空气动力学模型：将阻力和马格努斯力系数建模为雷诺数和旋转比的函数，覆盖更广的速度和旋转范围。2) 球台碰撞模型：考虑球体屈曲效应对恢复系数的影响，并引入残差项修正瞬时点接触模型。3) 球拍碰撞模型：引入残差神经网络组件，补充法向/切向恢复系数以及扭转旋转阻尼系数的建模。这些模型用于训练强化学习策略，并部署到真实机器人上。

Result: 所提出的物理模型首次使真实机器人乒乓球AI智能体能够与职业选手对战，证明了高保真仿真在sim-to-real迁移中的关键作用。

Conclusion: 高保真物理仿真模型是实现机器人竞技级运动技能从仿真到现实迁移的关键，通过精细建模空气动力学和碰撞动力学，可以弥合仿真与现实的差距，使机器人达到与人类职业选手竞技的水平。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了高保真物理仿真模型（空气动力学、球台碰撞、球拍碰撞）用于机器人乒乓球策略的sim-to-real迁移，属于物理世界建模与交互式模拟器范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "可交互与物理世界建模"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong sim-to-real physics modeling but outside user's core priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: world model, sim-to-real, robot table tennis, physics simulation, reinforcement learning

Abstract: At competitive speeds and spins, a table tennis ball follows complex, counterintuitive trajectories that a robot must track and precisely counter within fractions of a second. Training a reinforcement learning policy capable of these skills is prohibitively expensive and dangerous in the real world, making high-fidelity simulation essential. Transferability of such policies, however, critically depends on how faithfully the simulation captures real-world dynamics--a requirement made even more stringent by the adversarial nature of the game, where any regime in which a model fails to approximate reality becomes an exploitable weakness for the opponent. Prior state-of-the-art in robot table tennis generally focuses on a limited range of velocities and spins and fails to capture the richness of ball behaviors encountered in professional-level play. In this work, we present physics models for the aerodynamic ball flight, for the contact dynamics between the ball and the table, as well as between the ball and the racket that accurately capture the ball behavior over a vast range of speeds and spins relevant to the game. Specifically, we model drag and Magnus force coefficients as functions of Reynolds number and spin ratio in the aerodynamics equations. For the table contact model we model effects of ball buckling on the coefficient of restitution and incorporate residuals into the instantaneous point-contact models. For the racket contact model we introduce a residual neural network component to complement coefficients related to normal and tangential coefficients of restitution as well as torsional spin damping. The resulting models were used for the first real-world robot table tennis AI agent capable of competing against professional players, to train reinforcement learning policies.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出HExA（Hierarchical Experimentalist Agents），一个无需训练的上下文自改进框架，使LLM智能体能够通过主动实验在物理仿真环境中学习可复用的技能并回答复杂查询。在基于PHYRE 2D物理环境的Interphyre基准上，HExA将Claude Sonnet 4.6的成功率从2%提升至77%，并展示了所学技能的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体依赖参数化知识、检索或搜索，在陌生领域和复杂物理系统中无法有效回答查询或完成长时域任务。即使知道物理定律，LLM也无法直接推理复杂物理系统的行为。因此需要一种能让智能体通过主动实验来获取知识、学习技能的框架。

Method: HExA是一个层次化框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 实验设计模块——迭代设计查询相关的实验（在PHYRE环境中进行干预操作）；(2) 可复用技能库——从实验经验中学习并存储可组合的技能；(3) 证据整合模块——将实验证据整合以回答查询或采取行动。框架无需训练，兼容任何黑盒模型，无需外部监督或离线数据。

Result: 在Interphyre基准上，Claude Sonnet 4.6基线仅2%成功率，HExA提升至77%。HExA还改进了开源模型性能，优于ReAct和Reflexion等智能体基线。仅使用从简单关卡学到的技能迁移到新场景（不进行主动实验），HExA仍达到44%成功率，证明了技能的可复用性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: HExA证明了通过主动实验学习可以帮助智能体发现有用知识、获取可复用技能，并在新颖的长时域任务上取得高效进展。该框架无需训练、兼容任何黑盒模型，为LLM智能体在物理世界中的推理和决策提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出HExA框架，让LLM智能体在PHYRE 2D物理仿真环境中通过主动实验（interventions）学习可复用的技能并回答查询。这属于世界模型方向下的"可交互与物理世界建模"（interactive_physical_world）子主题，因为论文核心是在物理仿真环境中进行交互式实验、假设检验和技能学习，与物理世界建模和交互式模拟器高度相关。

Importance reason: Strong world model and agentic learning framework, but lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal or video generation priorities. Signals: world model, active experimentation, skill transfer, LLM agent, physical simulation

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to take actions in the real world and support human decision-making, yet most agents rely on parametric knowledge, fixed post-training data, retrieval, or search. This paradigm breaks down in novel domains and for sophisticated queries that cannot be answered from prior knowledge alone. Knowing the laws of physics, for instance, does not by itself enable LLMs to answer queries or complete long-horizon tasks in a complex physical system. To address this, we introduce Hierarchical Experimentalist Agents (HExA), an in-context self-improvement framework to learn from active experimentation. HExA iteratively designs and refines query-relevant experiments, learns a reusable library of composable skills from experience, and integrates experimental evidence to answer queries or take actions. HExA is training-free, compatible with any black-box model, and does not require external supervision, oracles, or offline data. To evaluate active experimentation, we introduce Interphyre, a tool-calling benchmark built on the PHYRE 2D procedural physics environment, where agents propose interventions and test hypotheses through simulation APIs. Experiments show that current LLM agents struggle in these settings, especially on the hardest levels of Interphyre. Claude Sonnet 4.6 achieves only 2% success, while HExA improves the same model to up to 77% success. HExA also improves open-weight models and outperforms agentic baselines such as ReAct and Reflexion. Moreover, using only skills learned from easier levels and transferred without active experimentation, HExA achieves 44% success, demonstrating the reusability and generalization of its learned skills. Overall, HExA shows that learning through active experimentation can help agents discover useful knowledge, acquire reusable skills, and make efficient progress on novel long-horizon tasks.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式视频生成系统将状态转移与高频观测合成纠缠在一起，导致长时一致性差且推理效率低。作者希望将生成过程分解为紧凑场景状态的随机演化与确定性渲染，从而解耦这两个问题。

Method: 提出 NeuWorld：1）Transformer VAE 从稀疏带姿态帧中学习局部锚定的神经隐式场景（NIS）；2）扩散 Transformer 在给定未来相机轨迹和几何感知检索历史条件下演化 NIS；3）复用 VAE 编码器作为统一条件器，将相机、参考图像和历史线索映射到同一 NIS 模态，避免外部异构编码器。

Result: 在公开带姿态视图数据上从头训练，无需预训练视频骨干或辅助 3D 重建器，NeuWorld 实现了强长时一致性和良好的推理效率。

Conclusion: 以场景为中心的隐式状态分解范式能有效解耦交互式世界探索中的状态转移与观测生成，为长时一致且高效的交互式视频生成提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 NeuWorld，通过神经隐式场景（NIS）实现相机控制的世界探索与交互式视频生成，属于可交互世界建模与神经模拟器范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 可交互与物理世界建模 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and continuous multimodal representation with a novel implicit scene state for interactive video generation. Signals: world model, neural implicit scene, interactive video generation, continuous latent state, transformer VAE

Abstract: Interactive video generation systems for camera-controlled world exploration roll out growing sequences of latent video frames, entangling state transition with high-frequency observation synthesis. We propose Walking in the Implicit, a scene-centric paradigm that changes the rollout variable from frame latents to a fixed-length, renderable implicit state, termed Neural Implicit Scene (NIS). This factorizes interactive generation into stochastic transition of a compact scene state and deterministic pose-conditioned rendering given the sampled state. We instantiate this paradigm as NeuWorld: a transformer VAE learns locally anchored NIS from sparse posed frames, and a diffusion transformer evolves NIS conditioned on future camera trajectories and geometry-aware retrieved history. By reusing the VAE encoder as a unified conditioner, NeuWorld maps camera, reference-image, and history cues into the same NIS modality, avoiding external heterogeneous encoders. Trained from scratch on public posed-view data without pretrained video backbones or auxiliary 3D reconstructors, NeuWorld achieves strong long-horizon consistency with favorable inference efficiency.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型是强大的统计引擎，但模式掌握不等于通用智能。人类婴儿从少量显式知识开始，逐渐发现物体恒存、因果、他人心智、身体能动性和物理世界的持久性。作者认为通往超级人工智能需要一种缺失的能力——情境感知。

Method: 本文为概念性/理论性论文，通过分析大语言模型的局限性，提出情境感知的三个核心组件：抽象预测（在潜在空间中模拟未来状态）、长期压缩记忆（高效存储和检索经验）、以及由目标引导的主动学习（自主探索和获取新知识）。

Result: 论文没有实验性结果，而是提出了一个理论框架和衡量情境感知进展的测试方法，包括评估机器模拟未来、追求自定目标和判断创造者的能力。

Conclusion: 情境感知是通往超级人工智能的必要基础，当前大语言模型缺乏这一能力。需要发展能够构建内部世界模拟、进行抽象预测、拥有长期记忆和主动学习能力的系统，并建立相应的评估测试来度量进展。

Classification reason: 论文提出"情境感知"(situation perception)概念，核心是构建、修正并在内部模拟可能世界的能力，包含抽象预测、长期压缩记忆和主动学习三个组件，这与世界模型方向中的"可交互与物理世界建模"高度吻合，该子主题关注物理一致的世界模型、交互式模拟器与环境动力学建模。

Importance reason: Proposes situation perception as a missing primitive for ASI, aligning with world model direction and continuous multimodal reasoning. Signals: world model, situation perception, abstract prediction, long-term compressed memory, active learning

Abstract: Current large language models are extraordinary statistical engines. They compress vast amounts of text into useful patterns and can explain science, write code, imitate reasoning, and participate in philosophical conversation. Yet pattern mastery is not the same as general intelligence. A human infant begins with little explicit knowledge, but gradually discovers object permanence, cause and effect, other minds, bodily agency, and the persistence of the physical world. We make an argument that the path to artificial superintelligence (ASI) depends on a missing capacity we call \emph{situation perception}: the ability to construct, revise, and act within internal simulations of possible worlds across latent time. \emph{ perception} requires at least three core components: abstract prediction, long-term compressed memory, and active learning guided by objectives. In this work, we analyse why modern large language models remain incomplete, and propose the appropriate tests for measuring progress and consequences of machines that can simulate futures, pursue self-directed goals, and possibly judge their own creators.

</details>

*Alexander Scheinker*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁流体动力学（MHD）的演化预测在等离子体物理和聚变能研究中至关重要，但传统数值模拟计算成本高，且实验测量往往稀疏、不完整。现有深度学习方法缺乏可靠的误差估计机制，难以在无真实标签的情况下评估预测置信度。本文旨在开发一种既能准确预测MHD场演化，又能自监督估计不确定性的方法。

Method: 提出双向自回归潜扩散模型（Bidirectional Autoregressive Latent Diffusion），在潜空间中对多个物理场（质量密度、压力、速度、磁场分量）进行时序建模。模型可以沿时间正向和反向流动预测场演化，通过比较前向和反向预测的一致性作为自监督误差/不确定性度量。此外，利用自适应反馈机制，基于稀疏诊断或有限视角的观测数据来增强模型鲁棒性。

Result: 实验表明，该双向流方法能够有效预测MHD场的演化，其前向-反向一致性度量与真实误差高度相关，可作为无真实标签时的可靠不确定性估计。该方法在非侵入式等离子体诊断任务中展现出潜力，自适应反馈机制在仅有稀疏观测时仍能保持较好的预测性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的双向自回归潜扩散模型为MHD场演化预测提供了一种兼具准确性和自监督不确定性估计的新范式。该方法有望应用于等离子体诊断、聚变能研究等物理场景，其自一致性度量和自适应反馈机制为在实验数据稀疏、测量受限的实际应用中提供了实用价值。

Classification reason: 该论文提出双向自回归潜扩散模型用于磁流体动力学（MHD）的场演化预测，属于物理世界建模与动力学仿真，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "可交互与物理世界建模"（interactive_physical_world）高度匹配。

Signals: Bidirectional autoregressive latent diffusion, Self-supervised consistency metric, Non-invasive plasma diagnostic, Adaptive feedback for robustness, Physics field evolution prediction

Abstract: This work presents a new bidirectional autoregressive latent diffusion approach for predicting the evolution of multiple fields (mass density, pressure, velocity, and magnetic field components) for magnetohydrodynamics. We show that this bidirectional flow can be used as a self-supervised consistency metric for uncertainty and error estimation, which enables the model to estimate test-time uncertainty and error without access to ground truth, by comparing how closely flowing forwards and backwards in time returns to the same predicted fields. We also demonstrate this methods's potential to serve as a non-invasive plasma diagnostic, and show how adaptive feedback can be used to make the model more robust based on sparse diagnostics or limited views/measurements.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: RoboGaze 是一个三阶段多智能体 VLM 框架：(1) 任务-场景对齐阶段，将任务指令与视频内容进行语义对齐；(2) 维度特定专家路由阶段，将视频路由到6个维度（如物理、时序、任务逻辑等）的专家VLM进行专项分析；(3) 批评验证阶段，通过批评验证器减少误报。输出包含时间定位的故障报告，基于30种故障类型的机器人特定分类体系。

Conclusion: RoboGaze 提供了一个可扩展、高度可解释的诊断工具，用于严格评估机器人世界模型。其结构化多智能体框架有效克服了传统指标和单一VLM评判器的局限性，为机器人世界模型的评估提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in robot world models enable synthetic video generation for embodied prediction and planning. However, evaluating these videos is challenging: visually realistic outputs often violate physical laws, temporal consistency, or task logic, while conventional metrics and monolithic Vision-Language Model (VLM) judges fail to generalize or provide precise diagnostic value. We present RoboGaze, a training-free, multi-agent VLM framework that provides structured, interpretable evaluation for generated robot-manipulation videos. Given a task instruction and video, RoboGaze operates via a three-stage pipeline: task-scene grounding, dimension-specific specialist routing, and critic-based verification. It outputs temporally localized glitch reports categorized under a novel 6-dimension, 30-type robotics-specific taxonomy. To benchmark RoboGaze, we introduce a human-validated dataset of 382 clips spanning simulated and real-world multi-view manipulation. Evaluating eight open-source and proprietary VLM backbones, RoboGaze dramatically outperforms zero-shot baselines, improving description-F1 by up to +43 points and temporal alignment (F1 x IoU) by up to +37 points, closing approximately 85% of the gap to the human ceiling. Furthermore, its critic verifier mitigates the "cry-wolf" false-positive flaw of standard VLMs, lifting clean-clip accuracy from under 25% to over 80%. RoboGaze offers a scalable, highly interpretable diagnostic tool for the rigorous evaluation of robot world models.

</details>

<details>

Conclusion: CrashTwin 通过定量暴露这些失败模式，为开发能够进行可靠真实世界仿真的物理可信世界模型提供了重要的诊断工具。

Abstract: Generative world models hold immense promise as scalable simulators for autonomous systems, particularly for synthesizing rare but safety-critical multi-agent interactions, such as vehicle collisions. However, current evaluation paradigms index heavily on visual fidelity and semantic alignment, leaving a critical blind spot: they cannot reliably quantify whether generated dynamics actually obey the fundamental physical laws required for reliable simulation. Assessing this physical plausibility is inherently difficult due to a lack of physical metrics and the challenge of extracting metric-scale kinematics from uncalibrated video rollouts. To bridge this gap, we introduce CrashTwin, a physics-grounded evaluation framework designed to stress-test the physical trustworthiness of world models. CrashTwin couples a diverse dataset of multi-agent collision scenarios, comprising 25K controllable synthetic and 12K in-the-wild real-world collision sequences with a novel calibration-free reconstruction pipeline, enabling the recovery of 3D physical attributes directly from world model rollouts. We propose a diagnostic suite that systematically evaluates three dimensions: spatio-temporal consistency, momentum and kinetic energy conservation, and world-dynamics integrity. Extensive benchmarking of state-of-the-art models reveals a crucial insight: high perceptual quality frequently masks severe physical violations during complex interactions. By quantitatively exposing these failure modes, CrashTwin provides a vital diagnostic tool for developing physically grounded world models capable of reliable real-world simulation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人长期场景建图面临物体重排（rigid object rearrangement）带来的动态维护挑战。现有 3DGS 方法在物体移动后需要从头重建或被动重优化，导致持续的鬼影（ghosting）和缓慢的恢复速度。亟需一种能够主动管理场景中可移动物体的高效动态维护方法。

Method: CubifyGS 将每个可移动物体建模为独立的"高斯资产"（Gaussian asset），通过物体检测识别物体的出现和消失，利用资产检索、刚性变换和显式剪枝来更新地图，而非从头重建。针对编辑后产生的几何空洞和局部光度不匹配，提出事件触发的自适应优化策略，将计算资源集中在受影响区域进行修复。

Result: 在自建的高保真动态基准数据集上，CubifyGS 在物体重排场景下相比可复现的基线方法，在伪影抑制和维护效率方面均取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: CubifyGS 通过将动态场景维护从被动重优化转变为主动资产管理，有效解决了 3DGS 在长期动态场景中的鬼影和恢复慢问题，为机器人场景建图提供了一种高效、可扩展的物体级解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CubifyGS，一个面向机器人长期动态场景维护的物体级 3DGS 建图框架，将可移动物体建模为可复用的高斯资产，通过物体检测、检索、刚性变换和显式剪枝实现动态场景更新。这属于动态 3D 场景中的世界建模，与 canonical 子主题 "空间 3D/4D 世界模型"（spatial_3d_4d_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Object-centric 3DGS for dynamic scene maintenance is relevant to world models but lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal or video generation priorities. Signals: object-level mapping, 3D Gaussian Splatting, lifelong scene maintenance, robotics, dynamic scene

Abstract: Lifelong scene mapping under rigid object rearrangement remains a fundamental challenge in robotics. While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables high-fidelity modeling, primitive-level updates often cause persistent ghosting and slow recovery. We propose CubifyGS, an object-level mapping framework that shifts dynamic maintenance from passive re-optimization to active asset management. CubifyGS models movable instances as reusable Gaussian assets, detects object appearance and disappearance, and updates maps through asset retrieval, rigid transformation, and explicit pruning rather than reconstruction from scratch. To address geometric voids and local photometric mismatch after such edits, we further propose an event-triggered adaptive optimization strategy that focuses computation on affected regions. We validate our approach on a newly constructed high-fidelity dynamic benchmark, demonstrating that CubifyGS improves artifact suppression and maintenance efficiency over representative reproducible baselines in the evaluated object-rearrangement setting.

</details>

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，闭环设计显著提升了尺度稳定性和外观-几何一致性，仅使用单目输入即可达到与RGB-D方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: MyGO-Splat通过将3DGS图元光栅化为几何信息并反馈到位姿优化中，有效解决了单目SLAM的尺度模糊和开环问题，证明了闭环几何反馈在单目高斯SLAM中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于3D高斯泼溅的单目SLAM系统，涉及3D场景建图与定位，属于世界模型方向下的空间3D/4D世界模型子主题（spatial_3d_4d_world_model），该子主题涵盖3D场景、神经场与空间世界建模。

Importance reason: Focuses on SLAM and 3D reconstruction, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, monocular SLAM, closed-loop geometric feedback, scale-aware alignment, RGB-only performance

Abstract: Real-time monocular Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) fundamentally suffers from scale ambiguity and a lack of geometric self-correction. While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables high-fidelity rendering, existing RGB-only systems remain open-loop because depth priors are injected into mapping but refined geometry cannot effectively regulate tracking drift. We present MyGO-Splat, a closed-loop Gaussian SLAM framework that analytically rasterizes Gaussian primitives into pixel-wise depth and surface normals, allowing the map to actively supervise camera pose optimization. To bridge monocular priors and scale consistency, our framework introduces scale-aware adaptive alignment that projects foundation-model depth estimates into the globally optimized Gaussian space, forming a self-correcting cycle for scale feedback. Extensive evaluations show that this closed-loop design improves scale stability and appearance-geometry consistency, achieving performance comparable to RGB-D methods while using only monocular input.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: L2D2-GS 通过将可泛化重建转化为迭代优化与稠密化过程，有效解决了前馈式动态场景重建中的关键瓶颈，为自动驾驶仿真和大规模世界建模提供了高效且高质量的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文关注动态城市场景的高保真重建，属于动态3D场景建模，与 world_model 方向下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "空间 3D/4D 世界模型" 高度匹配。同时该方法基于3DGS进行动态场景重建，也涉及多模态生成方向下的3D生成与重建。

Importance reason: Advances feedforward dynamic scene reconstruction with self-supervised densification, relevant to world models and efficient inference. Signals: world model, dynamic scene reconstruction, feedforward 3DGS, self-supervised densification, autonomous driving

Abstract: High-fidelity reconstruction of dynamic urban environments is a cornerstone of autonomous driving simulation and large-scale world modeling. While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has established a new standard for real-time rendering, its reliance on expensive per-scene optimization limits scalability. Conversely, recent feedforward methods that infer Gaussian parameters offer faster speed but face fundamental bottlenecks: they are memory-prohibitive at high resolutions and struggle to fuse dense multi-view observations consistently. This paper presents L2D2-GS, a unified framework that reformulates generalizable reconstruction not as a one-shot regression, but as a robust iterative process of optimization and densification. To resolve the ambiguity of supervision in primitive generation, we propose a self-supervised densification policy that derives explicit reward signals from global reconstruction gains to guide local densification. Furthermore, we mitigate irreversible early-stage artifacts through a geometric regularization mechanism, utilizing reparameterization to constrain the optimization manifold and prevent convergence to poor local optima. Extensive experiments on the PandaSet and Waymo datasets demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction fidelity and strong zero-shot generalization, while using fewer primitives than competing baselines.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需任何标注的驾驶场景复杂度检测方法：在nuPlan数据集上训练一个最小化的JEPA（联合嵌入预测架构）世界模型，利用时序预测误差作为零样本复杂度分数。实验表明，该模型能自动识别无保护转弯、人行横道交互等复杂场景，并在下游异常检测任务中达到0.512的平均精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别大规模无标注驾驶数据集中的复杂和安全关键场景是一个重要但昂贵的问题。现有方法依赖人工标注、监督分类器或精心设计的规则集，都需要大量关于"什么构成困难场景"的先验知识。本文探索模型能否在没有标签的情况下自主发现场景复杂度。

Result: 模型对无保护转弯、人行横道交互和行人接近场景分配显著更高的复杂度分数，对车道跟随和静止交通场景分配显著更低的分数。四项消融实验验证了信号来源。下游异常检测任务中达到0.512的平均精度，优于0.436的随机基线。

Conclusion: 自监督潜空间世界模型中的时序预测误差可以作为驾驶场景复杂度的实用代理指标，无需任何人工标注即可有效识别复杂驾驶场景。

Classification reason: 该论文使用JEPA自监督世界模型，通过时序预测误差来检测驾驶场景复杂度，属于世界模型在驾驶场景中的应用。映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "视频世界模型"（video_world_model），因为其核心是使用潜空间世界模型进行未来状态预测和场景理解。

Importance reason: Directly aligns with world model and video world model priorities, using JEPA for zero-shot complexity detection in driving scenarios. Signals: world model, joint embedding predictive architecture, zero-shot complexity detection, driving scenario, self-supervised

Abstract: Identifying complex and safety-critical driving scenarios in large unlabelled datasets is an important but expensive problem. Existing approaches rely on human annotators, supervised classifiers, or carefully engineered rule sets, all of which require substantial prior knowledge about what constitutes a difficult scenario. We ask whether a model can discover scenario complexity on its own, with no labels at any stage. We train a minimal Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) on structured agent state data from the nuPlan mini dataset and use the temporal prediction error as a zero-shot complexity score. Without access to any ground-truth labels during training or evaluation setup, the model assigns significantly higher scores to scenarios involving unprotected turns, crosswalk interactions, and pedestrian proximity, and significantly lower scores to lane-following and stationary-traffic scenarios. We validate this finding through four ablation experiments that isolate the source of the signal, and through a downstream anomaly detection evaluation that achieves Average Precision of 0.512 against a 0.436 chance baseline. The results show that temporal prediction error in a self-supervised latent world model is a practical proxy for driving scenario complexity.

</details>

*Bohao Zhao, Chengrui Wei, Guangfeng Jiang, Ruixin Liu, Xuejie Lv, Liu Liang, Sutao Deng, Xiuyang Fan, Pengkun Zheng, Jinyun Zhou, Rui Guo, Hanpeng Liu, Yutong Zheng, Yi Guo, Xinlong Zheng, Qingyu Luo, Zhuangzhuang Ding, Yu Zhang, Hang Zhang, Xianming Liu*

TL;DR: X-Mind提出将预测世界模型内化为视觉思维链（Visual CoT），用于端到端自动驾驶。通过紧凑的BEV草图表示（12帧仅96 token）和循环块扩散方案，在资源受限的车载平台上实现了低延迟的未来状态推理与驾驶策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型缺乏预测未来状态的能力，仅依赖反应式的感知-动作映射。虽然预测世界模型（PWM）可以弥补这一缺陷，但现有方法要么级联延迟过高，要么仅作为浅层终端任务，未能将前瞻性推理深度嵌入模型。作者希望赋予VLA模型高效的前瞻推理能力。

Method: X-Mind将预测世界模型内化为视觉思维链（Visual CoT），在动作输出前强制模型先进行世界展开（world rollout）。具体包括：(1) 紧凑的视觉思维表示——融合BEV布局与驾驶先验（导航意图、交通规则）的抽象草图，通过深度压缩自编码器（DC-AE）将12帧未来展开压缩为仅96个token；(2) 循环块扩散方案——将去噪步骤跨大驾驶模型的层展开，将迭代细化折叠到骨干网络的一次前向传播中，加速生成。

Result: 在大规模真实世界数据上训练和验证，X-Mind实现了具有竞争力的端到端驾驶性能，同时保持了低延迟，成功将大规模认知推理部署到资源受限的车载平台上。

Conclusion: X-Mind通过将预测世界模型内化为视觉思维链，为VLA模型注入了高效的前瞻推理能力，在端到端自动驾驶中实现了性能与效率的良好平衡，是一种实用、低延迟的解决方案。

Classification reason: 论文提出X-Mind框架，将预测世界模型（PWM）内化为视觉思维链（Visual CoT），用于端到端自动驾驶。核心是未来状态预测与动作条件视频生成，属于视频世界模型（video_world_model）范畴。同时涉及视觉语言模型中的推理（Visual CoT），因此也匹配多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and continuous multimodal reasoning priorities, with efficient token compression and diffusion integration. Signals: world model, visual chain-of-thought, BEV sketch token compression, recurrent block diffusion, end-to-end driving

Abstract: Predicting future states is essential for autonomous agents, yet current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models fundamentally lack this capability, relying instead on reactive perception-action mapping. While integrating Predictive World Models (PWMs) addresses this gap, existing approaches either incur prohibitive cascaded latency or act as shallow terminal tasks that fail to deeply embed forward-looking reasoning. To endow VLA models with this reasoning capability, we propose X-Mind. Rather than treating PWMs as an external auxiliary module, this framework internalizes them as the Visual Chain-of-Thought (Visual CoT). By enforcing a world rollout prior to action, the model is constrained to imagine future evolution first, yielding a driving policy that is robustly grounded in environmental dynamics and aware of the future consequences its actions will unfold. The challenge here is efficiency, and we tackle it on two fronts. First, we introduce a compact representation of visual thinking: an abstract sketch that fuses a Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) layout with abstract driving priors (e.g., navigation intents and traffic rules). Rather than rolling out dense future frames, the model reasons over this sketch as a mental canvas; aided by a Deep Compression Autoencoder (DC-AE), a 12-frame future rollout is reduced to merely 96 tokens, alleviating the long-context computational bottleneck. Second, to accelerate generation further, we propose a recurrent block diffusion scheme that unrolls the denoising steps across the layers of the large drive model, folding iterative refinement into the backbone's one forward pass. Trained and validated on large-scale real-world data, X-Mind achieves competitive end-to-end driving performance, which makes it a highly practical, low-latency solution that successfully deploys large-scale cognitive reasoning directly onto resource-constrained vehicle platforms.

</details>

Method: 定义视觉空间（Visual Space）提供显式空间先验，包括末端执行器位姿的像素对齐投影、相机视角、深度场景几何和机器人形态掩码；定义参数空间（Parameter Space）注入原始动作序列和相机矩阵提供精确运动引导。两个空间协同，使生成状态同时受几何边界约束和数值指令驱动。

Result: ViPSim 与多种骨干网络兼容，显著提升轨迹一致性；在可变形物体（如叠衣服）交互中展现涌现能力，在分布外和跨本体场景中保持鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: ViPSim 通过视觉空间与参数空间的协同协作，有效弥合了动作-视频表征鸿沟，为具身智能体的自动评估和预测控制提供了高保真基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ViPSim，通过视觉空间与参数空间协同实现一致的长时序具身世界模型生成，属于动作条件视频生成与未来预测，与 [CANONICAL] 视频世界模型（video_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model consistency for embodied agents, a high-priority subtopic, with strong synergy to continuous multimodal representations. Signals: world model, embodied world model, long-horizon generation, visual and parameter space collaboration, backbone-agnostic

Abstract: Embodied World Models (EWMs) have emerged as a scalable and risk-free paradigm for advancing embodied intelligence, enabling the safety-critical evaluation of Vision-Language-Action systems. However, their reliability as evaluation benchmarks and foundational simulators is often hindered by the representation gap between low-dimensional actions and high-dimensional video synthesis. This gap results in a lack of geometric correspondence, manifesting as accumulated trajectory drift and inconsistent robot-object interactions during long-horizon rollouts. To bridge this gap, we propose ViPSim, a framework that achieves consistent long-horizon generation through the synergistic collaboration of Visual and Parameter Spaces. We define the Visual Space as a domain of explicit spatial priors, integrating pixel-aligned projections of end-effector pose, camera perspectives, depth-informed scene geometry, and robotic morphological masks to provide dense structural grounding. Concurrently, the Parameter Space serves as a domain of numerical drivers, injecting raw action sequences and camera matrices to provide precise motion guidance. By unifying these two spaces, ViPSim ensures that the generated states are simultaneously anchored by geometric boundaries and steered by numerical commands. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ViPSim is backbone-agnostic and significantly enhances trajectory consistency. Notably, our approach exhibits emergent capabilities in generating complex interactions with deformable objects (e.g., cloth folding) and maintains robust performance in out-of-distribution and cross-embodiment scenarios, providing a high-fidelity foundation for the automated evaluation and predictive control of embodied agents.

</details>


### [53] [A Path-Space Formulation of Prediction in World Models: From a Single Action to Prediction, Planning, and Irreversibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28751)
*Gunn Kim*

Main category: cs.LG, cond-mat.stat-mech

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

TL;DR: 本文提出一种路径空间公式化方法来描述AI世界模型中的预测问题。作者认为世界模型隐式定义了未来轨迹上的概率测度，在局部马尔可夫近似下该测度具有Onsager-Machlup形式。预测（最可能轨迹）、规划（约束优化）和不确定性（涨落）统一为对单个动作泛函的操作。通过将潜动力学分解为可逆与不可逆分量，引入基于模型rollout的熵产生度量。在小规模注意力模型实验中，发现注意力不对称性在训练中与数据不可逆性成比例地习得；对称化注意力会抑制熵产生并选择性退化不可逆动力学的长时程预测，同时保留弛豫预测能力。结果表明不可逆性可作为预测性世界模型的计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型通常将预测视为单步条件分布的序列，缺乏对轨迹级概率结构的统一理解。作者希望建立一种更根本的路径空间视角，将预测、规划和不确定性统一在同一理论框架下，并探索不可逆性在世界模型预测中的作用。

Method: 提出路径空间公式化：世界模型隐式定义未来轨迹的概率测度，在局部马尔可夫近似下该测度取Onsager-Machlup形式。将潜动力学分解为可逆（保守）和不可逆（耗散）分量，引入熵产生的操作化度量。在小规模注意力模型上进行控制实验，分析注意力不对称性与数据不可逆性的关系。

Result: 注意力不对称性在训练中与数据不可逆性成比例地习得；对称化注意力会抑制熵产生，选择性退化不可逆动力学的长时程预测，同时保留弛豫预测能力。

Conclusion: 不可逆性可作为预测性世界模型的计算资源。世界模型的基本预测对象是未来路径上的分布，而非状态序列。路径空间公式化为理解世界模型中的预测、规划和不确定性提供了统一的理论基础。

Classification reason: 论文提出世界模型中基于路径空间的预测公式化框架，将预测、规划和不确定性统一为对动作泛函的操作，并讨论不可逆性对长时程预测的影响。这与[视频世界模型]子主题（关注世界模型中的未来预测、长时序建模）高度相关，因此复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model theory with a path-space formulation and entropy production measure, highly relevant to your priority on video world models and continuous representations. Signals: world model theory, path-space formulation, entropy production, irreversibility as resource, attention asymmetry

Abstract: We propose a path-space formulation of prediction in AI world models. Rather than sequences of one-step conditional distributions, we argue that a world model implicitly defines a probability measure over future trajectories. In the local regime where latent dynamics admit an effective Markovian description, this path measure takes the Onsager-Machlup form. Within this framework, prediction (most probable trajectory), planning (constrained optimization), and uncertainty (fluctuations) emerge as operations on a single action functional. We decompose the latent dynamics into reversible and irreversible components and introduce operational measures of entropy production from model rollouts. In controlled small-scale attention-based models, we find that attention asymmetry is acquired during training in proportion to the irreversibility of the data. Symmetrizing the learned attention suppresses entropy production and selectively degrades long-horizon prediction of irreversible dynamics while preserving relaxational prediction. These results suggest that irreversibility may serve as a computational resource for predictive world models. More generally, the fundamental predictive object is a distribution over future paths rather than states.

</details>

*Francois Porcher, Nicolas Carion, Karteek Alahari, Shizhe Chen*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

TL;DR: FlowWM 提出在预训练特征空间（如 DINOv3）中直接进行 flow matching 的随机世界模型，通过可微的一步投影机制实现高效训练，在合成和真实世界基准上提升了感知性能、模式覆盖和时域鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉世界模型难以同时满足两个目标：VAE 类随机模型在低维重建潜空间中操作，限制了感知性能；而使用强预训练特征的确定性预测器会将多模态未来坍缩为单一模糊均值。因此需要一种既能保持多模态不确定性、又能利用高维预训练特征的世界模型。

Method: FlowWM 在预训练特征空间（DINOv3）中执行 flow matching 进行随机未来预测。针对高维特征空间使标准扩散配方效果不佳的问题，引入可微的一步投影机制，实现具有时序一致性和任务驱动目标的高效训练。

Result: 在合成基准和真实世界 FuturePerception 基准上，FlowWM 在感知性能、模式覆盖和时域鲁棒性方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: FlowWM 验证了在高维预训练特征空间中进行 flow matching 用于随机世界建模的可行性，为兼顾预测多样性与下游感知性能提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 FlowWM，在预训练特征空间（如 DINOv3）中进行 flow matching 以实现随机世界建模，属于视频世界模型/未来预测方向，与 [CANONICAL] 视频世界模型（video_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous multimodal world models via flow matching in pretrained feature space, aligning with top priority subtopics. Signals: world model, flow matching, continuous representation, stochastic video prediction, feature-space diffusion

Abstract: World modeling requires forecasting uncertain futures while preserving information useful for downstream perception. Existing visual world models often struggle to satisfy both goals: VAE-based stochastic models operate in low-dimensional reconstruction latents, which can limit perception performance, while deterministic predictors using strong pretrained features collapse multimodal futures into a single blurry mean. In this work, we propose FlowWM, a stochastic world model that performs flow matching directly within pretrained feature space (e.g., DINOv3). This is challenging because pretrained features are substantially high-dimensional, making standard diffusion recipes suboptimal. To address this, we investigate the design choices needed for feature-space flow matching and introduce a differentiable one-step projection mechanism that enables efficient training with temporal consistency and task-driven objectives. We evaluate FlowWM on two benchmarks: a synthetic benchmark for systematic evaluation of accuracy and diversity, and a real-world benchmark FuturePerception. FlowWM improves perception performance, mode coverage, and horizon robustness, validating our proposed design for stochastic world modeling in high-dimensional feature spaces.

</details>

Importance reason: Directly addresses low-compute real-time controllable world models on consumer GPUs, aligning with world_model and efficient inference priorities. Signals: world model, low-compute, real-time, controllable, consumer GPU

Abstract: We present DreamForge-World 0.1 Preview, a preview foundational world model for real-time interactive world simulation. The system adapts the LongLive 1 autoregressive video stack, itself derived from Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B, with a residual action pathway inspired by the Matrix-Game family. DreamForge-World 0.1 Preview focuses on a complementary axis to frontier-scale world simulators: low-compute adaptation, consumer-GPU runtime, and broad interactive capability coverage. It supports live keyboard and mouse control, multimodal initialization, mid-stream reprompting, dual-view operation, and minute-scale interactive rollouts at native 480p resolution, reaching up to 14 to 15 FPS FPS on a single RTX 4090 with a low memory footprint. By leveraging open video backbones and applying targeted adaptation runs, we build the preview system with high cost-efficiency. DF-World 0.1 Preview is not yet a memory-complete or frontier-quality world simulator, but demonstrates a practical low-compute route toward real-time controllable world-model previews on consumer GPUs.

</details>

*Junjie Cheng, Ruiqi Song, Ye Wu, Nanxing Zeng, Ximiao Li, Yunfeng Ai*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

TL;DR: OWMDrive 是一个基于4D占据世界模型的因果感知端到端自动驾驶框架，通过多步3D占据预测作为条件先验来引导扩散规划器生成更稳健的驾驶轨迹，在遮挡和意外事件等挑战性场景下显著提升了规划可靠性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶方法大多基于当前场景的静态表示做决策，缺乏对未来场景的显式展开和对交通交互中时序因果动态的建模，导致在高不确定性场景（如遮挡、突发事件）下规划不稳定或过于保守。

Method: OWMDrive 构建了一个4D Occupancy World Model，用于多步3D占据预测（未来场景预测）。该预测结果作为条件先验，引导基于扩散模型的规划器迭代优化轨迹候选，生成增强的驾驶轨迹。通过显式建模未来时间范围内的场景演化，捕捉时空因果依赖关系。

Result: 大量实验表明，OWMDrive 在规划可靠性和安全性方面显著提升，尤其是在具有挑战性和部分可观测的驾驶场景中表现优异。

Conclusion: 通过将4D占据世界模型引入端到端自动驾驶，显式建模未来场景演化并捕捉时空因果依赖，可以有效提升规划的前瞻性和鲁棒性，为高不确定性场景下的安全驾驶提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于4D Occupancy World Model的端到端自动驾驶框架，核心是使用占据世界模型进行多步3D占据预测（未来预测），并以此引导扩散规划器生成轨迹。这属于视频世界模型/未来预测的范畴，与[CANONICAL] video_world_model（视频世界模型）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and video world model for autonomous driving, a high-priority subtopic with strong alignment to user's focus on generative world models. Signals: world model, 4D occupancy forecasting, diffusion-based planning, end-to-end driving, causal temporal modeling

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems are steadily moving toward end-to-end paradigms to mitigate the limited adaptability of rule-based pipelines in complex traffic environments. However, most existing learning-based methods still make decisions from static representations of the current scene, without explicit future rollouts or modeling of the temporal causal dynamics in traffic interactions. This limitation often results in unstable or overly conservative planning under high-uncertainty conditions, such as occlusions and unexpected events. To overcome these challenges, we introduce OWMDrive, a generative end-to-end driving framework built upon an Occupancy World Model for multi-step 3D occupancy forecasting, which serves as a conditional prior to guide diffusion-based planning. Conditioned on both current observations and predicted future states, the planner iteratively refines trajectory candidates to generate a reinforced driving trajectory. By explicitly modeling scene evolution over future horizons, OWMDrive captures key spatiotemporal causal dependencies, which leads to more foresighted and robust trajectory generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OWMDrive significantly improves planning reliability and safety, especially in challenging and partially observable driving scenarios.

</details>


### [57] [Orca: The World is in Your Mind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30534)
*Yihao Wang, Yuheng Ji, Mingyu Cao, Yanqing Shen, Runze Xiao, Huaihai Lyu, Senwei Xie, Euan Liu, Klara Tian, Tianfeng Long, Yichi Zhang, Zhengliang Cai, Ruike Chen, Jifan Zhao, Ruochuan Shi, Zihan Tang, Jing Lyu, Wenxing Tan, Ningbo Zhang, Yangtao Hu, Yuming Gao, Xiansheng Chen, Junkai Zhao, Congsheng Xu, Boan Zhu, Ziqi Wang, Yupu Feng, Qiongqiong Zhang, Yingli Zhao, Yulong Ao, Shaoxuan Xie, You Liu, Guocai Yao, Leiduo Zhang, Xiaodan Liu, Yunyan Zhang, Yance Jiao, Xinyan Yang, Jiaxing Wei, Xu Liu, Tengfei Pan, Shaokai Nie, Chunlei Men, Sen Cui, Xiaojie Jin, Hongyang Li, Jianlan Luo, Yao Mu, Yunchao Wei, Jun Yan, Hang Zhao, Xiaolong Zheng, Jiaming Li, Yonghua Lin, Tiejun Huang, Zhongyuan Wang, Pengwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 值得关注 (76.02) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Orca是一个通用世界基础模型，通过Next-State-Prediction范式从多模态世界信号中学习统一的世界潜空间。它采用无监督学习（从连续视频捕获密集状态转换）和有监督学习（语言描述事件和VQA）两种互补范式，在125K小时视频和1.6亿事件标注上预训练。冻结主干后，仅训练轻量级模态特定解码器即可在下游任务（文本生成、图像预测、具身动作生成）上超越同等规模专用基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型通常针对孤立任务（next-token、next-frame、next-action预测）进行优化，缺乏统一的、可泛化的世界状态转换建模能力。作者希望构建一个通用世界基础模型，能够统一理解、预测和作用于世界，通过统一的Next-State-Prediction范式学习世界的状态转换规律。

Method: Orca采用Next-State-Prediction（NSP）建模范式，包含两种互补学习方式：(1) 无意识学习（Unconscious Learning）：从连续视频中捕获密集的自然状态转换，使用视频帧序列预测下一帧的连续潜表示；(2) 意识学习（Conscious Learning）：通过语言描述的事件和VQA监督建模稀疏的有意义状态转换。模型架构包括一个共享的Transformer主干（学习统一世界潜空间）和多个轻量级模态特定解码器（文本、图像、动作）。预训练数据包含125K小时视频和1.6亿事件标注。下游任务中冻结主干，仅训练解码器。

Result: Orca在文本生成、图像预测和具身动作生成三个下游任务上均优于同等规模的专用基线模型。实验验证了该范式的可扩展性，并证明更强的世界潜空间能够带来更强的下游任务表现。

Conclusion: Orca作为通用世界基础模型，通过Next-State-Prediction范式学习统一世界潜空间，为理解、预测和作用于世界提供了一种有前景的方法。论文讨论了当前局限性，为社区提供有益见解和启发。

Classification reason: 该论文提出Orca世界基础模型，核心是Next-State-Prediction（下一状态预测），通过无监督视频学习（连续视频中的密集状态转换）和有监督事件学习（语言描述的事件和VQA）构建统一世界潜空间，并支持文本生成、图像预测和具身动作生成等下游任务。这属于视频世界模型（video_world_model）的典型工作，同时涉及连续潜空间建模（continuous_language_multimodal方向）和多模态理解与生成（multimodal_understanding_generation方向）。

Importance reason: Directly advances the user's top-priority continuous multimodal world model via Next-State-Prediction and unified latent space. Signals: world foundation model, Next-State-Prediction, continuous latent space, 125K hours video, 160M event annotations

Abstract: We introduce Orca, an initial instantiation of a general world foundation model. Orca learns a unified world latent space from multimodal world signals and exposes it through multimodal readout interfaces. Rather than optimizing isolated next-token, next-frame, or next-action prediction, we are centered on Next-State-Prediction modeling, offering a unified state-transition modeling route toward understanding, predicting, and acting upon the world. Orca learns through two complementary paradigms: unconscious learning captures dense natural state transitions from continuous videos, and conscious learning models sparse meaningful state transitions by language-described events and VQA supervision. For pre-training, we construct a large-scale world-learning inventory data, including 125K hours of video data and 160M event annotations. After pre-training, Orca learns a unified world latent space. To examine whether the learned latent supports downstream, we evaluate it by three representative downstream readouts: text generation, image prediction, and embodied action generation. Orca's backbone is frozen, and only the lightweight modality-specific decoders are trainable. Experiments show the scalability of the proposed paradigm and verify that stronger world latent enables stronger downstream readouts. Orca outperforms similar-sized specialized baselines. These results show that Orca, as a general world foundation model, presents a promising approach to understanding, predicting, and acting upon the world. Finally, we discuss the current limitations, aiming to provide useful insights and inspiration for the community.

</details>

### [58] [SatSplat: Geometrically-Accurate Gaussian Splatting for Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28581)
*Shuang Song, Jiyong Kim, Rongjun Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (44.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SatSplat 是首个将 2D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS) 适配到卫星摄影测量的框架，结合在线相机调整、几何阴影映射和逐相机颜色校正，在 DFC2019 和 IARPA2016 基准上实现了比现有 3DGS 方法更高的几何精度和更低的内存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率卫星影像需要快速且几何精确的三维重建方法。现有 3DGS 方法在卫星影像上因多日期、高海拔采集带来的光照变化和小交会角导致重建质量下降，限制了其在遥感与视觉任务中的应用。

Method: 1) 用仿射模型近似卫星相机，学习最小 delta 参数化进行在线相机精调；2) 采用 2DGS 场景表示替代 3DGS；3) 集成几何阴影映射和逐相机颜色校正以处理时变阴影和光照变化。

Result: 在 DFC2019 基准上，相比之前最优 3DGS 方法，平均绝对误差降低 11.93%，峰值显存减少 31%。在 DFC2019 和 IARPA2016 基准上均实现了更强的几何精度。

Conclusion: SatSplat 证明了 2DGS 结合在线相机调整和光照处理策略能够有效提升卫星影像三维重建的几何精度和计算效率，为大规模数字表面建模提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文将 2D Gaussian Splatting 适配到卫星影像三维重建，属于 3D 场景重建与神经渲染范畴，与 canonical 子主题 "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on satellite-specific 3D reconstruction, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, satellite imagery, geometric accuracy, memory efficiency, not autoregressive

Abstract: High-resolution satellite imagery demands 3D reconstruction methods that deliver both speed and geometric accuracy. Recent adaptations of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) to satellite imagery demonstrate strong efficiency, but reconstruction quality often degrades under diverse illumination across multi-date, high-altitude acquisitions (with small intersection angles), limiting applicability to remote sensing and vision tasks. We present SatSplat, the first framework to adapt 2D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS) to satellite photogrammetry, with online camera adjustment. We approximate satellite cameras with an affine model and learn a minimal delta parameterization for in-splat camera refinement from dense observations. The formulation is implemented with a 2DGS scene representation. To handle time-varying shadows and illumination changes, we integrate geometric shadow mapping and per-camera color correction during training. Across the evaluated DFC2019 and IARPA2016 benchmark sites, SatSplat achieves strong geometric accuracy while significantly outperforming prior 3DGS-based baselines. On our processed DFC2019 benchmark, SatSplat reduces mean absolute error by 11.93% and peak video memory by 31% relative to the previous state of the art. Our approach enables large-scale digital surface modeling with practical computational efficiency. The project page is available at .

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: DLGStream 成功将语言特征高效嵌入 3DGS 的 FVV 表示中，在保持低存储开销的同时实现了开放词汇交互和场景编辑能力，为沉浸式自由视角视频应用提供了新方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DLGStream，一种将语言特征嵌入 3DGS 的动态自由视角视频流表示方法，核心属于 3DGS 的扩展与改进，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）。同时涉及开放词汇分割等理解能力，因此也匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Novel language-embedded 3DGS for video streaming, but tangential to user's core focus on continuous language/multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, open-vocabulary video, free-viewpoint, low frame size, dual-opacity

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting~(3DGS) has emerged as a promising paradigm for reconstructing streamable free-viewpoint video~(FVV) from multi-view videos. However, 3DGS-based FVVs typically lack user interaction and editing capabilities, which diminishes the immersive experience. Recent research has integrated language features from CLIP into 3DGS via distillation, enabling open-vocabulary queries and supporting many downstream applications. Nevertheless, the stringent requirements of FVV, low frame size and high FPS, make current language Gaussian representations unsuitable for language-embedded FVV. In this paper, we propose DLGStream, a novel language-embedded FVV representation that streams time-varying language features alongside Gaussian attributes to support 4D environment interaction, scene editing, and spatial intelligence. Specifically, we propose a dual-opacity dynamic language Gaussian representation, which maintains two opacity attributes for color and language features to deal with performance degradation that occurs when colors and features are jointly optimized. Furthermore, we introduce an interpolation-based deformation field to reduce temporal redundancy. This deformation field can also be used for 4D frame interpolation, boosting FVV sequences from low to high FPS. Experimental results demonstrate that DLGStream achieves superior performance in both on open-vocabulary segmentation and reconstruction quality with an average frame size of merely 43 KB. The code is available on \href{ }{ }.

</details>

*Francesca Pia Panaccione, Eugenio Lomurno, Francesca Fati, Carlotta Pecchiari, Marina Rosanu, Luigi De Vitis, Lucia Ribero, Gabriella Schivardi, Giovanni Damiano Aletti, Nicoletta Colombo, Maria Francesca Spadea, Francesco Multinu, Matteo Matteucci, Elena De Momi*

TL;DR: 本文提出 OvESyn，首个面向腹盆腔肿瘤（卵巢癌）的文本条件3D CT合成框架。它无需原始放射学报告，而是从CT影像描述符和临床元数据自动构建 Findings/Impression 文本，用于条件化潜扩散模型。系统消融实验表明，生成器域适应是跨域迁移的关键机制，而视觉-语言编码器对齐则优化细节保真度。最终模型在493例患者数据上取得最佳分布保真度（FID2.5D 29.35）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卵巢癌常在晚期才被诊断，术前增强CT对分期和手术规划至关重要，但标注影像数据稀缺且受隐私法规限制，阻碍了通用计算模型的发展。现有文本条件3D CT合成依赖配对放射学报告且仅在胸部CT上评估，无法直接应用于腹盆腔肿瘤场景。

Method: 提出 OvESyn 框架：(1) 从CT影像描述符和临床元数据自动构建标准化的 Findings 和 Impression 文本，无需原始放射学报告；(2) 使用这些文本条件化一个潜扩散模型，该模型在493例高级别浆液性卵巢癌患者数据上微调；(3) 系统消融两个适应轴：视觉-语言编码器对齐和生成器微调，分析各自对域适应的贡献。

Result: 完整 OvESyn 取得最佳分布和强度保真度（FID2.5D 29.35, Precision 0.671, Wasserstein-1 0.044）；仅生成器微调变体最大化覆盖度（Recall 0.645），体现了编码器适应带来的保真度/覆盖度权衡。无生成器域适应时，合成结果仍锚定在胸部预训练域，Precision和Recall降为零，FID2.5D超过140。

Conclusion: OvESyn 是首个面向腹盆腔肿瘤的文本条件3D CT合成框架，仅需自动分割和常规术前元数据即可工作，支持向报告稀缺场景迁移，为腹盆腔肿瘤影像的合成队列生成奠定了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出文本条件3D CT图像合成框架，属于3D医学图像生成任务，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）合理相关，因此复用该分类。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical CT synthesis for ovarian cancer, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and includes negative keywords like clinical and medical diagnosis. Signals: medical domain, 3D CT synthesis, ovarian cancer, domain adaptation, latent diffusion

Abstract: Ovarian cancer is frequently diagnosed at an advanced stage, making preoperative contrast-enhanced computed tomography (CT) central to staging and surgical planning; yet the scarcity of annotated imaging data, compounded by privacy regulations, limits the development of generalizable computational models in this domain. Text-conditioned 3D CT synthesis has shown promise, but existing pipelines depend on paired radiology reports and have been evaluated only on chest CT. We propose OvESyn (Ovarian Evidence-based Synthesis), a framework that constructs standardized Findings and Impression sections directly from CT-derived imaging descriptors and routine clinical metadata, without any original radiology report, and uses them to condition a latent diffusion model adapted to 493 high-grade serous ovarian carcinoma patients. This is the first text-conditioned 3D CT synthesis framework adapted to an abdomino-pelvic oncologic setting. A systematic ablation over two adaptation axes, vision-language encoder alignment and generator fine-tuning, identifies generator domain adaptation as the operative mechanism for crossing the domain gap and establishing the target anatomy: without it, synthesis remains anchored to the thoracic pretraining domain, with Precision and Recall collapsing to zero and FID2.5D exceeding 140, regardless of encoder alignment. Encoder alignment instead refines intensity and fine detail. The full OvESyn attains the best distributional and intensity fidelity (FID2.5D 29.35, Precision 0.671, Wasserstein-1 0.044), while the generator-only variant maximizes coverage (Recall 0.645), reflecting a fidelity/coverage trade-off governed by encoder adaptation. Requiring only automatic segmentations and routine preoperative metadata, OvESyn supports transferability to report-scarce settings and provides a foundation for synthetic cohort generation in abdomino-pelvic oncologic imaging.

### [61] [Pointer-CAD v2: Plan-Then-Construct CAD Generation with Dimension-Aware Parametric Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29301)
*Dacheng Qi, Chenyu Wang, Jingwei Xu, Yi Ma, Shenghua Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: Pointer-CAD v2 提出了一种"先规划后构建"的 CAD 生成框架，通过指针机制直接预测连续参数值，避免了传统量化误差，并引入分层几何精度指标，在参数化精度上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的 CAD 生成方法主要关注视觉相似性，忽略了精确的几何参数和正确的度量尺度。由于采用命令序列表示并激进地量化数值参数，导致微小数值偏差虽在形状层面可忽略，但可能违反工业公差要求。

Method: 提出 Plan-Then-Construct 范式，将参数推理与几何构建解耦。首先生成带有显式度量尺度参数的结构化设计计划，将参数组织为字典；在序列生成过程中通过指针机制直接引用这些参数，从而消除离散化误差，确保维度一致的执行。

Result: 构建了带有计划级标注的大规模新数据集，引入顶点、边、面三个层次的分层几何精度指标。大量实验表明 Pointer-CAD v2 在几何精度上持续优于现有基线，实现了面向精度关键工程应用的可靠 CAD 生成。

Conclusion: Pointer-CAD v2 通过解耦规划与构建、直接预测连续参数值，有效解决了 CAD 生成中的参数精度问题，为工业级精密制造提供了可行的生成方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 CAD（计算机辅助设计）生成，属于 3D 物体/场景生成范畴，与 canonical 子主题 "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on CAD generation with parametric precision, which is outside the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and video/image generation. Signals: CAD generation, parametric precision, plan-then-construct, pointer mechanism, continuous value prediction

Abstract: Computer-aided design (CAD) plays a fundamental role in modern manufacturing by providing the high precision required for industrial production. Recent large language model based approaches formulate CAD generation as a sequence prediction problem and have achieved promising results. However, existing methods and evaluation protocols primarily emphasize visual similarity, while overlooking precise geometric parameters and correct metric scale. Small numerical deviations that are negligible at the shape-level may still violate industrial tolerance requirements, a problem further compounded by current autoregressive paradigms that utilize command sequence representations, aggressively quantize numerical parameters to ease LLM prediction. In this work, we present Pointer-CAD v2. Compared with v1 ( ), this version directly predicts continuous values, bypassing the need for quantized numerical parameters and thereby eliminating quantization errors. Specifically, we propose a unified framework that decouples parameter reasoning from geometric construction through a Plan-Then-Construct paradigm. Our method first produces a structured design plan with explicit metric scale parameters. These parameters are organized into a dictionary and directly referenced during sequence generation via a pointer mechanism, eliminating discretization errors and ensuring dimensionally consistent execution. In addition, we construct a new large-scale dataset with plan-level annotation and introduce three hierarchical geometry accuracy metrics to evaluate parametric fidelity at the vertex, edge, and face levels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Pointer-CAD v2 consistently outperforms existing baselines and achieves substantial improvements in geometric accuracy, enabling reliable CAD generation for precision-critical engineering applications.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏且遮挡严重的真实视角进行多物体3D重建时，物体之间因遮挡和视角有限而相互耦合，现有方法通常依赖精确掩码，限制了实际应用。本文旨在无需掩码的条件下实现无损的多物体解耦重建，并支持物理交互仿真。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，该方法无需人工掩码即可生成完整的、可直接用于仿真的3D物体，实现了逼真的动态交互效果。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过将多物体解耦建模为稀疏视图重建问题，结合3DGS、SAM2分割场和扩散先验引导的SDS，可以在无需掩码的条件下实现遮挡鲁棒的多物体3D重建，并支持物理仿真。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于3D高斯泼溅的多物体3D重建方法，属于3D生成与重建方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel occlusion-robust multi-object decoupling for physics interaction, but tangential to user's priority on continuous language/multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, SAM2 segmentation, Score Distillation Sampling, MPM simulation, sparse-view reconstruction

Abstract: We propose a mask-free method for lossless multi-object 3D reconstruction from sparse and occluded real-world views, enabling physically plausible interaction via Material Point Method (MPM) simulation. Our key insight is that object coupling stems from occlusion and limited viewpoints, which we address by formulating multi-object decoupling as a sparse-view reconstruction problem. Using 3D Gaussian Splatting as base representation, we first obtain coarse instance partitions with a SAM2-trained segmentation field. Rather than relying on masks, we reconstruct fragmented geometries by leveraging a joint Score Distillation Sampling (SDS) process, which integrates reference-view supervision with novel-view synthesis guided by 2D and 3D diffusion priors to enforce both texture fidelity and 3D consistency. Furthermore, we incorporate geometry-aware priors such as intra-object and inter-object similarity to regularize geometric reasoning. Experimental results demonstrate that our method produces complete, simulation-ready 3D objects without requiring manual masks, enabling realistic dynamic interactions on both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3DGS 虚拟人动画缺乏物理合理的阴影投射，尤其是在多虚拟人或虚拟人与物体交互场景中。先前方法依赖网格重建或二值碰撞检测，无法在纯高斯空间中实现精确阴影计算。

Conclusion: RAGA 证明了在纯高斯空间中进行物理合理阴影投射的可行性，为 3DGS 虚拟人场景交互提供了高效且逼真的渲染方案。
