Importance reason: Focuses on 3DGS avatar shadow rendering, which is not aligned with user's priority subtopics on continuous language/video generation or world models. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, ray-traced shadows, avatar-scene interaction, real-time rendering, no mesh reconstruction

Abstract: We study the problem of physically plausible shadow casting when animating 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) avatars, either individually or in multi-avatar and object-interaction scenarios, within existing 3DGS scenes. In contrast to prior methods that rely on binary hit tests and mesh-based shadow casters, our method performs shadow computation entirely in Gaussian space, without requiring any mesh reconstruction. We introduce RAGA, a Ray-Traced Gaussian Shadow Casting formulation based on exact ray-Gaussian line integrals. For each occluding Gaussian, we integrate the opacity profile along the shadow ray and normalize by the theoretical maximum integral, producing a weight that captures how the ray traverses the occluder rather than merely whether an intersection occurred. To reduce temporal variance from clothing deformations in animated avatars, we further introduce an avatar proxy representation that stabilizes shadow casting while preserving visual fidelity. We implement RAGA using custom CUDA kernels integrated with the NVIDIA OptiX framework; as such, our shadow tracer runs at rates of about 50 FPS. We evaluate on single-avatar, multi-avatar, and avatar-object interaction scenarios across multiple datasets, demonstrating substantially improved shadow realism, temporal stability, and scene coherence. Our project page is available at .

</details>

Method: 将问题分解为两个子任务：(1) 前景3D高斯重建——提出尺度同步相机标定解决多视角监督的尺度模糊性，以及高斯级前景掩码通过调制高斯参数重建干净前景；(2) 高效高分辨率合成——提出高分辨率侧调优，在保持0.5K骨干网络的同时为高斯头补充额外特征，实现2K渲染。

Result: 实验表明，HiReFF 在高分辨率流式体积视频重建中显著优于现有方法，实现了2K分辨率的360°人体视频重建。

Conclusion: HiReFF 证明了从无标定稀疏视角视频进行高分辨率前馈式人体重建的可行性，为全息通信和AR/VR应用提供了高效解决方案。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D human reconstruction from video, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks alignment with key continuous/multimodal generation or world model directions. Signals: Sparse-view video reconstruction, 2K resolution, feed-forward method, uncalibrated cameras, 3D Gaussian splatting

Abstract: Uncalibrated volumetric video streaming for human reconstruction is essential for holographic communication and AR/VR, yet remains challenging due to the need for temporal consistency and computational efficiency from sparse-view inputs. Existing methods rely on per-scene optimization or calibrated cameras, while recent feed-forward models are limited to low-resolution (0.5K) single-frame synthesis. We present HiReFF, a feed-forward method for 2K-resolution 360° human video reconstruction from uncalibrated sparse-view videos. Our framework decomposes the problem into two key tasks: foreground 3D Gaussian reconstruction from sparse-view videos (four views separated by 90°) and computationally efficient high-resolution synthesis. To enable the former, we propose Scale-synchronized Camera Calibration to resolve scale ambiguity for multi-view supervision, and Gaussian-wise Foreground Masking to reconstruct clean foregrounds by modulating Gaussian parameters. For efficient high-resolution synthesis, our High-resolution Side-tuning achieves 2K rendering by augmenting the Gaussian head with supplementary features while keeping the backbone at 0.5K, drastically reducing computational overhead. Experiments demonstrate that HiReFF significantly outperforms existing methods in high-resolution streaming volumetric video reconstruction.

</details>


### [65] [DR-GS: Physically-Based Deformable and Relightable 2D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29379)
*Jiaxin Li, Tong Wu, Yi Wei, Tailin Wu, Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: DR-GS 提出了一种基于2D高斯泼溅的统一框架，通过显式解耦几何、光照和材质表示，实现了可变形物体的物理一致重光照与后编辑，在静态重建、动态变形和重光照任务上均达到领先的视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯泼溅的可变形物体方法存在两个关键局限：(1) 光照被错误地烘焙到纹理中，导致动态变形和光照变化下物理不一致；(2) 基于快照的重建限制了重建后的材质编辑。因此需要一种能解耦几何、光照和材质的统一框架。

Method: DR-GS 在2D高斯泼溅框架中引入基于物理的逆渲染，显式解耦几何（2D高斯位置与形状）、光照（环境光照）和材质（BRDF参数）表示，支持可变形物体的重光照和变形感知操作。

Result: 在静态重建、动态变形和重光照任务上均达到领先的视觉质量，能可靠地保持光泽表面的反射和镜面高光，并实现了完全解耦的几何-光照-材质管线。

Conclusion: DR-GS 克服了静态快照的限制，解决了不同条件下外观不真实的问题，同时支持重建后的参数编辑，为高质量3D资产创建和全面后编辑提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于2D高斯泼溅的可变形可重光照框架，涉及3DGS、神经渲染、重光照等核心内容，属于[3D 生成与重建]（three_d_generation）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Strong in 3D generation and relighting but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive video generation. Signals: 2D Gaussian splatting, physically-based rendering, deformable objects, relighting, material editing

Abstract: Gaussian splatting (GS) has garnered significant attention in VR/AR and digital content creation due to its explicit parameterization and efficient rendering capabilities. However, existing GS-based methods for deformable objects face two key limitations: (i) illumination is erroneously baked into textures, causing physically inconsistent responses under dynamic deformations and lighting changes; (ii) snapshot-based reconstruction restricts post-reconstruction material editing. To address these challenges, we propose Deformable and Relightable GS (DR-GS), a unified Gaussian framework that integrates physically-based inverse rendering, relighting, and deformation-aware manipulation. Through explicitly disentangling geometry, illumination, and material representations, DR-GS overcomes the limitations of static snapshots, resolving unrealistic appearance under varying conditions while enabling post-reconstruction parameter editing. Extensive experiments show that DR-GS achieves leading visual quality across static reconstruction, dynamic deformation, and relighting, reliably preserving reflections and specular highlights on glossy surfaces. It further establishes a fully decoupled geometry-illumination-material pipeline, enabling high-quality 3D asset creation and comprehensive post-editing.

</details>


### [66] [NaLA: A 3D Native LLM Layout Agent for High-quality 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29395)
*Cheng Wan, Yongsen Mao, Wenzheng Wu, Yuxuan Xie, Chucheng Xiang, Runze Wang, Xiang Zhang, Zhongyuan Liu, Rushi Dai, Yuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Importance: 普通相关 (55.66) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: NaLA 是一个原生 3D LLM 布局智能体，通过直接编码 3D 场景边界和资产到 LLM 中，并采用粗到细的位姿预测机制，实现了高质量、几何一致的 3D 场景布局生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的 3D 场景布局智能体大多将 3D 资产和布局转换为文本描述作为输入输出，导致严重的模态间隙和信息损失，生成的布局不合理。因此需要一种原生 3D 的 LLM 布局方法。

Method: NaLA 将 3D 场景边界和 3D 资产直接编码到 LLM 中，保留细粒度几何信息，支持碰撞、表面支撑、包含等关系的显式推理。采用粗到细的位姿预测机制：先自回归预测离散位姿，再通过连续回归进行精炼。在多样化布局数据集上训练。

Result: 实验表明 NaLA 在生成质量和推理效率上均优于先前的布局智能体，并通过全面的消融研究验证了各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: NaLA 通过原生 3D 编码和粗到细预测机制，有效弥合了文本与 3D 之间的模态间隙，为高质量 3D 场景生成提供了新的布局智能体范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 NaLA，一个原生 3D LLM 布局智能体，用于高质量 3D 场景生成。核心任务是 3D 场景中物体的布局生成与放置，属于 3D 场景生成范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 "3D 生成与重建"。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D scene layout generation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: 3D scene generation, LLM layout agent, coarse-to-fine pose prediction, geometry reasoning, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as promising layout agents for 3D scene generation. Existing layout agents still suffer from implausible layout generation because most of them convert 3D assets and 3D layouts into textual descriptions as inputs and outputs, which involves severe information loss due to the modality gap between texts and 3D assets and 3D layouts. We propose NaLA, a native 3D LLM layout Agent for high-quality 3D scene generation by placing 3D assets in the scene. For the inputs, NaLA encodes 3D scene boundaries and 3D assets directly into the LLM, preserving fine-grained geometry and enabling explicit reasoning over relationships like collisions, surface supporting, and containment. To accurately output the positions and orientations of assets, NaLA adopts a coarse-to-fine prediction mechanism that first predicts discrete poses in an autoregressive manner and then refines the discrete poses with a continuous regression. Trained on diverse layout datasets, NaLA attains strong geometric perception and layout coherence. Experiments demonstrate that NaLA outperforms prior layout agents in both generation quality and inference efficiency, with comprehensive ablation studies to verify each component's effectiveness.

</details>


### [67] [VCS-SLAM: Geometry-Validated Semantic Evidence Fusion for 3D Gaussian SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29494)
*Raman Jha, Shuaihang Yuan, Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (44.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VCS-SLAM 提出一种几何验证的语义证据融合框架，用于 RGB-D 3D Gaussian SLAM。通过可见性一致性、表面支撑边界证据和射线级冲突不确定性评估语义观测的几何可靠性，抑制遮挡语义更新、减少语义渗漏并延迟模糊区域的标签分配，在 Replica 和 ScanNet 上提升了语义一致性和重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义 3D Gaussian SLAM 方法在在线建图中将所有 2D 语义先验视为同等可靠的监督信号，忽略了遮挡、不支持的语义边界和模糊射线几何带来的不可靠性，导致全局高斯地图中出现持久的语义伪影。

Method: 提出 VCS-SLAM 框架，通过三个几何验证机制评估语义观测的可靠性：(1) 可见性一致性检查，判断观测是否被遮挡；(2) 表面支撑边界证据，验证语义边界是否有几何支撑；(3) 射线级冲突不确定性，检测射线路径上的语义冲突。基于这些可靠性评估构建加权优化目标，抑制不可靠的语义更新。

Result: 在 Replica 数据集上，VCS-SLAM 提升了语义一致性、边界保持和重建质量；在 ScanNet 数据集上，在真实 RGB-D 输入下保持了有竞争力的跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 通过几何验证的语义融合策略能有效减少 3D Gaussian SLAM 中的语义伪影，提升建图的语义一致性和几何精度，为语义 SLAM 提供了一种更鲁棒的在线融合方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 VCS-SLAM，一种基于 3D Gaussian Splatting 的 RGB-D SLAM 方法，核心涉及 3D 场景重建与语义建图，属于 3DGS 在 SLAM 中的应用，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" (three_d_generation)。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D Gaussian SLAM with semantic fusion, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: 3D Gaussian SLAM, semantic evidence fusion, geometry validation, RGB-D mapping, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Visual SLAM performance often deteriorates in complex real-world applications. Semantic 3D Gaussian SLAM commonly fuses 2D semantic priors into a persistent 3D map using uniform optimization weights. However, such priors are not equally reliable in online mapping: occlusions, unsupported semantic boundaries, and ambiguous ray geometry can introduce persistent semantic artifacts into the global Gaussian map. We propose VCS-SLAM, a geometry-validated semantic evidence fusion framework for RGB-D 3D Gaussian SLAM. Instead of treating all semantic observations as uniformly valid supervision, VCS-SLAM evaluates their geometric reliability through visibility consistency, surface-supported boundary evidence, and ray-level conflict uncertainty. The resulting reliability-aware objective suppresses occluded semantic updates, reduces unsupported semantic bleeding, and delays premature label assignment in ambiguous regions. Experiments on Replica demonstrate improved semantic consistency, boundary preservation, and reconstruction quality. Results on ScanNet further show that VCS-SLAM maintains competitive tracking performance under real RGB-D inputs

</details>

*Wongi Park, Jiyeon Lim, Minjae Lee, Myeongseok Nam, Seongjun Choi, Jungwoo Kim, Soomok Lee, William J. Beksi, SangHyun Lee*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3DGS 方法在处理颜色或语义上与静态场景高度相似的干扰物（如透明物体、颜色相近的行人）时，难以有效区分瞬态元素与静态场景，导致新视角合成中出现伪影。作者希望解决这一模糊场景下的干扰物去除问题。

Method: 提出 RefineSplat 框架：(1) 利用熵（entropy）和实例掩码（instance masks）构建自适应瞬态掩码，捕捉模糊干扰物；(2) 提出熵感知密度控制（entropy-aware density control），基于熵感知的位置梯度对齐高斯体；(3) 构建 Ambiguous wild 数据集用于验证。

Result: 在多个数据集上，RefineSplat 在无干扰物新视角合成任务上达到 SOTA 性能，尤其在颜色或语义模糊场景中显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 熵感知的自适应掩码方法能有效处理模糊场景中的干扰物去除，为 3DGS 在复杂真实场景中的应用提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 RefineSplat，基于 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) 进行无干扰物的新视角合成，属于 3D 场景重建与渲染方向，因此归入 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" (three_d_generation)。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3DGS distractor removal, not aligned with user's priority on continuous language/multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, Entropy-aware masking, Distractor removal, Novel view synthesis, Ambiguous wild dataset

Abstract: We present RefineSplat, a systematic framework that effectively constructs transient masks to identify diverse ambiguous distractors. To do this, we qualitatively and quantitatively analyze issues and propose a novel entropy-aware adaptive masking method. Unlike existing approaches that struggle to distinguish transient elements from static scenes due to color or semantic ambiguity, RefineSplat captures ambiguous distractors leveraging entropy and instance masks. Furthermore, we propose a simple yet effective entropy-aware density control to align Gaussians in ambiguous scenarios considering Entropy-aware positional gradients. Additionally, to rigorously validate our method, we first create and release the Ambiguous wild dataset, including 18 scenes where distractors and static scenes are hard to distinguish due to color or semantic resemblances. Experimental results on various datasets demonstrate that RefineSplat shows state-of-the-art performance, showing distractor-free novel view synthesis.

</details>


### [69] [UniTriSplat: A Unified 3D Gaussian Splatting Framework with Uniform Spherical Rasterization for Universal Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29794)
*Yipeng Zhu, Huajian Huang, Tristan Braud, Sai-Kit Yeung*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: UniTriSplat 提出一种基于 HEALPix 球面均匀栅格化的统一 3DGS 框架，将高斯泼溅从相机专用栅格化推广到单位球面，支持透视、鱼眼、全景等通用相机模型，并引入球面感知的 SSIM 损失，提升跨相机泛化能力与渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3DGS 框架依赖特定相机模型的栅格化（如透视投影），在不同相机模型（鱼眼、全景等）上存在立体角采样不一致的问题，导致性能下降。作者希望提出一个统一框架，使 3DGS 能无缝适用于任意相机模型。

Method: 1) 将高斯泼溅重新定义在单位球面上，利用 HEALPix 等面积离散化构建球面采样网格，使其与输入图像的角分辨率对齐。2) 在球面弧度域中推导前向渲染和梯度传播公式，实现从窄视场到360度全景的统一优化行为。3) 引入 HEALPix 感知的 SSIM 损失，尊重球面邻域结构以提升感知重建质量。

Result: 在多种相机模型（透视、鱼眼、全景）上的实验表明，UniTriSplat 在跨相机泛化、几何保真度和渲染质量上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: UniTriSplat 通过球面均匀栅格化实现了通用相机模型下的统一 3DGS 框架，为多相机系统、全景渲染等应用提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出统一的3D高斯泼溅框架，属于3D场景表示与渲染方法，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel unified 3DGS framework for universal cameras, but tangential to user's core focus on continuous language/multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, HEALPix spherical rasterization, universal camera models, cross-camera generalization, SSIM loss adaptation

Abstract: Existing 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) frameworks rely on camera-specific rasterization, suffering from inconsistent solid-angle sampling and degraded performance across heterogeneous camera models (e.g., perspective, fisheye, omnidirectional). To address this limitation, we propose UniTriSplat, a unified 3DGS framework for universal cameras that reformulates Gaussian splatting on the unit sphere via HEALPix discretization. Leveraging the equal-area property of HEALPix, we construct a spherical sampling grid aligned with the angular resolution of input images. We derive the forward rendering and gradient propagation of Gaussians directly in the spherical radian domain, yielding uniform optimization behavior from narrow-FoV images to full 360-degree panoramas. To enhance perceptual reconstruction quality, we additionally introduce a HEALPix-aware SSIM loss that respects spherical neighborhood structure. Extensive experiments across diverse camera models demonstrate that UniTriSplat consistently improves cross-camera generalization while preserving geometric fidelity and rendering quality.

</details>


### [70] [DCGrasp: Distance-aware Controllable Grasp Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29924)
*Hiroyasu Akada, Jesús Pérez, Emre Aksan, Vasileios Choutas, Cristian Romero, Alberto Garcia-Garcia, Vladislav Golyanik, Christian Theobalt, Thabo Beeler*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (50.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DCGrasp 提出了一种基于距离感知的抓取能量项（Distance Profile）和 Diffusion Transformer 的可控抓取生成系统，能够生成高质量、物理合理的 3D 手-物体交互，并支持灵活的用户控制与跨物体/手型泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 手-物体交互生成方法在灵活可控性和对不同物体几何的泛化能力上存在不足，难以满足机器人、XR 和合成数据生成等实际应用需求。

Method: 提出 Distance Profile（每个手部顶点到最近物体点的带符号距离及距离感知权重），基于 Diffusion Transformer 首先生成 Distance Profile 和候选手部姿态，再通过优化使手部姿态与生成的 Distance Profile 在近接触区域保持一致。

Result: 实验表明 DCGrasp 能生成高质量、物理合理的抓取姿态，支持灵活的用户控制，并能泛化到多样的物体和手部形状与尺度。

Conclusion: DCGrasp 建立了一个鲁棒且通用的可控 3D 手-物体交互合成管线，为相关应用提供了有效解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DCGrasp，基于 Diffusion Transformer 生成可控的 3D 手-物体抓取交互，属于 3D 交互生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D grasp generation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks alignment with key boost keywords. Signals: 3D hand-object interaction, controllable generation, diffusion transformer, distance-aware energy term, cross-object generalization

Abstract: Generating 3D hand-object interactions is essential for applications in robotics, XR, and synthetic data generation, where flexible controllability and strong generalization to diverse object geometries are required. However, existing methods rarely satisfy these requirements, limiting their practical applicability. We present DCGrasp, a distance-aware controllable grasp generation system built on a novel grasp energy term. This term computes Distance Profile, a signed distance from each hand vertex to the nearest object point, coupled with distance-aware weighting, effectively capturing the semantically similar hand-object interaction in near-contact regions while remaining invariant to object and hand identity. Given various controllable signals, DCGrasp first generates a Distance Profile based on a Diffusion Transformer, together with a corresponding candidate hand pose. We then refine the candidate pose through optimization, enforcing consistency between the optimized hand pose and the generated Distance Profile in near-contact regions. Our experiments show that DCGrasp produces high-quality, physically plausible grasps with flexible user control, generalizing to diverse object and hand shapes and scales. Our work establishes a robust and versatile pipeline for the synthesis of controllable 3D hand-object interactions.

</details>


### [71] [Variance Reduction on the Camera Axis: Multi-View Score Distillation for 3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29964)
*Marian Lupascu, Mihai Sorin Stupariu, Ionut Mironica*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MV-SDI（多视角聚合分数蒸馏），在不重新训练扩散先验、不使用多视角数据的前提下，通过在每步梯度计算中聚合 K 个视角的梯度（使用对跖对抗采样实现角度均匀覆盖），显著降低 SDS 的梯度方差，提升 3D 生成质量。在固定 10,000 UNet 调用预算下，K=2 将 CLIP R-Precision 从 74.8% 提升至 83.8%，且优化步数减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分数蒸馏（Score Distillation）方法在将 2D 扩散模型转化为 3D 生成器时，每步梯度仅从单个随机视角估计，导致梯度方差大且缺乏全局形状一致性。先前工作通过在多视角数据上重新训练扩散先验来缓解该问题，但这使得采样贡献与先验质量不可分离。本文希望在不修改先验、不依赖多视角训练数据的前提下，从采样策略角度降低梯度方差。

Method: 提出 Multi-View Aggregated Score Distillation (MV-SDI)。核心思想：每步梯度是对视角期望的一个噪声样本，通过在固定总 UNet 调用预算下每步聚合 K 个视角的梯度（梯度累积实现，峰值显存不变），降低方差。视角采样采用对跖对抗对（antithetic antipodal pairs），这是一种与先验无关的几何特性，实现角度均匀覆盖。该方法兼容基于梯度的分数蒸馏管线（如 Score Distillation via Inversion），无需重新训练，无需多视角数据。

Result: 在固定 10,000 UNet 调用预算下：K=2 时 CLIP R-Precision 从 74.8% 提升至 83.8%，CLIP score 从 0.297 提升至 0.312，HPSv2 和 ImageReward 指标一致提升，43 个 prompt 基准上发散率为 0.0%，优化步数减半。K=4 时步数减少四倍，R-Precision 达 86.9%，CLIP score 0.307，所有对齐指标仍显著优于单视角基线。

Conclusion: MV-SDI 通过多视角梯度聚合与对跖对抗采样，在不修改扩散先验、不依赖多视角训练数据的前提下，有效降低了分数蒸馏的梯度方差，显著提升了 3D 生成质量和对齐指标，且与现有梯度式分数蒸馏管线兼容。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MV-SDI，通过多视角梯度聚合改进基于分数蒸馏的 3D 生成（Score Distillation Sampling），属于 3D 生成方法，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"。

Importance reason: Improves 3D generation via variance reduction in score distillation, but is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: 3D generation, score distillation, variance reduction, multi-view aggregation, no retraining needed

Abstract: Score distillation turns a pretrained 2D diffusion model into a 3D generator, but the per-step gradient is estimated from a single randomly chosen view: it is high-variance and blind to global shape consistency. Prior work addresses this by retraining the diffusion prior on multi-view data; this improves consistency but makes the sampling contribution inseparable from prior quality. We instead isolate the sampling axis. The per-step gradient is one noisy sample of an expectation over views; aggregating K samples per step at a fixed total UNet budget reduces variance without touching the prior. We introduce Multi-View Aggregated Score Distillation (MV-SDI), which aggregates gradients from K views per step via gradient accumulation, keeping peak memory unchanged and the 2D prior frozen, and draws views as antithetic antipodal pairs, a prior-independent geometric property, for balanced angular coverage. At a fixed 10,000-UNet-call budget, K=2 raises CLIP R-Precision from 74.8% to 83.8% and CLIP score from 0.297 to 0.312, with consistent gains on HPSv2 and ImageReward and a 0.0% divergence rate on the 43-prompt benchmark; optimization steps halve as a consequence. K=4 gives a fourfold step reduction at R-Precision 86.9% and CLIP 0.307, still well above the single-view baseline on every alignment metric. MV-SDI is compatible with gradient-based score-distillation pipelines, including Score Distillation via Inversion, and requires no retraining and no multi-view data.

</details>


### [72] [Monte Carlo Energy Aggregation for Mobile 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30017)
*Xiaobiao Du, YuAn Wang, Hao Li, Bosheng Wang, Xun Sun, Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (64.76) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Flux-GS 提出了一种面向移动平台的实时 3D Gaussian Splatting 方法，通过蒙特卡洛能量聚合、属性条件 SH 增强和多视图 Alpha 密度剪枝，在显著降低参数量的同时保持高质量渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶球谐函数（SH）带来的巨大推理和存储开销是 3DGS 在移动端部署的主要瓶颈，现有方法依赖昂贵的蒸馏或预训练，且单视图梯度密度化易产生过多高斯原语和过拟合。

Method: 1) 蒙特卡洛镜面能量聚合器：采样三阶辐射残差，将镜面能量聚合到紧凑潜空间，保留低阶带中的视觉显著光照特征；2) 属性条件 SH 增强模块：基于高斯固有属性预测偏移量，增强一阶 SH 表示，不增加推理成本；3) 多视图 Alpha 密度化与剪枝策略：利用多视图引导确保结构一致性并精确移除冗余原语。

Result: 在多个数据集上，Flux-GS 实现了显著的参数减少（最高达 80%+），同时保持与全阶 SH 方法相当的视觉质量，在移动端实现实时高保真渲染。

Conclusion: Flux-GS 通过能量聚合、属性增强和多视图剪枝三项创新，有效解决了 3DGS 在移动端的高效部署问题，为资源受限平台上的实时 3D 渲染提供了鲁棒且可扩展的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于移动端 3D Gaussian Splatting 的实时渲染优化，属于 3D 场景重建与渲染范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on mobile 3D rendering optimization, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, mobile rendering, Monte Carlo energy aggregation, SH compression, multi-view pruning

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting have demonstrated unprecedented success in novel view synthesis. However, the substantial inference and storage overhead driven by high-order Spherical Harmonics (SH) are primary bottlenecks for mobile platforms. In this paper, we present Flux-GS, a real-time Gaussian Splatting method designed to achieve high-fidelity rendering with significantly reduced overhead for resource-constrained mobile platforms. We first propose a Monte Carlo Specular Energy Aggregator, sampling third-order radiance residuals and aggregating specular energy into a compact latent space. In this way, our method effectively preserves visually salient lighting features in lower-order bands without expensive distillation or pre-training. To mitigate the high-frequency details lost during compression, we introduce an Attribute-Conditioned SH Enhancement module. This module predicts Gaussian-aware offsets based on intrinsic Gaussian attributes, which enhance the first-order SH representation prior to inference, without extra inference costs. Furthermore, the original single-view gradient-based densification is prone to producing excessive Gaussians and overfitting to a certain view. We address these limitations by proposing a Multi-view Alpha-based Densification and Pruning strategy. By leveraging multi-view guidance, we ensure multi-view structure consistency and the precise removal of redundant primitives. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Flux-GS achieves substantial parameter reduction while maintaining competitive visual quality, offering a robust and scalable solution for real-time mobile rendering. Code: \textcolor{magenta}{\href{ }{ }}.

</details>

*Liang Wang, Zhaoyang Xi, Zekai Xiang, Heng Meng, Qishan Zhang, Pingyi Zhou, Jin Liu, Litao Chen*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到3D系统生成的形状仅用于渲染，无法作为可编辑的设计使用。作者希望将自然语言意图直接转化为可执行、可编辑的参数化 CAD 程序，而不仅仅是生成形状。

Method: Arko-T 采用 4B 参数的模型架构，核心创新在于将数据整理、代码归一化和执行监督等所有阶段与"设计状态"这一形式化概念对齐，从而保留 CAD 工件的特征、参数和构造逻辑，使其可编辑。

Result: 在 12 项指标上对七个前沿 LLM 进行基准测试，Arko-T 在 8 项上取得最佳分数，在另外 3 项上取得第二佳分数，且每个基准测试的成本约为前沿模型的十分之一。

Conclusion: 中等规模下针对设计级别的训练可以在结构化 CAD 生成任务上匹敌前沿通用模型，表明领域特化的设计级训练策略是高效且有效的。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文本到结构化3D CAD程序生成，属于3D生成范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on structured CAD generation, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Text-to-3D, parametric CAD, design-level training, moderate scale model, low cost inference

Abstract: Text-to-3D systems can now synthesize a mechanical part from a single sentence, yet the result is a shape to render, not a design to edit. We present Arko-T, a 4B-parameter text-to-design model that maps natural-language intent directly into executable, parametric CAD programs. Rather than optimizing for code executability alone, Arko-T aligns every stage of the pipeline to a formal notion of design state, so that data curation, code normalization, and execution-grounded supervision all work to preserve the features, parameters, and construction logic that make a CAD artifact editable. Benchmarked against seven frontier LLMs across 12 metrics, Arko-T attains the best score on 8 and the second-best on 3 more, at roughly one-tenth the per-benchmark cost. The results suggest that targeted design-level training at moderate scale can match frontier general-purpose models on structured CAD generation.

</details>

Conclusion: 通过大规模混合数据集和精心设计的网络架构，全景数据的度量3D重建可以取得显著进展，为室内场景重建提供了新的基准和方法。

Abstract: Metric feed-forward 3D reconstruction for panoramic data remains under-explored due to the lack of large-scale panoramic RGB-D training data. We present Realsee3D, a hybrid dataset of 10K indoor scenes (1K real, 9K synthetic) with 299K panoramic viewpoints and precise metric annotations, and Argus, a feed-forward network trained on it for metric panoramic 3D reconstruction. In the sparse unordered capture setting of Realsee3D, a poorly chosen coordinate anchor can cause global pose drift. Argus addresses this with a learned covisibility module that selects the geometrically optimal reference view to anchor the metric world frame. To further improve multi-task learning, we decompose the bidirectional pixel-to-world mapping into interpretable sub-steps with per-step supervision and cross-coordinate joint constraints, reinforcing geometric consistency across prediction branches. On the Realsee3D benchmark, Argus achieves state-of-the-art metric performance in camera pose estimation, depth estimation, and point cloud reconstruction. Project page: .

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出T2LDM++扩散模型，核心是自条件表示引导（SCRG）：1）引导网络（GN）为去噪网络（DN）提供基于重建的软监督，使DN学习几何感知表示；2）SCRG在推理时可解耦，不增加计算开销；3）设计方向位置先验缓解街道畸变；4）通过冻结DN学习控制编码器，支持多种条件生成；5）构建两个高质量文本-LiDAR基准数据集（>10万样本）及可控性度量指标。

Result: 在无条件和有条件设置下，T2LDM++均能生成具有丰富几何细节的真实LiDAR场景，有效缓解了物体过平滑和街道畸变问题，在多个条件生成任务上表现优异。

Conclusion: T2LDM++通过自条件表示引导和高质量基准数据集，有效解决了文本到LiDAR场景生成中的过平滑和可控性不足问题，为LiDAR数据生成提供了新的范式。

Importance reason: Focuses on LiDAR scene generation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks overlap with user's key interests in continuous language/multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: LiDAR generation, diffusion model, self-conditioned representation, text-to-3D, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Recent progress in Text-to-Image generation benefits from large-scale Text-Image pairs. However, the scarcity of Text-LiDAR pairs often causes over-smoothed scenes and limited controllability. In this paper, we rethink the limitations of Text-LiDAR generation task, focusing on alleviating insufficient training priors and constructing controllable Text-LiDAR data. We propose a \textbf{T}ext-\textbf{to}-\textbf{L}iDAR \textbf{D}iffusion \textbf{M}odel for LiDAR scene generation, T2LDM++, with a Self-Conditioned Representation Guidance (SCRG). Specifically, to alleviate object over-smoothing, SCRG employs a Guidance Network (GN) to provide reconstruction-based soft supervision to the Denoising Network (DN). This enables DN to learn geometry-aware representations through reconstruction guidance, leading to more accurate denoising in DDPMs. Meanwhile, through analysis and design, SCRG exhibits more effective and lightweight, while decoupled in inference, avoiding computational overhead. Furthermore, we construct two high-quality Text-LiDAR benchmarks ($>$100K samples) using a generalized strategy of geometric annotations, along with a controllability metric. Moreover, a directional position prior is designed to mitigate street distortion, further improving scene fidelity. Additionally, T2LDM++ supports multiple conditions, including (Semantic, Box, BEV, Camera)-to-LiDAR, Sparse-to-Dense, and Dense-to-Sparse generation, by learning a control encoder via frozen DN. With effective prior modeling and high-quality Text-LiDAR benchmarks, T2LDM++ can generate realistic LiDAR scenes with rich geometric details in unconditional and conditional settings.

</details>

Motivation: 现有3D场景重建方法在渲染质量、推理效率和数据依赖之间存在权衡，尤其是基于全景图的方法受限于投影畸变和非均匀特征分布，且现有3DGS方法依赖多视图监督或逐场景优化，速度慢。本文旨在实现从单张全景图快速、高质量地重建3D室内场景。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D scene reconstruction have highlighted the intricate trade-offs among rendering quality, inference efficiency, and data dependency. To address the challenge of rapidly reconstructing detailed 3D indoor scenes from minimal input, we introduce FastPano3D, an end-to-end framework that directly generates renderable 3D Gaussian representations from a single panoramic image. Unlike perspective-based methods, panoramic images inherently suffer from equirectangular projection distortions and spatially non-uniform feature distributions, making direct feed-forward Gaussian generation particularly challenging. In contrast to existing Gaussian Splatting based methods that rely on multi-view supervision or per-scene optimization, FastPano3D employs a lightweight feature encoder, adaptive Gaussian sampling, and a point-cloud-guided refinement strategy to achieve efficient and accurate scene generation without any test-time optimization. Our approach reconstructs high-fidelity 3D scenes within seconds, achieving up to 156 times faster inference than prior state-of-the-art methods such as Pano2Room, while using only half the parameters. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FastPano3D delivers rendering quality comparable to NeRF- and 3DGS-based reconstructions, establishing a new benchmark for rapid, single-view 3D scene inference.

</details>

*Sicheng Yu, Dongxu Shen, Beizhen Zhao, Guanzhi Ding, Hao Wang*

TL;DR: 本文提出KiloGS-SLAM，一个高效鲁棒的单目3DGS-SLAM系统，通过运动自适应混合跟踪模块和生命周期管理的高斯建图策略，首次将3DGS-SLAM扩展到千米级室外场景（超10000帧），在跟踪精度和渲染质量上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目3DGS-SLAM方法在扩展到千米级室外场景时面临两个紧密耦合的瓶颈：长期位姿跟踪容易漂移，以及大规模建图时内存开销过大。现有方法无法同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 1) 运动自适应混合跟踪模块：采用条件触发的三级求解管线，动态切换Essential矩阵和PnP模型以处理几何退化，并可按需激活基础模型（foundation model）来挽救灾难性漂移。2) 生命周期管理的高斯建图策略：结合概率初始化与基于分块的多视图稠密化和剪枝，减少原始冗余同时保持高频细节。

Result: 在三个具有挑战性的室外数据集上，该方法在跟踪精度和渲染质量上均达到SOTA，成功在单GPU上处理超过10000帧的序列。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的跟踪保证了精确建图的几何基础，而内存高效的生命周期管理建图使大规模操作成为可能，两者协同实现了千米级室外场景的高效3DGS-SLAM。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于单目3DGS的SLAM系统，面向千米级室外场景的3D重建与定位，属于3D场景重建与神经渲染范畴，与[3D 生成与重建]（three_d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3DGS SLAM for outdoor scenes, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous language/multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: 3DGS SLAM, kilometer-scale outdoor, monocular SLAM, robust tracking, memory-efficient mapping

Abstract: Scaling monocular 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) SLAM to kilometer-level outdoor environments poses two tightly coupled challenges: fragile long-term pose tracking and excessive memory overhead during large-scale mapping. In this paper, we propose KiloGS-SLAM, a highly efficient and robust monocular 3DGS-SLAM system that jointly addresses both bottlenecks. Since high-fidelity scene reconstruction fundamentally relies on drift-free camera poses, we first introduce a motion-adaptive hybrid tracking module. This module features a condition-triggered three-tier solving pipeline. It dynamically switches between Essential matrix and PnP models to handle geometric degeneracies. An on-demand foundation model can also be activated to rescue the trajectory from catastrophic drift. To ensure the system can sustain these long trajectories without memory exhaustion, we subsequently design a lifecycle-managed Gaussian mapping strategy. By integrating probabilistic initialization with chunk-based multi-view densification and pruning, this full-pipeline optimization effectively reduces primitive redundancy while preserving high-frequency details. Together, the robust tracking guarantees the geometric foundation required for accurate mapping, while the memory-efficient lifecycle-managed mapping enables large-scale operation. Extensive experiments across three challenging outdoor datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art tracking accuracy and rendering quality, successfully scaling to sequences of over 10,000 frames on a single GPU.

</details>

*Wenhao Yuan, Yiyuan Ge, Deli Cai*

TL;DR: StereoGS 提出一种稀疏视角 3DGS 框架，利用立体深度正则化（Stereo Depth Regularization）引入双目一致性先验，结合梯度感知的不透明度衰减策略和一致性感知的稠密初始化，在稀疏视角下实现高质量新视角合成，无需额外推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 在稀疏视角设置下因几何约束不足而严重过拟合，现有方法引入单目深度先验但存在尺度模糊和跨视角不一致问题，导致几何缺陷。

Method: 1) Stereo Depth Regularization：在优化过程中构建虚拟立体对，利用基础立体模型强制绝对尺度和双目一致结构；2) Gradient-Aware Opacity Decay：根据高斯原语的相对不透明度梯度幅值动态惩罚冗余原语；3) Consistency-Aware Dense Initialization：使用零样本多视角深度估计进行稠密初始化，将原语锚定到准确的场景表面。

Result: 在 LLFF、DTU、Mip-NeRF360 和 Blender 数据集上，StereoGS 在稀疏视角设置下达到了最先进的性能，且不引入额外推理开销。

Conclusion: 立体先验能够有效替代单目深度先验，为稀疏视角 3DGS 提供可靠的几何约束，实现高质量新视角合成。

Classification reason: 该论文提出稀疏视角下的3D高斯泼溅方法，属于3D场景重建与渲染方向，与 canonical 子主题 "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D reconstruction, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Sparse-view 3DGS, Stereo depth regularization, No inference overhead, Not autoregressive or video generation, No continuous token or world model

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has achieved remarkable success in real-time novel view synthesis, yet it suffers from severe overfitting under sparse-view settings due to insufficient geometric constraints. While recent methods introduce monocular depth priors to mitigate this, they inherently struggle with scale ambiguity and cross-view inconsistency, leading to defective geometry. In this paper, we propose StereoGS, a novel sparse-view 3DGS framework that integrates stereo priors to establish reliable binocular consistency. Unlike scale-agnostic monocular constraints, StereoGS introduces a Stereo Depth Regularization by constructing virtual stereo pairs during optimization and leveraging a foundation stereo model to enforce absolute scale and binocular-consistent structures. To further suppress overfitting and eliminate redundant primitives, we design a Gradient-Aware Opacity Decay strategy that dynamically penalizes Gaussians based on their relative opacity gradient magnitudes. Combined with a Consistency-Aware Dense Initialization using zero-shot multi-view depth estimation, StereoGS effectively anchors primitives to accurate scene surfaces. Extensive experiments on LLFF, DTU, Mip-NeRF360, and Blender datasets demonstrate that StereoGS achieves state-of-the-art performance in sparse-view settings without incurring any additional inference overhead. Project Page:

</details>


### [79] [UnfoldArt: Zero-Shot Recovery of Full Articulated 3D Objects from Text or Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30608)
*Mohamed el amine boudjoghra, Ivan Laptev, Angela Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (58.98) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: UnfoldArt 提出首个基于辩论驱动的智能体方法，从文本或图像输入零样本恢复完整可活动3D物体。高层智能体利用视觉语言和视频模型推理物体语义与运动，低层智能体估计活动参数和交互点，通过两轮结构化辩论达成一致，并利用视频生成先验暴露被遮挡的内部几何结构，最终联合推断活动结构并重建高保真完整3D物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可活动3D物体对具身AI、机器人、虚拟现实等交互环境至关重要，但现有方法受限于监督数据缺乏或先验不足，难以从稀疏观测中可靠恢复活动结构、隐藏几何和内部物体结构。

Method: 提出辩论驱动的智能体方法：高层智能体（使用VLM和视频模型）推理物体语义和运动，低层智能体估计活动参数和交互点；两轮结构化辩论先利用全局-局部不一致性，再基于自由生成的视频进行对齐；最后利用视频生成先验驱动各部件运动，暴露被遮挡的内部几何。

Result: 在多个数据集上展示了高质量的可活动3D物体重建结果，能够恢复超出直接观测表面的高保真几何、内部结构和运动一致的状态。

Conclusion: 通过结合智能体推理与视频生成先验，该方法能够零样本地从文本或图像联合推断活动结构并重建完整3D可活动物体，为缺乏监督数据的场景提供了有效解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从文本或图像输入重建完整可活动3D物体（articulated 3D objects），属于3D生成与重建范畴，映射到 canonical subtopic "3D 生成与重建"。

Importance reason: Novel debate-driven agentic approach for articulated 3D reconstruction, but only tangentially touches user's priority on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: 3D articulated object reconstruction, zero-shot from text/image, debate-driven multi-agent, video generative prior for occlusion, no direct continuous token or autoregressive video generation

Abstract: Articulated 3D objects are essential for interactive environments in embodied AI, robotics, and virtual reality, but reconstructing their structure and motion from sparse observations remains challenging. Existing approaches remain largely constrained by lack of supervised data or lack the priors needed to reliably recover articulation, hidden geometry, and internal object structure. We present the first debate-driven agentic approach to articulated 3D object reconstruction from text or image inputs that both grounds articulation reasoning in concrete motion and exposes the occluded geometry revealed under articulation. High-level agents reason about object semantics and motion using knowledge from vision-language and video models, while low-level agents estimate articulation parameters and interaction points; together, they engage in a two-round structured debate that first exploits global--local disagreement and then grounds the agents in freely generated video. The same video prior, conditioned on the agreed articulation, then drives each part through its motion to expose occluded interiors and geometry that cannot be inferred from a single static view. By combining agentic reasoning with a video generative prior, our approach jointly infers articulation and reconstructs complete 3D articulated objects, producing high-fidelity geometry, internal structure, and motion-consistent states beyond directly observed surfaces.

</details>


### [80] [RenderFormer++: Scalable and Physically Grounded Feed-Forward Neural Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30380)
*Huangsheng Du, Haoran Zhu, Youcheng Cai, Jinyang Meng, Ligang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (49.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: RenderFormer++ 是一个可扩展且物理可解释的前馈神经渲染框架，通过物理信息引导的注意力机制（PITG）和层次化物体级 Token 化（HOCT），实现了对复杂大规模网格场景的高效全局光照渲染，在物理准确性和计算效率上优于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 Transformer 的神经渲染方法（如 RenderFormer）虽能跨场景泛化，但存在物理一致性不足和可扩展性差的问题，主要源于三角形级 Token 化的二次注意力复杂度。

Method: 提出 Physics-Informed Transport Guidance (PITG)，将渲染方程归纳偏置嵌入注意力机制并施加传输一致性损失；提出 Hierarchical Object-Centric Tokenization (HOCT)，通过可学习查询的交叉注意力将三角形级特征聚合成紧凑的物体级 Token，降低计算和内存开销。

Result: 在复杂大规模场景上实现了可扩展、稳定且泛化的前馈全局光照渲染，物理准确性和效率均优于先前神经渲染方法。

Conclusion: RenderFormer++ 通过物理引导和层次化 Token 化，有效解决了神经渲染的物理一致性和可扩展性瓶颈，为大规模场景的实时全局光照提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向网格场景全局光照的可扩展前馈神经渲染框架，属于3D场景的神经渲染与重建范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D neural rendering with physical grounding, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: 3D rendering, physical consistency, feed-forward neural rendering, scalable tokenization, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: We present RenderFormer++, a scalable and physically grounded feed-forward neural rendering framework for global illumination in mesh scenes. Existing Transformer-based neural rendering methods such as RenderFormer achieve promising cross-scene generalization, but suffer from limited physical consistency and poor scalability due to the quadratic attention complexity of triangle-level tokenization. To address these issues, we introduce Physics-Informed Transport Guidance (PITG), which embeds rendering-equation inductive biases into the attention mechanism and enforces transport consistency loss, enabling physically consistent light transport modeling. We further propose Hierarchical Object-Centric Tokenization (HOCT), which aggregates triangle-level features into compact object-level tokens via cross-attention with learnable queries, substantially reducing computational and memory costs while preserving geometric and radiometric information. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RenderFormer++ achieves scalable, stable, and generalizable feed-forward global illumination rendering across complex large-scale scenes with improved physical accuracy and efficiency over prior neural rendering methods.

</details>

### [81] [SemDynReg: Semantics-Guided Deformation Regularization for Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28656)
*Ruitao Chen, Mozhang Guo, Jinge Li*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 4D 与动态场景生成

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 SemDynReg，一种语义引导的变形正则化框架，用于动态 3D 高斯泼溅渲染。通过 SAM 提取分割掩码和语义特征，构建物体 ID 图，并对每个物体的 top-k 高斯点的变形参数（位置、尺度、旋转）施加一致性正则化，从而在物体级别实现更一致的变形，提升动态场景渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可变形 3DGS 方法缺乏显式的物体级建模，导致单个物体内部的高斯变形不一致，以及不同物体之间的变形耦合，影响动态场景渲染质量。

Method: 1) 使用 SAM 从输入图像中提取分割掩码和语义特征；2) 通过特征相关性匹配与预定义的物体字典构建物体 ID 图；3) 基于物体 ID 图，为每个物体识别像素级 top-k 贡献高斯点；4) 对这些高斯点的变形参数（位置、尺度、旋转）施加一致性正则化约束。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的语义感知正则化方法在物体级变形一致性上优于基线方法，在动态 3DGS 渲染中取得了更高的 PSNR 和 SSIM，以及更低的 LPIPS。

Conclusion: 语义引导的物体级变形正则化能有效提升动态 3DGS 的渲染质量和物体级变形一致性，为动态场景建模提供了新的思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究动态场景的 3D 高斯泼溅渲染，属于动态 3D/4D 场景生成与重建范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "4D 与动态场景生成"（dynamic_4d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on dynamic 3DGS rendering with semantic regularization, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's key interests. Signals: Dynamic 3DGS, Semantic regularization, Object-level deformation, SAM-guided, Rendering quality improvement

Abstract: Deformable 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as an efficient approach for rendering dynamic scenes in a wide range of 3D applications. However, existing deformation field-based approaches largely lack explicit object-level modeling, often resulting in inconsistent Gaussian deformations within individual objects and unwanted coupling between different objects. To address this limitation, we introduce a semantics-guided framework that enforces dynamic regularization at the object level, aiming to achieve spatially consistent object-wise deformation. Specifically, we first extract segmentation masks using the Segment Anything Model (SAM) and derive semantic features from input images. An object-ID map is then constructed via feature relevance matching with a predefined object dictionary. Guided by this object-ID map, we identify the pixel-wise top-k contributing Gaussians for each object and impose consistency regularization on their deformation parameters, including position, scale, and rotation. Unlike prior methods that learn deformation fields without explicit object-level constraints, our approach incorporates semantic cues to guide deformation behavior at the object level. Experimental results demonstrate that our semantics-aware regularization improves object-level deformation consistency and outperforms baseline methods in rendering quality, achieving higher PSNR and SSIM and lower LPIPS in dynamic 3DGS rendering. Our project page is available at .

</details>


### [82] [Ground4D: Consistency-Aware 4D Reconstruction from Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28828)
*Qing Zhao, Weijian Deng, Pengxu Wei, Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 4D 与动态场景生成

Importance: 普通相关 (61.48) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Ground4D 提出一种两阶段框架，先用3D基础模型（VGGT）以无训练方式从单目视频重建多视图一致的几何与相机位姿，再通过动态高斯泼溅进行几何一致性感知的优化，实现高质量动态4D场景重建与新视角合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态高斯泼溅方法通过光度优化实现强渲染性能，但未显式强制多视图几何一致性；而3D基础模型能恢复一致几何与相机运动，但其点云输出不适用于逼真渲染。如何结合两者的优势实现鲁棒的4D场景重建是核心动机。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段利用VGGT（3D基础模型）以训练自由方式从单目视频重建多视图一致的3D几何与相机位姿，为动态高斯表示提供结构化初始化；第二阶段通过动态高斯泼溅进行几何一致性感知的细化，在可微渲染中同时保持观测视角和合成视角的多视图几何一致性，并支持任意时间戳的连续4D渲染。

Result: 在多个动态场景数据集上，Ground4D在重建保真度和渲染性能上均优于现有方法，验证了几何约束在鲁棒4D场景建模中的关键作用。

Conclusion: 将基础模型级别的几何先验融入动态高斯优化，能够有效提升4D场景重建的几何一致性与渲染质量，为单目视频动态场景建模提供了新的有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文从单目视频重建动态4D场景，使用动态高斯泼溅实现新视角合成，属于4D与动态场景生成方向，匹配[CANONICAL] subtopic "4D 与动态场景生成"（dynamic_4d_generation）。同时论文涉及动态场景的连续4D动力学建模，也与世界模型方向下的 spatial_3d_4d_world_model 相关。

Importance reason: Strong geometry-grounded 4D reconstruction from monocular video, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: 4D reconstruction, dynamic Gaussian splatting, monocular video, geometry consistency, 3D foundation model

Abstract: Learning a 4D scene representation from a single monocular video that supports dynamic novel-view synthesis while maintaining faithful geometry over time remains challenging. Dynamic Gaussian Splatting achieves strong rendering performance through photometric optimization, yet does not explicitly enforce multi-view geometric consistency. In contrast, 3D foundation models recover coherent scene geometry and camera motion, but their point-based outputs are not designed for photorealistic rendering. We propose Ground4D, a geometry-grounded framework built on two stages. First, we perform geometry initialization via 3D foundation models, leveraging VGGT in a training-free manner to reconstruct multi-view-consistent 3D geometry and camera poses from monocular video. The recovered geometry provides a structured and reliable initialization for dynamic Gaussian representations. Second, we conduct geometry-consistency-aware refinement via dynamic Gaussian Splatting, optimizing the representation through differentiable rendering while maintaining multi-view geometric consistency across both observed and synthesized viewpoints. Furthermore, Ground4D inherently models the continuous 4D dynamics of the scene, naturally supporting rendering at arbitrary timestamps. By integrating foundation-level geometric priors into dynamic Gaussian optimization, Ground4D achieves stronger reconstruction fidelity and rendering performance, underscoring the role of geometry-grounded constraints in robust 4D scene modeling.

</details>


### [83] [Learning Efficient 4D Gaussian Representations from Monocular Videos with Flow Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29976)
*Shengjun Zhang, Jinzhao Li, Xin Fei, Yueqi Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 4D 与动态场景生成

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Flow Splatting方法，通过构建速度场并扩展4D高斯表示（时变均值和协方差），利用光流监督从单目视频高效重建动态4D场景，在渲染质量和速度上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于3D高斯泼溅的4D动态场景重建方法存在训练时间长、渲染速度慢或内存消耗高的问题，未能充分利用密集的动态信息。本文旨在解决这些效率瓶颈。

Method: 提出Flow Splatting方法：1）扩展4D体积表示，使用时变均值和协方差建模复杂动态；2）基于该表示构建并近似速度场；3）将传统体渲染策略扩展到速度场的splatting，考虑相机运动影响，从速度场渲染光流来监督动态学习。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，相比SOTA方法，本文方法在更少的训练时间和更高的渲染速度下取得了更好的图像质量。

Conclusion: Flow Splatting通过速度场构建和光流监督，有效提升了单目视频动态4D场景重建的效率和质量，为4D高斯泼溅方法提供了新的高效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从单目视频重建动态4D场景，使用4D高斯泼溅和速度场渲染光流，属于多模态生成方向下的4D与动态场景生成（dynamic_4d_generation）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on 4D Gaussian splatting for dynamic scenes, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or autoregressive generation. Signals: 4D Gaussian, dynamic scene reconstruction, flow splatting, monocular video, rendering efficiency

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic 3D scenes from monocular videos is challenging due to scene complexity and temporal dynamics. With the advancement of 3D Gaussian Splatting in novel view synthesis, existing methods extend 3D Gaussians to 4D domain with deformation fields, trajectories or spatiotemporal 4D volumes to model scene element deformation. However, these methods suffer from long training time, low rendering speed or high memory consumption for per-frame reconstruction of 4D volumes, without fully exploiting dense dynamic information. To address this issue, we propose Flow Splatting, which constructs the velocity field and enables the conventional splatting technique to render optical flow from the velocity field to supervise dynamics learning process from monocular videos. Specifically, we extend 4D volumes with time varying means and covariance to represent complex dynamics. Then, we construct and approximate the velocity field naturally based on this representations. While conventional volume rendering techniques support to render color fields, we extend the volume rendering strategy to splat the velocity field by considering the influence of camera motions. We conduct experiments on various benchmarks to demonstrate the efficiency and effectiveness of our method. Compared to the state-of-the-art methods, our model achieves better image quality with less time consumption and higher rendering speed.

</details>

*Guoqiu Li, Jin Song, Yiyun Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HomeDiffusion 提出一种基于扩散模型的零样本物体定制化生成方法，利用多视角参考图像提取高保真细节，通过交叉注意力机制在潜空间中与噪声潜变量交互，从而在室内场景指定区域生成与背景协调的定制化物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本物体定制化生成方法在细节保持（如图案、曲线）方面与参考物体差异较大，尤其对于非对称参考物体，缺乏多视角信息导致生成的物体姿态无法与背景场景和谐融合。

Method: 构建了包含家具多角度图像和室内场景的新数据集；基于扩散模型，利用参考物体的多视角图像提取高保真细节特征，在潜空间中通过交叉注意力机制与噪声潜变量交互，生成与指定区域背景协调的物体姿态。

Result: 大量定性和定量实验表明，HomeDiffusion 在零样本及少样本物体定制化生成任务上优于现有方法，生成的物体在细节保真度和场景协调性方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 多视角参考图像结合扩散模型潜空间交叉注意力机制，能有效提升零样本物体定制化生成的细节保真度和场景协调性，为电商等应用场景提供了可行的技术方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究零样本物体定制化生成（object customization），属于主体与概念驱动生成（customization_generation）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。论文利用多视角参考图像在室内场景中生成与背景协调的定制化物体，核心是参考图引导的个性化生成。

Importance reason: Focuses on zero-shot object customization for indoor scenes, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct alignment with user's key interests in continuous models, video generation, or world models. Signals: diffusion model, zero-shot customization, multi-view representation, indoor scenes, cross-attention

Abstract: Recently, zero-shot object customization generation methods have rapidly developed and shown tremendous potential for applications. For instance, in the e-commerce domain, consumers can observe the visual effect of furniture placed within their personal living spaces or clothes worn on their own bodies. Many existing approaches perform object customization generation based on diffusion models and extracted reference object features. However, the generated object significantly diverges from the original reference object in details such as patterns and curves. Particularly for asymmetrical reference objects, the absence of comprehensive multi-viewpoint information prevents the generation of object poses that harmonize with the background scene. To address these shortcomings, we have constructed a novel dataset comprising multi-angle images of furniture and indoor scenes. Based on diffusion models, we introduce HomeDiffusion, which can leverage multi-viewpoint images of the same reference object to accurately generate visually harmonious object poses within specified areas of the background scene. During the diffusion process, we further extract high-fidelity details of the reference object and perform cross-attention with the noise latents in the latent space, thereby ensuring the preservation of details in the customized object generation. Extensive qualitative and quantitative experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior performance over other existing zero-shot as well as few-shot object customization approaches.

</details>

### [85] [Semantic-Aware, Physics-Informed, Geometry-Grounded Weather Video Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29020)
*Chenghao Qian, Nedko Savov, Lingdong Kong, Yeying Jin, Rui Song, Wenjing Li, Zhun Zhong, Jiaqi Ma, Gustav Markkula, Luc Van Gool*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.MM, cs.AI, cs.ET

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 值得关注 (77.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出一个语义感知、物理信息、几何对齐的天气视频合成框架，利用现成视频编辑器，通过语义锚定、物理粒子模拟和几何对齐三个条件信号，生成多样且物理真实的天气效果，并提升自动驾驶语义分割的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有天气合成方法缺乏天气外观的多样性和对天气动态（如时间演化和粒子运动）的有效控制，且通用视频编辑器倾向于抑制密集粒子效果，难以生成重天气现象。

Method: 将天气合成分解为三个条件信号：1) 语义感知外观锚定（从场景语义和用户输入建立目标外观）；2) 物理信息动态模拟（用高斯表示的粒子场在重力、风和湍流下模拟粒子效果）；3) 几何对齐视频合成（将模拟粒子与目标场景几何对齐并合成最终视频）。

Result: 实验表明该方法能生成多样、物理和视觉上真实的天气效果，且合成数据显著提升了自动驾驶语义分割在恶劣天气条件下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过分解语义、动力学和几何三个条件信号，可以有效引导视频编辑器生成多样且物理真实的天气效果，并具有提升下游任务鲁棒性的实际价值。

Classification reason: 该论文研究天气效果视频合成，属于视频编辑范畴（向输入视频添加天气效果），与 canonical subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）高度匹配。

Importance reason: While technically solid, the paper's focus on weather video synthesis for autonomous driving is a niche application that does not align with the user's priority subtopics on continuous language/multimodal models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: weather synthesis, physics-informed, geometry-grounded, autonomous driving, video editing

Abstract: Weather synthesis aims to add weather effects to input videos while preserving scene identity, structure, and motion. The key limitation of existing methods is the lack of diversity in weather appearance and effective control over weather dynamics (e.g., temporal evolution and particle motion). Most approaches rely on text prompts, which are inherently underspecified and often fail to produce detailed weather characteristics. Additionally, general-purpose video editors optimized for clean and aesthetic outputs tend to suppress heavy weather phenomena, making dense particle effects difficult to generate. To address these, we propose a Semantic-Aware, Physics-Informed, and Geometry-Grounded framework that steers an off-the-shelf video editor to synthesize diverse global appearances and detailed particle dynamics. We factorize the synthesis into three conditional signals, so that each provides a distinct and stable source of guidance: semantics specifies what the weather should look like, dynamics governs how it evolves over time, and geometry determines where it should appear in the scene. Specifically, we introduce (1) semantic-aware appearance anchoring to establish the target appearance from scene semantics and user input; (2) physics-informed dynamic simulation to generate particle effects by simulating a Gaussian-represented particle field under gravity, wind, and turbulence; and (3) geometry-grounded video synthesis to align the simulated particles with target scene geometry and synthesize the final video. Experiments demonstrate that our method produces diverse, physically and visually realistic weather effects. Furthermore, we show that our synthesized data significantly improves the robustness of autonomous driving semantic segmentation under adverse weather conditions. Project page: .

</details>


### [86] [HorizonRelight: Relighting Long-horizon Videos Consistently via Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29095)
*Jing Yang, Mayoore Jaiswal, Zian Wang, Steven Zeng, Rochelle Pereira, Yajie Zhao, Jianyuan Min*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: HorizonRelight 提出一种基于扩散Transformer的长视频一致性重光照方法，通过掩码目标域自条件化和暖启动提示，解决分块滑动窗口推理导致的时序不连续问题，实现长视频的稳定重光照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型基于短视频片段训练，处理长视频时需分块滑动窗口推理，导致块边界处出现时序不连续和外观突变。需要一种能保持长视频时序一致性的重光照方法。

Method: 将长视频重光照重新定义为"时序条件潜空间域翻译"问题。核心包括：(1) 跨块传播目标域潜变量以强制连续性；(2) 掩码目标域自条件化（masked target-domain self-conditioning），训练模型从时序掩码的传播上下文中继续生成；(3) 暖启动提示（warm-start prompting），利用可控生成模型建立初始目标域状态作为提示锚点。

Result: 在真实世界长视频上的实验表明，该方法显著提升了时序一致性，大幅减少了块边界伪影，并有效抑制了跨块的外观变化。

Conclusion: 通过潜空间域翻译框架和掩码自条件化训练，成功实现了扩散模型在长视频重光照任务上的时序一致性，为长视频可控编辑提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于扩散Transformer的长视频重光照（relighting），属于视频编辑/可控编辑范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Addresses temporal consistency in long-video relighting but is not aligned with user's priority subtopics. Signals: video relighting, diffusion transformer, temporal consistency, long-horizon video, masked self-conditioning

Abstract: Diffusion-based video relighting enables controllable relighting from a single input video, but modern video diffusion backbones are trained on short clips and applied to long-horizon videos through chunked sliding-window inference, often causing temporal discontinuities at chunk boundaries. We address this by reframing long-horizon relighting as \emph{temporally conditioned latent domain translation}. Our framework enforces cross-chunk continuity by propagating target-domain latents across boundaries and makes this behavior learnable using \emph{masked target-domain self-conditioning}, training the model to continue from temporally masked propagated context. We further introduce \emph{warm-start prompting} with a relit prompt anchor from a controllable generative model, which establishes the initial target-domain state and creates a general interface for prompt-based relighting. Experiments on in-the-wild long-horizon videos show markedly improved temporal consistency, with chunk-boundary artifacts largely reduced and unwanted appearance changes across chunks greatly suppressed.

</details>


### [87] [ASTAD: Asymmetric Style Transfer for Synthetic-to-Real Adaptation in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29286)
*Dingyi Yao, Xinqi Zhang, Lihui Peng, Jianming Hu, Danya Yao, Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 值得关注 (70.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 ASTAD 任务和 ASTModel 框架，解决自动驾驶中合成数据到真实数据的非对称风格迁移问题：仅有合成数据有标注，真实参考图无标注。ASTModel 是无需训练的两阶段框架，先提取粗语义先验，再在去噪过程中动态精炼并注入类别一致的风格，实现语义对齐的风格迁移，提升下游感知性能并加速 3.2 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶感知中合成数据与真实数据之间存在域差异，现有风格迁移方法假设对称语义引导（即源和目标都有标注），但现实中真实参考图通常无标注，导致标注成本高或语义错位。本文旨在解决这一非对称约束下的语义一致风格迁移问题。

Method: 提出 ASTModel，一个无需训练的两阶段框架：第一阶段从无标注的真实参考图中提取粗语义先验（使用预训练分割模型）；第二阶段在扩散模型去噪过程中，动态精炼该先验并注入类别一致的风格信息，实现语义对齐的合成到真实风格迁移。

Result: 在多个自动驾驶数据集上，ASTModel 在下游感知任务（语义分割）性能和结构保真度上显著优于现有方法，同时推理速度提升 3.2 倍。

Conclusion: ASTModel 有效解决了合成到真实域迁移中的非对称标注问题，为自动驾驶系统的大规模部署提供了实用且高效的域适应方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究自动驾驶场景下的合成到真实风格迁移（style transfer），属于图像编辑/风格化范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on autonomous driving style transfer, not aligned with user's priority subtopics on continuous language/multimodal models or video generation. Signals: autonomous driving, style transfer, synthetic-to-real, diffusion model, training-free

Abstract: Synthetic data mitigates the data scarcity problem in autonomous driving perception. However, the synthetic-to-real gap leads to performance degradation, hindering real-world model generalization. Although current methods leverage diffusion models for photorealistic style transfer to bridge this gap, they critically ignore a practical asymmetry: while synthetic data possesses perfect pixel-level annotations, real-world style reference images generally lack corresponding labels. Consequently, existing methods relying on symmetric semantic guidance suffer from either prohibitive annotation costs or severe semantic misalignment. To address this dilemma, we formally propose a novel task: Asymmetric Style Transfer for Autonomous Driving (ASTAD), which requires semantically consistent transfer using only labeled synthetic content and unlabeled real-world references. We further introduce the ASTModel, a training-free two-stage framework designed to bridge this domain gap under asymmetric constraints. ASTModel first extracts a coarse semantic prior from the unlabeled target, followed by dynamic prior refinement and class-consistent style injection during the denoising process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ASTModel significantly outperforms existing methods in downstream perception utility and structural fidelity, while offering a 3.2$\times$ inference speedup. This work aligns synthetic-to-real adaptation with practical constraints, holding the potential to accelerate the scalable deployment of robust autonomous driving systems. Code: .

</details>


### [88] [MirrorPPR: Exemplar-Based Portrait Photo Retouching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29308)
*Zhihong Liu, Zheng Li, Jiachun Jin, Siqi Kou, Yitao Jian, Fengpei Yu, Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MirrorPPR，一种基于示例的人像照片修图框架，通过 Retouching Operation Extractor 提取示例对中的细微修图操作，并注入预训练 DiT 模型实现结构级人像修图。同时构建了包含 4700 万修图对的大规模数据集 MirrorPPR47M，采用课程学习策略优化训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本引导的图像编辑在结构级人像修图（如面部特征和身体比例的精细调整）中表现有限，因为文本描述难以传达细粒度的结构变化。现有基于示例的编辑方法主要针对视觉变化明显的任务，而结构级人像修图涉及极其精细和局部的修改，准确提取和迁移这些编辑操作具有挑战性。

Method: 提出 MirrorPPR 框架，包含：(1) Retouching Operation Extractor，从示例对中捕捉细微差异；(2) 通过 connector 和 LoRA 模块将提取的表征注入预训练 DiT；(3) 提出数据自增强范式解决跨身份训练对的操作不对齐问题；(4) 构建大规模数据集 MirrorPPR47M（4700 万修图对），分为模拟和专家子集，支持渐进式课程学习。

Result: 大量实验表明，MirrorPPR 在修图质量和身份保持方面显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文通过示例驱动的框架有效解决了结构级人像修图中精细操作提取与迁移的难题，大规模数据集和课程学习策略进一步提升了模型性能，为该任务提供了新的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于示例的人像照片修图（exemplar-based portrait retouching），属于图像编辑任务。现有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing（图像与视频编辑）" 涵盖图像编辑、可控编辑等，与该论文高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on exemplar-based portrait retouching, a niche editing task with limited overlap with user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: DiT-based editing, exemplar-based retouching, large dataset, no autoregressive or continuous token focus, no video or world model connection

Abstract: While text-guided image editing has made remarkable progress, it remains limited in structural portrait retouching. Textual descriptions struggle to convey fine-grained changes to facial features and body proportions. To address this gap, we introduce Exemplar-Based Portrait Photo Retouching, where the model is given an exemplar pair and tasked with inferring and applying the same retouching operations to a new query image. Existing exemplar-based editing methods primarily focus on tasks with pronounced visual transformations. In contrast, structural portrait retouching involves extremely delicate and localized modifications, making accurate extraction and transfer of these edits challenging. To tackle this, we propose MirrorPPR, a novel framework designed to capture and transfer subtle structural retouching operations. Our method uses a Retouching Operation Extractor to capture the subtle differences from the exemplar pair. The extracted representations are then injected into a pre-trained Diffusion Transformer (DiT) through a connector and Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) modules. Furthermore, constructing perfectly aligned cross-identity training pairs is severely hindered by operation misalignment. To overcome this, we propose an advanced data self-augmentation paradigm that ensures strictly aligned retouching operations. To alleviate data scarcity and support this novel task, we introduce MirrorPPR47M, a large-scale dataset with over 47 million retouched pairs. By structuring the dataset into simulated and professional subsets, we enable progressive curriculum learning to smoothly optimize the network. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MirrorPPR significantly outperforms existing baselines in both retouching quality and identity preservation. The project page is available at .

</details>


### [89] [FDM-MFVT: Few-step Sampling Diffusion Model for Mask-Free Virtual Try-On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29319)
*Jiaxin Liu, Xiaoye Liang, Lai Jiang, Mai Xu, Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出FDM-MFVT，一种少步扩散模型用于无掩码虚拟试穿，包含OANO噪声初始化模块和IDT指令驱动模块，仅需6步采样即可生成高质量试穿图像，并构建了30,000对的MFVT无掩码数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的虚拟试穿方法需要大量采样步数，且依赖掩码和昂贵的辅助网络；同时缺乏大规模无掩码配对数据集，限制了无掩码虚拟试穿的发展。

Method: 提出FDM-MFVT框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) Outfit-aware Noise Optimization Module (OANO) — 利用输入图像初始化噪声对齐空间，仅需6步即可生成高保真试穿图像；(2) Instruction-driven Try-on Module (IDT) — 使用虚拟试穿提示和高效适配方法，仅从服装和人物图像生成高质量结果。同时构建了30,000对的MFVT无掩码数据集。

Result: 实验表明，FDM-MFVT在更少的推理步数下（6步 vs 30步），在定量和定性指标上均优于基于掩码和无掩码的基线方法。

Conclusion: FDM-MFVT通过噪声优化和指令驱动模块，实现了高效的无掩码虚拟试穿，大幅减少了采样步数，为虚拟试穿领域提供了新的高效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究虚拟试穿（Virtual Try-On），属于图像编辑/可控图像生成范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on virtual try-on, a niche application not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or world models. Signals: Few-step diffusion, mask-free virtual try-on, dataset contribution, not autoregressive, not video generation

Abstract: Image-based Virtual Try-On (IVTON) has greatly advanced through diffusion models, yet existing methods require many sampling steps and depend on masks with costly auxiliary networks. In addition, the absence of large-scale mask-free paired datasets further limits the development of mask-free IVTON. We propose FDM-MFVT, a few-step diffusion model for mask-free IVTON, integrating an Outfit-aware Noise Optimization Module (OANO) and an Instruction-driven Try-on Module (IDT) to enhance efficiency and OANO module initializes the alignment space with noise using the input image and only needs 6 steps to generate a higher-fidelity try-on image compared to 30 IDT module uses virtual try-on prompts and efficient adaptation to generate high-quality results from garment and person images alone. We further introduce MFVT, a 30,000-pair mask-free IVTON dataset. Experiments show that FDM-MFVT achieves superior quantitative and qualitative results with fewer inference steps than mask-based and mask-free baseline methods.

</details>


### [90] [GeoEdit: Geometry-Aware Object Editing via Dual-Branch Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30003)
*Yi He, Jiangming Wang, Xinyu Wang, Mark Fong, Songchun Zhang, Yuxuan Xue, Hai-Tao Zheng, Yue Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GeoEdit提出了一种无需训练的双分支去噪管线，通过3D提升-操作-渲染-去噪流程实现单张照片中物体的几何感知编辑（平移、旋转、缩放），并引入GeoEditBench基准进行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的图像编辑方法缺乏3D空间感知，在物体平移、旋转、缩放等操作时会产生透视失真、鬼影和背景模糊等问题。核心难点在于：被移动的物体需要遵循刚体几何约束，而被覆盖的背景区域则需要自由合成合理内容，这两种需求是不对称的。

Method: GeoEdit采用Lift-Manipulate-Render-Denoise四阶段流程：(1) Lift：将2D图像提升到3D，获取深度和点云；(2) Manipulate：通过点对应关系对齐场景和物体；(3) Render：渲染几何对齐的结构深度图代理；(4) Dual-Branch Denoising：双分支去噪阶段，视频扩散骨干保持物体身份，3D约束通过方差同质注入（variance-homogeneous injection）在窄去噪窗口内注入前景，背景自由去噪，避免自注意力泄漏。

Result: 在GeoEditBench和多个真实图像上的实验表明，GeoEdit在几何精度、身份保真度和背景质量方面均取得了一致提升，优于现有2D编辑方法。

Conclusion: GeoEdit通过3D感知的双分支去噪框架有效解决了扩散编辑器中物体几何操作的空间感知缺失问题，无需额外训练即可实现符合3D物理约束的精确物体编辑。

Classification reason: 该论文提出GeoEdit，一种基于扩散模型的图像物体编辑方法，支持平移、旋转、缩放等几何操作，属于图像编辑（visual_editing）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。论文还引入了GeoEditBench评测基准，但核心贡献是编辑方法，因此归入visual_editing而非generation_benchmark。

Importance reason: Focuses on 2D image editing with 3D awareness, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous language models, video generation, or world models. Signals: Training-free pipeline, Dual-branch denoising, 3D-aware object editing, GeoEditBench benchmark, No direct link to user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Precisely manipulating objects in a single photograph (translation, rotation, scaling) while obeying 3D physical constraints remains unsolved for diffusion-based editors. Current 2D methods lack spatial awareness and produce perspective violations. Forcing structural proxies into the latent space also disrupts variance homogeneity, and the resulting self-attention leakage leads to ghosting and background blur. The core difficulty is asymmetric: the relocated object must follow a rigid geometry, yet the uncovered background needs freedom to synthesize plausible content. We present GeoEdit, a training-free Lift-Manipulate-Render-Denoise pipeline that satisfies both constraints. We decouple scene and object in 3D, align them through point correspondence, and render a geometry-aligned proxy with a structural depth map. A Dual-Branch Denoising stage then refines this proxy: a video diffusion backbone preserves object identity, while 3D constraints are injected into the foreground within a narrow denoising window at matching noise variance (variance-homogeneous injection). The background denoises freely. Because the injected signal matches the native latent statistics, self-attention stays undisturbed. We also introduce GeoEditBench, a pose-aware benchmark covering object translation, object rotation, and camera movement with pose-aware evaluation metrics. Experiments confirm consistent gains in geometric accuracy, identity fidelity, and background quality. Our codes are available at .

</details>


### [91] [Envisage: Diffusion-Based Rhinoplasty Goal Visualization with Mask-Decomposed Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28628)
*Mudit Agarwal, Amit D. Bhrany*

Main category: eess.IV, cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (38.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Envisage 是一个基于 FLUX.1-Fill 的鼻整形目标可视化管线，结合临床预设、MediaPipe 掩码和硬掩码合成进行局部图像编辑。同时提出 SurgicalScore，一种掩码分解的 0-1 评估协议，用于衡量编辑方向、幅度、LPIPS、真实感和掩码外保持。实验表明全脸身份指标在硬合成局部编辑下存在结构性混淆，应使用编辑区域保真度进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部生成式编辑需要局部化的评估指标，但现有全脸身份指标（如 ArcFace）在硬掩码合成编辑下会被未编辑像素主导，无法准确衡量编辑区域的保真度。作者希望为整形手术可视化这一特定局部编辑任务提供更合理的评估方法。

Method: 使用 FLUX.1-Fill 作为 inpainting 骨干，结合 8 种鼻整形临床预设（以及眼睑整形和面部除皱预设）、MediaPipe 面部解析掩码和硬掩码合成。提出 SurgicalScore 评估协议，包含编辑方向、编辑幅度、掩码 LPIPS、真实感和掩码外保持五个维度，输出 0-1 综合评分。

Result: 在 N=211 数据集上，Envisage 的 ArcFace 增益（输出-真实 vs 输入-真实）为 -0.048，优于 ICEdit (-0.139)、Kontext (-0.242)、InstructPix2Pix (-0.294)。SurgicalScore 上 Envisage 达到 0.599，领先所有方法。但所有方法的 ArcFace 增益均为负值，表明全脸身份指标与局部编辑准确性不一致。

Conclusion: 对于局部编辑任务，应使用编辑区域保真度指标而非全脸身份指标进行评估。Envisage 管线在鼻整形可视化上优于现有方法，但全脸身份指标的结构性混淆问题需要社区关注。作者开源了 Envisage、SurgicalScore、预设定义和匹配分割清单。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于 FLUX.1-Fill 的鼻整形目标可视化管线，核心是局部图像编辑（inpainting）与编辑区域评估，属于图像编辑（visual_editing）范畴。

Importance reason: Niche medical image editing paper with limited relevance to the user's priority on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: medical domain, image editing, diffusion inpainting, evaluation metric, no continuous language model

Abstract: Localized generative editing needs localized evaluation: full-image identity metrics are structurally confounded under hard-composited edits. We present Envisage, a FLUX.1-Fill inpainting reference pipeline for rhinoplasty goal visualization from a single frontal photograph. The pipeline combines 8 rhinoplasty clinical presets (the released framework also includes 8 blepharoplasty and 8 rhytidectomy presets), MediaPipe masks, and hard-mask compositing. The composite preserves outside-mask pixels by construction, so full-face identity scores are dominated by copied pixels rather than by the diffusion backbone. Because full-face identity metrics cannot grade localized edits, we introduce SurgicalScore, a mask-decomposed 0-1 protocol scoring edit direction, edit magnitude, masked LPIPS, realism, and outside-mask preservation; SS_raw assigns 0.919 [0.918, 0.920] to a perfect-predictor control , anchoring the ceiling. On N=211, the paired ArcFace gain (output-to-GT minus input-to-GT) is negative for all methods (Envisage -0.048 smallest, vs. ICEdit -0.139, Kontext -0.242, InstructPix2Pix -0.294; p < 1e-4), with external validation on a 457-pair ASPS/PCA corpus showing a larger negative gap. With SurgicalScore, Envisage achieves the highest score (0.599 [0.579, 0.619]) and leads on both metrics, but the all-negative ArcFace gap shows that full-face identity is poorly aligned with localized surgical accuracy under hard compositing. A 5-seed GT-oracle (an upper bound, not a deployable result) reduces the residual ArcFace gap by 73% (-0.054 to -0.015), with positive output-to-GT gain on 33.9% of cases, indicating candidate-space headroom for a learned ranker. For localized edits, progress should be measured with edit-region fidelity rather than full-face identity metrics. We release Envisage, SurgicalScore, preset definitions, and matched split manifests.

</details>


### [92] [Goku: A Million-Scale Universal Dataset and Benchmark for Instruction-Based Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30599)
*Sen Liang, Cong Wang, Zhentao Yu, Fengbin Guan, Zhengguang Zhou, Teng Hu, Youliang Zhang, Yuan Zhou, Xin Li, Qinglin Lu, Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 值得关注 (71.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了Goku，一个包含200万高质量指令-视频编辑对的大规模数据集，首次将视频编辑任务从单一外观编辑扩展到多任务和结构操控（如主体运动控制）。同时提出了Goku-Edit模型，采用MLLM作为文本编码器与解耦双分支设计（掩码分支处理结构控制，主分支处理外观渲染），以及包含1000个人工验证测试用例和7个新指标的Goku-Bench评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令视频编辑数据集通常局限于单一任务的外观编辑，无法满足真实场景中复杂的创意需求，缺乏支持多任务和结构操控（如精确控制主体运动）的大规模高质量数据集。

Method: 设计了一个高效的数据合成管线，将复杂编辑分解为可控子问题，并引入渐进式过滤系统保证数据可靠性。模型方面，Goku-Edit使用MLLM作为文本编码器以深入理解复杂编辑指令，采用解耦双分支设计：专用掩码分支处理结构控制，主分支专注于外观渲染。

Result: 在Goku-Bench上，Goku-Edit在指令跟随方面相比其他开源模型取得了高达+8%的提升。

Conclusion: Goku数据集和Goku-Edit模型有效填补了现有视频编辑数据集在复杂多任务和结构操控方面的空白，为视频编辑领域提供了大规模高质量的数据基础和更优的模型方案。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于指令驱动的视频编辑，提出了大规模视频编辑数据集Goku和视频编辑模型Goku-Edit，属于多模态生成方向下的图像与视频编辑（visual_editing）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on video editing dataset and benchmark, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like autoregressive generation or world models. Signals: Video editing dataset, MLLM text encoder, Decoupled dual-branch design, New benchmark and metrics, Not autoregressive or diffusion generation

Abstract: Existing instruction-based video editing datasets commonly focus on single-task appearance editing, failing to meet the complex creative demands of real-world scenarios. To bridge this gap, we present Goku, a large-scale dataset featuring 2 million high-quality, instruction-aligned video editing pairs, which is the first to extend task boundaries from basic appearance editing to multi-task and structural manipulations(e.g., precise control of subject movement). To tackle the data synthesis challenges inherent in these complex tasks, we design an efficient data synthesis pipeline that decomposes complex edits into controllable sub-problems and introduce a progressive filtering system for data reliability throughout the whole process. Furthermore, we explore the optimal network structures on Goku, and propose Goku-Edit. To deeply comprehend complex editing instructions, Goku-Edit leverages an MLLM as its text encoder and adopts a decoupled dual-branch design: a dedicated mask branch handles structural control, freeing the main branch for appearance rendering. A comprehensive video editing benchmark, Goku-Bench, is also proposed with 1,000 human-verified test cases and 7 novel editing-specific metrics. Evaluated on Goku-Bench, Goku-Edit obtains up to +8% improvement on other open-source models in terms of instruction following.

</details>
