### [93] [KM-Speaker: Keypoint-Based Style Control for High-Quality Speech-Driven 3D Facial Animation and Dialogue Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28568)
*Arthur Josi, Emeline Got, Abdallah Dib, Luiz Gustavo Hafemann, Rafael M. O. Cruz*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: KM-Speaker 提出了一种基于关键点条件的流式生成框架，用于语音驱动的3D面部动画。该方法通过解耦音频驱动的唇部运动与关键点驱动的上半脸动态，并结合全局风格上下文保持机制，在数据受限条件下实现了高保真动画与灵活的风格控制，在唇形同步、风格遵循和时序控制方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音驱动3D面部动画方法难以同时实现高保真运动与精确的艺术控制。可控模型通常依赖大规模低质量野外数据集，损害了动画的真实感，且缺乏对话定位（如配音）所需的细粒度时序精度。

Method: 提出关键点条件流式生成框架 KM-Speaker，包含：(1) 解耦策略，将音频驱动的唇部运动与关键点驱动的上半脸动态分离；(2) 全局风格上下文保持机制，确保全脸表现力的一致性；(3) 基于流匹配（flow matching）的生成模型，以参考表演的关键点为条件，提供全局风格引导和帧级时序控制。

Result: 在唇形同步精度、风格遵循度和表现力时序控制方面持续优于现有最先进方法，在数据受限条件下实现了高保真运动与灵活可控性。

Conclusion: KM-Speaker 推进了基于示例的3D面部动画生成，证明了关键点条件流式框架在实现高保真动画与精细时序控制方面的有效性，为语音驱动面部动画的生产级应用提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究语音驱动的3D面部动画生成，属于数字人/说话头/面部重演范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "avatar_motion_generation（数字人与动作生成）"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D facial animation and dubbing, which is outside the user's core priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: speech-driven 3D facial animation, keypoint-based style control, dialogue localization, flow-based generative framework, disentanglement strategy

Abstract: Speech-driven 3D facial animation methods face significant challenges in simultaneously achieving high-fidelity motion and precise artistic control at production quality. Existing controllable models typically learn global style control by relying on large-scale, low-quality \emph{in-the-wild} datasets that compromise overall animation realism. Furthermore, these frameworks often lack the fine-grained temporal precision required for demanding tasks such as dialogue localization (e.g., dubbing), where matching specific facial expressions is as critical as lip synchronization. We present KM-Speaker (Keypoint-Matching Speaker), a novel keypoint-conditioned flow-based generative framework that provides both global style guidance and frame-level temporal control from reference performances. We propose a disentanglement strategy that separates audio-driven lip motion from keypoint-driven upper-face dynamics, together with a global style context preservation mechanism to ensure coherent full-face expressiveness. KM-Speaker advances example-based 3D facial animation by achieving high-fidelity motion and flexible controllability in a data-constrained setting, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art methods in lip-sync accuracy, style adherence, and expressive temporal control.

</details>


### [94] [Zero-Gated Language-conditioned Human Motion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29208)
*Guanhui Qiao, Lu Zhou, Ding Jiang, Jinqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ZGL 提出一种轻量级语言条件人体运动预测方法，通过零门控交叉注意力适配器将观测动作的文本描述作为语义先验注入 DCT 时空 Transformer，初始时与纯运动基线等价，仅在语言能降低预测误差时激活语言信息，在 Human3.6M 和 CMUMocap 上提升了预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体运动预测中，姿态历史提供了核心运动学证据，但缺乏显式的高层语义指导。现有方法仅依赖运动序列，无法利用语义信息来辅助预测。

Method: ZGL 方法包含：(1) 对观测姿态进行渲染，用 VLM 生成一句话描述；(2) 用冻结的 CLIP-L 文本编码器编码描述，投影为少量条件 token；(3) 通过带零门控（zero gate）的紧凑交叉注意力适配器将条件 token 注入 DCT 时空 Transformer 中。零门控初始化为 0，使网络初始化时与纯运动基线数值一致，仅在语言能降低预测误差时才学习使用语言信息。

Result: 在 Human3.6M 上，ZGL 在整体 MPJPE 指标上优于多种代表性运动预测基线。在 CMUMocap 上的实验表明，紧凑的文本描述条件可以迁移到第二个基准数据集，为 3D 人体运动预测提供了实用的语义线索。

Conclusion: ZGL 验证了轻量级语言条件可以有效提升人体运动预测性能，零门控机制确保了语言信息仅在有益时被利用，且该方法具有良好的跨数据集迁移能力。

Classification reason: 该论文研究语言条件的人体运动预测（3D human motion prediction），属于人体动作生成范畴，与现有 canonical subtopic "数字人与动作生成"（avatar_motion_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on human motion prediction with language conditioning, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key directions. Signals: Human motion prediction, language-conditioned, zero-gated adapter, DCT transformer, Human3.6M

Abstract: Pose histories provide the core kinematic evidence for 3D human motion prediction, but they lack explicit high-level semantic guidance. This paper introduces ZGL, a lightweight language-conditioned predictor that uses captions of the observed motion as a semantic prior while preserving a strong motion backbone as the main source of dynamics. We render only the observed poses, generate a one-sentence description with a vision-language model, encode the caption with a frozen CLIP-L text tower, and project it into a small set of conditioning tokens. These tokens are injected into a DCT-based spatial-temporal Transformer by compact crossattention adapters with zero gates: each adapter output is multiplied by a learnable gate initialized to zero, so the full network is numerically identical to the pose-only baseline at initialization and can learn to use language only when it reduces prediction error. On Human3.6M, ZGL improves overall MPJPE over representative motion-prediction baselines in our comparison. Results on CMUMocap further show that compact caption conditioning transfers to a second benchmark and provides a practical semantic cue for 3D human motion prediction.

</details>


### [95] [FacePlex: Full-Duplex Joint Speech-Facial Motion Generation for Conversational Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30145)
*Habin Lim, Jae-Ho Lee, Hah Min Lew, Ji-Su Kang, Gyeong-Moon Park*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FacePlex 是一个统一的流式框架，通过 Rolling Flow Matching 和 Rolling Cross-Attention 实现全双工（在线流式）的联合语音与面部运动生成，在唇形同步和运动保真度上优于纯音频驱动的面部动画基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统要么只做全双工语音生成而不产生面部运动，要么需要先有完整音频再驱动面部动画，无法实现真正的实时对话式虚拟人。本文旨在弥合这一差距，首次形式化定义全双工联合语音-面部运动生成任务。

Method: FacePlex 包含两个核心组件：(1) Rolling Flow Matching——将流匹配（flow matching）适配到在线运动生成，每个流式步骤提交新的运动帧；(2) Rolling Cross-Attention——将流式音频队列与运动队列耦合，使语音和面部运动在生成过程中相互条件化。

Result: 实验表明 FacePlex 在在线流式约束下实现了全双工联合语音-面部运动生成，在唇形同步质量和运动保真度上优于音频驱动的面部动画基线，消融实验和用户研究验证了各组件有效性。

Conclusion: FacePlex 首次实现了全双工流式联合语音-面部运动生成，为实时对话式虚拟人提供了有效方案，Rolling Flow Matching 和 Rolling Cross-Attention 是其中的关键技术。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 FacePlex，一个面向对话式虚拟人的全双工联合语音-面部运动生成框架，涉及说话头、面部动画、唇形同步等核心内容，与 [CANONICAL] 数字人与动作生成 (avatar_motion_generation) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel full-duplex streaming framework for joint speech and facial motion, but tangential to user's core focus on continuous language/multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Full-duplex streaming, Rolling Flow Matching, Joint speech-facial motion, Online generation, Conversational avatars

Abstract: Natural face-to-face conversation requires real-time speech generation together with synchronized facial motion. Existing systems only partially address this problem: speech-only full-duplex models can generate speech in real time but do not produce facial motion, while audio-driven facial motion models animate a face from already available audio rather than jointly generating speech and motion online. To bridge this gap, we first formalize full-duplex joint speech-facial motion generation, where speech tokens and facial motion tokens are produced together every step. Building on this formulation, we propose FacePlex, a unified streaming framework with two key components. First, Rolling Flow Matching adapts flow matching to online motion generation by committing new motion frames at each streaming step. Second, Rolling Cross-Attention couples the streaming audio queue with the motion queue, allowing speech and facial motion to condition each other as generation progresses. Through extensive experiments, ablation studies, and a user study, we show that FacePlex enables full-duplex joint speech-facial motion generation under online streaming constraints, while achieving stronger lip-sync quality and motion fidelity than audio-driven facial motion baselines.

</details>


### [96] [SICAGE: Speaker-Independent Culture-Aware Gesture Generation using TED4C-L Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30001)
*Ariel Gjaci, Antonio Sgorbissa, Vittorio Murino*

Main category: cs.HC, cs.CV, cs.SD, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 SICAGE，一个说话者无关的文化感知共语手势生成框架，利用对抗学习和 Fishr 正则化从音频和文本中学习说话者无关的文化表征，并引入 ALaDiT 实时扩散手势生成器。同时构建了 TED4C-L 数据集（106小时，764位TED演讲者，4个文化群体），实验表明该方法在动作真实感、多样性、节拍同步、语义相关性和文化一致性上均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共语手势生成方法往往忽略文化差异，且文化条件模型很少在说话者无关的划分下评估，导致所谓的"文化"行为可能与说话者个人风格混淆。本文旨在解耦文化因素与说话者身份，实现真正的文化感知手势生成。

Method: 提出 SICAGE 模块化框架：1）将每个说话者视为独立域，通过对抗学习或 Fishr 正则化实现域泛化，学习说话者无关但文化可区分的嵌入；2）提出 ALaDiT，一种基于扩散的实时手势生成器，高效融合文化嵌入；3）构建 TED4C-L 数据集，包含4个文化群体（西方、东亚、南亚、中东）的764位TED演讲者共106小时多模态数据。

Result: 实验表明 SICAGE 在说话者无关的跨文化评估中，在运动真实感、多样性、节拍同步、语义相关性和文化一致性指标上均优于基线方法。消融实验验证了文化嵌入和域泛化策略的有效性。

Conclusion: SICAGE 成功实现了说话者无关的文化感知手势生成，证明了通过域泛化学习文化表征的有效性。TED4C-L 数据集为文化感知手势生成研究提供了重要资源。未来工作可探索更多文化群体和更细粒度的文化表征。

Classification reason: 该论文研究共语手势生成（co-speech gesture generation），属于数字人与动作生成（avatar_motion_generation）范畴，该方向涵盖虚拟人、人体动作生成等主题，与论文高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on gesture generation and cultural adaptation, which is outside the user's core priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: gesture generation, cultural awareness, speaker-independent, diffusion-based, TED dataset

Abstract: Recent co-speech gesture generation methods often overlook cultural differences, limiting their effectiveness in human-agent interaction. Moreover, culture-conditioned models are rarely evaluated under speaker-disjoint splits, so apparent "cultural" behavior may be confounded with speaker-specific gesturing style. We introduce SICAGE, a modular framework for culture-aware co-speech gesture generation that conditions motion synthesis models on speaker-independent cultural representations. SICAGE learns these representations from audio and text by treating each speaker as a separate domain while imposing invariance across speakers. This encourages representations to remain culture-discriminative while reducing dependence on speaker identity. The resulting cultural embeddings condition a multimodal generator to produce culturally appropriate gestures. We instantiate this idea with two domain generalization approaches: adversarial learning and Fishr regularization. We further introduce ALaDiT, a real-time diffusion-based gesture generator designed to efficiently incorporate the learned cultural embeddings. To validate our method, we built TED4C-L, a 106-hour multimodal dataset of 764 TED speakers from four cultural groups. Experiments show that SICAGE improves motion realism, diversity, beat synchronization, semantic relevance, and cultural consistency.

</details>


### [97] [Towards Continual Motion-Language Agents: LoRA Variants for Incremental Motion Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30266)
*Bertram Taetz, Hugo Albuquerque Cosme da Silva, Gabriele Bleser-Taetz*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 普通相关 (49.91) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究运动-语言智能体在持续学习场景下的增量动作理解与生成问题。基于冻结的大语言模型骨干，提出多种LoRA变体（包括基于自编码器路由的混合专家架构）来缓解任务间干扰，在HumanML3D构建的五任务基准上实现了接近零遗忘的双向（M2T和T2M）性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动-语言基础模型在静态设定下表现良好，但自主智能体需要在动态环境中持续学习新的动作概念（如新的运动风格或专业手势），同时不遗忘已学技能。如何平衡稳定性-可塑性（stability-plasticity trade-off）是核心挑战。

Method: 基于冻结的LLM骨干，引入低秩适配（LoRA）变体。具体提出混合专家（MoE）架构，使用自编码器作为路由器在推理时选择任务特定专家，无需任务标签。从HumanML3D通过语义聚类构建了五个任务的持续学习基准，评估M2T和T2M两个方向。

Result: 实验表明，所提方法在M2T和T2M两个方向上均实现了接近零遗忘，同时保持了高质量的生成和描述能力。硬专家选择（hard expert selection）在质量指标上显著优于软专家混合（soft expert blending），表明保持专家隔离对持续学习性能至关重要。此外，发现token级准确率与下游生成质量之间存在差异。

Conclusion: 本文验证了基于LoRA的MoE架构在运动-语言持续学习中的有效性，揭示了专家隔离的重要性，并指出了当前评估协议中token准确率与生成质量之间的不一致性，为未来终身运动-语言智能体的研究提供了方向。

Classification reason: 论文研究文本到动作（T2M）和动作到文本（M2T）的双向运动语言理解与生成，属于人体动作生成与理解范畴。现有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "avatar_motion_generation"（数字人与动作生成）涵盖人体动作生成，且该 subtopic 的描述包含"人体动作"，与本文主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on continual learning for motion-language agents, which is peripheral to the user's core priorities on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: LoRA variants for incremental learning, motion-to-text and text-to-motion, near-zero forgetting, HumanML3D benchmark, mixture-of-experts with autoencoder router

Abstract: Motion-language agents must possess the bidirectional capability to both understand human movement (motion-to-text, M2T) and generate it from natural language (text-to-motion, T2M). While foundational models have achieved strong performance in static settings, autonomous agents operating in dynamic environments must continuously incorporate new motion concepts -- such as novel athletic styles or specialized gestures -- without catastrophic forgetting of previously acquired skills. We investigate the stability-plasticity trade-off in bidirectional motion-language learning under sequential task exposure. Building on a frozen large language model backbone, we introduce low-rank adaptation (LoRA) variants designed to mitigate inter-task interference. We specifically propose mixture-of-experts architectures that utilize an autoencoder-based router to select task-specific experts at inference time, so that no task-label is needed. To evaluate these methods, we establish a reproducible five-task benchmark derived from HumanML3D through semantic clustering of motion descriptions. Our experimental results demonstrate near-zero forgetting across both M2T and T2M directions while maintaining high generation and captioning quality. Furthermore, we show that hard expert selection via routing significantly outperforms soft expert blending in quality metrics, indicating that preserving expert isolation is critical for maintaining performance in our continual learning setting. Finally, we observe that a divergence between token-level accuracy and downstream generation quality may occur, highlighting the need for more comprehensive evaluation protocols in future research on lifelong motion-language agents.

</details>


### [98] [OmniDance: Multimodal Driven Dance Video Generation with Large-scale Internet Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30019)
*Kaixing Yang, Jiashu Zhu, Xulong Tang, Ziqiao Peng, Xiangyue Zhang, Chubin Chen, Puwei Wang, Jiahong Wu, Xiangxiang Chu, Hongyan Liu, Jun He*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 值得关注 (75.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 OmniDance，一个将音乐作为条件信号集成到文本驱动视频生成基础模型中的框架，并构建了 CIPE-Dance 大规模互联网舞蹈视频数据集（30万片段/400小时），支持文本到舞蹈视频、音乐到舞蹈视频以及文本+音乐到舞蹈视频三种生成任务，在多项指标上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有舞蹈视频生成方法受限于缺乏大规模高质量舞蹈视频数据集，且缺乏将音乐作为互补条件信号集成到视频生成基础模型中的系统性框架。

Method: 提出 CIPE-Dance 数据集（通过渐进式专家流水线构建，含编舞文本标注）和 OmniDance 框架：深度感知专业化架构（depth-aware specialization architecture）、锚定式易到难课程学习策略（anchored easy-to-hard curriculum learning）、模态特定时间依赖 CFG 策略（modality-specialized time-dependent CFG），将音乐集成到 TI2V 基础模型中。

Result: 在 CIPE-Dance 数据集上，OmniDance 在 TI2V、MI2V 和 MTI2V 三个任务上均达到 SOTA 性能，展现出鲁棒的多模态集成能力。

Conclusion: 本文通过大规模数据集和框架级方案有效解决了音乐驱动舞蹈视频生成的两大瓶颈，验证了音乐作为高频时序动态信号与文本作为低频语义信号互补融合的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究音乐驱动的舞蹈视频生成，属于人体动作生成与虚拟人动画范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"数字人与动作生成"（avatar_motion_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong dataset contribution but dance video generation is a niche subtopic not aligned with user's priority subtopics. Signals: large-scale dance dataset, music-conditioned video generation, multimodal integration, not in priority subtopics, no autoregressive or continuous model focus

Abstract: Music-driven dance video generation aims to synthesize expressive human motion that is temporally aligned with music while maintaining high visual fidelity. Despite recent progress, existing methods still face two key limitations: the lack of large-scale, high-quality dance video datasets, and the absence of principled frameworks for integrating music as a complementary conditioning signal into Video Generation Foundation Models. To address these limitations, we introduce CIPE-Dance, a large-scale Internet-sourced dance video dataset with choreography-informed text annotations, constructed via a progressive expert pipeline. To the best of our knowledge, CIPE-Dance is the largest dataset for dance video generation to date, comprising 300k high-quality clips over 400 hours and covering diverse dancers, environments, and dance genres. We further propose OmniDance, a framework-level recipe for integrating music into a TI2V foundation model without sacrificing its original controllability or visual fidelity. Motivated by the complementary roles of text as low-frequency semantics and music as high-frequency temporal dynamics, OmniDance co-designs a depth-aware specialization architecture, an anchored easy-to-hard curriculum learning strategy, and a modality-specialized time-dependent CFG strategy, enabling unified TI2V, MI2V, and MTI2V generation. Extensive experiments on CIPE-Dance demonstrate that OmniDance achieves state-of-the-art performance across all three tasks and exhibits robust multimodal integration capability. Project is available at .

</details>


### [99] [FFAvatar: Feed-Forward 4D Head Avatar Reconstruction from Sparse Portrait Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30347)
*Jianjiang Yao, Ke Xian, Renxiang Dai, Robert Caiming Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FFAvatar是一个基于Transformer的3D高斯框架，能够从一张或多张参考肖像图像快速构建高质量、可动画化的4D头部数字人，支持增量重建、交替注意力机制解耦身份与表情/视角变化、稀疏到稠密的学习范式以及运动细化模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式头部数字人重建方法通常需要固定数量的输入视角，且难以在视觉保真度与计算效率之间取得平衡，同时缺乏对动态表情和个性化运动的精细建模能力。

Method: 提出Transformer-based 3D高斯框架，核心包括：(1) 交替注意力机制解耦身份外观与表情/视角变化，重建一致的规范3D外观；(2) 稀疏到稠密学习范式，先在FLAME顶点级别学习稀疏粗粒度特征，再在UV域稠密化以捕捉细粒度几何与纹理细节；(3) 即插即用的运动细化模块，通过建模参数化变形之外的残差运动实现主体特定的动态个性化。

Result: 实验表明FFAvatar能高效生成高保真、可控的4D头部数字人，在灵活性、驱动效率和跨不同表情与视角的身份一致性渲染方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: FFAvatar通过稀疏到稠密的学习和运动细化模块，实现了从稀疏肖像图像到高质量可动画化4D头部数字人的高效前馈重建，为数字人重建提供了灵活且高效的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于Transformer的3D高斯框架，从稀疏肖像图像快速重建可动画化的4D头部数字人，属于数字人与动作生成（avatar_motion_generation）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on 4D head avatar reconstruction, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's configured direction weights. Signals: 4D head avatar, feed-forward reconstruction, sparse-to-dense learning, motion refinement, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: We present FFAvatar, a Transformer-based 3D Gaussian framework for fast construction of high-quality and animatable 4D head avatars from one or more reference portrait images. Unlike existing feed-forward approaches that require a fixed number of input views, FFAvatar supports incremental reconstruction, progressively refining the avatar representation as additional reference images become available. At the core of our method is an alternating attention mechanism that disentangles identity appearance from expression and viewpoint variations, enabling the reconstruction of a canonical 3D appearance that remains consistent across poses and facial expressions. To balance visual fidelity and computational efficiency, we introduce a sparse-to-dense learning paradigm. Coarse appearance features are first learned using sparse primitives anchored to the FLAME vertex level and are subsequently densified in the UV domain to capture fine-grained geometric and texture details. We further propose a plug-and-play motion refinement module that enables subject-specific dynamic personalization by modeling residual motion beyond parametric deformation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FFAvatar efficiently produces high-fidelity and controllable 4D head avatars, achieving superior flexibility, driving efficiency, and identity-consistent rendering across diverse expressions and viewpoints.

</details>


### [100] [3D Scene-Adaptive Trajectory-Controllable Human Image Animation with Camera Movement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30514)
*Deyin Liu, Jicheng Xu, Lin Yuanbo Wu, Xiaowei Zhao, Xiatian Zhu, Zhe Jin, Anjan Dutta*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 值得关注 (75.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种场景自适应的人体图像动画框架，在重建的3D环境中同时控制人体运动和相机轨迹进行视频生成。通过地面自适应3D运动重定向实现用户友好的运动轨迹控制，并设计视点自适应潜融合机制将点云几何先验注入生成过程，在标准基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人体图像动画方法在合成自然场景中轨迹可控的人体运动时面临挑战，尤其是在相机视角变化的情况下难以生成场景自适应的人体动画。

Method: 1) 地面自适应3D运动重定向：自动适应地面高度和方向变化，实现用户友好的运动轨迹控制；2) 视点自适应潜融合机制：通过场景可见性掩码将点云几何先验注入扩散/流生成过程，提供相机控制下的视角变化精确引导。

Result: 在两个标准人体图像动画基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在相关视频生成指标上显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的场景自适应人体动画框架能够有效控制人体运动和相机轨迹，在3D场景中生成更自然、更可控的人体动画视频。

Classification reason: 该论文研究人体图像动画（human image animation），即根据参考人物和动作序列生成视频，属于数字人与动作生成（avatar_motion_generation）这一规范主题。论文还涉及3D场景自适应和相机轨迹控制，但核心仍是人体动作视频生成。

Importance reason: Focuses on human animation with camera control, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: Human image animation, 3D scene-adaptive, Camera trajectory control, Diffusion-based video generation, Not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Human image animation, which aims to generate a video of a reference subject following a provided action sequence, has received increasing research interest. With the development of diffusion-based/flow-based video foundation models, existing animation works have began to upgrade the guidance information from 2D skeleton/pose to 3D modeling conditions. Despite achieving reasonable results, these approaches face challenges in synthesizing trajectory-controllable human motion within natural scene under changed camera views. In this work, we present a scene-adaptive human image animation framework that controls both human motion and camera trajectories within a reconstructed 3D environment for video generation. To achieve this, we first develop a ground-adaptive 3D motion retargeting approach to enable user-friendly motion trajectory control adapting to the changes of elevations of ground and orientations automatically. Then we design a viewpoint-adaptive latent fusion mechanism to inject point-cloud geometric priors through scene-visibility masking into the generative process, providing precise guidance of viewpoint changes under camera control. Experiments on two standard human image animation benchmark datasets demonstrate remarkable improvements of our method over the state of the arts in related video generation metics. Project page:

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_generation-text_to_image_diffusion'></div>

### [101] [JuZhou 1.0 Technical Report: The First Edge-Native Text-to-Image Foundation Model Trained Entirely on China-Developed AI Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28421)
*Ce Chen, Congrui Wang, Yonglin Li, Zhenchen Wan, Mingyang Geng, Junhao Xiao, Zhengpeng Xing, Yaqing Hu, Yao Wu, Zhaoyang Qu, Long Lan, Xinwang Liu, Yingqi Peng, Shijia Li, Zufeng Zhang, Chen Ma, Jingjing Zhou, Xingyu Wang, Qilin Lu, Bin Jiang, Qilin Sun, Shanzhi Gu, Yaoguang Jin, Tongliang Liu, Kede Ma, Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (85.38) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: JuZhou 1.0 是一个完全在国产 AI 加速器上训练的超轻量级文生图扩散基础模型（0.387B 参数），支持完全离线端侧部署。采用 Rectified Flow + DMD2 蒸馏实现 4 步推理，在 GenEval 上以 0.69 分超越 SDXL、SD3-Medium 等大模型，并在骁龙 8 Elite Gen 5 手机上实现约 1.6 秒的 4 步去噪推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图扩散模型通常需要大量计算资源和云基础设施，在边缘设备部署面临延迟高、成本高和用户隐私问题。作者希望构建一个完全离线、端侧可运行的超轻量级文生图基础模型，同时支持中文语义对齐和国产算力训练。

Method: JuZhou 1.0 采用四个关键设计：(1) 紧凑的图像生成骨干，包含 0.385B 参数的 U-Net 去噪网络和 1.90M 参数的蒸馏解码器；(2) Rectified Flow 训练结合 DMD2 蒸馏，将推理降至 4 步；(3) 在 900 万中文图文对上训练中文语义对齐，推理时无需外部翻译；(4) 整个训练和蒸馏流程在国产 Sugon K100 AI 加速器上完成，不依赖 NVIDIA GPU。

Result: 28 步基础模型在 GenEval 上取得 0.69 分，超越 SDXL (2.6B, 0.55)、SD3-Medium (2B, 0.62) 和 IF-XL (4.3B, 0.61)。在骁龙 8 Elite Gen 5 手机上，4 步 U-Net 去噪分支约 1.6 秒，完整 Android 诗意图生成管线（含端侧提示优化）在小米 17 Pro Max 上约 4.5 秒。

Conclusion: JuZhou 1.0 验证了超轻量级文生图模型在移动端完全离线部署的可行性，为中文原生生成、国产算力训练和端侧部署提供了具体参考。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 JuZhou 1.0，一个超轻量级文生图扩散基础模型，属于文本到图像生成方向，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"文生图与图像扩散"（text_to_image_diffusion）高度匹配。

Importance reason: 国产AI加速器训练+端侧部署的极致轻量文生图模型，与用户配置中的模型高效性、图像生成方向高度匹配。 Signals: 国产AI加速器训练, 0.387B超轻量参数, 4步推理, 端侧部署, GenEval 0.69超越SDXL

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models typically require substantial computational resources and cloud infrastructure, posing significant challenges for edge deployment in terms of latency, cost, and user privacy. We present JuZhou 1.0, an ultra-lightweight T2I foundation model designed for fully offline, on-device execution. JuZhou 1.0 achieves its efficiency through four key designs: (1) a compact image-generation backbone consisting of a 0.385B-parameter denoising U-Net and a 1.90M-parameter distilled decoder, totaling approximately 0.387B parameters; (2) Rectified Flow training combined with DMD2 distillation, reducing inference to 4 sampling steps; (3) Chinese semantic alignment trained on 9M curated image-text pairs, enabling direct Chinese prompting without external translation at inference time; and (4) a training and distillation pipeline completed on domestically developed Sugon K100 AI accelerators without relying on NVIDIA GPUs for training or distillation. Despite its compact scale, the 28-step base model of JuZhou 1.0 achieves an overall GenEval score of 0.69, outperforming published baselines including SDXL (2.6B, 0.55), SD3-Medium (2B, 0.62), and IF-XL (4.3B, 0.61). We further validate the full poetry-to-image pipeline on Android and the core CLIP-U-Net-VAE generation branch on iOS. On a smartphone powered by the Snapdragon 8 Elite Gen 5 Mobile Platform, the 4-step U-Net denoising branch runs in approximately 1.6 seconds, while the full Android poetry-to-image pipeline takes 4.5 seconds with on-device prompt refinement on Xiaomi 17 Pro Max. These results position JuZhou 1.0 as a practical approach to mobile text-to-image generation and provide a concrete reference for Chinese-native generation, domestic-compute training, and fully offline on-device deployment after one-time installation.

</details>


### [102] [MammoFlow: Multiview Mammogram Synthesis with Anatomically Consistent Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28537)
*Yuexi Du, Leya Barrientos, Laura Sheiman, John Lewin, Hemant D. Tagare, Nicha C. Dvornek*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 值得关注 (74.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MammoFlow 提出了一种基于 flow matching 的多视角乳腺X光图像合成方法，通过隐式几何对齐和像素级自一致性损失（基于推土机距离）保证生成的 CC 和 MLO 视图之间具有解剖学一致的软组织分布，在图像质量和下游分类任务上均取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角乳腺X光检查依赖成对的 CC 和 MLO 视图提供互补的3D乳腺投影信息，但获取高质量、平衡的数据集对深度学习应用而言仍然困难。现有生成方法未考虑双视图间的几何解剖一致性。

Method: 提出 MammoFlow 框架：1) 设计对齐模块在2D仿射变换子空间中搜索最优解剖对应关系；2) 基于推土机距离(EMD)引入像素级自一致性损失，约束生成图像沿前后轴(AP)的1D组织分布一致；3) 将上述模块集成到预训练的 flow matching 模型中，强制合成对共享从胸壁到乳头的物理合理组织分布。

Result: 生成的图像质量优于基线方法，通过了放射科医生的专家评估，生成的物理一致图像对使下游分类 AUC 提升5%。

Conclusion: MammoFlow 是首个利用隐式几何组织对应关系引导多视角乳腺X光生成的工作，证明了在生成过程中施加解剖一致性先验的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文使用 flow matching（扩散类方法）进行乳腺X光图像生成，属于图像生成范畴。虽然应用领域为医学影像，但核心方法是 flow matching 生成模型，可归入 [CANONICAL] 文生图与图像扩散 这一广泛主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical image generation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and has negative keywords. Signals: flow matching, multiview mammogram synthesis, anatomical consistency, medical imaging, downstream classification

Abstract: Multiview mammography relies on paired craniocaudal (CC) and mediolateral oblique (MLO) views to provide complementary projections of a 3D breast volume, enabling precise anomaly localization. However, acquiring high-quality, balanced datasets remains challenging for deep learning applications. We propose a novel method to synthesize multiview mammograms by leveraging the inherent geometric relationship between CC and MLO views. To enforce an implicit 3D consistency prior during generation, we develop an alignment module that searches a 2D affine transformation subspace to establish optimal anatomical correspondence. Leveraging this alignment, we introduce a pixel-space self-consistency loss based on the Earth Mover's Distance (EMD) between the 1D anteroposterior (AP) axis tissue distributions of the generated images. Integrated into a pretrained flow matching model, MammoFlow forces synthesized pairs to share physically plausible tissue distributions from the chest wall to the nipple. To our knowledge, this is the first work to guide multiview mammogram generation using implicit geometric tissue correspondence. Our method demonstrates superior image quality, passes expert radiologist evaluation, and generates physically consistent pairs that improve downstream classification AUC by 5%. Code is available at

</details>


### [103] [Mural: Transferring LLM knowledge to image generation via Mixture-of-Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29013)
*Achin Jain, Jie An, Siddharth Chaudhary, Davide Modolo*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (87.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Mural架构，通过Mixture-of-Transformers（MoT）中的共享注意力机制，将冻结的、具备推理能力的LLM与扩散图像生成器集成，仅使用标准文本-图像对进行训练。实验表明该模型在GenEval、DPG-Bench等基准上表现优异，并涌现出跨语言图像生成、颜色引导构图、表情符号/ASCII场景构建以及世界知识引导生成等训练数据中不存在的新能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否仅使用标准文本-图像对训练，将冻结LLM的丰富知识有效迁移到文生图（T2I）任务中，无需交错多模态信号或显式推理监督，从而在资源受限条件下利用预训练模型能力。

Method: 提出Mixture-of-Transformers（MoT）架构，将冻结的、具备推理能力的LLM与基于扩散的图像生成器通过共享注意力机制集成。LLM部分保持冻结，图像生成器部分进行训练，两者通过MoT中的共享注意力层进行信息交互，使图像生成器能够利用LLM的语义和世界知识。

Result: 在统一理解-生成系统中取得强性能：GenEval 0.85，DPG-Bench 86.75，WISE 0.66（含推理时推理）。涌现出多种训练数据中不存在的能力：跨语言图像生成、颜色引导构图、表情符号/ASCII场景构建、世界知识引导生成。

Conclusion: 预训练LLM的知识可以在标准文生图训练范式下有效引导图像合成，无需交错多模态信号或显式推理监督。这一发现为在资源受限的多模态学习中利用冻结模型能力开辟了新途径。

Classification reason: 该论文研究将冻结LLM知识与扩散图像生成器结合进行文生图（T2I），属于多模态生成方向。核心方法是基于扩散的文生图，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "文生图与图像扩散"（text_to_image_diffusion）。同时论文也涉及理解与生成统一系统，因此匹配多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous multimodal generation by integrating frozen LLM knowledge into diffusion image generation, aligning with top-priority continuous language/multimodal models and multimodal generation. Signals: Mixture-of-Transformers, shared attention with frozen LLM, emergent cross-lingual generation, world knowledge guided synthesis, strong GenEval/DPG-Bench scores

Abstract: Leveraging capabilities of large language models (LLMs) in text-to-image (T2I) synthesis is an important research direction. In this work we investigate whether the knowledge of a frozen LLM can be effectively utilized in T2I generation when trained exclusively on standard text-image pairs. We integrate a frozen, reasoning-capable LLM with a diffusion-based image generator via shared attention within the Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture. Our experiments span two critical questions: (1) what degree of the LLM's intrinsic knowledge remains accessible during T2I training, and (2) what novel capabilities emerge in the resulting system. Across established benchmarks, our models achieve strong performance among unified understanding-generation systems: 0.85 on GenEval, 86.75 on DPG-Bench, and 0.66 on WISE with inference-time reasoning, using only text-image data. Remarkably, we uncover emergent behaviors absent from training data, including cross-lingual image generation, color-guided composition, emoji / ASCII scene construction, and generation directed by world knowledge. These results demonstrate that pretrained LLM knowledge can guide image synthesis under standard text-to-image training paradigms, without interleaved multimodal signals or explicit reasoning supervision. Our findings open new avenues for harnessing frozen model capabilities in resource-constrained multimodal learning.

</details>


### [104] [Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion-Image: Advancing Masked Discrete Diffusion for High-Resolution Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29814)
*Shufan Li, Greg Heinrich, Hanrong Ye, Yonggan Fu, Aditya Grover, Jan Kautz, Pavlo Molchanov*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion-Image 是一种改进的掩码离散扩散模型（MDM），通过引入 token 编辑机制和分组交叉熵（GCE）损失函数，解决了标准 MDM 缺乏自纠正能力和大词表下训练信号稀疏的问题，在 GenEval、DPG 和 HPSv3 上取得了 SOTA 结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准掩码离散扩散模型（MDM）存在两个关键缺陷：(1) 离散 token 一旦被解掩码就无法修改，缺乏连续扩散模型那种逐步细化的自纠正能力；(2) 增大离散图像 tokenizer 的词表大小虽能提升重建质量，但会导致生成建模时每个 token 的训练信号变得极其稀疏，优化困难。

Method: (1) 提出 token-editing 机制，允许模型在推理过程中动态修改已解掩码的 token，类似雕塑家逐步精修作品；(2) 提出分组交叉熵（Grouped Cross-Entropy, GCE）损失，在 embedding 空间中为与 ground truth 相邻的 token 也分配正学习信号，缓解信号稀疏问题；(3) 为 GCE 实现自定义融合算子，大幅降低大词表场景下的 VRAM 占用。

Result: 在 GenEval 上达到 0.90，DPG 上达到 86.9，HPSv3 上达到 10.76，均取得 SOTA 性能，显著提升了掩码离散图像生成器的训练效率和图像保真度。

Conclusion: 通过 token 编辑机制和分组交叉熵损失，有效克服了掩码离散扩散模型在自纠正能力和训练信号稀疏性方面的固有缺陷，使离散扩散模型在高分辨率文生图任务上达到了与连续扩散模型竞争甚至更优的性能。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种改进的掩码离散扩散模型（MDM）用于高分辨率文生图，属于文本到图像生成的基础方法。虽然使用了离散扩散而非连续扩散，但核心任务仍是文生图，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "文生图与图像扩散" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Advances masked discrete diffusion for high-res image synthesis with token editing and grouped cross-entropy, directly relevant to continuous language/multimodal model priorities. Signals: masked discrete diffusion, token editing, grouped cross-entropy, high-resolution image synthesis, GenEval 0.90

Abstract: We propose Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion-Image, a state-of-the-art masked discrete diffusion model (MDM) for high-resolution text-to-image synthesis. Compared with prior work on masked image generation, Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion-Image addresses two key challenges. First, unlike continuous diffusion models which progressively refine latent representations across the entire image, standard MDMs lack self-correcting capability because discrete tokens cannot be modified once they are unmasked. Second, although increasing the vocabulary size of discrete image tokenizers improves reconstruction fidelity, it introduces optimization difficulties for generative modeling as the per-token training signal becomes increasingly sparse. To address the first challenge, Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion-Image incorporates a token-editing mechanism that enables the model to dynamically revise already-unmasked tokens during inference, similar to how a sculptor iteratively refines their work. To tackle the second challenge, we propose a Grouped Cross-Entropy (GCE) objective that assigns positive learning signals to tokens neighboring the ground truth in embedding space, thereby alleviating signal sparsity. To further improve training efficiency, we implement a custom fused operator for GCE that significantly reduces VRAM usage in large-vocabulary settings. Experimental results demonstrate that these innovations substantially improve both training efficiency and image fidelity of masked discrete image generators, achieving a score of 0.90 on GenEval, 86.9 on DPG and 10.76 of HPSv3.

</details>

### [105] [Physics-Grounded Disentangled Flow Modeling for Brain Disease Progression Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28630)
*Jun Wang, Peirong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 值得关注 (78.12) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 PDF（Physics-grounded Disentangled Flow matching）框架，将脑部病变进展分解为形态演化（结构变形）和强度演化（信号变化）两个过程，分别用 flow matching 网络建模，并引入 PDE 正则化损失（扩散-反应-平流方程）增强物理合理性，在多个公开数据集上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纵向脑部病变预测方法通常直接从基线图像映射到未来图像，将结构变形和强度变化纠缠在一起建模，缺乏物理可解释性和泛化能力。作者希望显式解耦这两个过程并引入物理约束，以提高预测的物理合理性和可解释性。

Method: 提出 PDF 框架，将病变进展分解为两个解耦的 flow matching 过程：(1) 形态演化——用 flow matching 网络学习病变生长和结构变形；(2) 强度演化——用另一个 flow matching 网络建模由病变浓度变化驱动的信号变化。引入基于病变生长动力学的 PDE 正则化损失（扩散-反应-平流方程）约束形态演化过程。

Result: 在三个涵盖不同脑部疾病的公开纵向数据集上取得 SOTA 性能，验证了解耦建模框架和物理约束学习的有效性。

Conclusion: 物理引导的解耦 flow matching 框架能有效提升脑部病变进展预测的准确性和物理合理性，为医学影像中的时序生成任务提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文使用 flow matching 进行纵向脑部病变预测，属于视频/时序生成任务（从基线图像预测未来病变演变），与"文生视频与视频扩散"（text_to_video_diffusion）这一 CANONICAL 子主题相关，因为 flow matching 是扩散/生成模型的一种形式，且任务本质是时序视频帧预测。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical brain lesion progression, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords. Signals: flow matching, disentangled modeling, PDE-regularized loss, brain disease, medical imaging

Abstract: Forecasting longitudinal brain lesion evolution is critical for disease monitoring and treatment planning. Existing approaches typically learn a direct mapping from a baseline image to a future observation, without explicitly modeling the physical mechanisms underlying the lesion progression. Such an entangled modeling of structural deformation and image intensity variation limits physical plausibility, model generalization, and interpretability. To address this, we propose PDF, a Physics-grounded Disentangled Flow matching framework for longitudinal brain disease forecasting. We explicitly decompose the longitudinal modeling of lesion growth into two processes, each learned by a dedicated flow matching network: morphology evolution, which captures lesion growth and structural deformation; and intensity evolution, which models signal changes driven by variations in lesion concentration. To enforce physics-grounded constraints, we introduce a PDE-regularized loss based on lesion growth dynamics, that enforces a diffusion-reaction-advection formulation for morphological evolution. Experiments on three public longitudinal datasets spanning diverse brain diseases demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, validating the effectiveness of the disentangled modeling framework and physics-grounded learning design. Code is publicly available at .

</details>


### [106] [RainODE: Continuous-Time Precipitation Forecasting with Latent Neural ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29855)
*Yeeun Seong, Doyi Kim, Minseok Seo, Changick Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: RainODE 提出了一种基于潜空间神经 ODE 的连续时间降水预报框架，通过确定性连续动力学与布朗桥随机细化模块结合，实现任意时间分辨率的高质量降水预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降水预报不仅需要准确性，还需要高时间分辨率。然而，提高时间分辨率受限于观测条件和密集离散建模的计算成本。现有方法难以在连续时间尺度上建模降水系统的演化。

Method: 将降水预报建模为连续时间动力系统，使用 Neural ODE 在潜空间中建模降水演化，实现导数一致的时间动力学。针对确定性 ODE 无法捕捉非平流强度变化（如局部增长、衰减）的问题，引入基于布朗桥的随机源建模模块，细化残差强度变化并恢复细粒度结构。

Result: 在 SEVIR 和新提出的 RAPID 数据集上，RainODE 在多个时间间隔和降水强度下均取得一致改进，支持任意时间点的推理并保持预测清晰度。

Conclusion: RainODE 通过结合确定性连续动力学与随机细化，有效解决了降水预报中时间分辨率与预测质量的权衡问题，为气象预报提供了一种新的连续时间建模范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究降水预报（视频帧预测），使用 Neural ODE 在潜空间进行连续时间建模，属于视频生成/预测范畴。映射到 [CANONICAL] 的"文生视频与视频扩散"（text_to_video_diffusion），因为该 subtopic 涵盖视频生成方法，降水预报可视为条件视频预测任务。

Importance reason: Applies Neural ODE for continuous-time forecasting, but domain is weather, not the user's priority subtopics. Signals: Neural ODE, continuous-time dynamics, precipitation forecasting, latent space modeling, Brownian Bridge refinement

Abstract: In precipitation forecasting, not only accuracy but also temporal resolution is critical. However, increasing temporal resolution is constrained by observational limitations and the computational cost of dense discrete modeling. To overcome this limitation, we reformulate precipitation forecasting as a continuous-time dynamical system and propose RainODE, a framework that models precipitation evolution in latent space using a Neural ODE. This formulation enables derivative-consistent temporal dynamics and captures the dominant large-scale advective motion of precipitation systems. Nevertheless, a purely deterministic ODE struggles to represent non-advective intensity changes such as localized growth, decay, and sub-grid variability, often leading to over-smoothed predictions. To address this issue, we introduce a stochastic source modeling module based on a Brownian Bridge formulation, which refines residual intensity variations and restores fine-grained structures while preserving advective consistency. By combining deterministic continuous dynamics with stochastic refinement, RainODE enables arbitrary-time inference while maintaining sharp predictions. Experiments on SEVIR and the newly introduced Radar-based Precipitation Integrated Dataset (RAPID) demonstrate consistent improvements across multiple temporal intervals and precipitation regimes. The code is available at .

</details>


### [107] [The Surprising Effectiveness of Video Diffusion Models for Hand Motion Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30308)
*Yuxi Wang, Chengkai Jin, Yufei Liu, Wenqi Ouyang, Tianyi Wei, Zhiwei Zeng, Siyuan Huang, Zhiqi Shen, Xingang Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (83.58) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 ViDiHand，利用预训练视频扩散模型的表征来从第一视角视频中重建4D双手姿态，无需检测器、填充器或测试时优化，在多个基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图像的手部运动重建方法依赖检测器，在严重遮挡下失效；基于视频的方法依赖从稀缺的手部姿态标注中学习的时序模块，难以建模运动动力学、遮挡推理和手物交互。视频扩散模型在互联网规模数据上训练时隐式习得了这些能力，因此本文探索利用其表征来解决手部运动重建问题。

Method: ViDiHand 使用预训练的视频扩散模型作为特征提取器，通过手部叠加渲染目标（hand-overlay rendering objective）对模型进行适配，使其特征专门化于手部同时保留世界先验知识。然后使用解码器从适配后的特征中恢复度量尺度的姿态。整个流程直接在完整帧上运行，无需检测器、填充器或测试时优化。

Result: 在 ARCTIC、HOT3D 和 HOI4D 三个基准上，ViDiHand 显著优于现有方法，展示了视频扩散模型作为手部运动重建新基础的有效性。

Conclusion: 视频扩散模型可以作为手部运动重建的强大新基础，并为具身智能的可扩展野外数据收集提供有前景的途径。

Classification reason: 该论文利用预训练视频扩散模型（video diffusion model）的表征进行4D手部运动重建，核心方法基于视频扩散模型的特征提取与适配，属于视频扩散模型的应用。因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "文生视频与视频扩散"（text_to_video_diffusion）。

Importance reason: Novel use of video diffusion representations for hand motion reconstruction, but only tangentially touches priority subtopics like world models or continuous representations. Signals: video diffusion model, hand motion reconstruction, egocentric video, representation adaptation, no detector needed

Abstract: 4D hand motion reconstruction from egocentric video is bottlenecked by clear limitations of existing methods: image-based pipelines depend on a detector that fails under heavy occlusion, while video-based methods rely on temporal modules learned only from scarce hand-pose annotations, a narrow signal insufficient to model motion dynamics, occlusion reasoning, and hand-object interaction. These capabilities, however, are exactly what video generative models must implicitly acquire when trained to synthesize coherent video at internet scale. Motivated by this, we present ViDiHand, which leverages the representations of a pretrained video diffusion model to reconstruct 4D two-hand pose. We adapt it via a hand-overlay rendering objective that specializes its features for hands while preserving its world priors. A decoder then recovers metric-scale pose from the adapted features. The whole pipeline operates directly on full frames--no detector, no infiller, and no test-time optimization. On ARCTIC, HOT3D, and HOI4D, ViDiHand substantially outperforms prior methods, establishing video diffusion models as a powerful new foundation for hand motion reconstruction and a promising route to scalable in-the-wild data collection for embodied AI. Project page: .

</details>

### [108] [SATB-VR: Training Few-Step Video Restoration Diffusion Model using SNR-Aware Trajectory Blending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28677)
*Haoran Bai, Xiaoxu Chen, Xiaoyu Liu, Zongsheng Yue, Sibin Deng, Wangmeng Zuo, Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 普通相关 (57.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SATB-VR 提出一种少步视频复原扩散模型范式，通过辅助预测器跳过低信噪比去噪步骤，并引入 SNR-Aware Trajectory Blending (SATB) 策略解决训练-推理不一致问题，同时提出 Denoiser-Driven Consistency (DDC) 损失提升预测器精度，在 ≤5 步推理下取得优于现有方法的视频复原效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在视频复原任务中效果优异，但依赖大量迭代步骤导致效率低下；而激进的单步蒸馏方法又往往牺牲精细纹理恢复。如何在少步推理下兼顾效率与质量是核心挑战。

Method: 提出 SATB-VR 框架：(1) 使用辅助预测器跳过低 SNR 的早期去噪步骤，直接启动中高 SNR 阶段的去噪过程；(2) 提出 SNR-Aware Trajectory Blending (SATB) 策略，在前向过程中根据 SNR 动态混合预测器输出与真实轨迹构建噪声输入，迫使去噪器鲁棒地补偿预测误差；(3) 引入 Denoiser-Driven Consistency (DDC) 损失，利用同步更新的去噪器作为动态评估器对齐内部特征，提升预测器精度。

Result: 在合成、真实世界和 AIGC 视频复原基准上，SATB-VR 在 ≤5 步推理下性能优于现有方法，实现了效率与质量的良好平衡。

Conclusion: SATB-VR 通过 SNR 感知的轨迹混合策略有效解决了少步扩散视频复原中的训练-推理不一致问题，为高效视频复原提供了可行的少步范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出SATB-VR，一种少步视频复原扩散模型，通过辅助预测器跳过低SNR步骤并结合SNR感知轨迹混合策略加速去噪过程，属于扩散模型加速与少步采样方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化" (efficient_generation) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a practical few-step video restoration diffusion model with a novel SNR-aware trajectory blending strategy to address train-inference mismatch. Signals: few-step video restoration, SNR-aware trajectory blending, denoiser-driven consistency loss, efficient inference, diffusion model acceleration

Abstract: While diffusion models excel in video restoration, their reliance on extensive iterative steps limits efficiency. Conversely, aggressive single-step distillation often compromises fine texture recovery. To achieve an optimal balance, we present SATB-VR, a few-step paradigm that jump-starts the denoising process via an auxiliary predictor, explicitly bypassing early low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) steps. However, naive joint training of the predictor and the denoiser inherently introduces a severe train-inference discrepancy. To resolve this, we propose the SNR-Aware Trajectory Blending (SATB) strategy. During the forward process, SATB constructs the noisy input by dynamically blending the predictor's output with the ground-truth trajectory based on the SNRs. This forces the denoiser to robustly compensate for initial prediction errors while smoothly converging to the clean data manifold. Furthermore, we introduce a Denoiser-Driven Consistency (DDC) loss, leveraging the concurrently updated denoiser as a dynamic evaluator to explicitly align internal features and boost predictor accuracy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, under flexible few-step inference regimes (\eg, $\le 5$ steps), SATB-VR performs favorably against existing approaches on synthetic, real-world, and AIGC benchmarks.

</details>


### [109] [Stochastic Optimal Control Sampling for Diffusion Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28785)
*Jie Zhang, Youmei Qiu, Hanling Tian, Jingyuan Zhang, Xiang Yin, Xiaolin Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 普通相关 (57.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出随机最优控制采样（SOCS），将扩散模型的去噪过程建模为动态系统，通过闭式控制更新在每个采样步注入测量一致性信号，高效解决图像逆问题，在多种线性SDE骨干上实现更优的视觉质量和定量性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在图像逆问题中表现出色，但现有基于随机最优控制（SOC）的方法需要对整个轨迹进行优化，计算开销大。本文旨在推导闭式控制更新，实现逐步高效控制，在保证测量一致性的同时提升采样效率。

Method: 将扩散去噪过程建模为动态系统，在每个采样步推导出闭式控制更新，将测量一致的干净预测拉回到去噪流上。通过调节控制强度来对齐扩散模型的固有能力，从而增强感知质量。该方法兼容多种线性SDE骨干。

Result: 在多种图像逆任务（如超分辨率、修复、去模糊等）上，SOCS实现了准确的测量对齐重建，视觉保真度和定量指标均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SOCS通过闭式控制更新高效解决了扩散逆问题，兼顾了测量一致性与感知质量，且具有良好的通用性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出随机最优控制采样（SOCS）方法，通过闭式控制更新加速扩散模型在逆问题中的采样过程，属于扩散模型采样效率优化，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes efficient closed-form control for diffusion inverse problems, aligning with model efficiency and generation acceleration priorities. Signals: diffusion inverse problems, stochastic optimal control, closed-form control, efficient sampling, image reconstruction

Abstract: Benefiting from the strong ability to capture data distributions, diffusion models have become powerful tools for solving image inverse problems. The key is to controllably steer the sampling trajectory toward the measurements while respecting the diffusion prior. In this work, we introduce Stochastic Optimal Control Sampling (SOCS), which models the denoising process as a dynamical system and injects control signals via SOC. Previous SOC-based approach addresses inverse problems by optimizing over the entire trajectory, which is computationally expensive. In contrast, we derive a closed-form control update and apply it at each sampling step, pulling the measurement-consistent clean prediction back onto the denoising flow. In SOCS, we can readily modulate the control strength to align with the diffusion model's native capabilities and thereby enhance perceptual quality. Our method is compatible with a variety of linear stochastic differential equation backbones. Extensive experiments across a broad spectrum of image inverse tasks demonstrate that SOCS achieves accurate measurement-aligned reconstructions with improved visual fidelity and stronger quantitative performance.

</details>


### [110] [Few-Step Boltzmann Generators via Scalable Likelihood Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29110)
*RuiKang OuYang, Hanlin Yu, Xinyue Ai, Yutong He, Nicholas M. Boffi, Pradeep Ravikumar, Jose Miguel Hernandez-Lobato, Max Simchowitz, Benjamin Kurt Miller, Omar Chehab*

Main category: stat.ML, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出SCALLOP，一种基于流匹配的似然蒸馏方法，能够在少量函数评估下同时生成高质量样本并估计其似然，在分子科学（Boltzmann生成器）和图像生成任务上取得SOTA性能，推理速度提升最高10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流的生成模型在少步采样方面取得了进展，但在似然估计方面缺乏类似进步——要么依赖限制性架构进行精确计算，要么使用Hutchinson迹估计器等随机近似引入较大方差。本文旨在解决少步生成模型的高效似然估计问题。

Method: 基于F2D2（似然流映射模型），提出SCALLOP（SCAlable LikeLihood distillation of flOw maPs），引入一种无需Hutchinson估计器的可扩展似然蒸馏目标函数，支持向量化实现，显著降低训练方差和时间。

Result: 在分子科学Boltzmann生成器和图像数据集上，SCALLOP相比F2D2在训练方差和训练时间上均有显著降低，性能持续提升，与SOTA方法竞争力相当，且相比最快基线实现最高10倍推理加速。

Conclusion: SCALLOP为少步流生成模型提供了一种可扩展的似然蒸馏方法，在保持生成质量的同时大幅提升推理效率，在分子科学和图像生成领域均有应用价值。

Classification reason: 该论文提出SCALLOP方法，通过似然蒸馏实现少步（few-step）生成，显著加速推理（最高10倍加速），属于生成模型加速与高效化（efficient_generation）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on molecular science and image generation with flow-based likelihood estimation, not directly aligned with user's priority subtopics on continuous language/video models. Signals: Flow matching for likelihood estimation, Few-step generation, Molecular Boltzmann generator, Image generation, Inference speedup up to 10x

Abstract: Recent progress in flow-based generative modeling has led to models that output high-quality samples while using only a small number of function evaluations. However, at present, there is a lack of similar advances in estimating the model likelihood. In particular, most existing methods either rely on restrictive architectures that enable exact calculations, or use stochastic approximations such as Hutchinson's trace estimator that introduce substantial variance. In this work, we introduce SCAlable LikeLihood distillation of flOw maPs (SCALLOP). SCALLOP builds on the recently proposed F2D2, a likelihood flow map model that can generate samples and their densities in a small number of function evaluations. While F2D2 uses Hutchinson's estimator during training, we introduce an alternative and more scalable likelihood distillation objective that is Hutchinson-free and admits a vectorized formulation. Empirically, we demonstrate the effectiveness of SCALLOP as a Boltzmann generator in molecular science, and further validate its benefit on image datasets. SCALLOP significantly reduces both training variance and training time while consistently improving performance compared to F2D2, and is competitive with the state-of-the-art while achieving up to 10x inference speedup over the fastest baseline.

</details>


### [111] [Beyond Trajectory Matching: Reflow with Marginal Distribution Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29287)
*Chen Wang, Peiran Yun, Pan Xie, Ke Deng*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 值得关注 (76.92) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文指出reflow蒸馏中的轨迹匹配存在理论局限——不同学生模型在相同轨迹匹配损失下可能产生不同的端点边际分布，从而影响生成质量。为此提出边际对齐正则化器，通过沿学生ODE追踪对数密度变化并利用冻结教师模型的分数来惩罚分布差异，无需额外可训练网络或对抗优化，在少步生成任务上取得改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的reflow蒸馏方法通过轨迹匹配让学生模型逼近教师模型的ODE轨迹，但轨迹匹配损失无法唯一确定学生模型诱导的分布——两个学生模型可以有相同的轨迹匹配损失，但端点边际分布不同，导致生成质量差异。作者希望弥补这一理论缺陷，提升少步生成的质量。

Method: 提出边际对齐正则化器（marginal-alignment regularizer），在reflow蒸馏的每个区间端点，计算学生模型诱导的边际分布与教师模型边际分布之间的差异。该正则化器通过沿学生ODE追踪对数密度变化（利用瞬时变化公式）并查询冻结教师模型的分数函数来实现，无需额外可训练网络或对抗训练。该方法统一适用于vanilla reflow和piecewise reflow。作者还证明了局部边际对齐可以控制最终时刻学生与教师分布之间的总变差界（telescoping TV bound）。

Result: 在多个基准骨干网络上的实验表明，所提出的边际对齐正则化方法在少步生成场景下有效提升了生成质量，优于标准reflow蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: 轨迹匹配不足以唯一确定学生模型的分布，边际对齐正则化是弥补这一缺陷的有效手段，且无需额外复杂模块。该方法为扩散/流模型的少步蒸馏提供了新的理论视角和实践改进。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种基于边际分布对齐的正则化方法改进reflow蒸馏，用于扩散/连续流生成模型的少步采样加速，属于生成模型加速与高效化（efficient_generation）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for generative models, a high-priority subtopic, with a novel theoretical fix for reflow distillation. Signals: efficient inference, generative model acceleration, reflow distillation, marginal distribution alignment, few-step generation

Abstract: Diffusion and continuous-flow generative models achieve high-quality generation, and their deterministic sampling can be formulated as solving learned ODE dynamics. However, accurate ODE discretization often requires many steps, making efficient few-step generation a key challenge. Among acceleration strategies, reflow-based distillation simplifies teacher ODE trajectories so that a student model can approximate the teacher transport with fewer steps. We identify a theoretical limitation of this paradigm, namely that trajectory matching can under-determine the distribution induced by the student model. In particular, two student models can attain the same trajectory-matching loss while inducing different endpoint marginal distributions, which may lead to different generation quality. To address this limitation, we introduce a marginal-alignment regularizer that penalizes the discrepancy between the student-induced marginal and the corresponding teacher marginal at the endpoint of each distillation interval. The regularizer is computed by tracking log-density changes along the ODE induced by the student model and evaluating scores from the frozen teacher model, without requiring auxiliary trainable networks or adversarial optimization. The resulting framework applies uniformly to the reflow family, including vanilla reflow and piecewise reflow. We further prove a telescoping total-variation bound showing that local marginal alignment controls the final-time discrepancy between the student-induced and teacher-induced distributions. Experiments on benchmark backbones demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method for few-step generation.

</details>


### [112] [W4A4 Quantization for Inference on Wan2.2-I2V-A14B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29337)
*Yidong Chen, Chengyu Shi, Jiahao Liu*

TL;DR: 本文针对 ICME 2026 低比特大模型量化挑战赛，提出了一种 W4A4 量化方案用于 Wan2.2-I2V-A14B 视频生成模型的推理加速。结合 MixQ 风格的混合精度处理激活异常值、SmoothQuant 风格的逐通道平滑以及块级 HiF4 打包，在 VBench 指标上相比 FP16 仅下降 2-3.5%，优于原生 HiFloat4 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型（如 Wan2.2）参数量大、推理成本高，亟需低比特量化（W4A4）来降低显存占用和加速推理。然而，直接应用 4-bit 量化会导致显著的质量下降，需要针对视频生成模型的特点设计专门的量化策略。

Method: 提出三阶段量化方案：(1) 使用 OpenS2V-5M 校准数据识别激活值中的重尾异常通道；(2) 采用 SmoothQuant 风格的逐通道平滑，在量化前重新平衡权重和激活的动态范围；(3) 设计双分支 GEMM 策略：异常值列保留更高精度，其余通道使用严格的 W4A4，结合块级 HiF4 打包处理前馈线性层。

Result: 在官方 VBench I2V 指标上，该方案在大多数质量维度上相比 FP16 仅下降 2-3.5%，运动平滑度甚至有所提升，显著优于原生 HiFloat4 基线（在所有指标上下降约 5%）。

Conclusion: 将 LLM 量化中的混合精度和通道平滑技术成功迁移到视频生成模型上，实现了 W4A4 量化推理，在保持生成质量的同时大幅降低计算开销，为大规模视频生成模型的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 Wan2.2-I2V-A14B 视频生成模型的 W4A4 量化推理，属于生成模型量化加速，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses model efficiency and quantization for video generation, a priority subtopic with weight 1.2, and uses techniques (SmoothQuant, MixQ) relevant to continuous multimodal models. Signals: W4A4 quantization, video generation inference acceleration, MixQ mixed precision, SmoothQuant per-channel smoothing, HiF4 packing

Abstract: We summarize our submission to Sub-Challenge 1: W4A4 Quantization for Inference (HiF4 / MXFP4) of the ICME 2026 Low-Bit-width Large-Model Quantization Challenge. The sub-challenge targets 4-bit weight and 4-bit activation inference on Wan-AI/Wan2.2-I2V-A14B under HiF4 or MXFP4 numerical formats. We adapt two complementary ideas from LLM quantization, MixQ-style mixed precision for sparse activation outliers and SmoothQuant-style per-channel smoothing, together with block-wise HiF4 packing for Wan2.2 feed-forward linear layers. Calibration on representative OpenS2V-5M batches identifies heavy-tailed activation channels; smoothing rebalances dynamic range before W4A4 rounding; and a dual-branch GEMM preserves outlier columns in higher precision while the bulk of channels use strict W4A4. On official VBench I2V metrics, our pipeline stays within 2-3.5 percent of FP16 on most quality axes and improves motion smoothness, outperforming a native HiFloat4 baseline that degrades roughly 5 percent relative to FP16 across all reported scores.

</details>


### [113] [SAFE-DiT: Semantics-Aware Fast-path Execution for High-Resolution Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29360)
*Xuanhua Yin, Yuxuan Jia, Chuanzhi Xu, Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (94.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SAFE-DiT 提出了一种无需训练的语义感知快速路径框架，通过识别并移除冗余的空间自注意力掩码（这些掩码会导致 FlashAttention 路径变慢 4-5 倍），结合提示条件 token 分区、选择性状态更新和周期上下文刷新，在高分辨率 DiT 推理中实现了 2.69×-5.09× 端到端加速和显著的内存降低，同时保持视觉质量不降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率 Diffusion Transformer (DiT) 推理存在大量空间冗余，现有空间自适应实现通过注意力掩码编码区域计算，但这些掩码会将 SDPA 从 FlashAttention 快速路径中移出，导致严重的系统瓶颈（Mask-Induced Dispatch Tax, MIDT），且该瓶颈随序列长度增长而加剧。作者旨在消除这一不必要的性能开销。

Method: SAFE-DiT 包含两个核心组件：1) SAFE-Core：仅移除经证明可产生行级常数偏移的图像自注意力掩码（如空间冗余掩码），保留语义关键掩码（如文本填充掩码）；通过提示条件 token 分区、选择性状态更新和周期上下文刷新实现空间自适应。2) SAFE-DiT+SW：在 SAFE-Core 基础上引入敏感度加权无分类器引导，提供额外的提示对齐操作点，且不重新引入空间自注意力掩码。

Result: 在 Lumina-Next 上，SAFE-DiT 在 1024² 分辨率下实现 2.69× 端到端加速，在 2560² 下实现 5.09× 加速；峰值内存从 94.1 GB 降至 27.9 GB（2560²），并支持 3072² 生成（密集推理已 OOM）。冗余掩码使长序列注意力比无掩码路径慢 4.1×-5.8×。配对指标、组件消融和盲人研究均支持 SAFE-Core 在视觉质量上与密集快速路径基线无显著差异。

Conclusion: SAFE-DiT 有效解决了高分辨率 DiT 推理中由冗余注意力掩码引起的系统瓶颈，实现了显著的加速和内存节省，同时保持视觉质量。该工作揭示了系统级优化（而非仅算法优化）在高分辨率生成模型中的重要性，为未来高效 DiT 推理提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于高分辨率 Diffusion Transformer (DiT) 推理加速，通过去除冗余注意力掩码、token 分区与选择性状态更新实现端到端加速，属于生成模型加速与高效化的典型工作，因此复用 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化" (efficient_generation)。

Importance reason: Directly addresses model efficiency for high-resolution DiT inference, a key priority subtopic with strong alignment to user's boost keywords. Signals: model efficiency, diffusion transformer, high-resolution inference, training-free acceleration, FlashAttention optimization

Abstract: High-resolution Diffusion Transformer (DiT) inference contains substantial spatial redundancy, but many spatially adaptive implementations encode regional computation as attention masks, which can inadvertently move scaled dot-product attention (SDPA) away from FlashAttention fast paths. We identify this avoidable systems bottleneck as Mask-Induced Dispatch Tax (MIDT) and show that it grows with latent sequence length. We introduce SAFE-DiT, a training-free Semantics-Aware Fast-path Execution framework that separates exact mask elision from approximation-based spatial scheduling. SAFE-DiT removes only provenance-certified image self-attention masks that induce a row-wise constant shift in attention logits, preserves semantics-bearing masks such as text-padding masks, and realizes spatial adaptation through prompt-conditioned token partitioning, selective state updates with global context, and periodic context refresh. We call this acceleration-only configuration SAFE-Core and report sensitivity-weighted classifier-free guidance separately as SAFE-DiT+SW. On the evaluated PyTorch SDPA stack, redundant masks make long-sequence attention $4.1\times$ to $5.8\times$ slower than the mask-free path. On Lumina-Next, SAFE-DiT achieves $2.69\times$ end-to-end acceleration at $1024^2$ resolution and $5.09\times$ at $2560^2$, reduces peak memory at $2560^2$ from 94.1 to 27.9 GB, and enables $3072^2$ generation when dense inference runs out of memory. Paired metrics, component ablations, and a blinded human study support visual non-inferiority of SAFE-Core to the dense fast-path baseline, while SAFE-DiT+SW provides a separate prompt-alignment operating point without reintroducing spatial self-attention masks. Code is available at .

</details>


### [114] [Simplifying Flow Matching Transformations with Low-Rank Mixture Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29724)
*Liam A. Kruse, Houjun Liu, Alexandros E. Tzikas, Mansur M. Arief, Mykel J. Kochenderfer*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (87.8) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出使用混合概率主成分分析器（MPPCA）作为归一化流的潜分布，通过使潜分布更接近数据分布来简化流变换，从而加速训练收敛并提升生成质量。MPPCA可通过EM算法高效拟合，适用于高维生成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准归一化流通常使用标准正态分布作为潜分布，但由于潜分布与数据分布之间的拓扑不匹配，导致流变换学习不必要的复杂性，训练速度慢且性能次优。本文旨在通过选择更贴近数据分布的潜分布来简化流变换。

Method: 使用混合概率主成分分析器（MPPCA）作为归一化流的潜密度。MPPCA通过期望最大化（EM）算法快速拟合数据分布，学习一个在KL散度上更接近数据分布的潜分布，从而简化所需的流变换。该方法适用于任意归一化流架构。

Result: 在表格数据集和图像数据集上的实验表明，相比标准正态潜分布基线，该方法在训练效率和生成质量上均取得了一致的提升。

Conclusion: MPPCA作为归一化流的潜分布是一种实用且有效的方法，能够通过简化流变换来加速训练并提升生成性能，且EM初始化计算开销低，适合高维生成任务。

Classification reason: 该论文提出使用MPPCA作为归一化流的潜分布，通过简化流变换来加速训练并提升生成质量，属于生成模型效率优化（训练加速与收敛改进），因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化" (efficient_generation)。

Importance reason: Proposes a practical latent distribution initialization for flow matching that could accelerate training and improve generation quality, aligning with model efficiency and continuous multimodal priorities. Signals: flow matching acceleration, low-rank mixture model, training efficiency, generative quality improvement, practical EM initialization

Abstract: Normalizing flows are powerful generative models that learn an invertible mapping between complex data distributions and simple latent distributions, typically a standard normal density. However, this choice of latent density can impose unnecessary complexity on the learned flow transformation due to the topological mismatch between the latent and data densities, leading to slower training and suboptimal performance. In this work, we propose using mixtures of probabilistic principal component analyzers (MPPCA) as the latent density for normalizing flows. We simplify the learned flow transformation by learning a latent distribution that more closely aligns with the data distribution in terms of KL divergence, thus enabling faster convergence and improved generative performance. Critically, MPPCA models can be fit quickly and cheaply using the expectation-maximization algorithm, making them a practical choice for initializing latent distributions even in high-dimensional generative tasks. We validate our method on both tabular and image datasets, demonstrating consistent gains in training efficiency and generation quality compared to baselines.

</details>


### [115] [MUSE: Unlocking Timestep as Native Task Steering for One-Step Dense Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30370)
*Shuo Zhou, Zhaoxin Li, Xiujuan Chai*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 普通相关 (61.48) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现单步扩散模型中固定的正弦时间步嵌入可以天然作为任务引导信号，据此提出MUSE方法，无需额外参数即可用单一模型同时完成深度估计和法线估计等多任务密集预测，在10个数据集上取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多任务扩散方法通常引入参数繁重的适配器、专家模块或可学习任务token，导致计算冗余。作者希望探索一种更简洁高效的多任务学习范式，利用预训练扩散模型本身的内在机制来实现参数无关的多任务统一。

Method: MUSE方法的核心发现是：预训练单步扩散模型中固定的正弦时间步嵌入（sinusoidal timestep embedding）可以天然作为任务引导信号。通过将不同任务（如深度估计、法线估计）映射到不同的固定时间步值，模型在推理时根据输入的时间步嵌入自动将生成过程引导到对应的任务特定流形上。作者将此机制解释为"流形解耦"（Manifold Decoupling）。该方法无需额外参数，兼容U-Net和DiT架构。

Result: 在10个数据集上的实验表明，MUSE在单目深度估计和法线估计任务上取得了极具竞争力的性能，且该方法在U-Net和DiT两种架构上均有效。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种简洁高效的通用视觉模型路径——通过解锁现有生成基础设施中潜藏的潜力（时间步嵌入），无需额外参数即可实现多任务密集预测，为构建通用视觉模型提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出利用预训练扩散模型中固有的时间步嵌入作为任务引导信号，实现单步（one-step）密集预测的多任务统一模型，无需额外参数。核心贡献在于扩散模型的高效化（单步推理）和多任务统一，属于生成模型加速与高效化（efficient_generation）范畴。

Importance reason: Novel discovery of timestep embedding as task steering in one-step diffusion models, enabling parameter-free multi-task dense prediction. Signals: parameter-free multi-task, one-step diffusion, timestep embedding steering, dense prediction, efficient inference

Abstract: Monocular dense prediction has recently seen remarkable success by repurposing pre-trained diffusion models. This opens a promising yet challenging avenue for more efficient multi-task learning paradigm. However, existing multi-task diffusion methods often introduce parameter-heavy adapters, experts, or learnable task tokens, leading to computational redundancy. In this paper, we reveal an inherent mechanism within one-step diffusion models: the native, fixed sinusoidal timestep embedding can be repurposed as an endogenous task steering signal. Based on this discovery, we propose Multi-task Unified eStimation via timestep Embedding (MUSE), a parameter-free, single-model multi-tasking approach for dense prediction. We interpret this mechanism via Manifold Decoupling, where discrete, fixed timestep values deterministically steer the generation process towards decoupled, task-specific manifolds in the latent space. Extensive experiments across 10 datasets demonstrate that MUSE achieves highly competitive performance on both monocular depth and normal estimation, and its efficacy generalizes across U-Net and DiT architectures. Our work offers a concise and efficient path toward generalist vision models by simply unlocking the latent potential of existing generation infrastructure.

</details>


### [116] [EcoVideo: Entropy-Orchestrated Video Generation Paradigm in Cloud-Edge Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30557)
*Jiayu Chen, Hengyi Zhang, Maoliang Li, Minyu Li, Zihao Zheng, Xuanzhe Liu, Guojie Luo, Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (92.6) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: EcoVideo 提出了一种基于熵驱动的动态帧间解耦框架，利用自注意力熵估计帧信息密度，由云端大模型生成稀疏高熵关键帧，边缘轻量模型通过运动感知插值重建其余帧，并自适应调整关键帧预算和边缘细化深度以优化端到端延迟，在低带宽边缘场景下实现最高 2.9 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT 视频生成模型由于迭代式全帧去噪导致延迟极高，现有云边协同方法依赖静态帧间解耦，无法利用帧间相似性且不能适应系统动态变化（如带宽和计算资源波动），亟需一种能动态、自适应地分配计算资源的加速方案。

Method: EcoVideo 利用早期自注意力熵作为帧信息密度的无训练估计，选择高熵帧作为关键帧；云端大模型对稀疏关键帧进行去噪生成；边缘轻量模型通过运动感知插值（结合光流引导）重建非关键帧，并辅以细化模块保证时序稳定性；同时根据实时带宽和计算资源动态调整关键帧预算和边缘细化深度，优化端到端延迟。

Result: 在代表性 DiT 视频生成器上的实验表明，EcoVideo 在质量与效率之间取得了更好的权衡，在低带宽、计算受限的边缘场景下实现了最高 2.9 倍的端到端加速。

Conclusion: EcoVideo 通过熵驱动的动态帧间解耦和云边协同，有效解决了 DiT 视频生成的高延迟问题，验证了利用帧间信息密度差异进行自适应计算分配的有效性，为视频生成在边缘部署提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 EcoVideo，一种面向 DiT 视频生成的云边协同加速框架，通过熵驱动的动态帧间解耦、关键帧选择、边缘轻量模型插值等策略加速视频生成，属于生成模型加速与高效化方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化" (efficient_generation)。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for video generation, a high-priority subtopic, with a novel cloud-edge dynamic decoupling method. Signals: efficient inference, video generation, cloud-edge dynamics, entropy-driven, DiT acceleration

Abstract: DiT video generation is latency-intensive due to iterative full-frame denoising, while prior cloud-edge methods largely rely on static inter-step decoupling and cannot leverage inter-frame similarity or adapt to system dynamics. We propose EcoVideo, an entropy-orchestrated framework for dynamic inter-frame decoupling: early-stage self-attention entropy provides a training-free estimate of frame-wise information density for frame selection; a cloud large model denoises sparse high-entropy keyframes; and an edge lightweight model reconstructs the remaining frames via motion-aware interpolation with refinement for temporal stability. EcoVideo further adapts the keyframe budget and edge refinement depth to real-time bandwidth and compute availability, optimizing end-to-end latency under constraints. Experiments on representative DiT video generators show improved quality--efficiency trade-offs and up to 2.9x end-to-end speedup in low-bandwidth, compute-limited edge settings. Code is available at .

</details>


### [117] [Notes on generative modeling: flow matching, diffusion, optimal transport and Schr{ö}dinger bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30053)
*Titouan Vayer*

Main category: stat.ML, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (83.21) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于生成建模数学原理的综述笔记，系统阐述了flow matching、扩散模型、最优传输和Schrödinger桥之间的联系，展示了最优传输如何统一这些生成建模技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成建模领域有多种看似不同的技术（扩散模型、flow matching、最优传输、Schrödinger桥），它们之间的数学联系尚未被清晰梳理。作者希望通过统一的数学视角揭示这些方法的内在关联，帮助研究者理解生成建模的核心原理。

Method: 采用数学综述的方式，从最优传输理论出发，逐步推导并展示其与扩散模型、flow matching和Schrödinger桥之间的理论联系。通过统一的数学框架（如连续归一化流、概率路径、最优传输映射）来连接这些方法。

Result: 清晰地展示了最优传输理论如何作为统一视角连接扩散模型、flow matching和Schrödinger桥等生成建模技术，阐明了这些方法在数学原理层面的等价性和差异性。

Conclusion: 最优传输为理解现代生成建模技术提供了统一的数学框架，flow matching、扩散模型和Schrödinger桥都可以从最优传输的角度得到解释和统一。

Classification reason: 该论文综述了flow matching、扩散模型、最优传输和Schrödinger桥等生成建模技术的数学原理，属于生成模型的基础方法论。虽然不直接涉及加速，但flow matching本身是扩散加速和高效采样的核心方法之一，与efficient_generation（生成模型加速与高效化）主题紧密相关。同时论文也涉及连续潜空间中的flow matching，与continuous_language_multimodal方向下的continuous_language_flow也有联系。

Importance reason: Provides a mathematical unification of flow matching and diffusion, which is foundational for continuous language and multimodal models. Signals: Flow matching theory, Diffusion model foundations, Optimal transport connections, Schrödinger bridge, Generative modeling survey

Abstract: These notes recapitulate the high level mathematical principles behind different techniques for generative modeling. I show the connections between optimal transport and standard techniques such as Schr{ö}dinger bridge and flow matching.

</details>
