### [118] [DiffRGD: An Inference-Time Diffusion Guidance Through Riemannian Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28417)
*Jia-Wei Liao, Li-Xuan Peng, Mei-Heng Yueh, Min Sun, Cheng-Fu Chou, Jun-Cheng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 值得关注 (69.96) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DiffRGD 提出一种分布感知的推理时扩散引导框架，通过黎曼梯度下降在球面流形上求解约束优化问题，在保持潜变量高斯分布的同时实现条件生成控制，无需重新训练即可集成到预训练扩散模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时引导方法（如 classifier-free guidance、classifier guidance）在控制生成时会导致潜变量分布偏移，破坏原始高斯分布，从而降低生成质量。作者希望在不重新训练的前提下，实现既能有效控制生成又能保持潜变量高斯分布特性的引导方法。

Method: DiffRGD 将每个采样步建模为球面流形上的约束优化问题：在保持潜变量位于高斯分布诱导的球面流形上的前提下，最小化引导目标函数。通过黎曼梯度下降（Riemannian Gradient Descent, RGD）高效求解该优化问题，将梯度投影到切空间并沿流形更新，确保更新后的潜变量仍服从原始高斯分布。该方法即插即用，可无缝集成到任意预训练扩散模型中。

Result: 在图像恢复（超分辨率、去噪、修复）和条件生成任务上，DiffRGD 在大多数任务中优于现有推理时引导方法（如 DPS、MPGD、FreeDoM 等），在保持生成质量的同时实现了更好的条件控制效果。

Conclusion: DiffRGD 通过分布感知的黎曼梯度下降框架，有效解决了推理时引导方法中的分布漂移问题，在保持生成质量的同时实现了灵活的条件控制，是一种通用且有效的即插即用引导方法。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DiffRGD，一种推理时扩散引导方法，通过黎曼梯度下降在球面流形上保持潜空间高斯分布，实现条件生成控制。这属于扩散模型的 inference-time guidance 方法，与 canonical subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）中的 guidance 与可控性方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel inference-time guidance method for diffusion models, but focuses on image restoration and conditional generation, not the user's priority subtopics. Signals: Inference-time guidance, Riemannian gradient descent, Distribution-aware, Plug-and-play, Image restoration

Abstract: Recently, diffusion models have been widely adopted in generative modeling and have served as foundational models for many image generation tasks. To control the generation without costly re-training or fine-tuning, many works seek inference-time guidance methods to steer the latent via a differentiable objective at inference time. However, these methods cannot effectively preserve the original Gaussian distribution because they introduce distributional drift, thereby degrading the sample quality. To address this gap, we propose DiffRGD, a distribution-aware guidance framework that explicitly preserves the latent Gaussian structure. DiffRGD formulates each sampling step as a constrained optimization problem on a spherical manifold induced by the latent Gaussian distribution, and solves it efficiently via Riemannian Gradient Descent (RGD). DiffRGD is a plug-and-play method that can be seamlessly integrated into any pre-trained diffusion model. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DiffRGD outperforms previous methods in most image restoration and conditional generation tasks. Our codebase is available at .

</details>


### [119] [Obliviate: Erasing Concepts from Autoregressive Image Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28643)
*Hossein Shakibania, Jonas Henry Grebe, Tobias Braun, Ege Aktemur, Saleh Aslani, Mehmet Görkem Yiğit, Marcus Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 高优先级 (85.83) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Obliviate 是一种基于引导的概念擦除方法，专门针对自回归图像生成模型，通过 KL 散度监督视觉 token 分布、轨迹级更新和对齐视觉前缀，有效移除显式内容、暴力和品牌图像，同时保持模型生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式 AI 模型被广泛使用，但存在生成不安全或令人不适内容的隐患。现有概念擦除方法主要针对扩散模型，而自回归图像生成模型（在统一多模态架构中日益重要）的概念擦除尚未被充分探索。

Method: Obliviate 基于三个关键设计：(1) KL 散度监督视觉 token 分布，引导模型远离目标概念；(2) 轨迹级更新，在完整的自回归 rollout 上进行优化而非单步；(3) 对齐视觉前缀，构建稳定的目标分布。该方法无需修改模型权重，通过引导实现概念擦除。

Result: 在 Liquid、Emu3-Gen 和 Janus-Pro 三个自回归文生图模型上评估，覆盖显式内容、暴力和品牌图像擦除。在 RAB 基准上，将裸体内容从 91.58 降至 3.15，显著优于现有方法，同时保持模型整体生成能力。

Conclusion: Obliviate 有效填补了自回归图像生成模型概念擦除的空白，为统一多模态模型的安全对齐提供了可行方案，且不牺牲模型效用。

Classification reason: 该论文提出概念擦除方法，属于生成模型的安全对齐与可控性范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）高度匹配。同时该方法应用于自回归图像生成模型，也涉及多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: Addresses concept erasure for autoregressive image generation, a niche but growing area, with solid technical contributions. Signals: Autoregressive image generation, concept erasure, guidance-based method, KL divergence supervision, trajectory-level updates

Abstract: The widespread adoption of generative AI models has intensified concerns about misuse, including the creation of unsafe or disturbing imagery. To mitigate such issues, several concept erasure approaches have been proposed to remove harmful content from multimodal generative models. Yet concept erasure for autoregressive image generation remains largely unexplored, despite the growing relevance of these models in recent trends toward unified multimodal architectures. In this work, we fill this gap by introducing Obliviate, a guidance-based concept erasure method for autoregressive image generation. Our method builds on three key design choices: KL-based supervision over visual token distributions, trajectory-level updates over full autoregressive rollouts, and aligned visual prefixes for stable target construction. We evaluate Obliviate on three state-of-the-art autoregressive text-to-image models, Liquid, Emu3-Gen, and Janus-Pro, covering the erasure of explicit content, graphic violence, and branded imagery. Obliviate consistently outperforms current alternatives, reducing nudity on the defensive RAB benchmark from 91.58 to 3.15 while preserving overall model utility.

</details>


### [120] [TrafficAlign: Aligning Large Language Models for Traffic Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29097)
*Zhi Tu, Liangkun Niu, Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 普通相关 (46.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TrafficAlign 是一个自动化框架，利用真实驾驶视频合成交通场景并对齐LLM，使生成的场景更符合真实交通分布，在自动驾驶测试中比SOTA方法多发现10.8%的碰撞，并用于微调驾驶模型降低36.1%的碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的LLM在生成交通场景时往往无法与真实世界的交通分布对齐，导致生成的场景不够真实有效，难以用于自动驾驶模型的鲁棒性测试和训练。

Method: 提出TrafficAlign框架，包含三个步骤：(1) 基于真实驾驶视频自动合成交通场景；(2) 进行数据验证确保场景合理性；(3) 利用合成场景对LLM进行对齐微调，使LLM生成的场景更贴近真实交通分布。

Result: TrafficAlign生成的场景在三个自动驾驶模型上平均比SOTA方法多发现10.8%的碰撞；用其生成场景微调驾驶模型后，碰撞率降低36.1%；在六个地理区域的交通数据集上验证了生成场景与区域交通分布的良好对齐。

Conclusion: TrafficAlign能够有效对齐LLM生成真实交通场景，既可用于评估自动驾驶模型的鲁棒性，也可用于生成训练数据提升模型安全性。

Classification reason: 该论文关注使用LLM生成交通场景时与真实世界分布的对齐问题，属于生成模型的对齐（alignment）范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）。

Importance reason: Focuses on traffic scenario generation for autonomous driving, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and includes a negative keyword (traffic prediction). Signals: Traffic scenario generation, LLM alignment, autonomous driving, collision detection, negative keyword match

Abstract: Recent research has investigated the use of large language models (LLMs) to generate traffic scenarios for autonomous driving. However, pretrained LLMs often fail to align with real-world traffic distributions. In this work, we present TrafficAlign, an automated framework that synthesizes traffic scenarios based on real-world driving videos, performs data validation, and aligns LLMs with the synthesized scenarios. The evaluation shows that traffic scenarios generated by TrafficAlign are highly effective, revealing up to 10.8% more collisions on average across three autonomous driving models than state-of-the-art methods. Furthermore, fine-tuning these driving models with TrafficAlign-generated scenarios significantly reduced collision rates by 36.1% compared with the original models. A qualitative study using traffic datasets from six geographically diverse regions shows that TrafficAlign-generated scenarios exhibit strong alignment with corresponding traffic distributions in these regions.

</details>


### [121] [ScaleErasure: Inference-Time Minimal Intervention for Precise Concept Erasure in Next-Scale Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29282)
*Cong Wang, Haiyu Wu, Zhiwei Jiang, Zifeng Cheng, Fei Shen, Yafeng Yin, Qing Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 高优先级 (87.5) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ScaleErasure 是一种针对 next-scale 自回归图像生成模型的推理时概念擦除方法，通过最小干预地选择并引导与不安全概念最相关的 logits，在严重语义纠缠下实现精确擦除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Next-scale 自回归图像生成范式中，语义信息在早期尺度高度压缩，导致不安全语义与无关语义严重纠缠，现有概念擦除方法难以有效处理。

Method: ScaleErasure 在推理时执行两次额外前向传播（分别以不安全概念和对应安全概念为条件），利用其输出来引导目标 logits 远离不安全概念。通过尺度、token 和比特通道三个维度进行 logits 选择和引导，实现精确且最小干预的擦除。

Result: 实验表明，ScaleErasure 在 next-scale AR 范式下优于适配的基线方法，实现了更精确的概念擦除，同时较好地保持了通用生成能力。

Conclusion: ScaleErasure 为 next-scale 自回归图像生成提供了有效的推理时概念擦除方案，在安全性与生成质量之间取得了良好平衡。

Classification reason: 该论文研究概念擦除（concept erasure），属于生成模型的安全对齐与可控性问题，与 canonical 子主题「生成模型对齐与奖励」中的对齐、可控性高度相关。

Importance reason: Directly addresses autoregressive image generation with a novel inference-time concept erasure method, aligning with high-priority subtopics. Signals: autoregressive image generation, concept erasure, inference-time intervention, next-scale prediction, semantic entanglement

Abstract: Concept erasure aims to prevent image generative models from producing unsafe content while preserving their general generative capability. Meanwhile, next-scale autoregressive (AR) image generation has recently emerged as a new generative paradigm characterized by next-scale prediction, for which concept erasure remains largely unexplored. In this paradigm, semantic information is highly compressed at early scales, leading to severe entanglement between unsafe and unrelated semantics. In this paper, we propose ScaleErasure, an inference-time concept erasure method that performs minimal intervention. ScaleErasure precisely selects and guides predicted logits that are most relevant to the unsafe concept, thereby enabling effective erasure under severe semantic entanglement. Specifically, ScaleErasure performs two additional forward passes conditioned on the unsafe concept and the corresponding safe concept, and leverages their outputs to guide the target logits away from unsafe concepts toward safe concepts. To enable precise and minimal intervention, logits selection and guidance are conducted across three dimensions: scales, tokens, and bit channels. Experiments demonstrate that ScaleErasure outperforms adapted baselines in the next-scale AR paradigm, achieving more precise concept erasure while largely preserving general generative capability. The code is available at .

</details>


### [122] [FlowAWR: Online Adaptive Flow Reinforcement via Advantage-Weighted Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30376)
*Zheming Fu, Ruizhe He, Wei Shang, Xiaoxiao Ma, Lei Wang, Chang Liu, Siming Fu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 值得关注 (74.15) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FlowAWR 提出了一种新的连续生成流模型在线对齐范式，将 KL 约束奖励最大化下的最优策略推导为优势加权校正形式的最优速度场，实现了无需 SDE 采样器训练、无需 CFG 推理的生成模型对齐，在 SD3.5-Medium 上相比 DiffusionNFT 取得 2-5 倍收敛加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于连续空间的生成流模型在线强化学习对齐方法受限于不可解的轨迹似然，依赖随机 SDE 采样器构造可解转移核，导致训练-推理不一致且需要分类器无关引导（CFG）。隐式框架如 DiffusionNFT 直接优化前向过程速度场，但其启发式的固定幅度校正无法根据组内相对质量调整优化强度。因此需要一种无需 SDE、无需 CFG 且能感知优势幅度的对齐方法。

Method: FlowAWR 从 KL 约束奖励最大化的最优策略出发，推导出最优速度场具有优势加权校正（advantage-weighted rectification）形式。具体地，将连续生成策略优化重新表述为对理论最优速度场的监督回归，校正幅度由优势函数（advantage）加权，从而实现 SDE-free 优化和 CFG-free 生成。该方法避免了构造可解转移核的需要，直接对速度场进行回归学习。

Result: 在 SD3.5-Medium 上的对比评估中，FlowAWR 实现了更好的对齐性能，同时相比 DiffusionNFT 获得 2-5 倍收敛加速（例如 1.2k 步达到 24.12 PickScore，而 DiffusionNFT 需 2.0k 步达到 23.82，FlowGRPO 需超过 4k 步达到 23.50）。在多奖励约束下，FlowAWR 能保持生成质量，满足结构规则的同时维持稳定的域外性能。

Conclusion: FlowAWR 为连续生成流模型的在线对齐提供了一种理论上更优、实践上更高效的范式，消除了 SDE 训练和 CFG 推理的需求，同时实现了更快的收敛和更好的对齐效果，为扩散/流模型的强化学习对齐开辟了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 FlowAWR，通过优势加权校正（advantage-weighted rectification）对连续生成流模型进行在线强化学习对齐，属于生成模型对齐与奖励优化范畴，与 canonical subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language/multimodal model alignment with a novel advantage-weighted rectification method, matching top priority subtopic. Signals: continuous generative flow alignment, advantage-weighted rectification, SDE-free optimization, CFG-free generation, convergence acceleration

Abstract: Aligning generative flow models on continuous spaces via online reinforcement learning is constrained by intractable trajectory likelihoods. Existing density-approximated policy gradient methods rely on stochastic SDE samplers to construct tractable transition kernels, which introduce training-inference inconsistencies and necessitates Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG). While implicit frameworks such as DiffusionNFT directly optimize forward-process velocity fields, its heuristic fixed-magnitude corrections prevent optimization strength from relative intra-group quality. We propose \textit{Flow Advantage-Weighted Rectification} (\textbf{FlowAWR}), a paradigm that recasts continuous generative policy optimization as supervised regression toward a theoretically optimal velocity field. Starting from the optimal policy of a KL-constrained reward maximization, FlowAWR derives the optimal velocity field that admits a magnitude-aware, advantage-weighted rectification form, yielding SDE-free optimization and CFG-free generation. In comparative evaluations on SD3.5-Medium, FlowAWR achieves improved alignment performance alongside a 2$\times$ to 5$\times$ convergence acceleration over DiffusionNFT (e.g., reaching a 24.12 PickScore in 1.2k steps, versus 23.82 in 2.0k steps for DiffusionNFT and 23.50 in $>$4k steps for FlowGRPO). Under multi-reward constraints, FlowAWR sustains generation quality, satisfying structural rules while maintaining stable out-of-domain performance.

</details>


### [123] [Diffusion Fine-tuning with Rewarded Moment Matching Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30414)
*Alexis Jacq, Guillaume Couairon, Valentin De Bortoli, Quentin Berthet, Arnaud Doucet, Romuald Elie*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 普通相关 (54.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Rewarded Moment Matching Distillation (RMMD)，一种同时进行扩散模型蒸馏和奖励最大化的统一框架。RMMD 通过将采样循环改造为在线训练、将蒸馏损失重新解释为积分 KL 正则化，在保持 Moment Matching 蒸馏高质量的同时优化奖励函数。在 ImageNet 上实现了优于 DI++ 和 DRaFT 等方法的 FID-Reward Pareto 前沿，并在天气预测模型 GenCast 上取得 7.5 倍加速且超越教师模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的后训练通常分为蒸馏（加速采样）和强化学习微调（优化奖励）两个独立阶段，但两者之间的相互作用尚不明确，尤其是微调如何影响蒸馏模型的生成质量。现有方法要么是单步蒸馏（如 DI++）要么是多步 RL 微调（如 DRaFT），缺乏一个统一的框架来同时实现高效采样和奖励优化。

Method: RMMD 基于 Moment Matching Distillation 框架，将蒸馏损失重新用作积分 KL 正则化项，同时引入奖励最大化目标。具体地，将采样循环改造为在线策略训练（on-policy），使模型在蒸馏过程中也能根据奖励信号进行优化。通过调整蒸馏损失和奖励项之间的平衡，在 FID 和奖励之间实现 Pareto 最优权衡。

Result: 在 ImageNet 上，RMMD 在 FID-Reward Pareto 前沿上优于单步基线 DI++ 和多步竞争者 DRaFT、HyperNoise。在 GenCast 天气预测模型上，蒸馏后的模型实现 7.5 倍加速，在 93% 的目标天气变量上超越教师模型，且校准性更好。

Conclusion: RMMD 证明了蒸馏和奖励优化可以统一在一个框架中，且能取得比单独进行任一阶段更好的效果。该方法可扩展到复杂的高维科学领域（如天气预测），为扩散模型的后训练提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 RMMD 框架，在扩散模型蒸馏的同时最大化奖励函数，属于生成模型的后训练对齐与奖励优化。映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励" (generation_alignment)，该 subtopic 涵盖 RLHF、奖励建模、偏好优化等，与本文高度吻合。

Importance reason: Novel unified distillation+reward framework for diffusion models, but focuses on general alignment rather than user's priority subtopics. Signals: diffusion model distillation, reward maximization, Pareto frontier, GenCast weather model, 7.5x speedup

Abstract: Distillation and Reinforcement Learning (RL) fine-tuning are the primary pillars of diffusion post-training. While traditionally studied in isolation, the interaction between these phases remains poorly understood, and in particular how fine-tuning impacts the generative quality of distilled models. We introduce Rewarded Moment Matching Distillation (RMMD), a novel framework that simultaneously distills diffusion models and maximizes a reward function. RMMD preserves the high-fidelity ``naturalness'' characteristic of advanced distillation (such as 8-step Moment Matching) by adapting the sampling loop for on-policy training and repurposing the distillation loss as a proxy for integral KL regularization. By evaluating the FID-Reward Pareto fronts on ImageNet, we demonstrate that RMMD achieves superior trade-offs compared to single-step baselines (DI++) and multi-step competitors (DRaFT, HyperNoise). Finally, we apply RMMD to GenCast, a state-of-the-art weather forecasting model, to distill it while optimizing the Continuous Ranked Probability Score (CRPS) metric. The resulting distilled model achieves a 7.5x speedup while outperforming the teacher model on 93% of target weather variables, and being better calibrated. This proves that RMMD scales to complex, high-dimensional scientific domains.

</details>


### [124] [Your Data Manifold is Secretly a Reward Model: Shell-LCC for Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30248)
*Shihao Zhang, Yuguang Yan, Junzhe Zhang, Wei Zhao, Bohan Wang, Hanwang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 高优先级 (83.6) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Shell-LCC 方法，通过显式建模高质量 SFT 数据的流形结构，为文生视频扩散模型提供密集、可微且几乎零成本的奖励信号，以改善生成视频的细节质量和真实感，避免传统奖励模型的高计算开销和人工标注成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生视频扩散模型依赖辅助奖励信号（如奖励模型或 DPO）来对齐人类偏好，但这些方法计算开销大、需要昂贵的人工标注，且对细粒度局部细节的改善有限。作者希望利用数据本身的流形结构作为隐式奖励，实现低成本、高质量的对齐。

Method: 基于 Local Coordinate Coding (LCC) 建模数据流形的"骨架"，但发现直接使用 LCC 会导致均值回归（向几何中心收缩，丢失高频细节）。为此提出 Shell-LCC，将流形"表面"建模为各向同性壳层（isotropic shell），使其对齐真实的高密度区域，从而提供更准确的奖励信号来引导视频潜变量优化。

Result: 实验表明，Shell-LCC 方法提升了生成视频的真实感，增强了高频细节，减少了过平滑伪影，并缓解了运动模糊问题。

Conclusion: 数据流形本身可以作为一种隐式奖励模型，Shell-LCC 通过更精确的流形表面建模，在不引入额外计算开销和人工标注的情况下，有效提升了文生视频的生成质量。

Classification reason: 该论文提出利用数据流形结构作为隐式奖励信号来对齐文生视频扩散模型，属于生成模型对齐与奖励建模范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel Shell-LCC method for dense, zero-cost reward signals directly addresses key priority of improving video generation quality and realism. Signals: text-to-video generation, diffusion model alignment, reward model, manifold learning, local coordinate coding

Abstract: Recent text-to-video (T2V) diffusion models rely heavily on auxiliary reward signals (e.g., via reward models or DPO) to align generated content with human aesthetics and improve realism. These signals, however, incur substantial computational overhead, require costly human annotations, and often yield limited improvement in fine-grained local details. In this paper, we argue that your data manifold is secretly a reward model. By explicitly modeling the manifold structure of high-quality Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) data and encouraging video latents to lie on this manifold, we derive dense, differentiable, and nearly cost-free reward signals that significantly improve video quality, particularly in mitigating low-level distortions. Our modeling builds upon Local Coordinate Coding (LCC), which captures the `skeleton' of the manifold. However, directly applying LCC suffers from mean regression, pulling latents toward the geometric mean and losing high-frequency details. We therefore extend it to Shell Local Coordinate Coding (Shell-LCC), which models the manifold `surface' as an isotropic shell to align with the true high-density region. Experiments demonstrate that our approach improves realism, enhances high-frequency details, reduces over-smoothing artifacts, and alleviates motion blur.

</details>


### [125] [Intermediate Text Representation Guided Text-to-Image Generation for Enhancing One-and-Only Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30262)
*Soyoun Won, Aryan Yazdan Parast, Basim Azam, Jean Honorio, Naveed Akhtar*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 高优先级 (81.63) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现文生图扩散模型对"唯一性物体"（如天体、地标、艺术品）存在概念关联偏差，导致难以通过提示词修改其固有视觉特征。通过信息论分析发现最终文本嵌入丢弃了中间层表示中的概念级信息，提出无需训练的中间文本表示引导扩散方法（IR-guided diffusion），在早期去噪步骤注入文本编码器的中间隐藏状态以恢复被抑制的概念，在OAO-AttackBench等基准上VQAScore提升最高19.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图扩散模型对"唯一性物体"（如月亮、蒙娜丽莎、埃菲尔铁塔）存在强烈的概念关联偏差，即模型倾向于生成这些物体的标准视觉形态，难以通过提示词改变其外观（如"蓝色月亮"）。现有方法需要额外训练或外部模型，缺乏轻量级解决方案。

Method: 通过信息论分析发现文本编码器最终层嵌入丢弃了中间层表示中的概念级信息，导致条件信号与生成过程的互信息降低。提出中间文本表示引导扩散（IR-guided diffusion），在早期去噪步骤中将文本编码器中间隐藏状态注入到条件信号中，无需额外训练、优化或外部模型。同时构建OAO-AttackBench基准，包含与唯一性物体核心视觉特征直接冲突的反事实提示。

Result: 在四个基准上评估，包括OAO-AttackBench，VQAScore提升最高19.1个百分点，同时保持生成保真度和人类偏好。消融实验验证了中间层注入的有效性和早期去噪步骤注入的重要性。

Conclusion: 中间文本表示引导扩散是一种简单有效的零样本方法，能够缓解文生图模型对唯一性物体的概念关联偏差，提升文本-图像对齐的忠实度，且无需额外训练或外部模型。

Classification reason: 该论文解决文生图扩散模型中文本描述与生成结果的对齐问题（one-and-only objects的忠实渲染），属于生成模型对齐与可控性范畴，映射到 canonical subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"。

Importance reason: Addresses concept association bias in text-to-image generation with a training-free intermediate representation injection method, relevant to multimodal generation alignment. Signals: training-free method, intermediate text representation, concept association bias, OAO objects, VQAScore improvement

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models often fail to faithfully render explicit textual descriptions, instead defaulting to strongly learned visual priors due to a phenomenon referred to as concept association bias. We show that such bias is particularly strong for one-and-only (OAO) objects, entities that exist in a single canonical form, such as celestial bodies, landmarks, and artworks. The deeply ingrained visual identity for these concepts often resists modification through prompting alone. Addressing this challenge, we first identify through an information-theoretic analysis that the final text embedding discards concept-level information present in the intermediate-layer text representations, reducing the mutual information available to the subsequent denoising process. We then propose Intermediate Text Representation (IR)-guided diffusion, which injects intermediate hidden states of the text encoder into the conditioning signal during early denoising steps, recovering suppressed concepts without any additional training, optimization, or external models. To systematically evaluate the challenging task of aligning generative outputs with unusual prompts for OAO objects, we introduce OAO-AttackBench, a benchmark comprising counterfactual prompts that directly conflict with the core visual identity of OAO objects. Experiments on four benchmarks, including OAO-AttackBench, show that our method achieves up to a 19.1 percentage-point improvement in VQAScore while preserving generation fidelity and human preference. Project page: .

</details>


### [126] [Rethinking Forgery Attacks on Semantic Watermarks in Black-Box Settings: A Geometric Distortion Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29807)
*Cheng-Yi Lee, Yichi Zhang, Yuchen Yang, Chun-Shien Lu, Jun-Cheng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 普通相关 (47.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文从率失真理论角度重新审视黑盒环境下对语义水印的伪造攻击，揭示了代理模型与目标模型之间的结构失配导致不可约的失真下限，并将该失真刻画为潜流形上的几何偏差（全局漂移与局部形变）。基于此，提出了一种与具体方案无关的检测方法，在验证水印前区分伪造样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义水印伪造攻击方法主要依赖经验证据，缺乏对攻击成功或失败条件的严格理论理解。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，从率失真角度分析黑盒伪造攻击的本质，并基于理论洞察设计更有效的检测方法。

Method: 1) 从率失真理论出发，分析代理模型与目标模型之间的结构失配，证明存在不可约的失真下限。2) 将潜空间中的失真刻画为几何偏差（全局漂移+局部形变），而非随机噪声。3) 基于几何偏差特征，提出一种与具体水印方案无关的检测方法，在验证水印前区分伪造样本。

Result: 在多种黑盒场景下的实验表明，所提出的检测方法能有效区分伪造样本，同时对常见失真保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文为语义水印的黑盒伪造攻击提供了严格的理论分析框架，揭示了失真的几何本质，并据此设计了有效的检测方法，为生成模型的安全防护提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究语义水印（semantic watermark）在黑盒环境下的伪造攻击与检测，属于生成模型的安全与可控性范畴。虽然论文侧重攻击与检测而非对齐，但"生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）是现有最相关的[CANONICAL]子主题，涵盖生成模型的安全、可控性与鲁棒性。该子主题的keywords包含guidance与可控性，与本文的水印安全主题一致。

Importance reason: Focuses on watermark security, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Semantic watermark security, Black-box forgery analysis, Rate-distortion theory, Geometric distortion detection, Not aligned with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that semantic watermarks, which embed information into the initial noise of latent diffusion models (LDMs), are vulnerable to black-box forgery attacks. However, existing methods primarily rely on empirical evidence and lack a rigorous theoretical understanding of the conditions under which such attacks succeed or fail. To bridge this gap, we rethink the nature of such attacks through the lens of rate-distortion in the latent space. Our analysis identifies an irreducible distortion floor due to structural mismatches between proxy and target models, which fundamentally limits the fidelity of forged watermarks. We further characterize this distortion as structured geometric deviations on the latent manifold, in the form of global drift and local deformation rather than stochastic noise. Leveraging these insights, we propose a scheme-agnostic detection method that distinguishes forged samples before watermark verification. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method across diverse black-box scenarios, while preserving robustness to common distortions.

</details>

### [127] [Data Provenance for Image Auto-Regressive Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28386)
*Bihe Zhao, Louis Kerner, Michel Meintz, Tameem Bakr, Franziska Boenisch, Adam Dziedzic*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 值得关注 (75.45) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种后验数据溯源框架，用于检测自回归图像生成模型（IAR）输出中的特征模式，从而在不修改生成过程或输出的情况下追溯图像来源模型，适用于无法使用水印的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自回归图像生成模型（IAR）的广泛应用，需要可靠的数据溯源方法来追踪生成图像的来源模型，以防止虚假信息传播、检测欺诈和归因有害内容。现有水印方法需要修改生成过程或输出，不适用于已发布的未标记内容。

Method: 提出一种后验（post-hoc）框架，利用IAR生成过程中引入的输出特征模式作为溯源信号，无需修改生成过程或输出即可检测这些模式，实现来源模型追溯。

Result: 在多种IAR模型上验证了该方法的有效性，展示了其在自回归图像生成中实现鲁棒数据溯源的高潜力。

Conclusion: IAR生成图像虽视觉上与真实图像难以区分，但其生成过程会留下可检测的特征模式，利用这些模式可实现有效的后验数据溯源，弥补了传统水印方法在已发布内容上的应用空白。

Classification reason: 该论文研究自回归图像生成模型（IAR）的数据溯源（data provenance）问题，提出一种后验框架检测IAR生成图像中的特征模式以追溯来源模型。这属于生成模型的可信度与溯源评测范畴，与[generation_benchmark]（生成模型评测与数据集）高度相关，因此复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Addresses data provenance for autoregressive image generation, but lacks direct focus on user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Autoregressive image generation, Data provenance, Post-hoc detection, No watermark needed, cs.CV

Abstract: Image autoregressive models (IARs) have recently demonstrated remarkable capabilities in visual content generation, achieving photorealistic quality and rapid synthesis through the next-token prediction paradigm adapted from large language models. As these models become widely accessible, robust data provenance is required to reliably trace IAR-generated images to the source model that synthesized them. This is critical to prevent the spread of misinformation, detect fraud, and attribute harmful content. We find that although IAR-generated images often appear visually identical to real images, their generation process introduces characteristic patterns in their outputs, which serves as a reliable provenance signal for the generated images. Leveraging this, we present a post-hoc framework that enables the robust detection of such patterns for provenance tracing. Notably, our framework does not require modifications of the generative process or outputs. Thereby, it is applicable in contexts where prior watermarking methods cannot be used, such as for generated content that is already published without additional marks and for models that do not integrate watermarking. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach across a wide range of IARs, highlighting its high potential for robust data provenance tracing in autoregressive image generation.

</details>


### [128] [RadarTwin: Scene-Specific mmWave Radar Simulation and Learning for Mobile Indoor Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28396)
*Emily Bejerano, Federico Tondolo, Devang Gupta, Aaron Mano Cherian, Taeyoo Kim, Ayaan Qayyum, Xiaofan Yu, Xiaofan Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (51.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: RadarTwin 是一个场景特定的毫米波雷达仿真框架，利用 3D 重建、视觉语言模型推断材料属性、物理光线追踪合成 FMCW 雷达数据，并构建配对真实-仿真数据集，证明仿真数据可有效迁移到真实雷达感知任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达感知受限于数据稀缺：现有雷达数据集训练的模型难以泛化到新物体、新环境和新传感轨迹。作者希望能在真实数据采集之前，为特定部署场景生成雷达训练数据。

Method: 给定目标空间的 3D 重建（手机 LiDAR、机器人传感或 RGB 转 3D），RadarTwin 使用视觉语言模型推断雷达相关的表面材料属性，再通过基于物理的光线追踪器合成带多次反射传播的 FMCW 雷达测量数据。同时收集了配对真实-仿真数据集，涵盖家庭物体、材料类别、距离、旋转、平移和移动传感轨迹。

Result: 仿真与真实雷达共享相同的物体判别性形状和材料特征；建模环境多径对匹配真实测量至关重要。仅用仿真数据训练的表示在无真实雷达标签下识别真实物体的准确率是随机水平的 2.5 倍，加入少量标注样本后 12 类识别任务准确率达 95.3%。

Conclusion: RadarTwin 能够在采集任何真实雷达数据之前，为新的空间训练雷达感知模型，有效缓解了毫米波雷达的数据稀缺问题。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向毫米波雷达的仿真数据生成框架（RadarTwin），并构建了配对真实-仿真数据集用于评测仿真到真实的迁移能力。核心贡献在于生成仿真训练数据并评测其在下游感知任务中的有效性，属于生成模型评测与数据集范畴，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on radar simulation for mobile perception, which is outside the user's core interests in continuous language/multimodal models, video/image generation, and world models. Signals: mmWave radar simulation, scene-specific synthetic data, vision-language model for material inference, physics-based ray tracing, real-to-sim transfer for object recognition

Abstract: Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar perception is limited by data scarcity: models trained on existing radar datasets fail to generalize to new objects, environments, and sensing trajectories. We present RadarTwin, a framework for generating deployment-specific radar training data before real data collection. Given a 3D reconstruction of a target space (phone LiDAR, robot-mounted sensing, or RGB-to-3D), RadarTwin uses a vision-language model to infer radar-relevant surface materials and a physics-based ray tracer to synthesize raw frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radar measurements with multi-bounce propagation. To study what transfers from simulation to reality, we collect a paired real-simulated dataset spanning household objects, material classes, distances, rotations, translations, and mobile sensing trajectories. We show that simulated and real radar share the same object-discriminative shape and material features, and that modeling the environment's multipath is essential to matching real measurements. A representation trained on simulation alone recognizes real objects at 2.5 times chance with no real radar labels, and a few labeled examples raise this to 95.3% on a 12-way recognition task. RadarTwin enables training radar perception for a new space before any real radar data is collected there.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出 EffectivePresentationScorer，一个评估科学论文讲解视频教学质量的框架，关注视频是否清晰解释核心思想、引入必要背景知识、将技术细节与论文主要贡献关联。实验发现现有论文转视频生成系统虽然内容覆盖正确，但缺乏对前置概念的解释和方法原理的阐明，而现有评测指标无法捕捉这些不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有论文转视频生成系统的评测主要关注视觉质量和关键点覆盖，但忽略了视频是否真正帮助观众理解论文思想。缺乏衡量教学/解释质量的评估方法。

Method: 提出 EffectivePresentationScorer 框架，从三个维度评估视频的教学质量：1）是否清晰解释主要思想；2）是否引入必要的背景概念；3）是否将技术细节与论文主要贡献关联起来。该框架可应用于评估现有论文转视频生成系统的输出。

Result: 现有论文转视频生成系统生成的视频虽然提及了正确的主题并遵循论文结构，但未能解释前置概念或阐明方法为何有效。这些缺陷被现有视频评测指标所忽略，因为现有指标只关注内容存在性而非解释质量。

Conclusion: 论文转视频生成系统需要更好的评估指标来捕捉教学/解释质量，而不仅仅是内容覆盖。EffectivePresentationScorer 填补了这一空白，为未来生成系统的改进提供了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 EffectivePresentationScorer，用于评估论文到视频生成系统的教学/解释质量，属于生成模型（视频生成）的评测方法。这与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）高度匹配，该 subtopic 涵盖生成模型评测基准、自动评测指标等。同时该工作也涉及多模态理解与生成方向（视频内容理解与评估），因此也匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on evaluation of paper-to-video talks, not on core generative model advances. Signals: evaluation metric, instructional quality, not generative model, not priority subtopic, no boost keywords

Abstract: Automatically generated videos from scientific papers are increasingly used for education and research dissemination. However, existing evaluation metrics mainly measure visual quality or whether key points from the paper appear in the video without assessing whether the video actually helps viewers understand the ideas. We introduce EffectivePresentationScorer, a framework for evaluating the instructional quality of scientific presentation videos. It checks whether a video explains the main ideas clearly, introduces needed background concepts, and connects technical details to the main contribution of the paper. When we apply EffectivePresentationScorer to the existing paper-to-video generation systems, we find that generated videos mention the correct topics and follow the structure of the paper but fail to explain prerequisite concepts or clarify why the method works. These failures are often ignored by existing video evaluation metrics, which focus on content presence rather than explanatory quality.

</details>


### [130] [Can AI Draw Science? A Benchmark for Evaluating Scientific Figure Generation by Text-to-Image and Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28406)
*Davie Chen*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

TL;DR: 本文提出 SciDraw-Bench，一个包含32个科学图表生成任务的评测基准，覆盖8种图表类型和10个学科，从文本保真度、语义正确性、结构质量和惯例遵循四个维度评估文生图和多模态模型生成科学图表的能力。实验表明领域专用系统 SciDraw AI 在所有维度上显著优于通用文生图模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成评测基准（如 GenEval、T2I-CompBench、DPG-Bench）仅评估自然图像的组合性、物体计数或逼真度，无法衡量科学图表生成的关键要素：正确可读的文本标签、实体及其关系的忠实描绘、连贯的图表结构以及学科绘图惯例的遵循。因此需要专门针对科学图表生成的评测基准。

Method: 构建 SciDraw-Bench 基准，包含32个结构化科学图表生成任务，覆盖8种图表类型（机制图、实验设计示意图、概念框架图、图形摘要等）和10个学科。每个任务配有自然语言提示和机器可检查的规格说明（标签、关系、组件、惯例、负面约束）。提出四维评估协议：文本保真度（OCR标签召回率和字符错误率）、语义正确性（VLM根据规格判断）、结构质量和惯例遵循。同时设计了元评估协议和评分者间信度分析。

Result: 在全部8种图表类型的初步实验中，领域专用系统 SciDraw AI 在所有四个维度上显著优于通用文生图基线模型，其中语义正确性和惯例遵循的差距最大；文本保真度对所有系统来说仍是最困难的维度。

Conclusion: 科学图表生成需要专门的评测基准来衡量其可用性，现有通用图像生成评测不足以评估这一任务。SciDraw-Bench 提供了一个系统化的评估框架，揭示了当前模型在科学图表生成中的不足，特别是文本保真度方面仍有较大提升空间。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 SciDraw-Bench，一个面向科学图表生成的评测基准，属于生成模型评测与数据集方向。该 subtopic 在 multimodal_generation 和 multimodal_understanding_generation 两个方向下均有 canonical 条目，优先选用。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on a niche evaluation benchmark for scientific figure generation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct alignment with the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: Scientific figure generation benchmark, domain-specific system outperforms general models, four-dimensional evaluation protocol, low overlap with priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: Text-to-image and multimodal generative models are increasingly used to produce scientific figures such as mechanism diagrams, experimental-design schematics, conceptual frameworks, and graphical abstracts. Yet existing image-generation benchmarks (e.g., GenEval, T2I-CompBench, DPG-Bench) evaluate natural images and measure compositionality, object counting, or photorealism. None of them measure what makes a generated scientific figure usable: correct and legible text labels, faithful depiction of entities and their relations, coherent diagrammatic structure, and adherence to disciplinary drawing conventions. We introduce SciDraw-Bench, a benchmark of 32 structured scientific-figure generation tasks spanning eight figure types and ten disciplines, where each task pairs a natural-language prompt with a machine-checkable specification of required labels, relations, components, conventions, and negative constraints. We propose a four-dimensional evaluation protocol: Text Fidelity (OCR-based label recall and character error rate), Semantic Correctness (vision-language-model judging against the specification), Structural Quality, and Convention Adherence, together with a meta-evaluation protocol and a preliminary inter-judge reliability analysis (human-rating validation is ongoing). We evaluate a domain-specific system, SciDraw AI, against representative general-purpose text-to-image models, and outline a code-to-figure baseline as a planned extension. In a pilot over all eight figure types, the domain-specific system substantially outperforms the general-purpose baselines on every dimension and figure type, with the largest gaps on semantic correctness and convention adherence; text fidelity remains the hardest dimension for all systems.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个跨区域迁移评测基准，系统评估了 Prithvi、SpectralGPT 和 SatMAE 三个地理空间基础模型在美国四个州的农业作物分割与变化检测任务上的表现。实验发现所有模型在区域分布偏移下性能显著下降，仅能预测常见作物而遗漏稀有作物，且输入格式统一化对不同模型影响各异，揭示了当前地理空间基础模型在农业应用中的关键局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间基础模型在遥感任务上展现出广泛泛化潜力，但其地理迁移能力（尤其是农业应用场景）尚未被系统测试。现有评测多在同一区域划分训练/测试集，无法反映模型在未见过的地理区域上的真实表现。

Method: 设计了一个受控的跨区域评测基准：选取美国四个州（IA, NC, CA, MN）的多时相卫星影像数据，将训练、验证和测试集严格按区域划分，评估 Prithvi、SpectralGPT 和 SatMAE 三个模型在多时相作物分割和变化检测任务上的区域迁移能力。

Result: 所有模型在区域分布偏移下性能均大幅下降，仅能预测最常见的作物类别而遗漏稀有作物。将不同模型适配到统一输入格式对各模型影响不同，使得直接进行架构比较变得复杂。

Conclusion: 当前地理空间基础模型在农业应用中的地理迁移能力存在显著局限，区域感知的评测标准应成为必要规范。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了一个地理空间基础模型在农业应用上的跨区域迁移评测基准，属于生成模型评测与数据集（generation_benchmark）这一规范主题。虽然论文评测的是遥感基础模型（Prithvi, SpectralGPT, SatMAE）而非传统生成模型，但其核心贡献是系统性的基准测试与评估协议，与 generation_benchmark 的定义高度吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on geospatial foundation models for agriculture, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Geospatial foundation models, Agriculture application, Regional transferability, Crop segmentation, Change detection

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models pretrained on satellite imagery promise broad generalization across remote sensing tasks and regions, but their geographic transferability has not been systematically tested, especially in agriculture applications. This paper presents a controlled benchmark that evaluates three models, Prithvi, SpectralGPT, and SatMAE, on multi-temporal crop segmentation and change detection across four U.S. states, Iowa, North Carolina, California, and Minnesota. By assigning each train, validation, and test split to a separate region, we measure how well each model transfers to land it has not seen. All three degrade sharply under regional distribution shift, predicting only the most common crops while missing rare ones. We further find that fitting these models to a shared input format affects each one differently, which complicates direct architectural comparison. These results expose key limitations of current geospatial foundation models for agriculture and point to region aware evaluation as a necessary standard.

</details>


### [132] [The Human Creativity Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30561)
*Aspen Hopkins, Allison Nulty, Alexandria Minetti, Anoop Pakki, Angad Singh*

Main category: cs.HC, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出人类创造力基准（HCB），通过收集15000条来自领域专业人士的成对偏好、标量评分和定性理由，将创意AI评估中的收敛信号（专业共识）与发散信号（个体品味差异）分离，发现收敛集中在技术正确性等可验证维度，发散集中在美学方向等品味驱动维度，且没有模型在所有阶段表现一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI评估框架将评估者之间的分歧视为需要消除的噪声，但在创意领域，专业人士的分歧反映了真实的品味差异而非测量误差。因此需要一种新的评估方法，能够同时保留收敛（共识）和发散（个体品味差异）两种信号，以更准确地评估创意AI。

Method: 提出人类创造力基准（HCB），从五个创意领域（品牌、产品设计、室内设计、平面设计、时尚）和三个工作流阶段（构思、草图、精炼）收集数据。采用成对偏好比较、标量评分（提示遵循性、可用性、视觉吸引力）以及定性理由分析，共收集15000条专业判断。通过分析评分分布和定性理由，将评估维度分为收敛型（技术正确性、视觉层次）和发散型（美学方向、概念风险）。

Result: 发现收敛信号集中在可验证维度（如技术正确性、视觉层次），发散信号集中在品味驱动维度（如美学方向、概念风险）。没有模型在所有工作流阶段都表现优异。将收敛和发散信号合并为单一质量指标会丢失最有价值的信息——即模型在哪些方面必须正确，在哪些方面应保持可引导性。

Conclusion: 创意AI评估应保留收敛与发散的双重信号，而非将其合并为单一指标。这为创意AI的评估提供了新的方法论框架，有助于更准确地理解模型在创意任务中的优势和局限。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向创意AI的人类创造力基准（HCB），收集了15000条专业判断，涵盖五个创意领域和三个工作流阶段，旨在分离评估中的收敛（共识）与发散（个体品味差异）信号。这属于生成模型评测与人类偏好评估范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "generation_benchmark"（生成模型评测与数据集）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on human creativity evaluation framework, not on core technical advances in continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Human evaluation benchmark, creative AI assessment, professional disagreement, convergence vs divergence, no model excels across phases

Abstract: Modern AI evaluation frameworks treat evaluator disagreement as noise to be resolved. In creative domains, professional disagreement reflects genuine differences in taste, not measurement error. We argue that evaluating creative AI requires preserving two distinct signals: convergence, where professionals align around shared best practices, and divergence, where individual taste legitimately varies. We present the Human Creativity Benchmark (HCB), a benchmark that operationalizes this separation by collecting pairwise preferences, scalar ratings on prompt adherence, usability, and visual appeal, and qualitative rationale from domain professionals. Across 15,000 professional judgments spanning five creative domains and three workflow phases (ideation, mockup, refinement), we find that convergence concentrates on verifiable dimensions like technical correctness and visual hierarchy, while divergence concentrates on taste-driven dimensions like aesthetic direction and conceptual risk. No model excels uniformly across all phases. Collapsing these signals into a single quality metric discards the most actionable information: where models must be correct versus where they should remain steerable.

</details>


### [133] [SciIR: A Large-scale Training Dataset and Benchmark for Scientific Image Reasoning Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30124)
*Zhiyuan Ma, Zhengfeng Shi, Yuning An, Peize Li, Jiabao Wei, Ruijie Li, Junhao Xiao, Jianjun Li, Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 值得关注 (71.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 SciIR，一个面向科学图像推理生成的大规模训练数据集（SciIR-82k，8万+科学图像-文本对）和评测基准（SciIR-Bench）。基于皮尔斯符号学三元组将科学推理形式化为实体结构、科学过程、科学定律三个维度，并引入科学推理思维链（Sci-RCoT）。实验表明现有T2I模型在科学推理上存在显著缺陷，微调后的Qwen-Image-SciIR模型将基准分数从35%提升至43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图模型在生成逼真视觉内容方面表现出色，但在科学图像所需的严格语义对齐和逻辑推理方面仍存在困难。科学图像生成领域缺乏大规模训练数据和系统化的评测基准。

Method: 基于皮尔斯符号学三元组（Icon/Symbol/Index）将科学推理形式化为三个维度：实体结构（Icon）、科学过程（Index）、科学定律（Symbol）。构建 SciIR-82k 数据集（8万+科学图像-文本对），包含层次化标注和科学推理思维链（Sci-RCoT）。提出 SciIR-Bench 评测基准，采用原子检查表将结果导向的科学准确性转化为过程导向的可验证细粒度问题。基于 Qwen-Image 微调得到 Qwen-Image-SciIR 模型。

Result: 现有主流T2I模型在SciIR-Bench上表现不佳，科学推理能力显著不足。通过微调，Qwen-Image-SciIR模型在SciIR-Bench上的最终得分从35%提升至43%。

Conclusion: 科学图像生成需要专门的推理能力，本文提供的数据集和基准为未来研究奠定了基础。微调实验证明了数据驱动的科学图像生成能力提升潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了科学图像生成数据集 SciIR-82k 和评测基准 SciIR-Bench，属于生成模型评测与数据集（generation_benchmark）这一规范主题。同时论文也涉及科学图像生成方法（微调 Qwen-Image），因此也匹配多模态生成方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on scientific image reasoning, which is a niche generation task, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Scientific image generation, dataset and benchmark, reasoning chain-of-thought, not in priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: While Text-to-Image (T2I) models have shown remarkable success in generating photorealistic visual content, they still struggle with the rigorous semantic alignment and logical reasoning required for scientific imagery. Inspired by Peirce's Semiotic Triad, we introduce Scientific Image Reasoning (SciIR), a comprehensive resource for training and evaluation of scientific image generation. We formalize scientific reasoning into three core dimensions: Entity Structure (Icon), Scientific Process (Index), and Scientific Law (Symbol). Specifically, to overcome the scarcity of training data in scientific image generation, we elaborately create SciIR-82k, a large-scale dataset containing over 80,000 high-quality scientific image-text pairs from cutting-edge publications. The dataset is hierarchically organized according to the semiotic dimensions and incorporates a Scientific Reasoning Chain-of-Thought (Sci-RCoT) to explicitly model underlying visual logic. For evaluation, we propose SciIR-Bench, which aligns with these three semiotic levels and employs an Atomic Checklist to convert the outcome-oriented scientific accuracy into process-oriented, verifiable, fine-grained questions. Our extensive experiments reveal significant deficiencies in current models' scientific reasoning capabilities. Furthermore, by fine-tuning on the SciIR-82k dataset, we developed the Qwen-Image-SciIR model, which achieves a substantial improvement on the SciIR-Bench, increasing the final score from 35\% to 43\%, laying a solid foundation for future advances in scientific image generation.

</details>

*Xinrui Ruan, Zhenyu Zhao, Waverly Wei, Yueshan Zhang, Zeyu Zheng, Sui Huang, Jingshen Wang*

TL;DR: HERO 是一个利用历史评估数据来提升生成模型评测可靠性和敏感性的框架。它通过校准众包标注者的表现（抑制偏差）并锚定历史协变量信息（降低方差），在仅有少量黄金标注的情况下实现更稳定、更准确的模型性能估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型的质量评估严重依赖专家人工标注（黄金标签），但黄金标签成本高、数量有限。实践中常使用大量但噪声大的众包标注（银标签）作为替代，但直接聚合银标签会引入偏差，而基于稀疏黄金标签的估计方差大，难以可靠地分辨模型性能差异。此外，模型评估是持续进行的操作，历史评估数据未被充分利用。

Method: HERO 框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 偏差抑制——利用历史黄金标注数据校准银标签标注者的表现，学习其偏差模式并在当前评估中校正；(2) 方差降低——通过将估计器锚定到历史数据中高精度测量的协变量信息，稳定当前评估的估计结果。该方法适用于多种常见评估任务，且仅需部分历史标注者出现在当前轮次中即可生效。

Result: 在仿真实验和真实世界模型评估基准数据集上，HERO 展示了相比基线方法更低的偏差和方差，显著提升了模型性能评估的可靠性和敏感性，能够更准确地分辨不同模型之间的性能差距。

Conclusion: HERO 提供了一种利用历史数据改进生成模型评估的有效框架，在黄金标签稀缺的现实场景下具有重要实用价值。该方法理论上建立了偏差和方差降低的条件，实验验证了其有效性，可广泛应用于各类生成模型的持续评估流程中。

Classification reason: 该论文提出利用历史数据提升生成模型评估的可靠性和敏感性（HERO框架），属于生成模型评测方法论，与 canonical subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on evaluation methodology for generative models, not on core model architecture or generation methods prioritized by user. Signals: generative model evaluation, reliability and sensitivity, historical data calibration, bias and variance reduction, crowdsourced annotation

Abstract: Reliable generative AI models critically rely on expert human annotations to evaluate output quality, yet these "gold" labels are expensive to collect and limited in quantity. Organizations thus often turn to collecting vast but noisy "silver" labels from crowdsourced workers or vendor annotators as proxies for gold labels. Because gold remains the evaluation target, naively aggregating noisy silver labels may introduce bias, and estimators built on sparsely observed gold labels may have high variance to resolve the model performance gaps that guide practical decisions. Model evaluation has become an ongoing operational practice rather than a one-time exercise, with evaluation rounds repeating across model versions, releases, and content domains. A natural question is whether the previous historical evaluation data can be used to improve each new round of evaluation. We introduce HERO (History Enhanced RObust model evaluation), a novel framework that uses historical data to suppress bias (improve reliability) and reduce variance (improve sensitivity) in model performance evaluation. HERO calibrates silver labelers' performance learned from historical gold annotations, and stabilizes the resulting estimator by anchoring it to covariate information measured with high precision in the historical data. HERO can be broadly applied across multiple common evaluation tasks, and remains valid when only a subset of historical labelers appears in the current round. We establish conditions under which the bias and variance reductions hold, showcase HERO's performance in simulation studies, and demonstrate its effectiveness on real-world model evaluation benchmarking datasets.

</details>

### [135] [UniGP: Taming Diffusion Transformer for Prior-Preserved Unified Generation and Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30332)
*Qin Guo, Hao Luo, Dongxu Yue, Weixuan Jin, Xiao Fu, Fan Wang, Dan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (92.6) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: UniGP 基于 MMDiT 架构，通过简单联合训练统一了可控图像生成与密集预测任务，无需复杂任务特定设计，同时保持骨干网络的先验知识，在生成和感知任务上均达到与专用方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将扩散模型的可控生成与密集预测视为独立任务，忽视了联合建模异构分布（图像-几何对）的潜在收益。作者希望用一个统一框架同时处理生成与感知，并利用多任务联合训练的互补优势。

Method: UniGP 基于 MMDiT（Multi-Modal Diffusion Transformer）构建，包含两个核心组件：(1) DUGP（Dual-Use Generation and Perception），遵循奥卡姆剃刀原则，仅复制 MMDiT 的图像分支来建模 RGB 之外的密集分布（如深度、法线等）；(2) 统一数据集训练策略，将异构数据集整合到统一训练框架中，联合建模生成与感知任务。

Result: 大量实验表明，UniGP 在可控生成、密集预测和联合生成任务上超越了先前的统一方法，性能与专用方法相当。多任务联合训练具有互补优势：生成先验丰富了感知细节，感知学习改善了生成的结构对齐。

Conclusion: UniGP 证明了通过简单联合训练即可有效统一扩散模型的生成与感知能力，无需复杂任务特定设计，为构建通用视觉基础模型提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文将可控生成与密集预测（感知）统一在 MMDiT 框架下，属于理解与生成一体化方向。multimodal_generation 和 multimodal_understanding_generation 两个方向下均有 "统一多模态模型" 这一现有 subtopic，且论文核心贡献正是统一生成与感知，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Unifies controllable generation and dense prediction in a diffusion transformer, directly supporting your priority on unified multimodal models and efficient joint training. Signals: MMDiT backbone, unified generation and perception, prior-preserved training, no complex task-specific designs, joint distribution modeling

Abstract: Recent advances in diffusion models have shown impressive performance in controllable image generation and dense prediction tasks. However, existing approaches typically treat diffusion-based controllable generation and dense prediction as separate tasks, overlooking the potential benefits of jointly modeling the heterogeneous distributions. In this work, we introduce UniGP, a framework built upon MMDiT, which unifies controllable generation and dense prediction through simple joint training, without the need for complex task-specific designs or losses, while preserving the backbone's versatile priors. By learning controllable generation and prediction under different conditions, our model effectively captures the joint distribution of image-geometry pairs. UniGP is capable of versatile controllable generation, dense prediction, and joint generation. Specifically, the proposed UniGP consists of DUGP and a unified dataset training strategy. The former, following the principle of Occam's razor, uses only a copied image branch of MMDiT to model dense distributions beyond RGB, while the latter integrates heterogeneous datasets into a unified training framework to jointly model generation and perception tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our unified model surpasses prior unified approaches and performs on par with specialized methods. Furthermore, we demonstrate that multi-task joint training provides complementary benefits: generative priors enrich perceptual details, while perceptual learning improves structural alignment in generation.

</details>

### [136] [MAVIN: Multi-Shot Audio-Visual Generation with Narrative Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29473)
*Kaiqi Liu, Yunyao Mao, Ziqi Cai, Zheng Geng, Jing Wang, Qiulin Wang, Xintao Wang, Pengfei Wan, Kun Gai, Shuchen Weng, Boxin Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 普通相关 (56.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MAVIN 是首个支持叙事控制的多镜头音视频联合生成框架，通过边界感知注意力、ID感知传播和多智能体脚本管线，实现多镜头、多主体、音视频一致的生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型虽能生成高质量视频，但在多镜头音视频生成中缺乏叙事控制能力，存在时序错位、可控性差、脚本不完整等问题，无法满足专业影视制作需求。

Method: 1) 边界感知注意力（Boundary-Aware Attention）：利用层级化字幕和边界感知 token 路由，将音视频元素渲染到各自的时间边界内；2) ID感知传播（ID-Aware Propagation）：使用身份嵌入和身份感知掩码，将特定身份绑定到一致的视觉外观和语音音色；3) 多智能体脚本管线：将用户自由输入转化为层级化字幕；4) 构建 MAVINSet 多镜头音视频数据集用于训练和评测。

Result: 大量实验表明 MAVIN 在多个指标上达到最先进性能，实现了多镜头、多主体、音视频一致的叙事级生成。

Conclusion: MAVIN 开创了将生成模型集成到专业影视制作流程的新方向，证明了叙事控制的多镜头音视频生成的可行性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多镜头音视频联合生成框架，属于音频与多模态联合生成（audio_av_generation）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。同时涉及多镜头叙事控制，与统一多模态模型方向也有一定关联。

Importance reason: Novel multi-shot audio-visual narrative control, but tangential to user's core continuous multimodal and autoregressive generation focus. Signals: Multi-shot generation, Audio-visual consistency, Narrative control, Boundary-aware attention, ID-aware propagation

Abstract: While recent generative models produce high-fidelity videos, they struggle with the complex narrative control required for coherent multi-shot audio-visual generation. Existing methods suffer from temporal misalignment, limited controllability, and incomplete scripting. In this paper, we propose MAVIN, the first framework for multi-shot audio-visual generation with customized narrative control. To resolve temporal misalignment, we propose boundary-aware attention, which leverages hierarchical captions and boundary-aware token routing to render audio-visual elements within their respective temporal boundaries. To improve the controllability for multi-subject scenarios, we propose ID-aware propagation, utilizing identity embeddings and an identity-aware mask to bind specific identities to consistent visual appearances and vocal timbres. To provide comprehensive audio-visual narratives, we present a multi-agent scripting pipeline to transform free-form user inputs into hierarchical captions. Furthermore, we construct MAVINSet, a multi-shot audio-visual dataset for robust training and evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MAVIN achieves state-of-the-art performance, opening up a new avenue for integrating generative models into professional filmmaking workflows.

</details>


### [137] [LeVo 2: Stable and Melodious Song Generation via Hierarchical Representation Modeling and Progressive Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30642)
*Shun Lei, Huaicheng Zhang, Dapeng Wu, Yaoxun Xu, Lishi Zuo, Wei Tan, Hangting Chen, Guangzheng Li, Jianwei Yu, Zhiyong Wu, Dong Yu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.SD

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LeVo 2 是一个混合 LLM-Diffusion 框架，通过分层表示建模（语义规划 + 并行音轨细化）和渐进式后训练（SFT、离线 DPO、半在线 DPO）实现可控的全长度歌曲生成，在主观和客观评测中优于开源基线并接近商业系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语言模型的歌曲生成系统面临结构性的权衡：混合 token 建模保留了人声与乐器的协调但模糊了音轨细节，而双轨预测改善了声学质量但序列更长且全局规划能力弱。LeVo 2 旨在解决这一权衡，实现稳定且旋律优美的全长度歌曲生成。

Method: 提出混合 LLM-Diffusion 框架 LeVo 2，包含：(1) 分层架构：LeLM 先预测混合 token 进行语义规划，再并行预测人声和伴奏 token 进行音轨细化，扩散 Music Codec 重建波形；(2) 美学引导的训练调度：预训练时用自动音乐美学评估为数据分配音乐性条件；(3) 渐进式后训练：依次进行 SFT、大规模离线 DPO 和闭环半在线 DPO，分别提升生成质量、可控性和音乐性；(4) 模块化扩展：训练 Track-Specific LM 进行声学细化，同时保持对齐的语义规划器。

Result: 专家听测和客观评估表明，LeVo 2 在六个主观维度上优于开源基线，在多个听感指标上接近领先的商业系统。消融实验验证了训练策略、美学引导、规模扩展和分层架构的有效性。

Conclusion: LeVo 2 通过分层表示建模和渐进式后训练有效解决了歌曲生成中语义规划与声学细节之间的权衡，分离了音乐性学习、可控性对齐和声学细化，缓解了优化冲突和静态离线偏好对的局限性，为全长度歌曲生成提供了新的有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究全长度歌曲生成（song generation），属于音频生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "音频与多模态联合生成"（audio_av_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on song generation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: song generation, hierarchical representation, LLM-Diffusion hybrid, progressive post-training, music aesthetics

Abstract: Full-length song generation must preserve coherence and musicality, render detailed vocal and accompaniment acoustics, and follow lyrics and prompts. Existing language model-based systems face a structural trade-off: mixed-token modeling preserves vocal-instrument coordination but obscures track-specific details, whereas dual-track prediction improves acoustics but requires longer sequences and weakens global planning. We present LeVo 2, a hybrid LLM-Diffusion framework for controllable full-length song generation. LeVo 2 formulates this trade-off as hierarchical modeling: LeLM first predicts mixed tokens for semantic planning, then predicts vocal and accompaniment tokens in parallel for track-specific refinement, while a diffusion-based Music Codec reconstructs full-length waveforms. A central contribution of this extended version is an aesthetics-guided training schedule for alignment. During pre-training, an automated music aesthetic evaluation framework assigns musicality-tier conditions to large-scale data, providing musicality priors before preference alignment. Progressive post-training applies SFT, large-scale offline DPO, and closed-loop semi-online DPO to separately improve generation quality, controllability, and musicality. Modular extension then trains the Track-Specific LM for acoustic refinement while preserving the aligned semantic planner. This schedule separates musicality learning, controllability alignment, and acoustic refinement, mitigating optimization conflict and the limitations of static offline preference pairs. Expert listening tests and objective evaluations show that LeVo 2 outperforms open-source baselines across six subjective dimensions, and approaches leading commercial systems on several listening metrics. Ablations validate the effects of the training strategy, aesthetics guidance, scaling, and hierarchical architecture.

</details>

### [138] [Resolution Thresholds in VLM Detection of Harmful ASCII Art Across Construction Modes and Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29649)
*Yikai Hua, Peter West*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了图像分辨率对VLM检测有害ASCII艺术的影响，在8种字符构建模式、10种分辨率尺度下评估了8个SOTA VLM在英文和中文语料上的表现，发现检测率在特定分辨率阈值以上急剧下降，且基于单词的模式最难被检测，揭示了VLM内容审核系统的系统性漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM被广泛用于内容审核，但将有害文本编码为ASCII艺术可以绕过审核系统。现有研究缺乏对分辨率如何影响VLM检测ASCII艺术中隐藏有害内容的系统性理解，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 设计了一个流水线，将有害文本以8种字符构建模式（L1-L8，从密集块字符到嵌入单词的设计）生成ASCII艺术图像，在10个分辨率尺度下测试8个SOTA VLM在英文和中文语料上的检测性能，分析是否存在一致的检测失败阈值。

Result: 检测率在特定分辨率阈值以上急剧下降；基于单词的模式（word-based modes）在所有分辨率范围内最难被检测；不同模型、模式和语言之间存在一致的检测失败阈值模式。

Conclusion: VLM内容审核系统存在由分辨率导致的系统性漏洞，需要建立分辨率感知的评估标准来提升审核可靠性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM对ASCII艺术中隐藏的有害文本的检测漏洞，属于多模态安全与攻防中的越狱攻击与防御评测方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM safety and ASCII art detection, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and direction weights. Signals: VLM safety, ASCII art detection, resolution thresholds, content moderation, jailbreak attacks

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed as content moderation tools, yet they remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks in which harmful text is visually encoded as ASCII art. This can allow inappropriate or harmful content to bypass moderation systems. To address this vulnerability, this paper investigates how image resolution affects VLM detection of harmful ASCII art across eight character construction modes (L1-L8), ranging from dense block characters to word-embedded designs. We evaluate eight state-of-the-art VLMs on English and Chinese corpora using a pipeline that generates ASCII art images at ten resolution scales, probing whether a consistent detection-failure threshold exists across models, modes, and languages. Results indicate that detection rates decline sharply above certain resolution thresholds, and that word-based modes are the most resistant to detection across the full resolution range. These findings reveal a systematic vulnerability in VLM-based content moderation systems and motivate resolution-aware evaluation standards.

</details>


### [139] [Forewarned is Forearmed: When Non-Sequential Embedding Turns Into an Anomaly Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30196)
*Elys Allesiardo, Antoine Caubrière, Valentin Vielzeuf*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.LG, eess.AS

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文深入分析非序列多模态句子级嵌入（SONAR模型），发现某些嵌入维度对扰动敏感，可作为解码异常的指示器。通过编码-解码一致性构建了准确的异常检测器，并探索了修改特定维度以纠正异常的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态嵌入模型（如SONAR）广泛应用于跨语言跨模态任务，但其嵌入空间的鲁棒性和可靠性尚未被充分研究。作者希望理解嵌入本身的行为，利用编码-解码一致性来检测解码异常，从而提升多模态表征的可靠性。

Method: 以SONAR模型为研究对象，分析其非序列多模态句子级嵌入。通过比较编码后的嵌入与解码重建结果之间的一致性，识别对扰动敏感的嵌入维度，并基于此构建异常检测器。进一步探索通过修改特定维度来纠正解码异常。

Result: 成功构建了基于编码-解码一致性的准确异常检测器，能够有效检测解码异常。初步实验表明修改特定嵌入维度有潜力纠正部分异常。

Conclusion: 理解和分析多模态嵌入本身对于提升多模态表征的可靠性至关重要。编码-解码一致性可作为检测和纠正嵌入异常的有效工具，为多模态系统的安全性和鲁棒性提供了新的视角。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态句子级嵌入（SONAR模型）中的异常检测，通过编码-解码一致性检测解码异常（anomaly），属于多模态安全与可靠性范畴。虽然不直接涉及越狱/对抗攻击，但异常检测与防御密切相关，可归入[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防"。

Importance reason: Focuses on embedding anomaly detection for multimodal safety, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in generation, world models, or continuous representations. Signals: Non-sequential embedding analysis, SONAR model, anomaly detection via encoding-decoding consistency, multimodal representation reliability, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: This paper offers an in-depth analysis of non-sequential multimodal sentence-level embeddings, with a particular focus on the SONAR model. We demonstrate that certain embedding dimensions are sensitive to perturbations and can serve as indicators of decoding anomalies. By leveraging the consistency between successive encoding and decoding, we successfully build an accurate detector. Additionally, we explore modifying specific dimensions of interest to attempt to correct them. This work underscores the importance of understanding and analyzing the embeddings themselves to enhance the reliability of multimodal representations.

</details>


### [140] [When Medical Safety Alignment Fails: A Benchmark for Evaluating LLMs on High-Risk Medical Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28332)
*Yige Li, Jun Sun, Wei Zhao, Zhe Li, Yutao Wu, Hanxun Huang, Xiang Zheng, Xingjun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CY

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (47.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MedHarm，一个包含 1100 个高风险医疗查询的安全评测基准，覆盖毒理学、药理学、麻醉学等 10 个安全关键类别。评估 15 个 LLM 和 4 个护栏模型后发现：对齐模型仍可能产生不安全响应，医学微调会放大有害特异性，外部护栏存在脆弱阻塞和弱安全帮助性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 越来越多地被用于医疗健康问答，但它们在高风险医疗场景下的安全性仍缺乏系统评估。现有医疗 QA 基准侧重于回答准确性而非安全性，无法检测模型在涉及毒药、麻醉、胎儿伤害等敏感话题时是否会产生有害或可操作的建议。

Method: 构建 MedHarm 基准，包含 1100 个医学上合理的高风险查询，覆盖 10 个安全关键类别（毒理学、药理学、隐蔽投毒、麻醉学、胎儿伤害等）。查询分为临床、教育和技术三类提示。评估 15 个 LLM（通用、医学专用、闭源、下游 SFT 模型）和 4 个护栏模型，分析模型的安全响应行为。

Result: 对齐模型仍可能产生不安全或可操作的响应；医学微调会放大有害响应的具体性和可操作性；外部护栏模型能减少部分失败，但存在脆弱的阻塞行为和弱的安全帮助性（safe helpfulness）。

Conclusion: 医疗安全性不能从通用对齐或医学能力中推断出来，在将 LLM 部署到安全关键的医疗应用之前，需要进行领域特定的压力测试。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了高风险医疗安全基准 MedHarm，评估 LLM 在医疗场景下的安全性，属于多模态安全与攻防（multimodal_safety_attack）范畴下的安全评测方向。虽然论文主要关注 LLM 而非多模态模型，但该 canonical subtopic 涵盖安全评测与对齐失败分析，且论文涉及的安全对齐问题与多模态安全高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical safety, which is outside your configured priority subtopics and has negative keywords. Signals: Medical safety benchmark, LLM alignment failure, Domain-specific stress testing, Not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for medical and health-related questions, yet their safety in high-risk medical scenarios remains poorly understood. We introduce \textsc{MedHarm}\footnote{Code and data will be released upon acceptance. Due to the sensitive nature of high-risk medical queries, data access will be available to qualified researchers upon request.}, a high-risk medical safety benchmark with 1,100 medically grounded queries across 10 safety-critical categories, including toxicology, pharmacology, covert poisoning, anesthesia, and fetal harm. Unlike broad medical QA benchmarks, \textsc{MedHarm} targets realistic clinical, educational, and technical prompts that require refusal, caution, or safe redirection rather than direct helpfulness. We evaluate 15 LLMs spanning general-purpose, medical-purpose, closed-source, and downstream SFT models, together with 4 representative guardrail models. Results reveal a substantial gap between apparent alignment and medical safety: aligned models can still produce unsafe or actionable responses, medical fine-tuning can amplify harmful specificity, and external guardrails reduce some failures while introducing brittle blocking and weak safe helpfulness. These findings show that medical safety cannot be inferred from general alignment or medical capability alone, highlighting the need for domain-specific stress testing before deploying LLMs in safety-critical medical applications.

</details>


### [141] [From Failure Taxonomy to Intervention: A Diagnostic Methodology for Industry-Scale AVLM in Video and Live-Streaming Platform Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30059)
*Shuchang Ye, Jinqiang Yu, Zhujun Xiao, Yajing Kong, Yist Y. Lin, Yang Ma, Jiaxi Liu, Xiaolei Xu, Zheng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 值得关注 (74.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套面向工业级音视频语言模型（AVLM）的故障诊断方法论，将模型部署中的失败案例映射为可观察的故障签名分类体系，并将每类故障链接到对应的干预空间。作者在大型视频和直播平台的实际AVLM开发和对齐全生命周期中实例化了该方法，最终系统支持100+区域的内容审核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态基础模型研究主要报告架构、训练策略、数据缩放和基准结果，但缺乏系统性的故障定位与针对性干预指导。工业级视频和直播审核对平台特定数据分布、策略目标和安全约束有严格要求，部署失败的原因多样且不直观，改进往往沦为启发式试错。因此需要一套诊断方法论来系统化地定位故障根源并指导模型开发干预。

Method: 提出一个诊断方法论，将模型失败映射为可观察的故障签名（failure signatures）分类体系，并将每类故障链接到对应的干预空间（intervention space）。该方法在大型视频和直播平台的AVLM基础模型开发和对齐全生命周期中实例化，涵盖数据、训练、对齐等环节的系统性诊断与干预。

Result: 在支持100+区域的大规模视频和直播平台中实例化了该诊断方法论，构建了能够处理噪声、模糊和高度多样化内容的AVLM审核系统。

Conclusion: 本文填补了多模态基础模型研究中故障诊断与干预指导的空白，提供了一套从故障分类到针对性干预的系统化方法论，对工业级AVLM的可靠开发和部署具有重要实践价值。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于工业级音视频语言模型（AVLM）在视频和直播平台内容审核中的故障诊断与安全干预，属于多模态安全与可靠性范畴。虽然更偏向诊断方法论而非直接攻击/防御，但核心关注平台安全审核中的故障分类与干预，与多模态安全与攻防（multimodal_safety_attack）主题高度相关，故复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on industry AVLM failure diagnosis, not on core priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: industry-scale AVLM, failure taxonomy, content moderation, no continuous language model, no video generation

Abstract: Industry-scale video and live-streaming moderation imposes requirements that are difficult to satisfy with generic pretrained public models or external APIs, including adaptation to platform-specific data distributions, policy-specific objectives, and product-level safety constraints. As a result, platforms must undertake internal model development, naturally turning to shared public research for guidance. However, existing multimodal foundation-model studies primarily report architectures, training recipes, data scaling strategies, and benchmark results, but provide less systematic guidance on how failures should be localized and translated into targeted model-development interventions. Interventions are essential because deployment failures are rarely self-explanatory. Similar failures can originate from different causes. Without targeted interventions, improvement reduces to heuristic trial-and-error, where benchmark improvements are weakly attributable, and failures are difficult to trace to their underlying causes. To address this gap, we present a diagnostic methodology for industry-scale Audio-Visual-Language Models AVLM development. The methodology maps model failures into a taxonomy of observable failure signatures and links each class of failure to an intervention space. We instantiate this methodology across the development and alignment lifecycle of an AVLM foundation model for a large-scale video and live-streaming platform. The resulting system supports over 100 regions and is designed for noisy, ambiguous, and highly diverse content drawn from global platform traffic.

</details>


### [142] [A Classifier-Agnostic Zero-Shot Adversarial Attack Detection via CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30342)
*Hodaya Krakover, Meir Yossef Levi, Eyal Gofer, Guy Gilboa*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出A⁴D，一个完全黑盒、零样本的对抗攻击检测框架，利用CLIP的提示相似度分数来检测对抗样本，无需攻击先验知识或分类器信息，在多种攻击、数据集和分类器上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗攻击检测方法通常依赖攻击特定假设、需要对抗样本或白盒访问分类器，缺乏通用且实用的黑盒检测方案。本文旨在利用CLIP的视觉-语言对齐能力，实现无需任何先验知识的零样本对抗攻击检测。

Method: 基于两个关键观察：(i) CLIP对微小非语义扰动敏感；(ii) CLIP嵌入空间的偏移可作为鲁棒的攻击指示器。通过计算CLIP的提示相似度分数，设计攻击无关和架构无关的检测框架A⁴D，在完全黑盒（无分类器信息、无对抗样本）的零样本设置下进行检测。

Result: 在多种攻击方法、多个数据集和不同分类器上的实验验证了A⁴D在攻击无关和分类器无关的设置下达到了SOTA检测结果。

Conclusion: A⁴D首次将CLIP用于对抗攻击检测，证明了CLIP嵌入空间的偏移可作为通用攻击检测信号，为黑盒零样本对抗防御提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究利用CLIP进行零样本对抗攻击检测，属于多模态安全与攻防（对抗攻击检测/防御）范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防"（multimodal_safety_attack）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on adversarial detection for CLIP, which is outside the user's priority subtopics of continuous models, generation, and world models. Signals: CLIP-based detection, zero-shot adversarial defense, black-box attack detection, SOTA on multiple attacks, no direct link to user's core interests

Abstract: Adversarial attacks pose a challenge to the reliability of deep learning models, motivating effective detection methods. Existing techniques often rely on attack-specific assumptions, access to adversarial samples, or knowledge of the underlying classifier (white-box). We propose \textit{$A^4D$ (\textbf{A}ttack- and \textbf{A}rchitecture-\textbf{A}gnostic \textbf{A}dversarial \textbf{D}etector)}, a completely black-box, zero-shot adversarial attack detection framework that utilizes prompt-based similarity scores derived from CLIP. To the best of our knowledge this is the first attempt to utilize CLIP for such a task. The method is based on two key observations: (i) CLIP is sensitive even to small imperceptible non-semantic perturbations; (ii) The shift in CLIP embedding space is not arbitrary and can be used as a robust attack indicator. Experiments across multiple attacks, datasets and classifiers validate that $A^4D$ achieves SOTA detection results in the attack-agnostic and classifier-agnostic setting.

</details>
