### [143] [Vision-driven Preference Synthesis for Mitigating Hallucinations in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28401)
*Yunhun Nam, Jongheon Jeong*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (59.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出ViPSy框架，通过视觉驱动的偏好数据合成来缓解VLM幻觉，在AMBER和Object HalBench上分别降低幻觉率35.7%和24.5%，达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好对齐方法在构建偏好对时存在两个局限：(a) 基于干预的方法会显著偏离策略分布；(b) 基于采样的方法在构建过程中未充分利用视觉信息。需要一种既能保持策略对齐又能充分利用视觉信息的偏好数据构建方法。

Method: ViPSy包含两阶段：(1) 从语义对齐的图像变体中提取跨图像重复出现的物体级视觉线索（visual cue），使偏好构建依赖视觉信息而非语言先验；(2) 将策略自身的rollout条件化在该视觉线索上，生成既贴近策略分布又视觉对齐的候选响应，用于构建偏好对进行偏好对齐。

Result: 偏好对齐后的VLM在AMBER上幻觉率降低35.7%，在Object HalBench上降低24.5%，超越此前SOTA。同时在MMStar、MMVP、CV-Bench等通用视觉定位基准上也有提升，并在语义分割和ImageNet线性探测上取得增益。

Conclusion: ViPSy通过视觉驱动的偏好数据合成有效缓解了VLM幻觉，同时增强了模型的视觉能力，验证了视觉信息在偏好对齐中的关键作用。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于VLM幻觉缓解，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的[多模态幻觉与可靠性]（CANONICAL）子主题，直接对应。

Importance reason: Strong VLM hallucination mitigation with SOTA results, but outside user's priority subtopics and direction weights. Signals: VLM hallucination, preference alignment, visual grounding, SOTA on AMBER and Object HalBench, policy-aligned data synthesis

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have shown strong performance in visual understanding, yet they still suffer from hallucinations, generating content that is not grounded in the image. Preference alignment is a promising approach to improve visual faithfulness, but its success depends heavily on how preference pairs are constructed. Existing methods exhibit two key limitations; (a) intervention-based methods often introduce significant deviation from the policy distribution, and (b) sampling-based methods often underuse visual information during the construction. In this paper, we propose ViPSy (Vision-driven Preference Synthesis), a framework for constructing preference data that are both policy-aligned and visually grounded. Our framework consists of two stages; in the first stage, ViPSy derives a visual cue from recurring object-level content across semantically aligned image variants, so preference construction can rely on visual information rather than language priors. In the second stage, ViPSy conditions the policy's own rollouts on this cue, allowing candidates to be guided by visually grounded content while staying close to the policy's response distribution. The resulting candidates remain close to the policy's response distribution while better leveraging visual information from the image. Experiments show that the resulting VLM, preference-aligned with ViPSy-constructed preference pairs, achieves a new state-of-the-art in hallucination mitigation. Compared with the previous state-of-the-art method, it reduces hallucination rates on AMBER and Object HalBench by 35.7% and 24.5%, respectively. The resulting model further improves on general visual grounding benchmarks, e.g., MMStar, MMVP, and CV-Bench, while also yielding gains in semantic segmentation and ImageNet linear probing, underscoring the effectiveness of our framework in enhancing the model's visual capabilities.

</details>


### [144] [Detecting Clinical Hallucinations in LVLMs via Counterfactual Visual Grounding Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28520)
*Xiao Song, Haonan Qin, Zhaoxu Zhang, Jiong Zhang, Yuqi Fang, Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (49.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于反事实视觉基础不确定性的临床LVLM幻觉检测框架，通过外部视觉定位验证器对模型输出中的实体进行定位，并引入反事实扰动来估计视觉证据不确定性，无需修改模型或访问其内部状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在临床图像理解中容易产生幻觉（生成图像中不支持的文本发现或属性），现有检测方法通常需要修改模型或访问内部状态，缺乏可解释的视觉证据。因此需要一种无需模型内部访问、可跨模型迁移且提供可解释定位证据的幻觉检测方法。

Method: 提出视觉可追踪的幻觉检测框架：1）从LVLM响应中提取视觉可验证实体；2）使用医学领域适配的Qwen-VL基础验证器在输入图像上定位每个实体；3）引入反事实实体扰动方法，通过对比事实与反事实基础结果估计视觉证据不确定性；4）计算实体级不确定性分数（结合正置信度、反事实置信度及其基础重叠）进行二值幻觉判定。

Result: 在多种医学影像模态和LVLM骨干网络上，该方法在幻觉检测性能上持续优于近期基线方法，同时提供可解释的定位证据并展现出强跨模型迁移能力。

Conclusion: 基于反事实视觉基础不确定性的方法能有效检测临床LVLM幻觉，无需修改模型或访问内部状态，具有良好的跨模型泛化性和可解释性，为临床多模态系统的可靠性保障提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LVLM在临床图像理解中的幻觉检测问题，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态幻觉与可靠性"（multimodal_hallucination）完全匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on clinical hallucination detection, which is a negative keyword area, and does not align with priority subtopics. Signals: Clinical hallucination detection, Counterfactual visual grounding, Medical domain, LVLM auditing, Cross-model transferability

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) are increasingly used for clinical image understanding, yet they remain vulnerable to \emph{hallucinations}--producing textual findings or attributes not supported by the image. We present a vision-traceable hallucination detection framework that audits arbitrary LVLM responses via visual evidence grounding, requiring neither modification nor internal access to the hidden states of LVLMs. Given an LVLM response, we extract visually verifiable entities and use a medical-domain-adapted Qwen-VL grounding verifier to localize each entity on the input image. To enhance the robustness of our detection method, we introduce a counterfactual entity perturbation method and estimate visual evidence uncertainty by contrasting factual and counterfactual grounding results. Specifically, we compute an entity-level uncertainty score from the positive confidence, counterfactual confidence, and their grounding overlap for binary hallucination decision-making. Experiments on multiple medical imaging modalities and LVLM backbones demonstrate that our method consistently improves hallucination detection performance over recent baselines, while providing interpretable localization evidence and strong cross-model transferability. Code and dataset are available at .

</details>


### [145] [HKVLM: Faithful Reasoning Grounding by Binding Language Queries to a Frozen Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28862)
*Bo Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (56.96) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: HKVLM提出一种轻量级对齐钩子（alignment hook），将冻结的检测器（Grounding DINO）与冻结的语言模型（Qwen2.5-VL）通过对比检索和二部匹配在共享嵌入空间中绑定，并引入感知忠实性否决机制（faithfulness veto）防止模型说出无对应区域支持的对象，从而显著减少VLM的定位幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在视觉定位任务中存在"see but mis-speak"问题：模型能关注到正确区域，但通过自回归头输出坐标时产生错误框或标签。作者将此归因为binding failure——定位信息经过语言生成路径时被耦合或压缩，导致意图与感知脱节。

Method: HKVLM采用三组件架构：(1) 冻结的语言对齐检测器（Grounding DINO）生成类别无关的区域提议；(2) 冻结的语言模型（Qwen2.5-VL）将推理指令编码为referential query嵌入；(3) 可训练的轻量级alignment hook通过对比检索和二部匹配在共享嵌入空间中将query绑定到区域。此外引入perception-grounded faithfulness veto，禁止模型命名任何无对应区域支持的对象。

Result: 在RefCOCO/RefCOCO+/RefCOCOg上，仅训练hook使定位精度提升50-90倍（相比未训练的跨空间匹配）；faithfulness veto将POPE准确率从接近随机（0.50）提升至0.66-0.76，幻觉率从~0.99降至0.23-0.43。增加提议数量M从50到300可额外提升定位19-24%，表明残余误差主要来自感知层面（SeeErr）而非绑定层面（SayErr）。

Conclusion: HKVLM通过将定位从语言路径中解耦，证明了在少量数据冷启动场景下，轻量级对齐钩子可以显著提升VLM的定位忠实性并减少幻觉。论文提出的say-vs-see分解框架（SeeErr vs SayErr）为理解VLM定位错误提供了新的分析视角。

Classification reason: 该论文主要解决VLM中的"binding failure"问题（模型看到正确区域但输出错误框或标签），通过faithfulness veto机制减少幻觉，属于多模态幻觉缓解与可靠性提升方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态幻觉与可靠性"。

Importance reason: Addresses VLM hallucination via a novel binding approach, but focuses on a narrow subtopic (multi-modal hallucination) not in your priority list. Signals: VLM hallucination reduction, frozen detector + frozen LLM, alignment hook, faithfulness veto, small-data cold-start setting

Abstract: Many visual requests -- ``the object to open this bottle'', ``the person not wearing a helmet'' -- require reasoning, not just category matching. Pure open-vocabulary detectors need an explicit phrase; vision-language models (VLMs) can reason yet ``see but mis-speak'', attending to the right region but returning the wrong box or label. We argue this is a \emph{binding} failure: in coordinate-as-text VLMs localization passes through the autoregressive head, coupling it to language generation; in two-stage pipelines the model's intent is squeezed through a single class string. We present HKVLM, which removes localization from the language path. A frozen, language-aligned detector emits class-agnostic region proposals; a frozen language model encodes reasoning instructions as referential query embeddings; a lightweight \emph{alignment hook} binds queries to regions by contrastive retrieval and bipartite assignment in a shared embedding space. A perception-grounded faithfulness veto forbids naming an object that no region supports. Only the hook is trained, targeting small-data cold-start settings where monolithic VLM tuning struggles. We formalize a \emph{say-vs-see} decomposition separating localization error (SeeErr) from binding error (SayErr), and evaluate on RefCOCO/RefCOCO+/RefCOCOg and POPE. With frozen Grounding DINO and Qwen2.5-VL, training only the hook lifts grounding accuracy by $50$--$90\times$ over untrained cross-space matching; the faithfulness veto raises POPE accuracy from near-chance ($0.50$) to $0.66$--$0.76$ and reduces hallucination from ${\sim}0.99$ to $0.23$--$0.43$, with gains from $200$ expressions. Increasing proposals from $M{=}50$ to $M{=}300$ improves grounding by $19$--$24\%$ without retraining, confirming that residual error is perceptual (SeeErr) rather than binding (SayErr).

</details>


### [146] [AURORA: Asymmetry and Update-Induced Rotation for Robust Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29545)
*Zishuai Zhang, Hainan Zhang, Zhiming Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AURORA提出了一种基于权重-梯度动力学的幻觉检测框架，通过分析梯度更新的不对称性和旋转比来区分幻觉与忠实输出，无需输出级一致性检查或静态隐藏状态探针，在多个模型和数据集上表现优异，并可迁移到视觉-语言场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法要么依赖昂贵的输出级一致性检查，要么使用静态隐藏状态探针捕获浅层数据集特定模式，在跨数据集评估下性能大幅下降。需要一种更鲁棒、可迁移的幻觉检测方法。

Method: 提出AURORA框架，核心思想是幻觉样本与忠实样本在模型参数上引发性质不同的梯度更新模式。通过两个互补特征捕获这种差异：(1) 权重矩阵与其梯度更新方向之间余弦相似度分布的偏度（skewness）；(2) 旋转比（rotation ratio），通过SVD量化梯度更新对权重矩阵奇异向量基的重新定向程度。

Result: 在四个模型家族和四个基准数据集上实现了强幻觉检测性能。方法在模型规模上有效扩展，并可迁移到数学推理和视觉-语言场景等域外任务。

Conclusion: 从静态表征转向权重-梯度动力学是幻觉检测的有效新范式，AURORA具有跨模型、跨数据集和跨模态的良好泛化能力。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM幻觉检测，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性（multimodal_hallucination）这一[CANONICAL]子主题的范畴。虽然论文主要聚焦文本LLM，但摘要明确提到方法可迁移到vision-language场景，且该子主题涵盖幻觉缓解与可靠性提升，与论文核心问题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM hallucination detection, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: hallucination detection, weight-gradient dynamics, cross-dataset evaluation, vision-language transfer, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across a wide range of natural language processing tasks. However, their tendency to generate hallucinations, namely factually incorrect or unfaithful outputs, poses a critical obstacle to their deployment in high-stakes applications. Although recent hallucination detection methods have made encouraging progress, they typically rely on costly output-level consistency checks or static hidden-state probes that capture shallow dataset-specific patterns, leading to substantial degradation under cross-dataset evaluation. In this work, we propose AURORA, a novel hallucination detection framework that shifts the focus from static representations to the weight-gradient dynamics of LLMs. Our key insight is that hallucinated and faithful answers induce qualitatively different gradient update patterns on the model's parameters. Specifically, hallucinated samples trigger asymmetric and structurally misaligned gradients, which can be captured through two complementary features: (1) the skewness of the cosine similarity distribution between weight matrices and their gradient update directions, and (2) the rotation ratio, which quantifies how much the gradient update reorients the singular-vector basis of weight matrices via SVD. AURORA achieves strong hallucination detection performance across four model families and four benchmark datasets. Further analyses demonstrate that our method scales effectively across model sizes and transfers to out-of-domain tasks, including mathematical reasoning and vision-language scenarios.

</details>


### [147] [FADE: Mitigating Hallucinations by Reducing Language-Prior Dominance in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29431)
*Yichen Guo, Kai Tang, Fenglai Lin, Yiding Sun, Dongshuo Zhang, Wenya Wang, Lin William Cong, Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (58.35) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过分析LVLM中信息流，发现FFN模块是关键的语言先验来源，其输出会覆盖视觉证据导致幻觉。提出FADE方法，在解码时衰减FFN输出以缓解语言先验主导，无需训练即可有效降低幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM虽然能力强大但仍易产生幻觉（生成与图像不一致的内容）。已有研究归因于语言先验对视觉输入的支配，但机制根源尚不明确。本文旨在揭示幻觉产生的内部机制并提出缓解方法。

Method: 首先通过分析每个Transformer层的信息流，发现注意力模块始终聚合视觉证据，而关键层的FFN模块是语言先验的来源，这些先验会覆盖视觉证据导致正确预测漂移。基于此提出FADE（FFN Attenuation for DEcoding），一种无需训练的解码方法，在推理时衰减FFN模块的输出以降低语言先验的支配作用。

Result: 在POPE、CHAIR和MME基准上对LLaVA-1.5、mPLUG-Owl2和InstructBLIP进行评估，FADE有效缓解了幻觉，同时保持了推理效率。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了LVLM幻觉的机制根源——关键层FFN模块的语言先验覆盖视觉证据，并提出了FADE这一无需训练的解码方法，有效缓解幻觉且保持推理效率。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LVLM中的幻觉缓解问题，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的[多模态幻觉与可靠性]（CANONICAL）子主题，直接对应。

Importance reason: Addresses hallucination in LVLMs via a novel training-free decoding method, but is not aligned with the user's priority subtopics. Signals: LVLM hallucination, training-free decoding, FFN attenuation, language-prior dominance, mechanistic analysis

Abstract: Despite the impressive capabilities of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs), they remain susceptible to hallucination, generating content inconsistent with the input image. Recent studies attribute this to the dominance of language priors over visual inputs and employ contrastive decoding methods to mitigate this dominance, but the mechanistic origin remains unexplored. We investigate the information flow through each transformer layer and find that attention modules consistently aggregate visual evidence, while FFN modules at critical layers act as the source of language priors. These priors can override visual evidence, causing correct predictions in intermediate layers to drift toward incorrect outputs. Based on this insight, we propose FADE (FFN Attenuation for DEcoding), a training-free method that attenuates FFN outputs to reduce language-prior dominance. Evaluations on POPE, CHAIR, and MME benchmarks across LLaVA-1.5, mPLUG-Owl2, and InstructBLIP show that FADE effectively mitigates hallucinations while preserving inference efficiency.

</details>


### [148] [SEVA: Self-Evolving Verification Agent with Process Reward for Fact Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29713)
*Aojie Yuan, Yi Nian, Haiyue Zhang, Zijian Su, Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 值得关注 (79.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SEVA提出了一种结构化验证Agent，通过将验证质量分解为五个独立组件的过程奖励来解决多组件输出中的优势坍塌问题，并利用Verify→Reflect→Probe→Refine自进化循环提升事实归因能力。SEVA-3B在ClearFacts上匹配GPT-4o-mini的F1分数，同时生成更丰富、可审计的输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的事实归因验证器仅输出不透明的二元标签，导致Agent无法自我纠正，操作员也无法审计。需要一种结构化、可解释且可自我进化的验证机制来缓解LLM的幻觉问题。

Method: SEVA是一个结构化验证Agent，输出证据对齐、逐步推理链、校准置信度和六类错误诊断及可操作修复。训练采用过程奖励机制，将验证质量分解为五个独立组件（70/30权重偏向过程信号），解决标准二元奖励在多组件输出中导致的优势坍塌和GRPO梯度消失问题。此外设计了Verify→Reflect→Probe→Refine四轮自进化循环。

Result: 过程奖励恢复了梯度并诱导隐式课程学习：Agent先掌握验证行为（对齐0.917→0.997，格式72%→100%），再提升结果（F1 64.9→69.0）。自进化循环发现每轮产生基准专家而非通才（HaluEval +15 pp，TruthfulQA -10~-14 pp）。SEVA-3B在ClearFacts上以69.0 F1匹配GPT-4o-mini的69.8 F1。

Conclusion: 验证了对于多组件生成任务的RL训练，奖励粒度必须匹配输出粒度这一原则。SEVA展示了结构化输出+过程奖励+自进化循环的有效性，为可审计、可自我纠正的事实归因验证提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于LLM/Agent的幻觉缓解与事实归因验证，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性（multimodal_hallucination）这一规范主题。论文提出的SEVA是一个结构化验证Agent，通过过程奖励和自进化循环提升事实归因的可靠性，直接对应幻觉缓解与事实性提升。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM hallucination verification, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: process reward for multi-component output, self-evolution loop, matches GPT-4o-mini on fact attribution, addresses advantage collapse in RL, structured verification agent

Abstract: Hallucination is the reliability bottleneck for LLM-based agents, and fact attribution verifiers are the last line of defense -- yet today's verifiers emit only opaque binary labels, leaving agents unable to self-correct and operators unable to audit. We present SEVA, a structured verification agent that emits evidence alignments, step-by-step reasoning chains, calibrated confidence, and a six-category error diagnosis with actionable fixes. Training such an agent with RL is non-trivial: standard binary reward on multi-component output triggers advantage collapse -- within-group reward variance vanishes and the GRPO gradient disappears. We resolve this with a process reward that decomposes verification quality into five independent components weighted 70/30 toward process signals, restoring the gradient and inducing an implicit curriculum -- the agent first masters verification behavior (alignment 0.917 -> 0.997, format 72% -> 100%), then outcomes (F1 64.9 -> 69.0). Structured output further enables a Verify -> Reflect -> Probe -> Refine self-evolution loop, which over four rounds on a 7B model surfaces an unexpected structural finding: each round produces a benchmark-specialist, not a generalist (+15 pp on HaluEval, -10 to -14 pp on TruthfulQA in the same model, persistent at 4x data). On ClearFacts, SEVA-3B matches GPT-4o-mini (69.0 vs. 69.8 F1) while producing substantially richer, auditable output -- confirming a principle that should generalize: for any RL task with multi-component generation, reward granularity must match output granularity.

</details>


### [149] [Reliability-Prioritized Fine-Grained Generation in Multimodal Large](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29573)
*Xiaomeng Fan, Wu Wei, Yuwei Wu, Zhi Gao, Shiyu Luo, Mingyang Gao, Haoyu Zhao, Zhenxin Diao, Yuxuan Ba, Lijia Feng, Yunde Jia, Mehrtash Harandi*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 高优先级 (81.73) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态大模型在细粒度描述生成中比粗粒度生成更容易出错，提出了 GranFact 基准（含粗到细类别标注的多物体图像）和层级感知评估算法，并基于 DPO 设计了可靠性优先的偏好优化方法，在提升细粒度生成的同时保持可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型被期望生成细粒度的视觉内容描述，但细粒度生成比粗粒度生成更容易出错（理论上和实验上均得到验证）。现有方法缺乏对细粒度生成可靠性的系统研究，因此需要一种能平衡细粒度与可靠性的方法。

Method: 1) 构建 GranFact 基准，包含专家验证的多物体图像及粗到细的类别层级标注；2) 设计层级感知评估算法，同时评估预测的视觉正确性和正确预测的具体程度；3) 提出基于 DPO 的可靠性优先偏好优化方法，惩罚不可靠的细粒度断言，奖励可靠的细粒度描述。

Result: 在 GranFact 基准上的实验表明，所提方法在提升细粒度生成能力的同时保持了可靠性，优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 细粒度生成存在固有的可靠性挑战，通过层级感知评估和可靠性优先的偏好优化可以有效缓解这一问题，为多模态大模型生成更可靠、更细粒度的描述提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态大模型在细粒度描述生成中的可靠性问题，提出偏好优化方法惩罚不可靠的细粒度生成。核心关注生成内容的可靠性与事实性，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性范畴，因此复用 [CANONICAL] subtopic 'multimodal_hallucination'。

Importance reason: Addresses reliability in fine-grained MLLM generation, but subtopic is not a priority and lacks direct connection to continuous models or video generation. Signals: MLLM reliability, fine-grained generation, DPO preference optimization, GranFact benchmark, hierarchy-aware evaluation

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly expected to generate fine-grained descriptions of visual content. However, we observe and theoretically show that generating fine-grained responses poses a reliability challenge, \textit{i.e.}, fine-grained generation is more error-prone than coarse-grained generation. This phenomenon suggests that models should generate the finest description that remains reliable rather than simply produce more specific outputs. To investigate this problem, we develop \textsc{GranFact}, a granularity-aware benchmark consisting of expert-verified multi-object images with coarse-to-fine category annotations. Then, we design a hierarchy-aware evaluation algorithm, which assesses both whether model predictions are visually correct and how specific the correct predictions are. We also propose a reliability-prioritized preference optimization method based on Direct Preference Optimization, which penalizes unreliable fine-grained claims while rewarding reliable specificity. Experiments on \textsc{GranFact} show that our method improves fine-grained generation while preserving reliability. Code and data are available \href{ }{here}.

</details>


### [150] [Clearer Sight, Fewer Lies: Oriented Pickup Preference Optimization for Multimodal Hallucination Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29805)
*Xin Zou, Haolin Deng, Yibo Yan, Shuliang Liu, Zhiwei Jin, Chen Chen, Haonan Lu, Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 值得关注 (76.55) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出OPPO（Oriented Pickup Preference Optimization），一种面向多模态大模型幻觉缓解的证据感知对齐方法。通过对比同一忠实回答在不同视觉证据强度下的偏好，将视觉证据强度纳入对齐目标，并结合细粒度正则化稳定训练，有效缓解MLLM的幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在生成时倾向于依赖语言先验而非视觉证据，导致幻觉。作者发现当显式增强与查询相关的视觉证据时，生成变得更准确，说明问题不仅在于感知缺失，更在于模型对已注意到的证据信任不足。

Method: 提出OPPO方法，核心思想是对同一忠实回答在更强、锚定、更弱三种视觉证据视图下进行对比学习，将视觉证据强度偏好转化为有序的视觉证据对齐。同时结合span级和token级细粒度正则化来稳定训练。理论上证明了有序证据间隔能保证局部视觉敏感性的正下界。

Result: 在幻觉评测和通用基准上的大量实验表明，OPPO持续优于基线方法，有效缓解了多模态幻觉问题。

Conclusion: OPPO通过将视觉证据强度纳入偏好优化目标，有效提升了MLLM对视觉证据的信任程度，从而缓解幻觉。该方法在多个基准上取得一致改进，为多模态对齐提供了新视角。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于多模态大模型的幻觉缓解问题，提出基于视觉证据强度的偏好优化方法OPPO，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Addresses MLLM hallucination via evidence-aware alignment, but is not directly aligned with your priority subtopics. Signals: Multimodal hallucination mitigation, Preference optimization, Visual evidence alignment, MLLM reliability, Not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are prone to hallucination as their generation preferences are insufficiently calibrated to visual evidence, causing them to fall back on linguistic priors, rather than faithful grounding. In this work, we start from an empirical observation: when query-relevant visual evidence is explicitly strengthened using the model's own attention, generation becomes more accurate, suggesting that many failures do not arise solely from missing perception, but from an insufficient tendency to trust the evidence the model has already attended to. Motivated by this finding, we propose Oriented Pickup Preference Optimization (\texttt{OPPO}), an evidence-aware alignment objective that learns preferences over the strength of visual evidence, rather than only response quality. Concretely, \texttt{OPPO} contrasts the same faithful response under stronger, anchored, weaker-evidence views, turning naive visual preference into ordered visual-evidence alignment. We further combine this objective with fine-grained span-level and token-level regularization to stabilize the training. Besides, we provide a theoretical analysis showing that ordered evidence margins induce a positive lower bound on local visual sensitivity. Extensive evaluations across hallucination and general-purpose benchmarks demonstrate that \texttt{OPPO} consistently outperforms baseline methods.

</details>


### [151] [See Only When Needed: Context-Aware Attention Intervention for Mitigating Hallucinations in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29847)
*Yuqing Lei, Wenbo Lyu, Yingjun Du, Xiantong Zhen, Cees G.M. Snoek, Ling Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (61.6) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文感知注意力干预（CAI），一种训练无关的推理时机制，通过"仅在需要时看"的原则，在解码时选择性增强视觉注意力以缓解LVLM的物体幻觉，同时保持语言流畅性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM在多模态任务中表现优异，但仍存在物体幻觉问题。现有训练无关的缓解方法通常均匀增强视觉信号，这可能会放大无关区域并引入虚假证据，损害生成流畅性。需要一种更精细的、选择性增强视觉注意力的方法。

Method: CAI通过两轴选择性实现：1）从早期层表示中推导token特定的视觉相关性，定位语义对齐区域；2）在深层中仅对不确定性高的token应用保守的、基于熵和层深门控的注意力倾斜，对置信token和无关区域不做改变。对比解码作为可选的辅助偏置抑制模块。

Result: 在多个LVLM骨干网络和基准上的实验表明，CAI在幻觉缓解方面达到了最先进水平。分析表明CAI是一种KL最小化的注意力重加权方法，在非激活门控或小倾斜下具有有界干扰。

Conclusion: CAI通过选择性注意力干预有效缓解了LVLM的物体幻觉，同时保持了语言流畅性，是一种训练无关、即插即用的有效方法。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LVLM中的物体幻觉缓解问题，提出了一种训练无关的推理时注意力干预方法。这直接属于多模态幻觉与可靠性这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Addresses hallucination in LVLMs with a training-free method, but is not aligned with user's priority on continuous models or generation. Signals: training-free inference, hallucination mitigation, visual attention intervention, LVLM reliability, context-aware selectivity

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) excel at multimodal tasks but remain prone to object hallucinations. Prior training-free remedies often uniformly strengthen visual signals, which may also amplify irrelevant regions and introduce spurious evidence, harming fluency. We propose Context-aware Attention Intervention (CAI), a training-free inference-time mechanism that enforces a see only when needed principle via two-axis selectivity: where to look and when to intervene. At each decoding step, CAI derives token-specific visual relevance from early-layer representations to localize semantically aligned regions, and applies a conservative, entropy- and depth-gated attention tilt only for uncertainty-spiking tokens in deeper layers where visual grounding degrades, leaving confident tokens and irrelevant regions largely unchanged. This targeted intervention strengthens visual grounding while preserving linguistic fluency, and it yields consistent improvements even without contrastive decoding, which remains optional as an auxiliary bias-suppression module. Extensive experiments across multiple LVLM backbones and benchmarks show that CAI achieves state-of-the-art hallucination mitigation, and our analysis characterizes CAI as a KL-minimal attention reweighting with bounded interference under inactive gates or small tilts. Code is available at .

</details>


### [152] [Latent Noise Mask for Reducing Visual Redundancy in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30168)
*Kai Jiang, Ruishu Zhu, Siqi Huang, Hongyuan Zhang, Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 高优先级 (80.98) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Noise Mask (Lens)，一种问题条件化的视觉证据净化框架，通过轻量级Lens Evidence Token (LET) 对视觉token进行相关性评分，并对低相关token注入自适应潜空间噪声以抑制干扰，在不改变模型主干或token序列的情况下提升MLLM的细粒度视觉推理能力，在VQA和定位任务上分别提升2.4-6.4和4.1-6.4个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在细粒度视觉推理中常因密集冗余的图像token稀释了问题相关的视觉线索而失败。现有方法多通过扩展思维链到视觉或潜空间来增加中间推理状态，但忽视了冗余视觉token的负面影响。

Method: 提出Lens框架，包含一个轻量级Lens Evidence Token (LET) 用于对每个视觉token进行问题相关性评分，然后根据评分对低相关token注入自适应的潜空间噪声，从而软性地抑制干扰信息，同时保持模型主干和token序列不变。仅需一个可学习的控制token和一个轻量噪声生成器。

Result: 在多个VQA数据集上提升基础MLLM 2.4-6.4个点，在定位任务上提升4.1-6.4个点，计算开销极小。

Conclusion: 多模态推理可以从更干净的问题相关视觉证据中直接受益，而非简单地扩展推理轨迹。Lens通过潜空间噪声掩码有效净化视觉输入，是一种轻量且有效的方案。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于MLLM中冗余视觉token导致的细粒度视觉推理失败问题，提出通过噪声掩码抑制低相关视觉token来净化视觉证据。这属于多模态幻觉与可靠性范畴（缓解视觉干扰/噪声导致的推理不可靠），因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态幻觉与可靠性"。

Importance reason: Addresses visual redundancy in MLLMs with a lightweight noise injection method, aligning with user's focus on efficient multimodal reasoning. Signals: MLLM visual redundancy reduction, lightweight noise injection, fine-grained VQA and grounding gains, no backbone change, minimal overhead

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often fail in fine-grained visual reasoning, as question-relevant visual cues are diluted by dense and redundant image tokens. Recent multimodal reasoning methods usually extend chain-of-thought from language models into visual or latent spaces, seeking to add intermediate reasoning states while overlooking the negative impact of redundant visual tokens. We propose LatEnt Noise maSk (Lens), a question-conditioned visual evidence purification framework that empowers MLLMs to reason with cleaner visual cues in latent space. Lens introduces a lightweight Lens Evidence Token (LET) to score which visual tokens support the current question and preserve them during decoding. Guided by the LET scores, it injects adaptive latent noise into low-relevance tokens, softly suppressing distractors without changing the model backbone or token sequence. With only one temporary learnable control token and a lightweight noise generator, Lens adds minimal overhead while improving the base MLLM by 2.4-6.4 points on most VQA datasets and by 4.1-6.4 points on grounding tasks. These results show that multimodal reasoning can benefit more directly from cleaner question-relevant visual evidence than from simply extending the reasoning trace.

</details>


### [153] [On the Faithfulness of Post-Hoc Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30498)
*Laines Schmalwasser, Jan Blunk, Niklas Penzel, Julia Niebling, Joachim Denzler*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文分析了事后概念瓶颈模型（Post-Hoc CBMs）中概念投影的忠实性问题，指出仅依赖目标任务准确率会掩盖概念是否语义有意义。作者识别了两种失败模式：辅助数据协变量偏移和 VLM 生成的代理标签噪声，并提出了解耦概念忠实性与预测准确率的新指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Post-Hoc CBM 主要依赖目标任务准确率来评估成功，但这无法区分学到的概念是语义有意义的还是仅仅是预测伪影。例如，随机概念投影也能达到有竞争力的准确率。因此需要直接分析投影的忠实性。

Method: 作者从理论上分析了两种失败模式：(1) 辅助数据协变量偏移导致目标任务的概念表征不忠实，并给出了误差上界；(2) VLM 生成的代理标签中的系统性标签噪声导致不忠实投影。在此基础上，提出了新的度量指标来解耦概念忠实性与预测准确率。

Result: 在真实世界和合成基准上的实验结果表明，所提出的新指标能够识别出标准准确率评估无法检测到的不忠实行为，验证了理论分析的正确性。

Conclusion: Post-Hoc CBM 的评估不能仅依赖准确率，需要直接度量概念投影的忠实性。本文提出的指标为更可靠地评估概念瓶颈模型提供了新工具。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 Post-Hoc Concept Bottleneck Models 中概念投影的忠实性（faithfulness），分析概念表征是否语义有意义而非仅仅是预测伪影，属于多模态模型的可靠性与忠实性范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态幻觉与可靠性（multimodal_hallucination）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on CBM faithfulness, not on user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Post-hoc CBM analysis, concept faithfulness metrics, covariate shift in auxiliary data, VLM label noise, decoupling accuracy from concept meaning

Abstract: Human decision-making interprets the world through high-level concepts, such as recognizing a bird by its belly color. To bridge the gap between opaque deep learning representations and human understanding, Post-Hoc Concept Bottleneck Models (post-hoc CBMs) project latent features onto interpretable concept spaces using auxiliary datasets or vision-language models. However, relying on target task accuracy as the primary measure of post-hoc CBM success obscures whether the learned concepts are semantically meaningful or merely predictive artifacts. For example, random concept projections can achieve competitive accuracy despite being semantically meaningless. In this work, we analyze the learned projections directly and identify two failure cases: First, for concept projections learned from auxiliary data, covariate shifts can lead to unfaithful concept representations for the target task. In particular, we provide an upper bound on the error introduced by this shift. Second, systematic label noise in surrogate concept labels generated by vision-language models leads to unfaithful projections. After formalizing these failure modes, we introduce novel metrics that decouple concept faithfulness from predictive accuracy. Our empirical results across real-world and synthetic benchmarks confirm that these metrics identify unfaithful behaviors that standard accuracy-based evaluation fails to detect.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_rl_alignment'></div>

## 多模态强化学习与对齐 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态 RL、RLHF、DPO、偏好学习、多模态对齐。

### [154] [ComMem: Complementary Memory Systems for Test-Time Adaptation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28719)
*Guanglong Sun, Shuang Cui, Bo Lei, Liyuan Wang, Zihan Zhai, Hongwei Yan, Hang Su, Jun Zhu, Yi Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (61.6) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ComMem 受生物大脑互补记忆系统启发，提出由快速适应详细记忆（类似海马体）和慢速整合抽象记忆（类似新皮层）组成的双记忆系统，用于视觉语言模型的测试时自适应，在15个基准数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 测试时自适应方法要么仅局部适应而不积累知识，要么在单一模态内操作，未能充分利用 VLM 固有的多模态特性。受生物大脑互补记忆系统（海马体与新皮层协同工作）启发，作者希望设计一种能同时进行快速局部适应和慢速全局知识积累的多模态 TTA 方法。

Method: ComMem 包含两个核心组件：(1) 快速适应详细记忆（类似海马体），从高置信度测试样本构建动态视觉缓存；(2) 慢速整合抽象记忆（类似新皮层），持续优化全局文本原型。对每个测试样本，联合优化两个记忆系统以确保跨模态一致性。

Result: 在15个基准数据集上的实验表明，ComMem 在自然分布偏移和跨数据集泛化两种场景下均显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: ComMem 通过模拟生物大脑的互补记忆系统，为 VLM 的测试时自适应提供了一种有效的新范式，增强了 VLM 在实际动态环境中的鲁棒性和适应性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉语言模型（VLM）的测试时自适应（TTA），属于多模态模型在部署阶段的适应与对齐问题。虽然 TTA 不完全等同于 RLHF/DPO，但该方向下的"多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）涵盖了多模态模型的对齐与适应，且该论文的核心是跨模态一致性优化，与对齐主题高度相关。现有 canonical 中无更精确的 TTA 子主题，该子主题是最佳匹配。

Importance reason: Novel bio-inspired memory system for VLM test-time adaptation, but not aligned with user's priority subtopics. Signals: Test-time adaptation, Vision-language models, Complementary memory systems, Bio-inspired, Cross-modal consistency

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) of vision-language models (VLMs) is essential for their robust deployment in dynamic, real-world environments. However, existing TTA methods often adapt locally without accumulating knowledge over time, or operating within a single modality without exploiting VLMs' inherently multi-modal nature. Inspired by the \textbf{Com}plementary \textbf{Mem}ory systems of the biological brain, we propose \textbf{ComMem}, an innovative approach that mimics the distinct but cooperative roles of the hippocampus and neocortex to enable effective TTA for VLMs. ComMem consists of two key components: a fast-adapting detailed memory, akin to the hippocampus, that forms a dynamic visual cache from high-confidence test samples; and a slow-integrating abstract memory, akin to the neocortex, that continually refines global textual prototypes. For each test instance, ComMem jointly optimizes both memory systems to ensure cross-modal consistency. Extensive experiments on 15 benchmark datasets show that ComMem significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods under both natural distribution shifts and cross-dataset generalization, offering a promising direction for enhancing VLMs' practical adaptability.

</details>


### [155] [Personalizing MLLMs via Reinforced Multimodal Reference Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28845)
*Deepayan Das, Davide Talon, Yiming Wang, Massimiliano Mancini, Elisa Ricci*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (81.73) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Reinforced Reference Game (RRG)，一种基于强化学习的多模态参考游戏框架，让 MLLM 同时扮演说话者和听者角色，通过对比性奖励（硬正例和硬负例）优化个性化概念描述的区分性，在三个个性化基准上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 个性化方法依赖概念描述和推理，但生成的描述常包含状态、上下文等无关信息，无法有效区分目标概念与视觉相似的其他物品。需要一种方法促使 MLLM 生成准确、有区分性且无干扰细节的概念描述。

Method: 提出 Reinforced Reference Game (RRG) 框架，MLLM 同时扮演说话者（生成概念描述）和听者（根据描述识别概念），在对比性游戏设置中通过强化学习优化。奖励信号基于硬正例（同一概念的不同视角）和硬负例（视觉相似但不同的概念）的对比区分能力，形成可验证的对比性奖励。

Result: 在三个个性化基准的多个任务上达到 SOTA，能够泛化到未见领域，优于基于概念描述和个性化 RL 框架的现有方法。

Conclusion: RRG 通过强化多模态参考游戏有效提升了 MLLM 个性化概念描述的区分性，验证了对比性奖励在个性化任务中的有效性，且具有良好的跨领域泛化能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Reinforced Reference Game (RRG)，通过强化学习（RL）框架优化 MLLM 对个性化概念的描述，使其更具区分性。核心是使用对比性奖励信号进行偏好学习，属于多模态强化学习与对齐方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐" (multimodal_rl_alignment)。

Importance reason: Strong MLLM personalization via RL, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: MLLM personalization, reinforcement learning, contrastive reward, hard positives/negatives, state-of-the-art on benchmarks

Abstract: Personalizing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) aims to recognize users' unique concepts from visual data and provide personalized responses. Although prior work has shown the benefit of concept descriptions and reasoning for this task, MLLM descriptions often include information, such as state and context, that does not help and may in fact hinder the unique identification of the target concept among other visually similar items. Effective descriptions of personal concepts should instead be accurate, discriminative, and free of distracting details. To achieve such descriptions, we introduce Reinforced Reference Game (RRG), a learning framework that promotes discriminative descriptions through a novel reinforced multimodal reference game. The MLLM plays both the roles of speaker and listener in a contrastive game setting, whose goal is to effectively communicate discriminative information about a target concept. Our approach formulates a verifiable contrastive reward over hard positives (dissimilar views of the same concept) and hard negatives (visually similar but different concepts). Empirically, RRG achieves state-of-the-art across multiple tasks on three personalization benchmarks. RRG generalizes to unseen domains and outperforms existing methods based on concept descriptions and personalization-specific RL frameworks. We will release code and models in the project page.

</details>


### [156] [KbSD: Knowledge Boundary aware Self-Distillation for Behavioral Calibration in Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29863)
*Tao Feng, Xinke Jiang, Chao Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: KbSD提出知识边界自蒸馏框架，通过密集token级监督、稀疏奖励和象限自适应优化，解决智能体搜索中知识边界校准（何时信任记忆、检索或拒绝）的奖励稀疏问题，在多个基准上提升了任务准确率并缓解了幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在智能体搜索中面临奖励稀疏问题，二元奖励只能惩罚不良结果，无法对推理过程提供有效指导，导致模型难以在不同知识状态下做出校准决策（何时信任参数记忆、何时依赖检索、何时拒绝回答）。

Method: 提出KbSD框架，包含：(1) 构建提示增强的教师模型（与学生架构相同），接收知识边界信号（参数确定性、检索质量、真实答案）生成校准推理示范；(2) 信息不对称的自蒸馏实现密集监督；(3) 象限自适应蒸馏目标：反向KL用于集中整合、前向KL用于多样化拒绝、Pareto最优双向KL用于需要精度和覆盖的不对称象限。

Result: 在多个基准上，KbSD在任务准确率和幻觉缓解方面持续优于强基线，在奖励稀疏最具挑战性的象限中提升最大。

Conclusion: 知识边界自蒸馏通过密集token级监督和象限自适应优化，有效解决了智能体搜索中奖励稀疏导致的知识边界校准问题，为LLM在动态检索场景中的行为校准提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于知识边界的自蒸馏框架（KbSD），通过密集token级监督和象限自适应优化来校准LLM在智能体搜索中的行为（何时信任参数记忆、何时依赖检索、何时拒绝回答）。这属于多模态/语言模型的对齐与偏好优化范畴，与[多模态强化学习与对齐]（multimodal_rl_alignment）方向高度吻合，因此复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on agentic search and knowledge boundary calibration, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Self-distillation for behavioral calibration, addresses reward sparsity in agentic search, uses quadrant-adaptive optimization, improves task accuracy and reduces hallucination, no direct link to user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Agentic search equips large language models with dynamic retrieval abilities, but existing reinforcement learning methods remain limited by reward sparsity in knowledge boundary calibration -- deciding when to trust parametric memory, when to rely on retrieved evidence, and when to abstain. Binary rewards can penalize undesirable outcomes, but provide little guidance on the reasoning process required to make calibrated decisions across different knowledge states. To address this, we propose KbSD (Knowledge boundary Self-Distillation), a framework that tackles this limitation through dense token-level supervision, outcome-level sparse rewards, and quadrant-adaptive optimization. KbSD constructs a hint-augmented teacher, architecturally identical to the student, that receives explicit knowledge boundary signals -- including parametric certainty, retrieval quality, and ground-truth answers -- to generate calibrated reasoning demonstrations. This information-asymmetric self-distillation enables dense supervision without requiring a larger external model. To further account for the heterogeneous reasoning distributions across knowledge states, we introduce a quadrant-adaptive distillation objective: reverse KL for concentrated integration, forward KL for diverse refusal, and Pareto-optimal bidirectional KL for asymmetric quadrants requiring both precision and coverage. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that KbSD consistently improves both task accuracy and hallucination mitigation over strong baselines, with the largest gains appearing in the challenging quadrants where sparse rewards are least informative.

</details>


### [157] [Be Faithful When Response: Returning Fluent and Grounded Answers for Vision-Language Models Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29984)
*Peng, Yin Zhang, Yanglin Zhang, Haonan Wu, Zishan Liu, Ruoxi Zang, Xin Zhu, Jiayin Zheng, Jian Yao, Zefeng Ji, Fei Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Faithful Warm-Start (FWS) 策略，通过构建具有显式视觉-语言因果关系的 FaithfulQA 数据集，在强化学习前对 VLM 进行预热，使其生成视觉忠实且流畅的推理轨迹，从而缓解 RL 训练中忽视视觉证据、利用语言先验导致的不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接对 VLM 应用强化学习进行多模态推理时，模型容易利用语言先验而忽视视觉证据，生成流畅但缺乏视觉依据的推理轨迹，导致训练不稳定。作者希望先引导策略朝向视觉忠实的推理方向，再进行 RL 优化。

Method: 1) 从六个通用 VQA 基准中筛选具有显式视觉-语言因果关系的样本，构建 FaithfulQA 数据集，每个样本包含视觉观察、问题需求、常识知识、领域知识和最终答案。2) 使用 VLM 作为裁判进一步净化数据集，确保因果一致性和视觉忠实性。3) 在预热阶段用该数据集训练模型，使其掌握因果对齐的视觉-语言模式，再在稀疏答案级奖励下进行后续 RL 优化。

Result: 实验表明，FWS 策略提升了答案准确率，稳定了 RL 训练过程，并减少了缺乏视觉支持的推理生成。

Conclusion: 在 RL 之前引入视觉忠实的预热阶段，能够有效引导 VLM 生成基于视觉证据的推理，提升多模态 RL 训练的稳定性和模型可靠性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Faithful Warm-Start (FWS) 策略，在强化学习前通过因果对齐数据预热 VLM，以提升视觉忠实性并稳定 RL 训练。核心贡献在于多模态 RL 训练中的对齐与稳定性，属于 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐 (multimodal_rl_alignment) 范畴。

Importance reason: Addresses VLM RL instability via visual faithfulness, but focuses on RL alignment rather than core continuous/multimodal generation or world model priorities. Signals: VLM reinforcement learning, visual faithfulness, RL warm-start, multimodal reasoning, not continuous token or video generation

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) is an important paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of Vision-Language Models (VLMs). However, directly applying RL to rollout multimodal reasoning can lead to instability, due to the exploitation of language priors, the neglect of visual evidence, and the generation of reasoning traces that are fluent yet not visually grounded. The question arises: Can initially steer the policy toward visually faithful reasoning regime before applying reinforcement learning? To this end, we propose a Faithful Warm-Start (FWS) strategy that first curates samples with explicit vision-language causal relationships from six general VQA benchmarks to construct the FaithfulQA dataset, where each of the image-question pairs gains a certain degree of visual observations, question requirements, commonsense knowledge, domain knowledge, and the final answer. Subsequently, a VLM-based judge is employed to further purify the dataset, ensuring strong causal consistency and visual faithfulness. This warm-start stage equips the model with the capability to understand causally grounded vision-language patterns before subsequent RL optimization under sparse answer-level rewards. Experimental results show that such faithful supervision improves answer accuracy, stabilizes RL training, and reduces visually unsupported reasoning.

</details>


### [158] [MIRROR: Aligning Semantic Relations from Language to Image via Gromov--Wasserstein](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29462)
*Hong-Han Wang, Yuntao Wang, Hu Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (80.98) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MIRROR 提出一种基于 Semi-Inverse Gromov-Wasserstein (SI-GW) 的几何正则化框架，将语言模型中蕴含的概念间关系先验对齐到视觉表征空间，以解决 MLLM 在视觉上下文中无法正确应用语义关系的问题。该方法具有闭式解且计算高效，无需额外参数或推理成本，在提升关系一致性的同时保持通用视觉语言任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）虽然从语言骨干中继承了丰富的语义关系先验，但在视觉上下文中应用这些关系时常常失败。作者将这一失败归因于结构盲点：基于投影的对齐方法训练每个视觉 token 携带正确的语义，但从未检查概念之间的关系是否在从语言到视觉的跨模态迁移中得以保留。

Method: 提出 MIRROR 框架，核心是 Semi-Inverse Gromov-Wasserstein (SI-GW) 问题——一个逆几何优化问题，通过最优传输正则化将视觉表征与语言导出的关系先验对齐。该问题具有唯一闭式解，可高效计算。在 decoder-only Transformer 中，作者设计了层、头、token 三个层面的策略来稳定提取关系结构，无需额外参数或推理成本。

Result: MIRROR 在多个视觉语言任务上提升了关系一致性，同时保持或提升了通用视觉语言任务的性能。实验表明该方法有效弥补了 MLLM 在视觉关系理解上的结构盲点。

Conclusion: MIRROR 通过几何正则化框架成功将语言中的关系先验迁移到视觉表征空间，解决了 MLLM 在视觉上下文中应用语义关系的结构性问题。该方法高效且无需额外参数，为多模态对齐提供了新的几何视角。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MIRROR 框架，通过 Gromov-Wasserstein 最优传输方法将语言中的关系先验对齐到视觉表征，属于多模态对齐（multimodal alignment）范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "多模态强化学习与对齐" (multimodal_rl_alignment)。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel geometric regularization for aligning language relational priors to vision, directly addressing a key MLLM limitation with efficient closed-form solution. Signals: MLLM relational consistency, Gromov-Wasserstein alignment, closed-form solution, no extra parameters, efficient computation

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) inherit rich relational priors from their language backbones, yet often fail when asked to apply these relationships in visual contexts. We trace this failure to a structural blind spot: projection-based alignment trains each visual token to carry the right semantics, but never asks whether the relationships between concepts survive the crossing from language to vision. To address this, we propose MIRROR (Mapping Inter-concept Relations from language to visual Representation via Optimal-transport-based Regularization), a geometric regularization framework that transfers relational priors from language to vision by exploiting the rich relational structure encoded in language representations. Specifically, we derive a surrogate loss from the proposed Semi-Inverse Gromov-Wasserstein (SI-GW) problem, an inverse geometric problem that aligns visual representations with language-derived relational priors. We show that this formulation admits a unique closed-form solution that prescribes the ideal visual relational structure implied by language geometry and cross-modal coupling. The structure of the formulation also enables efficient computation, making it applicable to long token sequences. Applying SI-GW inside decoder-only Transformers requires careful design. We introduce targeted strategies at the layer, head, and token levels to ensure stable extraction without additional parameters or inference cost. MIRROR improves relational consistency while preserving performance on general vision-language tasks.

</details>


### [159] [Does Role Specialization Matter for Explanation Faithfulness in Mixture-of-Experts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29613)
*Yeji Kim, Housam Babiker, Mi-Young Kim, Randy Goebel*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究 MoE 架构中角色分解（如 I2MoE 中的协同、冗余、唯一性角色）是否足以保证解释忠实性。发现专家间表征重叠会削弱角色分离效果，降低基于归因的解释忠实性。提出表征级去相关正则化来减少专家间相似性，实验表明该方法在多个多模态基准上显著提升了解释的全面性、充分性和 AOPC 指标，同时保持任务性能，且效果不限于特定角色架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 架构虽然通过角色分解（如 I2MoE 中的语义角色分配）提升了可解释性，但专家间表征重叠可能削弱角色分离效果，导致基于归因的解释忠实性下降。现有工作缺乏对结构角色分解是否足以保证解释忠实性的系统研究。

Method: 提出表征级去相关正则化（representation-level decorrelation regularization），通过最小化专家间表征重叠来鼓励更清晰的专家专业化。在 I2MoE 和标准稀疏 MoE 两种架构上应用该方法，使用 comprehensiveness、sufficiency 和 AOPC 等指标评估解释忠实性。

Result: 在多个多模态基准上，表征分离一致地提升了解释忠实性（comprehensiveness、sufficiency 和 AOPC 指标），同时保持了任务性能。效果在 I2MoE 和标准稀疏 MoE 上均成立，表明表征级分离是提升 MoE 解释忠实性的通用机制。

Conclusion: 结构角色分解本身不足以保证忠实解释，表征级分离是提升 MoE 系统解释忠实性的更通用机制。该发现为设计更可解释的 MoE 架构提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 MoE 架构中专家角色分解对解释忠实性的影响，并提出表征级去相关正则化来提升多模态场景下的解释忠实性。这与多模态对齐（解释忠实性可视为模型输出与人类可理解解释之间的对齐）密切相关，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐 (multimodal_rl_alignment)。

Importance reason: Focuses on MoE interpretability, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: MoE interpretability, explanation faithfulness, representation decorrelation, multimodal benchmarks, not aligned with user's top priority subtopics

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have recently been extended with role-based mechanisms for interpretability. This is typically done by assigning semantic roles to individual expert components, for example roles like synergy, redundancy, and uniqueness in multimodal settings. However, whether such structural role decomposition preserves explanation faithfulness of the overall architecture remains largely underexplored. We hypothesize that inter-expert representation overlap weakens effective role separation and degrades attribution-based faithfulness, even when semantic roles are explicitly defined. To address this limitation, we introduce representation-level decorrelation regularization to explicitly reduce inter-expert similarity in latent space. Using representation decorrelation objectives, we encourage clearer specialization among experts by minimizing representation overlap. Our experiments show that across multiple multimodal benchmarks, this separation consistently improves explanation faithfulness, as measured by comprehensiveness, sufficiency, and their Area Over the Perturbation Curve (AOPC) summaries, while preserving task performance. We further show that these improvements are not limited to role-based architectures such as Interpretable Multimodal Interaction-aware MoE (I2MoE). Similar trends are observed in a standard sparse MoE baseline, suggesting that representation-level separation may provide a more general mechanism for enhancing explanation faithfulness in MoE systems. Overall, our findings suggest that structural role decomposition alone may be insufficient to guarantee faithful explanations and that representation-level separation helps improve explanation faithfulness. To support reproducibility, the source code and supplementary material are publicly available at .

</details>


### [160] [DOPD: Dual On-policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30626)
*Xinlei Yu, Gen Li, Qingyi Si, Guibin Zhang, Yuqi Xu, Congcong Wang, Shuai Dong, Kaiwen Tuo, Xiangyu Zeng, Kaituo Feng, Qunzhong Wang, Yang Shi, Xiaobin Hu, Xiangyu Yue, Jiaqi Wang, Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DOPD 提出一种优势感知的双在线策略蒸馏范式，通过动态路由 token 级监督信号在特权教师和学生策略之间分配，缓解了特权信息带来的"特权幻觉"问题，在 LLM 和 VLM 蒸馏任务上均优于传统在线策略蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）通过监督学生采样的轨迹实现能力迁移，但引入特权信息（如未来 token、全局信息）会导致"特权幻觉"——学生无法区分可迁移的能力差距与不可复现的信息不对称差距，且 token 级监督存在非均匀性，仅有少量 token 承载关键能力信号。

Method: 提出 DOPD（Dual On-policy Distillation），构建特权教师和特权学生双策略，基于优势差距和相对概率动态路由每个 token 的监督来源：部分 token 从特权教师接收强监督，部分从学生自身接收辅助信号，从而在传递可信能力的同时缓解特权幻觉。

Result: 在 LLM 和 VLM 设置下，DOPD 一致优于传统 OPD 及其他对比方法；在稳定性、鲁棒性、持续学习和分布外任务上均验证了其优越性。

Conclusion: DOPD 通过优势感知的双策略蒸馏范式有效解决了特权信息引入的幻觉问题，为知识蒸馏提供了一种更可靠的能力迁移框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一种基于优势感知的双策略蒸馏方法 DOPD，用于 LLM 和 VLM 的知识蒸馏，核心涉及策略优化、优势函数、偏好学习等对齐技术，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on distillation for LLM/VLM alignment, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Privilege illusion in distillation, Token-level dynamic routing, LLM and VLM distillation, Outperforms vanilla OPD, Not in priority subtopics

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) offers superior capacity transfer by supervising student-sampled trajectories with dense token-level signals. To furnish high-quality supervision sources and thereby elevate the performance frontier of distillation, an intuitive direction is to infuse privileged information to either teacher or student itself. However, this additional input induces a potential failure mode we dub privilege illusion: a pattern that conflates the transferable capability gap that students are meant to close, and the information asymmetry gap that can only be mimicked but never replicated. This issue is further amplified by the inherent non-uniformity of token-level supervision, where only a small subset of tokens carries pivotal capability-bearing signals. To this end, we propose DOPD, an advantage-aware dual distillation paradigm that dynamically routes token-level supervision between privileged teacher and privileged student policies based on their advantage gap and relative probabilities. Each token receives supervision of different strength, objective, and strategy from either teacher or student itself, which transfers credible capability while simultaneously receiving auxiliary signals, to alleviate privilege illusion. Extensive experiments on both large language model (LLM) and vision-language model (VLM) settings demonstrate that DOPD consistently outperforms Vanilla OPD and other counterparts. Further results on stability, robustness, continual learning, and out-of-distribution tasks validate its superiority.

</details>


### [161] [Same Concept, Different Directions: Cross-Modal Feature Heterogeneity in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29888)
*Chungpa Lee, Jihoon Kwon, Kyle Min, Jy-yong Sohn*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉-语言模型的联合嵌入空间中，同一概念在图像和文本模态下对应的特征方向存在差异（称为跨模态特征异质性），导致模态分裂问题。为此提出训练模态特定的稀疏自编码器保留各模态特征几何，再事后对齐对应特征，提升了重建保真度及跨模态检索和概念操控性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏自编码器（SAE）应用于多模态联合嵌入空间时，隐含假设语义对应的特征在不同模态下共享相同方向。本文质疑这一假设，旨在揭示并解决跨模态特征方向不一致的问题，以提升多模态表征的可解释性和可控性。

Method: 1) 识别跨模态特征异质性现象：同一概念在图像和文本模态下激活不同的 SAE 潜变量。2) 提出模态特定 SAE 训练策略：分别为图像和文本模态训练独立的 SAE，保留各模态的特征几何结构。3) 事后对齐：通过匹配对应概念的特征方向，实现跨模态特征对齐。

Result: 实验表明，模态特定 SAE 相比联合 SAE 在重建保真度上有显著提升，并在跨模态检索和概念操控任务中取得更好性能，验证了跨模态特征异质性的存在及所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 跨模态特征异质性是多模态联合嵌入空间中一个被忽视的关键问题，模态特定 SAE 加事后对齐是一种有效的解决方案，为多模态表征的可解释性和可控性研究提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态（视觉-语言）嵌入空间的可解释性与可控性，通过稀疏自编码器分析跨模态特征对齐问题并提出对齐方法，属于多模态对齐（multimodal alignment）范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）子主题。

Importance reason: Addresses a fundamental assumption in multimodal interpretability but is not directly aligned with your priority subtopics. Signals: Cross-modal feature heterogeneity, Modality split in SAEs, Post-hoc feature alignment, Improves cross-modal retrieval and concept steering

Abstract: Vision-language models map images and text into a joint embedding space. However, these embeddings often entangle multiple semantic features, which limits their interpretability and controllability. While sparse autoencoders have emerged as a useful tool for decomposing these embeddings into monosemantic features, their application to joint embedding spaces has largely relied on an implicit, untested assumption that semantically corresponding features share the same directions across modalities. In this paper, we challenge this assumption by identifying discrepancies in feature directions for the same concept across image and text modalities, a phenomenon we term cross-modal feature heterogeneity. We demonstrate that this heterogeneity is a key driver of the modality split, where a shared concept activates different latents depending on the modality. This finding further reveals why aligning latent activations alone is insufficient to resolve the underlying feature mismatch. Motivated by this observation, we propose an approach that trains modality-specific sparse autoencoders to preserve each modality's feature geometry, and then aligns corresponding features post hoc. Our method improves reconstruction fidelity and enhances performance in cross-modal retrieval and concept steering.

</details>


### [162] [Consistency as Inductive Bias: Learning Cross-View Invariance for Robust Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29812)
*Xin Zou, Haolin Deng, Yibo Yan, Shuliang Liu, Kening Zheng, Zhiwei Jin, Chen Chen, Haonan Lu, Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (80.98) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ConsistRoll 通过在 GRPO 的组采样机制中将原始视图与语义不变增强视图放在同一生成组，并分配联合奖励（要求成对输出既正确又一致），将跨视角一致性作为归纳偏置注入 MLLM 的强化学习训练，在不增加生成开销和标注的情况下提升了多模态推理的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 RLVR 训练对每个视觉输入分配点态奖励，即使使用数据增强，变换后的视图也独立获得奖励，缺乏跨视角一致性约束。作者识别出 MLLM 中缺少一个关键的归纳偏置：同一实例的语义不变视图应产生相同答案。

Method: ConsistRoll 复用 GRPO 的组采样机制，将原始视图和语义不变的增强视图放在同一生成组中，只有当配对输出同时正确且一致时才分配联合奖励。该方法将一致性转化为在线信用分配信号，无需额外生成开销和标注。理论上证明了跨视角一致性是有效的归纳偏置，且 ConsistRoll 引入了 DA 所缺失的跨视角校正项，惩罚视图依赖性并缓解优势坍塌。

Result: 在数学、通用、幻觉等多个领域的综合基准上，ConsistRoll 在多模态推理中取得了鲁棒的改进。

Conclusion: 跨视角一致性是一种有效的归纳偏置，ConsistRoll 通过简单的组奖励机制将其注入 RLVR 训练，显著提升了 MLLM 的推理鲁棒性，且无需额外开销。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ConsistRoll 方法，将跨视角一致性注入 MLLM 的强化学习训练（RLVR/GRPO），属于多模态 RL 与对齐方向，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 "多模态强化学习与对齐" (multimodal_rl_alignment)。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel consistency-based inductive bias for MLLM RL training that improves multimodal reasoning robustness without extra cost. Signals: cross-view consistency, GRPO group-sampling reuse, joint reward for correct and consistent pairs, theoretical cross-view correction term, robust multimodal reasoning improvement

Abstract: Inductive biases steer learning toward generalizable solutions by encoding task structure. In this work, we identify a crucial missing bias in MLLMs: cross-view consistency, \textit{i.e.}, semantically invariant views of the same instance should lead to the same answer. Standard reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) objectives do not impose this constraint, but instead assign pointwise rewards to each visual input. Even with data augmentation (DA), transformed views are typically rewarded independently, providing little signal once within-view rewards saturate. We propose \textbf{ConsistRoll}, a simple but effective method that injects cross-view consistency into RLVR training by reusing the group-sampling mechanism of GRPO. Specifically, ConsistRoll places original and semantically invariant transformed views in the same generation group, and assigns a joint reward only when paired completions are both correct and consistent. In this way, ConsistRoll turns consistency into an online credit-assignment signal, \textbf{without extra generation overhead and annotations}. Theoretically, we show that cross-view consistency is a valid inductive bias, and ConsistRoll introduces a cross-view correction term absent from DA, penalizing view dependence and alleviating advantage collapse. Comprehensive benchmarks across math, general-purpose, hallucination domains confirm that ConsistRoll achieves robust improvements in multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [163] [DRIFT: Difficulty Routing Self-DIstillation with Rhythm-Gated Exploration and Success BuFfer Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30345)
*Haisen Luo, Yiwei Liu, Haoning Wang, Dan Liu, Junxi Yin, Haotian Wang, Lei Zhang, Xiaoyu Tian, Shuaiting Chen, Yuansheng Song, Baoyan Guo, Xiongfei Yan, Bolan Yang, Chengwei Liu, Ming Cui, Jiong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (77.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DRIFT是一个面向大语言模型的在线自我进化策略优化框架，通过难度路由（Difficulty Routing）识别问题级学习状态并动态分配自蒸馏与强化学习信号，结合节奏门控（Rhythm Gating）在token级别聚焦关键推理位置的探索，配合成功缓冲区和两阶段课程学习策略，在五个基准和三种模型规模上全面超越GRPO和SDPO，平均得分达79.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自蒸馏和强化学习方法缺乏显式机制来追踪问题级别的学习进度并相应调整优化策略，导致训练可能过度优化简单问题、从困难问题中获得弱监督信号、且未能充分探索边界情况。作者希望设计一种能够根据模型在每个问题上的学习状态动态调整优化策略的框架，实现稳定的自我改进。

Method: DRIFT框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 难度路由（Difficulty Routing）——在问题级别识别模型的学习状态，动态分配自蒸馏和强化学习信号；(2) 节奏门控（Rhythm Gating）——在token级别细化策略更新，将探索集中在关键推理位置；(3) 成功缓冲区（Success Buffer）与两阶段课程学习策略——保留高质量历史经验，逐步引导模型从可靠行为获取向稳定策略进化过渡。

Result: 在五个基准测试和三种模型规模上，DRIFT在所有评估指标上均超越GRPO和SDPO的峰值性能。五个基准的平均得分为79.5%，超过GRPO 9.5%、超过SDPO 7.5%。在ToolUse任务上达到79.2%的准确率，超过GRPO 13.5%、超过SDPO 10.7%，创下新的最先进结果。

Conclusion: DRIFT通过难度路由和节奏门控的联合使用，有效解决了现有方法在问题级学习状态追踪和优化策略自适应方面的不足，在多个基准上取得了显著提升，验证了在线自我进化策略优化的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出DRIFT框架，通过难度路由（Difficulty Routing）和节奏门控（Rhythm Gating）优化LLM的自我进化策略，属于强化学习与偏好优化范畴。虽然论文主要针对纯文本LLM，但其方法（自蒸馏、RL信号分配、成功缓冲区等）与多模态RL对齐方向（multimodal_rl_alignment）的方法论高度一致，且该方向涵盖RLHF、DPO、偏好学习等核心主题，DRIFT提出的改进策略可迁移至多模态场景。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM self-evolution optimization, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: LLM self-improvement, difficulty routing, self-distillation, reinforcement learning, GRPO/SDPO comparison

Abstract: Enabling large language models to achieve stable self-improvement without external expert supervision remains a central challenge in complex reasoning tasks. Existing self-distillation and reinforcement learning methods lack explicit mechanisms for tracking problem-level learning progress and adapting optimization strategies accordingly. Consequently, training may over-optimize easy problems, receive weak supervision from hard problems, and fail to sufficiently explore borderline cases. To resolve these issues, we propose DRIFT, an online self-evolution policy optimization framework for large language models. DRIFT regulates the model's self-improvement process through the joint use of Difficulty Routing and Rhythm Gating. The former identifies the model's learning state at the problem level and dynamically allocates self-distillation and reinforcement learning signals, while the latter refines policy updates at the token level, concentrating exploration on critical reasoning positions. By further incorporating a success buffer and a two-stage curriculum learning strategy, DRIFT preserves high-quality historical experience while progressively guiding the model from reliable behavior acquisition toward stable policy evolution. Evaluated across five benchmarks and three model scales, DRIFT surpasses the peak performance of both GRPO and SDPO across all evaluated metrics. On the average score over the five benchmarks, DRIFT achieves 79.5$\%$, outperforming GRPO by 9.5$\%$ and SDPO by 7.5$\%$, establishing a new state-of-the-art result. Notably, on ToolUse, DRIFT reaches an accuracy of 79.2$\%$, improving over GRPO by 13.5$\%$ and SDPO by 10.7$\%$, setting a new state-of-the-art and substantially outperforming all concurrent methods.

</details>


### [164] [Pessimism's Paradox: Conservative Offline Training Amplifies Reward Hacking During Online Adaptation in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30627)
*Subramanyam Sahoo, Aman Chadha, Vinija Jain, Divya Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, stat.ML

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (55.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"保守离线训练能安全支撑后续在线适应"的直觉，通过实验证明更高的离线保守性（DPO β）反而会加剧在线适应中的奖励黑客行为，并揭示了熵压缩→响应多样性降低→奖励模型不确定性被利用的因果链，提出需要"校准的"而非"最大的"保守性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍认为保守离线训练（如高β DPO）可以防止策略在在线适应中过度利用奖励模型的缺陷，但这一直觉缺乏实证检验。本文旨在系统性地验证离线保守程度与在线奖励黑客行为之间的关系。

Method: 使用 Qwen3-14B 策略，在三个保守级别（β_lo, β_mid, β_hi）下进行 DPO 离线训练，然后对每个检查点使用奖励模型集成（3×Qwen3-1.7B）进行在线适应，以 GSM8K 精确匹配准确率作为真实性能指标，通过 Goodhart gap 和 AUGC 量化奖励黑客损伤。

Result: 更高的离线保守性单调地加剧了奖励黑客损伤（Spearman ρ=1.0）。机制分析表明：高β DPO 压缩策略熵→低熵策略生成低多样性响应→响应集中在奖励模型训练分布的狭窄区域→尽管接近训练分布，但集成分歧（认知不确定性）随β增加且在线优化中更快被利用。进一步拟合出幂律曲线并识别出最优保守水平 β*。

Conclusion: 离线训练需要"校准的"而非"最大的"保守性，过度的保守性反而会适得其反。该发现对 RLHF/DPO 的实际应用具有重要指导意义，提示社区应重新审视保守离线训练的安全假设。

Classification reason: 该论文研究保守离线训练（DPO）对在线强化学习适应中奖励黑客行为的影响，属于多模态/语言模型的对齐与偏好优化范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）高度相关。

Importance reason: Challenges a core assumption in RLHF alignment, but is not directly about the user's priority subtopics. Signals: DPO conservatism paradox, reward hacking amplification, entropy-compression causal chain, Goodhart gap analysis, calibrated conservatism needed

Abstract: Conservative offline training is widely advocated as a safe foundation for subsequent online adaptation: if a policy stays close to well-supported behaviour, the argument goes, it is less likely to exploit imperfections in a learned reward model. We challenge this intuition empirically and mechanistically. We train a Qwen3-14B policy under Direct Preference Optimisation (DPO) with three levels of conservatism ($\beta \in \{\beta_{\mathrm{lo}}, \beta_{\mathrm{mid}}, \beta_{\mathrm{hi}}\}$ derived from empirical log-ratio percentiles), then adapt each checkpoint online against a learned reward ensemble (3\,$\times$\,Qwen3-1.7B) while measuring true performance on GSM8K exact-answer accuracy. We find that \emph{higher offline conservatism monotonically increases reward-hacking damage}, measured by the Goodhart gap and its area under the curve (AUGC), with Spearman $\rho = 1.0$ across all three conditions. Mechanistic analysis reveals a three-link causal chain: (i) high-$\beta$ DPO compresses policy entropy, (ii) Low-entropy policies generate responses with reduced diversity, concentrating in a narrow region of the reward model's training distribution (lower pairwise cosine distance), and (iii) despite this proximity, ensemble disagreement (epistemic uncertainty) increases with $\beta$ and is exploited faster during online optimisation. We further fit a power-law curve to the $(\beta, \augc)$ data and identify a practical optimal conservatism level $\beta^{\star}$ that balances alignment fidelity against hacking vulnerability. Our results suggest that the field needs \emph{calibrated}, not \emph{maximal}, conservatism.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_reasoning_cot'></div>

## 多模态推理与思维链 [[Back]](#toc)
