### [180] [DataComp-VLM: Improved Open Datasets for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28551)
*Matteo Farina, Vishaal Udandarao, Thao Nguyen, Selim Kuzucu, Maximilian Böther, Andreas Hochlehnert, Adhiraj Ghosh, Marianna Nezhurina, Karsten Roth, Joschka Struber, Yuhui Zhang, Sebastian Dziadzio, Elaine Sui, Soumya Jahagirdar, Dhruba Ghosh, Hasan Hammoud, Thomas De Min, Simone Caldarella, Jehanzeb Mirza, Sedrick Keh, Mehdi Cherti, Hilde Kuehne, Bernt Schiele, Serena Yeung-Levy, Muhammad Ferjad Naeem, Federico Tombari, Ana Klimovic, Elisa Ricci, Matthias Bethge, Sewoong Oh, Ameya Prabhu, Alessio Tonioni, Jenia Jitsev, Massimiliano Mancini, Ludwig Schmidt, Nikhil Parthasarathy*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (59.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 DataComp-VLM (DCVLM)，一个用于系统评估 VLM 训练数据策展策略的基准平台。它收集了 160 个数据集（6T 多模态 token），支持在 1B-8B 模型和 6.25B-200B token 预算下测试过滤、混合、格式化、采样等策展策略，并在 52 个下游基准上评测。实验发现数据混合比过滤更关键，指令数据占比高的混合策略在大规模下表现更好。DCVLM-Baseline 数据集在 8B 模型上达到 63.6% 准确率，比 FineVision 提升 5.4 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建高性能 VLM 需要精心策展大规模训练数据集，但社区缺乏系统性的基准来评估不同的数据策展策略（如过滤、混合、格式化、采样等），导致数据策展方法难以公平比较和系统优化。

Method: DCVLM 收集了 160 个数据集，涵盖四种数据类型（图像-文本对、多模态交错文档、纯文本、指令微调数据），组成 6T 多模态 token 的语料库。它提供了一个受控实验框架，允许参与者在 1B-8B 模型规模和 6.25B-200B token 预算下测试不同的数据策展策略，并在精心挑选的 52 个下游基准（涵盖 9 个领域）上进行评测。

Result: 实验发现数据混合（mixing）比数据过滤（filtering）对训练质量影响更大；指令数据占比高的混合策略比纯图文对混合策略扩展性更好，且在大规模下优势更明显。DCVLM-Baseline 数据集在 8B 模型上用 200B token 训练，在 33 个核心任务上达到 63.6% 准确率，比当前最优开源 VLM 训练数据集 FineVision 提升 5.4 个百分点。

Conclusion: DCVLM 为 VLM 数据策展研究提供了一个系统化的基准平台，揭示了数据混合策略比过滤策略更关键，以及指令数据在大规模训练中的重要性。该工作将开源所有相关资源，有望推动 VLM 数据策展领域的标准化研究。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 DataComp-VLM (DCVLM)，一个用于系统评估 VLM 训练数据策展策略的基准平台，包含 160 个数据集、52 个下游评测任务，属于多模态评测与基准方向。该论文的核心贡献是评测基准本身，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for VLM data curation but not aligned with user's priority subtopics on continuous models or generation. Signals: VLM data curation benchmark, data mixing over filtering, instruction-heavy scaling, 52 downstream benchmarks, 8B model 63.6% accuracy

Abstract: Building performant Vision-Language Models (VLMs) requires carefully curating large-scale training datasets, yet the community lacks systematic benchmarks for evaluating such curation strategies. We introduce DataComp for VLMs (DCVLM), a benchmark for controlled data-centric experiments to improve VLM training. As part of DCVLM, we collect 160 datasets spanning four data types -- image-caption pairs, multimodal interleaved documents, text-only, and instruction-tuning data -- into a corpus of 6T multimodal tokens. DCVLM allows participants to test curation strategies (filtering, mixing, formatting, sampling) across 1B-8B models and 6.25B-200B token budgets. Models are then evaluated on a carefully selected suite of up to 52 downstream benchmarks across 9 domains. We conduct extensive experiments on DCVLM and find that data mixing, not filtering, is key to a high-quality training dataset: instruction-heavy mixtures scale better than caption-heavy ones, with gains widening at larger scales. The resulting dataset, DCVLM-Baseline, enables training an 8B VLM to 63.6% accuracy on our 33-task core suite with 200B training tokens. Compared to FineVision, the state-of-the-art open VLM training dataset, this represents an improvement of +5.4pp. DCVLM and all accompanying artifacts will be made publicly available at .

</details>


### [181] [Animation2Code: Evaluating Temporal Visual Reasoning in Video-to-Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28593)
*Anya Ji, Abhijith Varma Mudunuri, David M. Chan, Alane Suhr*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Animation2Code 基准，包含 1069 个网页动画视频及其 HTML/CSS/JavaScript 实现，用于评估 VLM 在视频到代码生成任务中的时序视觉推理能力。实验表明当前 VLM 在保持时序一致性方面表现不佳，即使外观相似度较高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在静态视觉到代码任务（如网页、图表、SVG 生成）上取得了显著进展，但能否从包含运动信息的视频中恢复时序动态尚不清楚。缺乏专门评估时序视觉推理的视频到代码生成基准。

Method: 构建 Animation2Code 数据集（1069 个网页动画视频及对应代码），提出外观相似度和时序相似度两个人类对齐的评估指标，分别衡量视觉保真度和时序对齐程度。在多个 SOTA VLM 上进行基准测试，包括微调和迭代优化设置。

Result: 当前 VLM 在实现高外观相似度的同时，难以保持时序一致性。即使在微调和迭代优化设置下，时序重建质量仍然有限。

Conclusion: Animation2Code 揭示了现有 VLM 在时序视觉推理方面的显著不足，为未来研究提供了有价值的评测基准和方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 Animation2Code 基准，用于评估 VLM 从视频生成动画代码时的时序视觉推理能力，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on temporal visual reasoning in video-to-code, which is not a core priority subtopic. Signals: multimodal understanding generation, temporal consistency evaluation, VLM benchmark, not in priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: While recent vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved significant improvements on static visual-to-code tasks such as generating code for webpages, charts, or SVGs, it remains unclear whether they can recover temporal dynamics when motion is present. To this end, we introduce Animation2Code, a benchmark for evaluating temporal visual reasoning via reconstructing executable web animation code from videos. Animation2Code consists of 1,069 web animation videos with diverse visual appearances and motion patterns, paired with corresponding HTML/CSS/JavaScript implementations. We propose two human-aligned metrics, appearance similarity and temporal similarity, which allow us to disentangle visual fidelity from temporal alignment when comparing rendered animations against ground-truth samples. Benchmarking state-of-the-art VLMs on this dataset shows that current VLMs struggle to maintain temporal consistency in reconstruction, even when achieving high appearance similarity, including under finetuning and iterative refinement settings. Code and data are available at .

</details>


### [182] [GPTNT: Benchmarking Real-Time Collaboration Between Multimodal Agents on Keep Talking And Nobody Explodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28514)
*Amit Parekh, Sabrina McCallum, Kareem Al-Hasan, Malvina Nikandrou, Alessandro Suglia, Ioannis Konstas*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GPTNT 是一个基于合作游戏"Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes"的多模态智能体实时协作评测基准。两个智能体分别拥有炸弹视图（可操作）和拆弹手册（不可见炸弹），需在倒计时内通过实时通信协作拆弹。当前最强的闭源和开源多模态模型均无法在实时模式下成功拆弹，而人类玩家可以轻松完成。该基准通过控制变量（是否提供手册、伙伴等）分离模型即时推理与记忆能力，揭示了模型在状态追踪、时间压力下高效行动、歧义处理和错误恢复方面的关键缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常孤立地评估多模态模型的组件能力（如视觉问答、指令跟随），但真实协作场景中时间压力、信息不对称和不完美通信等条件同时存在，缺乏能够综合评估这些协作能力的评测基准。

Method: 基于合作游戏"Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes"构建评测基准 GPTNT。两个智能体分别扮演"操作者"（可看到并操作炸弹但无手册）和"专家"（有手册但看不到炸弹），需通过实时异步通信协作拆弹。基准支持三种条件设置：完整协作（有手册+有伙伴）、仅手册（无伙伴）、仅伙伴（无手册），以分离即时推理与记忆能力。评测在真实游戏环境中运行，利用程序化生成和模组社区持续更新。

Result: 测试的多个闭源（GPT-4o, Gemini 1.5 Pro, Claude 3.5 Sonnet）和开源模型在实时模式下均无法成功拆弹（0%成功率），而人类玩家可以轻松完成。在非实时（turn-based）设置下，模型表现有所提升但仍远低于人类。通过控制实验发现模型在状态追踪、时间压力下高效行动、歧义处理和错误恢复方面存在严重缺陷。

Conclusion: GPTNT 揭示了当前多模态模型在实时协作任务中的重大不足，现有评测无法覆盖这些关键能力。该基准通过程序化生成和模组社区支持可持续演化，不会因模型进步而被一次性解决。未来工作可探索更好的多智能体通信协议、实时推理优化和协作策略学习。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个基于合作游戏"Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes"的多模态智能体协作评测基准 GPTNT，属于多模态评测与基准方向。论文的核心贡献是设计了一个新的评测协议来评估多模态模型在实时协作、信息不对称、时间压力下的表现，这与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" 高度吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on multimodal agent collaboration benchmarking, not on core priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: multimodal agent collaboration, real-time communication, benchmark, Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes, state tracking weakness

Abstract: Multimodal models are increasingly deployed to solve tasks collaboratively with humans or other artificial agents. Existing benchmarks show that these models possess many of the required component capabilities, but the conditions that coincide in collaboration, including time pressure, information asymmetry, and imperfect communication, are usually studied in isolation. We introduce GPTNT, a benchmark built on the cooperative video game Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes, in which two agents must coordinate to defuse procedurally generated bomb puzzles against a live countdown. One agent can see and manipulate the bomb but does not have the defusal instructions; the other has the instructions but cannot see or manipulate the bomb. Neither agent can succeed alone: success requires effective and efficient communication. Unlike turn-based proxies, GPTNT requires agents to act asynchronously and communicate in real time. GPTNT is designed to separate collaboration from reliance on memorized solutions: the instruction manual, the partner, or both can be withheld to isolate what a model derives in the moment from what it already knows. We show that GPTNT poses a substantial challenge for state-of-the-art systems: none of the closed- or open-source models we test defuses a single bomb in real time, a bar that human players clear. Through controlled experiments, we identify critical weaknesses in state tracking, efficient action under time pressure, ambiguity handling, and error recovery. We release GPTNT as a benchmark for collaborative performance that current evaluations leave unmeasured. Because it runs on the real game, GPTNT benefits from procedural generation and inherits a living modding community, allowing the benchmark to evolve as models improve rather than being solved once and retired.

</details>


### [183] [IMCBench: A benchmark for multimodal LLMs in Image-grounded Medical Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28556)
*Maria Xenochristou, Ashutosh Joshi, Korosh Vatanparvar, Mohammad Abuzar Hashemi, Prasad Kasu, Deepak Bansal, Anchal Nema, Nivedita Wadhwa, Prashams S Jain, Rebecca Abraham, Will Kimbrough, Dilek Hakkani-Tur, Wilko Schulz-Mahlendorf*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (44.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 IMCBench，一个基于真实临床图像与合成患者档案的图像驱动多轮医学对话基准，从安全性、准确性和不确定性使用三个维度评估8个前沿多模态模型，发现 Claude Opus 4.6 综合最优（3.61分），但所有模型在恶性与罕见疾病上安全性下降，且准确的临床描述并不保证安全的患者指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学AI基准存在碎片化问题：有的支持多轮对话但缺乏图像输入，有的提供多模态输入但仅聚焦单轮问答。缺乏一个同时包含图像、多轮对话且从多维度（安全性、准确性、不确定性使用）评估的医学对话基准。

Method: 构建 IMCBench 基准：使用真实公开临床图像（胸部X光、皮肤镜、眼底照片等）与合成患者档案配对，模拟多轮医患对话。采用 LLM-as-Jury 评分机制，经临床专家标注校准，从安全性、准确性、不确定性使用三个维度对8个模型（Claude、GPT、Nova、Llama系列）进行1-5分评估。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6 综合得分最高（3.61），其次为 Claude Sonnet 4.6（3.30）和 GPT-5.2（3.29）。无模型在所有维度上占优。恶性与罕见疾病场景下安全性平均下降0.27。消融实验表明，移除视觉输入或EHR上下文分别导致安全性平均下降0.18和0.23，强模型更善于利用视觉特征。

Conclusion: 准确的临床描述并不保证安全的患者指导，医学AI评估需要多维度框架。视觉输入和EHR上下文对安全指导均有贡献，强模型能更有效利用视觉信息。该基准为医学多模态对话系统的安全评估提供了重要工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 IMCBench，一个面向多模态大语言模型在医学对话场景中的图像驱动多轮对话评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Medical conversation benchmark is outside user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and video/image generation. Signals: medical AI benchmark, multimodal LLM evaluation, safety-accuracy tradeoff, clinical conversation, LLM-as-Jury scoring

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models and vision-language models have enabled reasoning over multimodal data, offering opportunities for clinical applications such as decision support and triaging. However, existing medical AI benchmarks are fragmented: some support multi-turn dialogues but lack images, while others provide multimodal inputs but focus on single-turn QA tasks. To address this gap, we introduce IMCBench, an image-grounded, multi-turn medical conversation benchmark that pairs real, publicly available clinical images with synthetic patient profiles to simulate realistic patient-clinician interactions. Each conversation is evaluated across three clinical dimensions: safety, accuracy, and appropriate use of uncertainty in diagnosis. We benchmark eight multimodal frontier models across four model families (Claude, GPT, Nova, and Llama), scoring each on a 1-5 scale using LLM-as-Jury scoring calibrated against expert clinician annotations. Our results show that Claude Opus 4.6 achieves the highest overall score (3.61), followed by Claude Sonnet 4.6 (3.30) and GPT-5.2 (3.29), though no model dominates all dimensions and safety degrades for both malignant and rare conditions ($\Delta$ = -0.27 each). Ablation studies further reveal that both visual input and EHR context contribute to safe guidance (safety drops of 0.18 and 0.23 on average when each is removed), with stronger models leveraging visual features more effectively. Together, these findings demonstrate that accurate clinical description does not guarantee safe patient guidance, motivating the need for multi-dimensional evaluation frameworks in medical AI.

</details>


### [184] [Can OCR-VLMs Read Devanagari? A Stress-Test Benchmark and Post-Correction Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29213)
*Aditya Pratap Singh*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个天城文（印地语）OCR压力测试基准，对10个系统（经典OCR、开源VLM、专用OCR-VLM、前沿闭源模型）在合成退化条件和真实扫描图像上进行了系统评测。发现：合成文本无法区分系统性能；专用OCR-VLM在退化条件下出现灾难性重复错误；真实扫描下大多数系统性能崩溃；强英文OCR能力不能预测天城文OCR表现。此外，提出了错误分类体系和基于ByT5的后纠正器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OCR系统在英文和中文上表现强劲，但在天城文（印地语）等印度文字上的行为缺乏系统表征。作者希望填补这一空白，揭示OCR-VLM在天城文上的真实能力与局限性。

Method: 构建了包含合成退化文本（4种条件）和300张真实印刷扫描图像的基准。评测10个系统（EasyOCR、Qwen2.5-VL-3B、Qwen3-VL-8B、olmOCR-7B、DeepSeek-OCR、Unlimited-OCR、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Claude Opus 4.7、GPT-5.5、Mistral OCR），使用chrF++指标，并报告中位数和灾难性错误率而非均值。提出错误分类体系（表面错误 vs 结构错误），并训练基于ByT5的字节级后纠正器。

Result: 清洁合成文本上所有系统chrF++在91-98之间，无法区分；退化条件下专用OCR-VLM最脆弱，DeepSeek-OCR出现输出长度达参考71倍的灾难性重复错误；真实扫描下9/10系统性能崩溃，EasyOCR从93.6降至58.3，系统间差距达76个百分点的chrF++范围；Qwen3-VL-8B（75.2）在单卡24GB GPU上运行，超越GPT-5.5（58.5）并接近Mistral OCR；Gemini（86.3）和Claude（82.2）领先。ByT5后纠正器在自身错误分布上提升chrF++ 1.2-1.5，但无法跨引擎迁移。

Conclusion: 天城文OCR远未解决，合成评测严重高估真实场景性能；专用OCR-VLM在退化条件下存在灾难性失败模式；开源模型Qwen3-VL-8B在性价比上表现突出；后纠正器有效但缺乏泛化性。研究揭示了多模态模型在低资源文字上的显著差距。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个针对天城文（Devanagari）OCR的评测基准，对多种OCR-VLM和经典OCR系统进行了压力测试和错误分析，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on a narrow OCR benchmark for a specific script, far from user's priority on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Devanagari OCR benchmark, synthetic vs real scan gap, catastrophic repetition in OCR-VLMs, post-correction with ByT5, low overlap with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: OCR systems, ranging from classical engines to specialised OCR vision-language models (OCR-VLMs) and frontier multimodal LLMs, report strong results on English and Chinese document benchmarks, yet their behaviour on Indic scripts is largely uncharacterised. We benchmark ten systems on Devanagari (Hindi): classical EasyOCR; open VLMs (Qwen2.5-VL-3B, Qwen3-VL-8B, olmOCR-7B); specialised OCR-VLMs (DeepSeek-OCR, Unlimited-OCR); and frontier closed models (Gemini 2.5 Flash, Claude Opus 4.7, GPT-5.5, Mistral OCR), across four synthetic degradation conditions and 300 real printed scans. We report four findings. First, on clean rendered text all ten cluster within chrF++ 91 to 98, so synthetic text does not separate them. Second, under degradation the specialised OCR-VLMs are the most fragile: DeepSeek-OCR suffers rare but catastrophic repetition failures (outputs up to 71 the reference length) that wreck its corpus mean even though its median is the best of any system, which is why we report median and catastrophic-rate instead of the mean. Third, on real scans nine of the ten systems collapse (EasyOCR falls from chrF++ 93.6 to 58.3) and the field spreads across a 76-point range, so synthetic renders badly overstate Devanagari quality. Fourth, strong English OCR does not predict Indic OCR: GPT-5.5 drops to chrF++ 58.5 (tying classical EasyOCR) and olmOCR-7B, the model behind olmOCR-Bench, falls to 40.5, while the open Qwen3-VL-8B (75.2, runnable on a single 24 GB GPU) beats GPT-5.5 and approaches Mistral; Gemini and Claude lead at 86.3 and 82.2. An error taxonomy separates surface errors (numerals, punctuation) from structural ones (conjuncts, matras, nukta), and a byte-level (ByT5) post-corrector improves a cheap engine on its own error distribution (chrF++ +1.2 to +1.5) but does not transfer across engines. We release the benchmark, code, and models.

</details>


### [185] [SurgVLA-Bench: Towards Evaluating Vision-Language-Action Models for Laparoscopic Surgical Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29247)
*Jiashuo Sun, Yue He, Wenxuan Liu, Tao Mao, Jiazheng Wang, Xiang Chen, Min Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SurgVLA-Bench 是首个面向腹腔镜手术机器人的 VLA 模型综合评测基准，基于 SurRoL 仿真平台构建了从原子动作到完整手术流程的分层任务体系，并设计了多维评估框架。实验对比了自回归模型（OpenVLA）和流匹配模型（π₀, π₀.5, SmolVLA），发现自回归模型语义理解更优，流匹配模型任务精度更高但泛化性不足，且所有模型受限于内窥镜视野受限等物理瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人领域的 VLA 基准已有很多，但缺乏专门针对手术机器人场景的标准化评测平台，因此需要构建一个面向腹腔镜手术的 VLA 模型评测基准。

Method: 基于 SurRoL 仿真平台构建分层任务体系（原子动作→复合任务→完整手术流程），设计多维评估框架（动作精度+语义一致性），系统评测自回归模型（OpenVLA）和流匹配模型（π₀, π₀.5, SmolVLA）两类范式。

Result: 自回归模型在语义理解上表现更好，流匹配模型在任务精度上更高但泛化性存在权衡。即使最佳模型也远未达到令人满意的水平，内窥镜视野受限、视角限制和频繁遮挡是根本性的物理瓶颈。

Conclusion: SurgVLA-Bench 填补了手术机器人 VLA 评测的空白，揭示了现有模型在手术场景中的不足，为未来手术 VLA 模型的发展提供了标准化评估平台和方向指引。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 SurgVLA-Bench，是首个面向腹腔镜手术机器人 VLA 模型的综合评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。同时涉及具身世界模型与规划控制（embodied_world_model）方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on surgical robotics VLA benchmark, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords. Signals: Surgical robotics benchmark, VLA evaluation, laparoscopic simulation, autoregressive vs flow matching, domain-specific benchmark

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models represent a promising direction for embodied intelligence in surgical robotics. Despite the prevalence of VLA benchmarks for general robotics, standardized evaluation platforms specifically designed for surgical contexts remain absent. To address this limitation, we present SurgVLA-Bench, the first comprehensive benchmark for evaluating VLA models in laparoscopic surgical robotics. Leveraging the SurRoL simulation platform, we construct a hierarchical task taxonomy ranging from atomic actions to complete surgical procedures, complemented by a multi-dimensional evaluation framework assessing action accuracy and semantic consistency. We then systematically evaluate two representative paradigms, including autoregressive models such as OpenVLA, and flow matching models such as $\pi_{0}$, $\pi_{0.5}$, and SmolVLA. Our experiments show that autoregressive models tend to excel in semantic understanding, while flow matching models often achieve higher task precision but may face generalization trade-offs. However, even the best-performing models remain far from satisfactory, as the constrained endoscopic field of view, restricted viewing angles, and frequent occlusions persist as fundamental physical bottlenecks. The code and data are available at

</details>


### [186] [mamabench and mamaretrieval: Benchmarks for Evaluating Medical Retrieval-Augmented Generation in Maternal, Neonatal, and Reproductive Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29467)
*Yi Ren*

Main category: cs.IR, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (40.79) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了两个面向母婴及生殖健康领域的医学检索增强生成（RAG）评测基准：mamabench（25,949 道 QA 题）和 mamaretrieval（3,185 条查询与 63,650 个文本块的段落级相关性标注），填补了该领域公开评测数据的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学问答基准很少覆盖助产护士实际遇到的母婴、新生儿和生殖健康问题，且缺乏公开的段落级相关性标注基准用于评估医疗指南检索。

Method: 从七个现有专家撰写的来源中组装并筛选出 25,949 道 QA 题（mamabench），涵盖选择题、简答题和评分题；同时构建 mamaretrieval，包含 3,185 条临床查询与 63,650 个母婴健康指南文本块，使用分解式评分标准进行 0-6 级相关性标注。采用范围分类器一致性检查、前沿模型校验和池化完整性审计来量化标注质量。

Result: 构建了两个公开可用的评测基准，并提供了标注质量的多维度量化指标（范围分类器一致性、前沿模型校验、池化完整性审计）。

Conclusion: 这两个基准填补了母婴健康领域 RAG 评测的空白，为评估部署在设备上的医疗助手提供了标准化评测工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了两个面向母婴及生殖健康领域的医学检索增强生成评测基准（mamabench 和 mamaretrieval），属于多模态/文本评测基准范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical RAG benchmarks for maternal health, which is outside the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, and world models. Signals: medical RAG, maternal health benchmark, no overlap with priority subtopics, negative keyword "medical diagnosis" applies, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: Medical question-answering benchmarks rarely cover the maternal, neonatal, child, and reproductive-health questions a nurse-midwife asks, and, to our knowledge, no public chunk-level relevance benchmark exists for maternal-health guideline retrieval. We release two benchmarks that fill these gaps. mamabench is a scope-filtered QA set of 25,949 items assembled from seven existing expert-authored sources across multiple-choice, short-answer, and rubric-graded tracks; to help users calibrate the LLM judge that scores the rubric track, we re-scope HealthBench's physician-labelled meta-evaluation to the domain. mamaretrieval pairs 3,185 clinical queries with graded (0-6) relevance labels over a 63,650-chunk maternal-health guideline corpus, using a decomposed rubric that distinguishes a chunk that answers a query from one merely on its topic. Three decisions shape both: assemble and filter expert sources rather than author questions, grade relevance rather than binarise it, and measure and disclose the limits of the labels -- scope-classifier agreement, a frontier-judge check, and a pooling-completeness audit -- rather than treat them as an oracle. A companion paper uses the benchmarks to evaluate a deployed on-device assistant; both are released openly for research.

</details>


### [187] [OSWorld2.0: Benchmarking Computer Use Agents on Long-Horizon Real-World Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29537)
*Mengqi Yuan, Zilong Zhou, Xinzhuang Xiong, Weiming Wu, Jiayang Sun, Jiamin Song, Kaiqian Cui, Bowen Wang, Haoyuan Wu, Yitong Li, Dunjie Lu, Haikong Lu, Qi Zhen, Xinyuan Wang, Jiaqi Deng, Yuhao Yang, Cheng Chen, Boyuan Zheng, Alex Su, Xiao Yu, Hao Zou, Saaket Agashe, Xing Han Lu, Manpreet Kaur, Zhengyang Qi, Vincent Sunn Chen, Frederic Sala, Dayiheng Liu, Junyang Lin, Zhou Yu, Yu Su, Siva Reddy, Xin Eric Wang, Peng Qi, Tianbao Xie, Tao Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OSWorld 2.0 是一个包含108个长时程真实世界计算机使用工作流的评测基准，每个任务平均需要约1.6小时完成、约318次工具调用。在500步限制下，最佳模型 Claude Opus 4.8 仅完成20.6%的任务，表明当前智能体距离专业级计算机使用仍有很大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用基准无法捕捉真实世界的复杂性、长时程需求和实际挑战，限制了揭示前沿智能体局限性的能力。作者旨在构建一个更真实、更复杂、更长时程的基准来评估智能体的实际能力。

Method: 构建了108个长时程计算机使用工作流，涵盖日常和专业任务。每个任务基于真实输入工件和用户状态数据，设计了交互设计挑战（流式交互、动态环境）和智能体模式挑战（跨源推理、隐式状态推断、视觉空间精度）等挑战现象。采用二元完成度指标和部分得分指标进行评估，设置500步上限。

Result: Claude Opus 4.8（最大思考+批量工具调用）在500步限制下完成20.6%的任务，部分得分54.8%；GPT-5.5 token效率更高但仅达到约13%。智能体并非在基本GUI控制或编码上失败，而是在跟踪约束、捕获任务中到达的信息、主动询问用户以及验证结果等方面表现不佳。

Conclusion: 当前智能体距离专业级计算机使用仍有很大差距，主要瓶颈不在于基础操作能力，而在于对隐式状态的恢复、跨源推理、动态信息跟踪等高级认知能力。OSWorld 2.0 为计算机使用智能体提供了更具挑战性的评测平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 OSWorld 2.0，一个面向长时程真实世界计算机使用任务的评测基准，属于多模态智能体/计算机使用智能体的评测。虽然该基准聚焦于计算机使用（computer use）而非传统多模态理解，但其评测的是智能体在真实 GUI 环境中的交互能力，属于多模态智能体评测范畴。最匹配的现有 [CANONICAL] 子主题是 multimodal_benchmark（多模态评测与基准），因为该论文的核心贡献是一个新的评测基准。

Importance reason: This benchmark focuses on computer use agents, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Long-horizon real-world tasks, computer use agents, benchmark, multimodal understanding, not aligned with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Existing computer-use benchmarks fail to capture the realism, complexity, and long-horizon demands of real-world computer use, limiting their ability to reveal the limitations of frontier agents. We introduce OSWorld 2.0, a benchmark of 108 long-horizon computer-use workflows across everyday and professional tasks, designed to capture complex and challenging real-world phenomena. Each task represents a realistic end-to-end workflow that takes human users a median of about 1.6 hours to complete and requires an average of 318 tool calls with Claude Opus 4.7 using maximum thinking, compared with about 30 in OSWorld 1.0. OSWorld 2.0 targets challenge phenomena that are common in real workflows yet underrepresented in prior benchmarks, spanning interaction-design challenges such as streaming interaction and dynamic environments, as well as agent-pattern challenges such as cross-source reasoning, implicit-state inference, and visual-spatial precision. Tasks are grounded in authentic input artifacts and cross-referenced against realistic stateful user profile data, and include separate safety reports auditing safety-sensitive execution. Under our primary binary-completion metric at 500 steps, Claude Opus 4.8 with maximum thinking and batched tool calls scores best but still completes only 20.6% of tasks at a 54.8% partial score; GPT-5.5 is far more token-efficient yet plateaus near 13%. These results show that current agents are still far from professional-level computer use: rather than stumbling on basic GUI control or coding, they lose track of constraints, miss information that arrives mid-task, guess rather than ask the user, and skip verification, struggling most when a task hinges on hidden state they must recover.

</details>


### [188] [Bridging VideoQA and Video-Guided Agentic Tasks via Generalized Keyframe Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29445)
*Sunqi Fan, Qingle Liu, Runqi Yin, Meng-Hao Guo, Shuojin Yang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 VG-GUIBench 基准，用于评估 MLLM 能否从视频教程中学习知识并泛化到 GUI 交互任务；同时提出 TASKER 关键帧提取算法，联合考虑任务相关性和场景动态，在 VideoQA 和视频引导的智能体任务上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VideoQA 基准主要评估模型对浅层视觉线索的感知能力，很少考察 MLLM 能否从视频教程中学习深层知识或程序性技能并泛化到下游长时程智能体任务。作者希望填补这一评估空白。

Method: 提出 VG-GUIBench 基准，包含视频教程和对应的 GUI 交互任务。同时提出 TASKER 关键帧提取算法，联合考虑任务相关性（task relevance）和场景动态（scene dynamics）来选择信息量最大的帧。

Result: TASKER 在 EgoSchema fullset 上比最佳基线提升 2.0%，在 NExT-QA 上提升 1.8%，同时在视频引导的 GUI 智能体任务上也取得显著改进。

Conclusion: 视频理解中关键帧提取对 VideoQA 和视频引导的智能体任务都至关重要，TASKER 证明了通用关键帧提取方法在视频理解任务中的潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个新的视频理解基准 VG-GUIBench，用于评估 MLLM 在视频教程引导下的 GUI 交互任务能力，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on GUI agentic tasks and keyframe extraction, which are not aligned with user's priority subtopics on continuous multimodal models, video generation, or world models. Signals: GUI agent benchmark, keyframe extraction, VideoQA, not generative, no continuous token or flow matching

Abstract: Video understanding is a fundamental capability for multimodal intelligence, and recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable performance on Video Question Answering (VideoQA) benchmarks. However, existing benchmarks primarily evaluate whether models can perceive shallow visual cues, while rarely examining whether MLLMs can learn deeper knowledge or procedural skills from video tutorials and generalize them to downstream long-horizon agentic tasks. To address this gap, we introduce VG-GUIBench (Video-Guided GUI Benchmark), a new benchmark designed to evaluate whether MLLM-based GUI agents can follow video tutorials to complete corresponding GUI interactive tasks. Furthermore, we observe that the performance of models on both VideoQA and video-guided agentic tasks critically depends on effective keyframe extraction. Based on this observation, we propose TASKER (Task-driven And Scene-aware Keyframe searchER), a keyframe extraction algorithm that jointly considers task relevance and scene dynamics to identify informative frames. Experimental results demonstrate that TASKER achieves significant performance improvements on both VideoQA and video-guided agentic task benchmarks, outperforming the best baseline by 2.0% on the EgoSchema fullset and 1.8% on the NExT-QA dataset, respectively. These results further highlight the potential of generalized keyframe extraction methods for video understanding tasks. Our code and data are available at .

</details>


### [189] [One Scene, Two Depths: Probing Geometric Ambiguity in Monocular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29600)
*Xiaohao Xu, Feng Xue, Xiang Li, Haowei Li, Shusheng Yang, Tianyi Zhang, Matthew Johnson-Roberson, Xiaonan Huang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文指出单目深度估计将每个像素压缩为单一深度值，忽略了透明场景中一条光线可能包含多个有效几何表面的分层结构。作者提出 MultiDepth-3k (MD-3k) 双层深度序数基准，用于评测深度基础模型的层偏好，并发现 Laplacian Visual Prompting (LVP) 这种免训练的光谱输入变换可以显著改变某些冻结模型的深度层报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目深度估计方法将每个像素简化为单一标量深度，但在透明场景（如玻璃）中，一条光线可能包含多个可见且几何有效的表面。这种简化掩盖了深度监督目标本质上是标注约定而非场景固有真值的事实。作者希望揭示深度基础模型在不同层之间的偏好，并探索如何表达被标准 RGB 推理忽略的互补几何假设。

Method: 1) 构建 MultiDepth-3k (MD-3k) 基准，包含 3,000 个稀疏双层深度序数标注，用于测量深度层偏好和多层空间关系准确性 (ML-SRA)。2) 在 MD-3k 上评测多个主流深度基础模型（如 DAv2、Depth Anything 等），分析其层偏好。3) 提出 Laplacian Visual Prompting (LVP)，一种免训练的光谱输入变换方法，通过拉普拉斯算子处理输入图像来改变模型对深度层的感知。

Result: 不同深度基础模型在 MD-3k 上表现出多样化的层偏好，表明相同的分层几何结构在不同模型中被不同地解析。LVP 可以显著改变某些冻结模型的深度层报告。最佳 RGB/LVP 组合 (DAv2-L) 达到 75.5% ML-SRA。

Conclusion: 深度基础模型可能表达互补的几何假设，而这些假设在标准 RGB 推理中未被表达。社区应通过模糊感知的视角重新思考深度监督和评测，将多个有效的 3D 解释视为需要测量、保留和表达的几何结构。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 MultiDepth-3k (MD-3k)，一个稀疏双层深度序数基准，用于评测单目深度基础模型在透明场景下的深度层偏好和多层空间关系准确性。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）范畴，该 [CANONICAL] 子主题涵盖新基准和评测协议。

Importance reason: Novel benchmark for geometric ambiguity in depth models, but tangential to user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and generation. Signals: Monocular depth ambiguity, layered geometry benchmark, Laplacian Visual Prompting, depth foundation model evaluation, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: A faithful 3D world representation should account for layered geometry, where a single camera ray may contain multiple visible and geometrically valid surfaces. Monocular depth estimation, however, reduces this structure to one scalar depth per pixel. Transparent scenes make this ambiguity measurable: the same ray can pass through foreground glass and observe the background, turning the supervised target into a convention of annotation, data, and training rather than a scene-intrinsic truth. A learned predictor exposes this convention as its depth-layer preference. We introduce MultiDepth-3k (MD-3k), a sparse two-layer ordinal benchmark for measuring depth-layer preference and multi-layer spatial relationship accuracy (ML-SRA). On MD-3k, leading depth foundation models exhibit diverse layer preferences under standard RGB input, showing that the same layered geometry can be resolved differently across models. We further find that Laplacian Visual Prompting (LVP), a training-free spectral input transformation, can substantially change the reported layer for certain frozen models. The strongest RGB/LVP pair, DAv2-L, reaches 75.5% ML-SRA. These results suggest that depth foundation models may express complementary geometric hypotheses that standard RGB inference leaves unexpressed. We invite the community to rethink depth supervision and evaluation through an ambiguity-aware lens, where multiple valid 3D interpretations are treated as geometric structure to be measured, preserved, and expressed.

</details>


### [190] [Unlocking the Visual Record of Materials Science: A Large-Scale Multimodal Dataset from Scientific Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29667)
*Subham Ghosh, Shubham Tiwari, Mohammad Ibrahim, Abhishek Tewari*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI, cond-mat.mtrl-sci

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MatMMExtract 流水线，从材料科学文献中提取复合图像并分解为子图，构建了包含 391,606 个图像-文本对的大规模多模态数据集 MatSciFig，并引入 MaterialScope 检测数据集和检索基线，为材料科学领域的视觉语言学习提供基础资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料科学文献中蕴含大量实验知识以图像形式存在，但大多数科学图像是复合图（多子图共享一个标题），导致直接图像-文本配对不可靠，阻碍了 AI 大规模利用这些视觉记录。

Method: 提出 MatMMExtract 端到端开源流水线：1) 使用 YOLO12-m 检测器（在 MaterialScope 数据集上微调）定位和分解复合图中的子面板；2) 利用大语言模型（Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite）结合材料科学分类法为每个子面板生成结构化标注（子标题、可视化类别、科学摘要）。应用于 14,810 篇开放获取论文，构建 MatSciFig 数据集。

Result: MaterialScope 上微调 YOLO12-m 达到 mAP_50 0.9227；Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite 标注质量 82% 为良好，幻觉率 4.8%；基于 MatSciFig 的双编码器检索基线相比零样本 CLIP 在 R@1 上提升 4.4 倍。

Conclusion: MatMMExtract 流水线有效解决了科学文献中复合图的图像-文本配对难题，MatSciFig 数据集为材料科学领域的视觉语言学习提供了大规模高质量资源，检索实验验证了其直接可用性。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了材料科学领域的大规模多模态数据集 MatSciFig（391,606 个图像-文本对），并提供了评测基准 MaterialScope 和检索基线。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）范畴，是 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Dataset construction for materials science is valuable but far from the user's priority on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Materials science domain, Dataset construction, Multimodal extraction pipeline, Low priority subtopic, No overlap with boost keywords

Abstract: The materials science literature encodes decades of experimental knowledge in figures, yet this visual record remains locked away and inaccessible to AI at scale. The core difficulty is structural: most scientific figures are compound, with a single caption describing multiple sub-panels simultaneously, making direct image-text pairing unreliable. We present MatMMExtract, an end-to-end open-source pipeline that resolves this by decomposing compound figures into individual sub-panels and generating structured, grounded annotations using a large language model guided by a curated materials science taxonomy. Applied to 14,810 open-access articles, MatMMExtract produces MatSciFig; 391,606 panel-level image-text pairs from 180,571 figures, each annotated with a sub-caption, a two-level visualisation category spanning 19 classes and over 100 subtypes, and a scientific summary. To enable accurate panel localisation, we introduce MaterialScope, a domain-specific detection dataset of 2,811 manually annotated materials science figures, on which a fine-tuned YOLO12-m detector achieves mAP_50 of 0.9227. Among six benchmarked language models, Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite delivers the best cost-quality trade-off for annotation generation, with 82% of outputs rated good and a hallucination rate of 4.8%. A dual-encoder retrieval baseline on MatSciFig achieves a 4.4 times improvement in R@1 over zero-shot CLIP, demonstrating the dataset's immediate utility for vision-language learning. All resources are released openly to the community.

</details>


### [191] [MuseBench: Benchmarking Intent-Level Audiovisual Arts Understanding in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30026)
*Yuxuan Fan, Gyusik Seo, Jing Hao, Jaemin Cho, Mohit Bansal, Jaehong Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (73.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MuseBench 是一个用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在视听艺术领域意图级理解能力的新基准，包含 4016 道题目，覆盖电影、视觉艺术、舞台表演和游戏艺术四大领域。在 28 个 SOTA MLLM 上的零样本评测显示，最佳模型仅达 48.29% 准确率，远低于人类专家的 87.18%，揭示了当前模型在创意领域专业知识上的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 评测基准主要关注感知层面的识别能力（如识别物体、场景），而忽略了艺术理解中更关键的"为什么"——即创作者通过特定视听元素组合所传达的意图。为了填补这一空白，作者提出了 MuseBench，系统性地评估 MLLM 对艺术创作意图的深层理解能力。

Method: 从超过 10K 个视频论文（video essays）中提取内容，经过四阶段迭代流程（包括捷径过滤、对抗性干扰项、专家验证）生成 4016 道题目。题目涵盖电影艺术、静态视觉艺术、舞台表演艺术和游戏艺术四大领域，采用单选题和可变选项多选题两种形式，以捕捉艺术分析中开放性的特点。

Result: 对 28 个 SOTA MLLM 进行零样本评测，最佳模型（GPT-4o）仅达 48.29% 准确率，远低于人类专家 87.18% 的表现。所有模型在所有子领域均表现不佳，揭示了当前 MLLM 在创意领域专业知识上的显著差距。

Conclusion: MuseBench 揭示了当前 MLLM 在艺术意图理解方面的严重不足，表明现有模型虽然具备强大的感知能力，但在理解创作意图、艺术表达等深层语义方面仍有巨大提升空间。该基准为未来多模态模型在创意领域的评估提供了重要参考。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 MuseBench，一个评估 MLLM 在视听艺术理解方面的新基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for creative arts understanding, but low alignment with user's focus on continuous multimodal models and generation. Signals: New benchmark for intent-level arts understanding, reveals large gap vs human experts, zero-shot evaluation of 28 MLLMs, covers cinema, visual arts, stage, games, low relevance to user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Audiovisual arts encompass diverse creative disciplines, including cinema, visual arts, stage performance, and game design, where artistic meaning arises from deliberate combinations of visual, auditory, and narrative elements (e.g., fear amplified through claustrophobic framing, or grief conveyed through silence and lingering close-ups). True artistic understanding extends beyond recognizing what is depicted to reasoning about why it is expressed through particular creative choices. Despite the strong progress of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), this critical aspect of artistic understanding remains underexplored, as existing benchmarks largely measure perceptual recognition while overlooking reasoning about creative intent. To address this gap, we introduce Musebench, a comprehensive benchmark designed to evaluate MLLMs on nuanced artistic understanding. It comprises 4,016 questions spanning cinematic arts, static visual arts, stage performing arts, and game arts, distilled from over 10K candidate video essays that pair professional commentary with visual demonstration. To capture the open-ended nature of artistic analysis at scale, the benchmark combines single-select and variable-option multi-select questions. All questions are generated and refined through a four-phase iterative pipeline combining shortcut filtering, adversarial distractors, and expert validation. Comprehensive zero-shot evaluation of 28 state-of-the-art MLLMs reveals that even the best-performing model achieves only 48.29% accuracy, substantially below human expert performance of 87.18%, exposing a significant gap in current models' creative domain expertise.

</details>


### [192] [SHOVIR: A Benchmark for Evaluating Vision Shortcut Learning in Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30201)
*Filippo Ruffini, Marco Salmé, Rosa Sicilia, Valerio Guarrasi, Paolo Soda*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (49.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出SHOVIR基准，通过区域遮挡实验评估放射学报告生成（RRG）中VLM的视觉捷径学习（vision shortcut）行为，发现高报告质量不一定对应良好的空间定位能力，揭示了当前RRG评估的盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前放射学报告生成（RRG）的评估指标（如词汇重叠、临床正确性聚合指标）无法检验模型是否真正基于图像中的病理证据生成诊断陈述，模型可能通过学习先验或虚假相关性（即视觉捷径）获得高分，现有评估存在盲点。

Method: SHOVIR基准扩展了MIMIC-CXR和PadChest-GR两个胸部X光数据集，添加了逐框CheXpert标签，设计了图像级和疾病级遮挡实验：对比模型在干净图像与局部区域扰动后的预测差异，区分直接捷径（移除视觉证据后发现仍存在）和上下文捷径（遮挡共现病理后目标检测退化）两种失败模式。

Result: 对8个SOTA VLM的基准测试表明，捷径行为在不同架构和数据集上差异显著；基线报告质量最高的模型在空间定位上并不一定排名最高，表明临床流畅的生成可以与对视觉证据的浅层依赖共存。

Conclusion: 当前RRG评估存在盲点，需要区域感知的评估协议来更准确地衡量模型是否真正基于视觉证据生成诊断报告。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了SHOVIR基准，用于评估放射学报告生成中VLM的视觉捷径学习（vision shortcut）行为，属于多模态评测与基准方向。该工作属于多模态理解与生成方向下的评测基准，因此匹配到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）。

Importance reason: Focuses on radiology report generation evaluation, which is a niche medical application not aligned with user's priority subtopics. Signals: Radiology report generation, vision shortcut benchmark, spatial grounding evaluation, MIMIC-CXR, PadChest-GR

Abstract: Current evaluation protocols for Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in Radiology Report Generation (RRG) rely on report-level metrics that measure lexical overlap or aggregate clinical correctness. However, such metrics do not test whether individual diagnostic statements stem from the actual pathological evidence visible in the image. This allows models to achieve competitive scores by exploiting learned priors or spurious correlations, a failure mode we refer to as vision shortcut. We introduce SHOVIR, a benchmark for evaluating vision shortcut behavior in RRG. SHOVIR extends two spatially annotated chest X-ray datasets, MIMIC-CXR and PadChest-GR, with per-box CheXpert labels, and defines image-level and disease-level occlusion experiments that contrast baseline performance on clean images against localized, region-specific perturbations. Comparing predictions across these conditions isolates two failure modes at the disease-class level: direct shortcuts, where a finding persists after its visual evidence is removed, and contextual shortcuts, where detection degrades once co-occurring pathologies are occluded despite the target region remaining intact. Benchmarking eight state-of-the-art VLMs, we find that shortcut behavior varies substantially across architectures and datasets. Models achieving the highest baseline report quality do not necessarily rank highest in spatial grounding, revealing that clinically fluent generation can coexist with shallow reliance on visual evidence. These findings expose a blind spot in current RRG evaluation and motivate region-aware assessment protocols.

</details>


### [193] [From Accuracy to Visual Dependence: Auditing and Filtering Modality Collapse in Traffic VideoQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30220)
*Sena Korkut, María Alejandra Bravo Sarmiento, Sanghwan Kim, Zeynep Akata*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究了交通场景VideoQA中VLM的模态坍塌问题，发现多个开源VLM在去掉视频输入后性能反而更好。作者提出了Blind Gap、Visual Gain等数据集级诊断指标和实例级Shortcut Score，用于量化视觉依赖性和过滤捷径问题，从而提升评测的视觉基础性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高基准准确率并不保证模型真正使用了视觉证据。在安全关键的交通场景VideoQA中，模型可能依赖文本捷径而非场景特定的视觉信息来回答问题，现有评测无法区分这种模态坍塌问题。

Method: 1) 对四个公开交通VideoQA基准进行审计，比较VLM在有/无视频输入下的表现。2) 提出数据集级诊断指标：Blind Gap（纯文本性能超出随机猜测的程度）和Visual Gain（添加视频带来的边际收益）。3) 提出实例级Shortcut Score，结合纯文本置信度与视觉必要性信号，实现无需训练的连续捷径问题过滤。

Result: 在MM-AU基准上，移除视频反而持续提升准确率，增加更多帧进一步降低性能。多个开源VLM在无视频输入时表现相当甚至更好。通过Shortcut Score过滤后的子集减少了捷径偏差，提升了视觉基础性。

Conclusion: 不同基准的视觉基础质量差异巨大，在安全关键VideoQA中，视觉基础评测（而非仅仅高准确率）至关重要。所提出的诊断指标和过滤方法有助于构建更可靠的评测协议。

Classification reason: 该论文研究交通场景VideoQA中VLM的视觉依赖性问题，提出了Blind Gap、Visual Gain等数据集级诊断指标和实例级Shortcut Score来过滤捷径问题，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on modality collapse in traffic VideoQA, which is outside your priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Modality collapse audit, Blind Gap & Visual Gain metrics, Shortcut Score filtering, Traffic VideoQA domain, Safety-critical evaluation

Abstract: High benchmark accuracy does not guarantee genuine use of visual evidence. We study this problem in traffic accident Video Question Answering (VideoQA), where correct answers should depend on scene-specific visual evidence but may instead be inferred from textual shortcuts. Through an audit of four public benchmarks, we find that several recent open-weight Vision-Language Models (VLMs) perform competitively, and sometimes better, without video input. On the MM-AU benchmark, removing video consistently improves accuracy, and adding more frames further degrades performance. To quantify visual dependence, we introduce two dataset-level diagnostics: Blind Gap, measuring above-chance text-only performance, and Visual Gain, measuring the marginal benefit of adding video. We further propose an instance-level Shortcut Score that combines text-only confidence with visual necessity signals, enabling continuous, training-free filtering of shortcut-prone questions. The resulting subsets reduce shortcut bias and improve visual grounding. Our findings reveal large differences in grounding quality across benchmarks and show that visually grounded evaluation, not just high accuracy, is essential in safety-critical VideoQA.

</details>


### [194] [TUA-Bench: A Benchmark for General-Purpose Terminal-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28480)
*Shoufa Chen, Luyuan Wang, Xuan Yang, Zhiheng Liu, Yuren Cong, Yuanfeng Ji, Feiyan Zhou, Xiaohui Zhang, Fanny Yang, Belinda Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.SE

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TUA-Bench是一个面向终端通用智能体（TUA）的评测基准，包含120个跨5个任务族的真实世界任务，涵盖文档编辑、邮件管理、实时网页信息检索以及科学工程工作流。最强模型Claude Code + Claude Opus 4.8仅达65.8%性能，揭示了当前智能体在终端通用任务上的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准要么聚焦GUI操作，要么局限于编程/技术类终端任务，缺乏对通用终端智能体在多样化数字活动（如文档编辑、邮件管理、科学工程软件使用）中的全面评估。

Method: 构建了120个手动设计的真实终端任务，覆盖5个任务族（日常数字活动+科学工程工作流），每个任务有确定性设置脚本和基于执行的评分协议，在真实终端环境中运行评估。

Result: 最强模型Claude Code with Claude Opus 4.8 max reasoning effort达到65.8%整体性能，不同任务族间存在显著差距，表明当前智能体在通用终端任务上仍有较大提升空间。

Conclusion: TUA-Bench填补了终端通用智能体评测的空白，通过覆盖广泛真实任务揭示了现有智能体的能力边界，有望推动从窄域任务助手向通用终端智能体的发展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了TUA-Bench，一个面向终端通用智能体的评测基准，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。虽然基准聚焦终端操作而非纯多模态，但其评估的是LLM/Agent在真实数字环境中的通用能力，与多模态评测基准的范畴一致。

Importance reason: Benchmark for terminal agents is solid but far from user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: terminal-use agent benchmark, real-world task evaluation, strong model only 65.8%, not aligned with priority subtopics, no continuous language or video generation signals

Abstract: As large language models and harness frameworks continue to advance, agents operating in terminals are increasingly capable of performing a broader range of general computer-use tasks beyond coding. However, existing benchmarks do not adequately evaluate general-purpose terminal computer-use agents (TUAs): general computer-use benchmarks primarily target graphical user interfaces (GUIs), whereas terminal-based benchmarks largely emphasize technical and programming-centric workflows historically native to the shell. We introduce TUA-Bench, a general-purpose benchmark for terminal-use agents. TUA-Bench includes 120 real-world tasks across five task families, covering routine digital activities-including document editing, email management, and live-web information seeking-as well as scientific and engineering workflows co-designed with PhD-level domain experts that require specialized software. This breadth distinguishes TUA-Bench from prior shell-focused or domain-specific benchmarks. Each task is manually designed, runs in a real terminal with a deterministic setup script, and is evaluated by an execution-based scoring protocol. We find that the strongest frontier agent, Claude Code with Claude Opus 4.8 max reasoning effort, achieves 65.8% overall performance, with substantial gaps across both tracks. By providing a broad and realistic evaluation of terminal-use capabilities, TUA-Bench aims to accelerate the transition from narrow, task-specific assistants to general-purpose agents capable of operating reliably across diverse digital environments.

</details>


### [195] [OmniCoT: A Benchmark for Global and Multi-Step Panoramic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30378)
*Haocong He, Chenfei Liao, Zichen Wen, Zihao Dongfang, Xu Zheng, Bin Ren, Chang Su, Zixin Zhang, Harold Haodong Chen, Hongfei Zhang, Weijia Li, Kailun Yang, Conghui He, Xuming Hu, Nicu Sebe, Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OmniCoT是一个面向全景图像（360°×180°）的多步空间推理基准套件，包含评测集OmniCoT-B（6.7K）、真实世界子集OmniCoT-Real（1K）和训练集OmniCoT-T（14.3K），并基于此训练了OmniCoT-R1模型，采用SFT+GRPO两阶段训练策略增强全景空间推理的全局一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全景基准大多依赖局部线索或单步/少步推理，未能充分利用全景图像360°×180°视野所支持的全局多步推理能力，无法真正发挥全景在具身智能等应用中的优势。

Method: 构建OmniCoT基准套件：OmniCoT-B（6.7K）用于评测答案准确性和推理质量；OmniCoT-Real（1K）为人工标注的真实世界子集用于量化Sim-to-Real差距；OmniCoT-T（14.3K）包含结构化逐步Chain-of-Thought标注。基于OmniCoT-T训练OmniCoT-R1，采用两阶段策略：SFT将推理锚定到全景证据（如方位、邻近关系），GRPO惩罚几何不一致的推理路径以巩固全局360°空间一致性。

Result: OmniCoT基准揭示了现有MLLM在全景空间推理上的不足，OmniCoT-R1通过两阶段训练显著提升了全景多步推理的准确性和推理质量。

Conclusion: OmniCoT重新校准了全景空间推理的难度标准，使其更好地匹配全景图像的内在能力，推动该领域的有意义进展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了OmniCoT全景空间推理基准，包含评测集和训练集，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。同时涉及多模态推理（Chain-of-Thought），但核心贡献是基准构建，因此归入multimodal_benchmark。

Importance reason: Focuses on panoramic spatial reasoning benchmark, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Panoramic reasoning benchmark, Multi-step spatial reasoning, Chain-of-Thought, GRPO training, Sim-to-Real gap

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated promising spatial reasoning capabilities, while these abilities remain underexplored in the emerging visual modality of panoramic imagery. The full 360°$\times$180° field of view of panoramas essentially supports complex global multi-step reasoning, which is also the fundamental advantage of panoramas in applications such as embodied intelligence. However, existing panoramic benchmarks largely focus on simplistic queries that rely on local cues or single-/few-step reasoning, thereby ignoring the fundamental advantage of panoramas and failing to fully exploit their potential. To address this gap, we introduce OmniCoT, a panoramic spatial reasoning suite designed to enable MLLMs to use global evidence and perform multi-step inference across viewpoints. It includes OmniCoT-B (6.7K data) for evaluation, which measures both answer accuracy and reasoning quality, OmniCoT-Real (1K data) as a manually annotated real-world subset to quantify the Sim-to-Real gap. For training, OmniCoT-T (14.3K data) is purpose-built with structured stepwise Chain-of-Thought annotations that explicitly link intermediate reasoning steps to panoramic evidence. Based on OmniCoT-T, we introduce OmniCoT-R1 and adopt a two-stage training strategy tailored to the geometrically complex panoramic space, where Supervised Fine-tuning (SFT) anchors reasoning to panoramic evidence (e.g., bearings, proximity) and GRPO penalizes geometrically incoherent paths to consolidate global 360° spatial consistency. Through OmniCoT, we aim to recalibrate the difficulty of panoramic spatial reasoning to better align with the intrinsic capabilities of panoramic imagery, thereby fostering meaningful progress in this research area.

</details>


### [196] [SADL: What to Ignore? A Benchmark for Subject-Aware Distractor Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30393)
*Cao-Tri Nguyen, Nguyen-Khoa Luong, Vinh-Tiep Nguyen, Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (56.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出SADL，首个面向主体感知干扰物定位的真实世界基准，包含1,800个主体感知案例、14,617个标注候选和1,938个难负样本，用于系统评估VLM在识别干扰物并排除语义必要物体方面的能力。实验发现VLM能较好识别干扰物，但过度应用排除规则，系统性抑制了真正的干扰物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有显著性模型和开放词汇检测器缺乏主体感知能力，无法适应变化的用户意图；且上下文无关的物体移除可能破坏场景语义连贯性（如移除人坐的椅子）。因此需要形式化主体感知干扰物定位任务并建立标准评测基准。

Method: 构建SADL基准：1,000张照片、1,800个主体感知案例、14,617个标注候选（含1,938个难负样本）。设计包含5个包含因子和3个上下文排除规则的顺序流水线（干扰物分类→排除过滤），评估7个开源和闭源VLM。

Result: VLM在干扰物识别上表现良好，但在排除过滤阶段过度应用排除规则，系统性抑制了真正的干扰物，导致整体性能瓶颈。

Conclusion: SADL揭示了VLM在主体感知推理中的关键瓶颈——过度排除问题，为推进多模态系统的主体条件推理提供了基础诊断工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了SADL基准，用于评估VLM在主体感知干扰物定位任务上的能力，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Introduces a new benchmark for subject-aware distractor localization, but is a narrow evaluation task not aligned with user's priority subtopics. Signals: new benchmark, VLM evaluation, distractor localization, subject-aware, hard negatives

Abstract: Photographs frequently contain \emph{visual distractors} besides foregrounds and backgrounds of the intended subject, competing for attention and weakening composition. While modern editing tools streamline object removal, identifying which objects to remove remains a mostly manual process. Existing saliency models and open-vocabulary detectors operate without subject awareness, failing to adapt to shifting user intent. Furthermore, context-agnostic removal may disrupt the scene's semantic coherence (e.g., keep the person but remove the chair they are sitting on). To address these limitations, we formalize the task of subject-aware distractor localization, which identifies distractors while retaining compositionally essential objects. This paper introduces \textsc{SADL}, the first real-world benchmark for this task, comprising 1,800 subject-aware cases across 1,000 photographs to enable systematic evaluation and facilitate future research. In total, there are 14,617 annotated candidates, including a robust set of 1,938 hard negatives to stress-test exclusion calibration. We evaluate seven proprietary and open-weight Vision-Language Models (VLMs) on a sequential pipeline of distractor classification followed by exclusion filtering, structured around five inclusion factors and three contextual exclusion rules. Our analysis reveals that VLMs are highly capable of identifying distractors, but then over-apply exclusion, which systematically suppresses true distractors at scale. By exposing this critical bottleneck, \textsc{SADL} provides a foundational diagnostic tool to advance subject-conditioned reasoning in multimodal systems.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-efficient_multimodal'></div>

## 多模态高效化与轻量化 [[Back]](#toc)

高效 MLLM、PEFT、蒸馏、量化、token 压缩、稀疏推理。

### [197] [ScAle: Attention Head Scaling as a Minimal Adapter for Spatial Reasoning in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29579)
*Rahul Chowdhury, Timothy A Rupprecht, Xuan Shen, Pu Zhao, Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.MM

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (69.3) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ScAle 是一种超轻量级 VLM 适配方法，仅需学习少量标量系数来缩放冻结骨干网络中的 last-token 注意力和 MLP 激活，即可显著提升空间推理能力（最高相对增益 134.1%），仅用 1K 可训练参数即可恢复大部分标准 PEFT 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理是 VLM 的持久挑战，现有改进方法通常需要大量参数的微调。作者发现仅缩放选定 Transformer 层的激活值（不修改预训练权重）就能显著影响下游性能，由此提出一种极简的适配方案。

Method: ScAle 学习一组标量系数，对冻结 VLM 骨干网络中选定层的 last-token 注意力输出和 MLP 激活进行重新加权。该方法仅需 1K 可训练参数，无需修改预训练权重，是一种架构无关的极轻量适配方法。

Result: 在 SpatialEval 空间推理基准上取得最高 134.1% 的相对准确率提升；在 COCOQA 和 VGQA 等真实 VQA 数据集上保持非空间 VQA 准确率的同时，显著提升空间推理性能；仅用 1K 参数即可恢复大部分标准 PEFT（如 LoRA）的性能。

Conclusion: 有界激活重加权提供了一种简单、架构无关且高度参数高效的预训练 VLM 适配替代方案，在极紧凑的参数预算下实现了有竞争力的空间推理能力提升。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ScAle，一种超轻量级适配方法（仅1K可训练参数），通过缩放注意力头和MLP激活来提升VLM的空间推理能力，属于多模态模型的高效参数微调（PEFT）方法，因此映射到 canonical 子主题"多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）。

Importance reason: Ultra-lightweight VLM adapter with 1K parameters boosts spatial reasoning, aligning with user's model efficiency and multimodal understanding priorities. Signals: minimal adapter for spatial reasoning, 1K trainable parameters, 134.1% relative gain, frozen backbone, architecture-agnostic

Abstract: Spatial reasoning remains a persistent challenge for many vision language models (VLMs), and improving it typically requires fine-tuning with substantial additional parameters. Our preliminary analysis reveals that rescaling activations in selected transformer layers-without modifying pretrained weights-can significantly influence downstream performance. Motivated by this observation, we propose ScAle, an ultra-lightweight adaptation method that learns a small set of scalar coefficients to modulate last-token attention and MLP activations in a fully frozen backbone. We evaluate our method on the synthetic spatial reasoning benchmark SpatialEval and on real-world VQA datasets (COCOQA and VGQA) across multiple model families. Our method, ScAle, achieves up to 134.1% relative accuracy gains using only 1K trainable parameters without requiring millions of trainable parameters as in standard PEFT methods such as LoRA. Despite its extreme compactness, our approach recovers a substantial fraction of standard PEFT performance while preserving strong non-spatial VQA accuracy. These results demonstrate that bounded activation reweighting provides a simple, architecture-agnostic, and highly parameter-efficient alternative for adapting pretrained VLMs.

</details>


### [198] [Fast Enough to Act: Spatio-Temporal Visual Token Merging for Low-Latency Robotic VLMs and VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29350)
*Junzhou Chen, Jindong Wang, Gang Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (67.0) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ST-Merge 是一种即插即用、无需训练的视觉 token 合并框架，通过在视觉编码阶段显式构建3D时空坐标、多队列并行匹配与加权聚合，以及合并后位置校正机制，大幅降低 VLM/VLA 的推理延迟，在 Qwen2.5-VL 上实现2倍加速且精度仅损失1%，在 π0.5 VLA 策略上实现8.3倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）和视觉语言动作模型（VLA）在机器人应用中面临严重的推理延迟问题，因为视频和高分辨率图像产生大量视觉 token，阻碍了机器人的实时控制。现有 token 压缩方法通常需要训练或微调，且缺乏对时空一致性的考虑。

Method: 提出 ST-Merge 框架：(1) 显式构建3D时空坐标，将多帧视觉 token 映射到统一的时空网格；(2) 多队列并行匹配与加权聚合机制，高效融合帧间冗余 token；(3) 合并后位置校正机制，通过动态重评估加权质心的旋转位置编码来消除空间偏差。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-VL 的 Video QA 任务上实现2倍推理加速，精度仅下降1%；在 π0.5 VLA 策略上，1024×1024 分辨率下实现8.3倍加速且成功率与基线持平。

Conclusion: ST-Merge 是一种有效的即插即用 token 压缩方法，能在几乎不损失性能的情况下显著加速 VLM/VLA 推理，为机器人实时控制提供了可行的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ST-Merge，一种通过时空视觉 token 合并来加速 VLM/VLA 推理的方法，属于多模态模型的高效化与轻量化方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for multimodal models, a high-priority subtopic, with a training-free token merging method achieving significant speedups. Signals: training-free token merging, low-latency VLM/VLA, 2x speedup with 1% loss, 8.3x speedup for VLA, spatio-temporal visual tokens

Abstract: Vision-language models and vision-language action models endow the robot with unprecedented capabilities. However, the input of video and high-resolution images yields a massive number of visual tokens, leading to extremely high inference latency and severely hindering the robot's real-time control. To break through this computational bottleneck, we propose ST-Merge, a plug-and-play, training-free framework that efficiently fuses redundant tokens directly during the visual encoding phase. By explicitly constructing 3D spatiotemporal coordinates, it employs a multi-queue parallel matching and weighted aggregation mechanism to achieve efficient and geometrically consistent fusion of redundant tokens across frames. In addition, we introduce a post-merge positional correction mechanism that effectively eliminates spatial deviation caused by merging by dynamically re-evaluating the rotational position code of the weighted centroid of the vision token, thereby ensuring the high-precision spatial awareness required for dexterous operation. In the Video Question Answering task on the mainstream VLM, Qwen2.5-VL, ST-Merge achieves a 2$\times$ inference speedup with only a tiny 1\% loss in precision. When deployed on the $\pi_{0.5}$ VLA policy, ST-Merge achieves an 8.3$\times$ speedup at 1024 $\times$ 1024 resolution and matches the baseline success rate at this high-resolution setting. At lower resolutions, it introduces a small drop in accuracy.

</details>


### [199] [Before Thinking, Learn to Decide: Proactive Routing for Efficient Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30217)
*Yinan Zhou, Haokun Lin, Yichen Wu, Caifeng Shan, Zhenan Sun, Yuxin Chen, Teng Wang, Chen Ma, Li Zhu, Ying Shan*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (66.05) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出PRP（Proactive Routing Paradigm），一种面向多模态视觉推理的高效推理框架。通过草稿模型内部置信度估计（DRL）和联合评分学习（JRL）预测目标模型对查询的处理能力，实现实例级别的主动路由——将简单样本分配给草稿模型、将目标模型擅长的样本优先分配给它，从而在不牺牲整体性能的前提下显著加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在复杂视觉任务中虽推理能力强，但长思维链导致推理效率低下。现有路由方法要么依赖后验token概率（在多模态场景下效果不佳），要么依赖有监督微调（数据敏感），且仅在完整输出后才进行路由，忽略了目标模型是否真正能解决路由过来的实例。因此需要一种能在早期做出路由决策、同时评估草稿和目标模型能力的高效多模态推理方法。

Method: 提出PRP主动路由范式，包含两个核心组件：(1) Draft Rating Learning (DRL)——为草稿模型配备内部置信度估计器，使其能评估自身对查询的处理能力；(2) Joint Rating Learning (JRL)——预测目标模型对给定查询的处理能力，优先分配目标模型擅长的样本（而非最难的样本）。两者结合实现细粒度的实例级主动路由决策。

Result: 在多个多模态推理基准上进行了大量实验，验证了PRP在保持整体性能的同时显著加速推理的有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: PRP通过主动路由范式，在推理前就联合评估草稿和目标模型的能力，实现了更高效的多模态推理加速，为多模态大模型的高效部署提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出PRP主动路由范式，通过联合评估草稿模型和目标模型的能力，实现多模态推理中的自适应路由加速。核心贡献是提升多模态大模型推理效率，属于多模态高效化与轻量化方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化" (efficient_multimodal)。

Importance reason: Proposes a proactive routing framework for efficient multimodal visual reasoning, directly addressing the user's priority on model efficiency and unified multimodal models. Signals: efficient inference, multimodal reasoning, proactive routing, draft-target model, confidence estimation

Abstract: Large multimodal models have achieved strong reasoning on complex visual tasks, but their inference efficiency is often restricted by long chains of thought. A promising solution is to pair a small draft model with a large target model, enabling cooperative inference employing a routing signal that adaptively routes queries to either the draft or target model based on their difficulties for optimal efficiency and accuracy. Yet, the remaining bottleneck is to establish a reliable query difficulty signal under multimodal settings. Existing approaches designed for language models either rely on post-hoc token probabilities, which fall short in multimodal scenarios, or depend on supervised fine-tuning, which is a data-sensitive strategy. Both paradigms perform routing only after a complete output, and ignore whether the target model can actually solve the routed instances. To address this, we propose PRP, a Proactive Routing Paradigm that enables early decision-making by jointly evaluating the competence of both the draft and target models. Our Draft Rating Learning (DRL) equips the draft model with an internal confidence estimator, while Joint Rating Learning (JRL) predicts how well the target model can handle a given query, thereby prioritizing the allocation of samples it excels at rather than the hardest ones. These ratings enable fine-grained, instance-level \textbf{Proactive Routing} and substantially accelerate inference without compromising overall performance. Extensive experiments across multiple multimodal reasoning benchmarks validate our effectiveness and efficiency.

</details>

### [200] [Can MLLMs Critique Like Humans? Evaluating Open-Ended Aesthetic Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29689)
*Sajjad Ghiasvand, Maryam Amirizaniani, Haniyeh Ehsani Oskouie, Mahnoosh Alizadeh, Ramtin Pedarsani*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文评估了多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在开放式美学评论任务上的表现，发现基于参考的相似度指标会高估模型能力——模型实际上只是生成流畅、全面的评论风格，而非像人类那样具有选择性和特异性。模型在长度、覆盖广度、均匀性和重复性方面与人类存在系统性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的美学评估大多使用多项选择或数值评分，无法衡量MLLM生成开放式美学评论的能力。作者希望探究MLLM的评论是否真正接近人类评论，并揭示现有参考相似度指标的局限性。

Method: 使用Reddit Photo Critique数据集，对5个开源MLLM在6种提示条件下生成评论，与多个人类评论进行参考相似度比较（包括词法指标、学习指标和粗粒度嵌入余弦相似度），并加入图像接地控制实验（给模型错误的照片）来诊断指标的有效性。

Result: 严格的词法和学习指标显示MLLM与人类评论对齐较弱；粗粒度嵌入余弦相似度显示广泛主题重叠，但接地控制实验表明这源于模型的稳定"house style"而非图像特异性观察。模型在长度上比人类多2-3倍，覆盖几乎所有美学方面（人类有选择性），每个方面更均匀深入，且对同一照片的评论重复性高。

Conclusion: 参考相似度指标奖励的是流畅、全面的评论风格，而非人类评论的选择性和特异性。这为评估和训练开放式多模态生成提供了重要启示，需要开发更好的评测方法。

Classification reason: 该论文评估MLLM在开放式美学评论（aesthetic critique）上的表现，提出基于参考的相似度指标并分析其局限性，属于多模态生成（评论也是一种生成）的评测方法研究。映射到multimodal_understanding_generation方向下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "generation_benchmark"（生成模型评测与数据集），因为论文核心是提出评测方法和分析评测指标的有效性。

Importance reason: Evaluates MLLM critique quality but focuses on aesthetic reasoning, not the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: MLLM evaluation, aesthetic critique, reference-based similarity, human alignment, open-ended generation

Abstract: Open-ended aesthetic critique is a challenge for multimodal large language models (MLLMs): unlike multiple-choice aesthetic benchmarks, it has no single correct answer, and most aesthetic evaluation has measured models against numeric scores rather than the written critiques people actually give. We evaluate MLLM critiques against ranked human references and ask whether they are close to human ones. Using the Reddit Photo Critique Dataset, we score five open-weight MLLMs against multiple ranked human critiques per photo with reference-based similarity metrics, under six prompt conditions that disentangle persona framing, aspect hinting, length control, and single- versus multi-pass generation, and add an image-grounding control that feeds each model the wrong photograph. We find that reference-based similarity gives a misleading picture. Stricter lexical and learned metrics show only weak alignment with human critiques, while a coarse embedding cosine reports broad topical overlap that the grounding control traces to a stable house style rather than image-specific observation. Behaviorally, the models diverge from humans in consistent ways the scores do not surface: even under a length cap they write two to three times as much, cover nearly every aesthetic aspect where humans are selective, engage each aspect more uniformly and at greater depth, and repeat themselves across critiques of the same photo where humans vary. We argue that reference-based similarity rewards a fluent, comprehensive critique style rather than the selectivity and specificity of human critique, and discuss implications for evaluating and training open-ended multimodal generation.

</details>


### [201] [Rigel: Self-Distilled Score Adaptation for Image and Video Captioning Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29997)
*Shuitsu Koyama, Kazuki Matsuda, Yuiga Wada, Shinnosuke Hirano, Daichi Yashima, Komei Sugiura*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Rigel 是一种基于自蒸馏分数适应的图像和视频字幕自动评估指标，通过从冻结的 LLM 中蒸馏出评估专用评分头，并利用人工判断数据微调 LLM 骨干，在多个基准上超越现有指标，在 ActivityNet-Fact 上参考无关设置下提升超过 10 个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有字幕评估指标与人类判断的对齐程度有限，而基于 LLM-as-a-Judge 的方法虽然有所改进，但存在大词汇量语言建模与小标签集评估之间的不匹配问题。

Method: 提出 Rigel 指标，采用从冻结 LLM 中蒸馏出的评估专用评分头，将判断信号映射到任务对齐的空间中，避免依赖大词汇量 token 集；随后使用人工判断数据微调 LLM 骨干。同时构建了包含 3,338 个视频片段、33,380 条参考字幕和 5,637 条候选字幕的 Vid-Lepus 数据集用于训练。

Result: 在多个基准上，Rigel 优于现有最先进的评估指标，在 ActivityNet-Fact 的参考无关设置下取得超过 10 个点的提升。

Conclusion: 自蒸馏分数适应方法能有效解决 LLM-as-a-Judge 中词汇量与评估标签集不匹配的问题，显著提升字幕评估与人类判断的对齐度。

Classification reason: 该论文提出图像和视频字幕生成的自动评估指标 Rigel，并构建了 Vid-Lepus 数据集，属于多模态生成模型的评测与数据集方向。该论文主要关注多模态理解与生成（字幕生成）的评估，因此归入 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下的 generation_benchmark（生成模型评测与数据集）子主题，该子主题在 multimodal_understanding_generation 下已有记录。

Importance reason: Focuses on captioning evaluation, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Captioning evaluation, Self-distilled score adaptation, LLM-as-a-Judge, Vid-Lepus dataset, Reference-free improvement

Abstract: Automatic evaluation of image and video captioning is essential for benchmarking multimodal systems, although standard evaluation metrics show limited alignment with human judgments. Recent approaches using large language models (LLMs), commonly referred to as LLM-as-a-Judge, have improved alignment with human judgments but still suffer from a mismatch between large-vocabulary language modeling and evaluation over a small label set. To address this, we propose Rigel, an automatic evaluation metric for image and video captioning, based on self-distilled score adaptation. The metric employs an evaluation-specific scoring head distilled from a frozen LLM, which captures judgment signals in a task-aligned space without relying on large-vocabulary token sets. We then refine the LLM backbone with human judgment data. To train Rigel, we constructed the Vid-Lepus dataset, which contains 3,338 video clips, 33,380 reference captions, and 5,637 candidate captions. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that Rigel outperforms state-of-the-art metrics, achieving over 10-point improvements on ActivityNet-Fact in the reference-free setting.

</details>


### [202] [SABER-Math: Automated Benchmark for Information Retrieval Evaluation in Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29894)
*Nikolay Georgiev, Maria Drencheva, Kseniia Ibragimova, Ivo Petrov, Dimitar I. Dimitrov, Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.IR, cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SABER-Math 是首个无需人工标注的数学信息检索自动化评测基准，通过 LLM 提取解题摘要和主题、基于本体和词汇相似度发现相关文档、以及瑞士制 LLM 偏好锦标赛生成细粒度相关性评分，构建了 283K 高中数学问题的重排序评测任务。实验表明现代嵌入模型显著优于传统和数学专用检索器，但在代数和微积分等符号密集型领域仍表现不佳，且通用 IR 基准无法可靠预测数学检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索评测基准无法捕捉细粒度的数学相关性，且人工标注成本高昂；同时，通用 IR 基准（如 MTEB）在数学领域不可靠，亟需一个自动化、数学专用的检索评测基准。

Method: 从 283K 高中数学问题出发，三步构建重排序任务：(i) LLM 提取解题摘要和数学主题；(ii) 基于本体主题相似度和词汇解题摘要相似度发现相关文档；(iii) 瑞士制 LLM 偏好锦标赛生成细粒度相关性评分。整个过程无需人工标注。

Result: 现代嵌入模型（如 text-embedding-3-large）显著优于 BM25 等传统检索器和数学专用检索器（如 MathBERT），但在代数和微积分等符号密集型领域表现较差。MTEB 等通用基准无法可靠预测数学检索性能。

Conclusion: SABER-Math 提供了首个全自动的数学信息检索评测基准，揭示了数学领域检索的特殊挑战，强调了数学专用评测的必要性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了数学信息检索的自动化评测基准 SABER-Math，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）子主题，该子主题涵盖评测基准、新数据集与自动评测指标。

Importance reason: Focuses on math IR evaluation, not on the user's priority generative or continuous multimodal models. Signals: math-specific retrieval benchmark, automated evaluation, no human annotation, modern embeddings outperform, symbol-heavy domains struggle

Abstract: As agentic AI systems tackle more complex mathematical tasks, they increasingly rely on information retrieval (IR) to search problem databases, theorem libraries, and educational resources. However, choosing the right retriever remains difficult, as it is infeasible to directly isolate its effect on downstream performance. On the other hand, existing retrieval-specific benchmarks often fail to capture fine-grained mathematical relevance, penalizing relevant documents. We address this gap by introducing SABER-Math, the first fully automated benchmark for evaluating mathematical IR without expert annotation. Starting from 283K high-school-level math problems with solutions, SABER-Math builds challenging reranking tasks in three steps: (i) first, LLMs extract concise solution summaries and mathematical topics for each problem; (ii) then, per-query relevant documents are discovered using ontology topic-based and lexical solutions-summary-based similarities, and (iii) finally, a Swiss-style LLM preference tournament produces fine-grained relevance ratings for the documents. We evaluate lexical retrievers, specialized mathematical retrieval systems, and recent embedding models. We find that while modern embedding models substantially outperform classical and math-specific baselines, even the strongest systems struggle in symbol-heavy domains like Algebra and Calculus. Importantly, we show that general-purpose IR benchmarks such as MTEB do not reliably predict mathematical performance, especially for recent embedding models, highlighting the need for math-specific retrieval benchmarks.

</details>

### [203] [Open-Vocabulary and Referring Segmentation for 3D Gaussians Using 2D Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30638)
*Jameel Hassan, Yasiru Ranasinghe, Vishal Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GaussDet 利用2D开放词汇检测器替代密集CLIP特征蒸馏，通过为3D高斯学习实例特征、多视角语义投票聚合，实现3D场景的开放词汇分割与指代表达定位，在零样本设置下显著提升指代定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于3DGS的语言驱动场景理解方法依赖密集CLIP特征蒸馏，实例分组需要预定义数量或存在噪声，且CLIP仅支持简单名词短语，无法处理复杂空间推理与指代表达定位。

Method: 为每个3D高斯学习实例特征以分解场景为3D实例组；渲染这些组并从多视角2D检测中聚合语义投票，生成视图聚合语义标签分布（VASD），作为强正则化器抑制低质量分组导致的噪声标签；支持从简单语言查询零样本扩展到复杂指代定位。

Result: 在开放词汇分割（LeRF-OVS, ScanNet）和指代表达定位（Ref-LeRF）任务上均取得一致改进，指代定位零样本设置下mIoU提升16.7%。

Conclusion: GaussDet通过利用2D检测器替代CLIP蒸馏，有效实现了3D场景的开放词汇理解与指代定位，方法简洁且零样本泛化能力强。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于3D高斯泼溅的开放词汇分割与指代表达定位，属于3D场景理解与空间推理范畴，与现有 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "空间与 3D 多模态推理" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Uses 2D detectors instead of CLIP for 3D open-vocabulary segmentation, but is not aligned with user's priority subtopics. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, open-vocabulary segmentation, referring expression grounding, zero-shot, 2D detector

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged at the forefront of 3D scene reconstruction. Extending 3DGS with language-driven, open-vocabulary understanding has gained significant attention for real-world applications such as embodied AI. Recent methods achieve this by learning an instance feature attribute and assigning semantics by distilling high-dimensional Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) features directly into the scene representation. However, the instance grouping mechanisms of these methods either require a predefined number of instances or suffer from noise in their bottom-up grouping strategies. Furthermore, the reliance on CLIP restricts semantic understanding to simple noun phrases, preventing complex spatial reasoning and referential expression grounding. We present GaussDet, a method that circumvents the need for dense CLIP features by leveraging discrete, open-vocabulary 2D object detectors with referring expression capabilities. We learn instance features for individual Gaussians to decompose the scene into 3D instance groups. By rendering these groups and aggregating semantic votes from multi-view 2D detections, we generate a robust View-Aggregated Semantic Label Distribution (VASD) for each 3D instance. This view-aggregation strategy acts as a strong regularizer, attenuating spurious labels caused by low-quality instance grouping. Our approach enables a straightforward, zero-shot extension from simple language queries to complex referential grounding. Extensive evaluations across two key tasks -- open-vocabulary segmentation (LeRF-OVS, ScanNet) and referring expression grounding (Ref-LeRF) -- demonstrate that GaussDet achieves consistent improvements over existing methods. Most notably, we achieve a substantial 16.7% mIoU improvement in referential grounding within a strict zero-shot setting.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-unified_multimodal_model'></div>
