### [204] [COMPASS: Grounding Composition-Intent Guidance in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28696)
*Ziqi Zhou, Weize Quan, Mining Tan, Zhihan Chen, Dandan Zheng, Jingdong Chen, Jun Zhou, Weiming Dong, Dong-Ming Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (92.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: COMPASS 是首个统一多模态框架，通过共享专家 token τ_c 将构图意图理解与构图引导生成统一在单一系统中，并构建了大规模构图数据集 Comp-11，在构图理解和可控生成上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型在细粒度构图识别上不可靠，且难以将构图意图转化为可控生成。缺乏一个同时支持构图感知和构图引导生成的统一框架。

Method: COMPASS 采用 MoE 骨干网络注入构图专家知识，将推理出的构图意图蒸馏到共享专家 token τ_c 中；在生成侧，复用 τ_c 作为全局条件信号引导去噪轨迹，将被动构图分析转化为显式布局控制。同时构建了包含 11 类分类体系和推理增强标注的大规模数据集 Comp-11。

Result: 实验表明，COMPASS 在类别级构图理解上显著提升，并能生成比强基线更符合构图意图、更忠实于提示的图像。

Conclusion: COMPASS 证明了通过共享意图锚点 token 统一构图理解与生成的有效性，为统一多模态模型在细粒度视觉意图控制方面提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 COMPASS，一个统一多模态框架，同时支持构图感知（理解）和构图引导生成，属于理解与生成一体化方向。该论文与 multimodal_understanding_generation 下的 [CANONICAL] 统一多模态模型（unified_multimodal_model）高度匹配，同时与 multimodal_generation 方向也相关。

Importance reason: Directly advances unified multimodal models with composition-intent grounding, a high-priority subtopic. Signals: unified multimodal framework, composition understanding and generation, shared expert token, large-scale dataset Comp-11, MoE backbone

Abstract: Composition is a high-level visual intent that governs where subjects are placed and how a scene is organized, yet current unified multimodal models remain unreliable at fine-grained composition recognition and struggle to turn such intent into controllable generation. We present COMPASS, the first unified multimodal framework that grounds composition-intent control in a single system spanning both composition perception and composition-guided generation, with a shared expert token $\tau_c$ as the central intent anchor. On the perception side, COMPASS injects composition expertise into an MoE backbone in a minimally invasive manner and distills the inferred intent into $\tau_c$. On the generation side, COMPASS reuses $\tau_c$ as a global conditioning signal that steers the denoising trajectory, effectively converting passive composition analysis into explicit layout control. To support systematic instruction-following composition learning and evaluation at scale, we construct Comp-11, a large-scale dataset with an 11-class taxonomy and reasoning-augmented annotations. Extensive experiments show that COMPASS substantially improves category-level composition understanding and delivers more composition-consistent, prompt-faithful generation than strong baselines.

</details>


### [205] [Illuminating Unified Multimodal Model for Free-form Interleaved Text-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30054)
*Chonghuinan Wang, Zhikai Chen, Chunwei Wang, Yecong Wan, Junwei Yang, Zhixin Wang, Wei Zhang, Jiaqi Xu, Renjing Pei, Xiaohe Wu, Fan Li, Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ILLUME-X 是一个支持自由形式交错文本-图像生成的统一多模态模型，通过改进数据管道、渐进式训练策略和自适应目标函数，在风格迁移、图像分解和故事叙述等交错生成任务上超越现有统一模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型在自由形式交错文本-图像生成方面能力有限，主要受限于多模态数据效率低下和训练过程不稳定。作者旨在构建一个能够自主生成高质量、自由形式交错文本-图像序列的统一多模态模型。

Method: ILLUME-X 包含三个关键组件：(i) 针对交错文本-图像生成优化的扩展训练数据管道；(ii) 渐进式训练策略，结合自适应目标函数以处理自由长度的多模态 token 序列；(iii) 用于交错文本-图像序列的客观综合评估方法 ILScore。

Result: ILLUME-X 在风格迁移、图像分解和故事叙述等多个交错文本-图像生成任务上优于之前的统一模型。

Conclusion: ILLUME-X 通过改进数据效率和训练稳定性，有效提升了统一多模态模型在自由形式交错文本-图像生成上的性能，为该方向提供了有前景的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ILLUME-X，一个支持自由形式交错文本-图像生成的统一多模态模型，属于理解与生成一体化范式。该论文同时匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 和 multimodal_generation 两个方向，但核心贡献在于统一多模态模型架构。现有 subtopic 中 "统一多模态模型"（unified_multimodal_model）在 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下已有定义且完全匹配，故直接复用。

Importance reason: Directly advances unified multimodal generation with strong data and training innovations, highly relevant to your priority subtopic. Signals: unified multimodal model, interleaved text-image generation, progressive training, self-adaptive objectives, ILScore evaluation

Abstract: The advancement of generative AI models capable of producing text and image marks a critical step forward in the realm of multimodal intelligence, particularly for tasks involving the interleaving of both modalities. To advance this intelligence to the next stage, it is crucial for models to autonomously generate free-form interleaved text-image sequences. In this paper, we introduce ILLUME-X, an advanced unified multimodal paradigm that enables high-quality, free-form interleaved text-image generation by improving multimodal data efficiency and stabilizing the multimodal training process. ILLUME-X comprises three key components: (i) an expanded training data pipeline optimized for interleaved text-image generation, (ii) a progressive training strategy with self-adaptive objectives for free-length multimodal token sequences, and (iii) an objective and comprehensive evaluation method ILScore for interleaved text-image sequences. Notably, our ILLUME-X outperforms previous unified models across multiple interleaved text-image generation tasks like style transfer, image decomposition and storytelling.

</details>


### [206] [BrainJanus: A Unified Model for Understanding and Generation across Brain, Vision, and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30319)
*Haitao Wu, Qirui Zhang, Zhouheng Yao, Shangquan Sun, Qihao Zheng, Mianxin Liu, Chi Zhang, Wanli Ouyang, Chunfeng Song, Changqing Zhang, Jiamin Wu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: BrainJanus 是首个将脑信号、视觉和语言统一在单一框架中的脑模型，通过 Unified Brain Tokenizer 将连续神经活动量化为离散 token，并利用 All-in-One 自回归架构实现 any-to-any 的脑编码（图像/文本→脑）与脑解码（脑→图像/文本），在多个基准上取得最优性能，并展现出零样本泛化与可解释的脑地形图保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑编码与脑解码方法通常被当作孤立任务处理，依赖单模态对齐和外部先验，忽视了大脑作为多模态整合系统的本质。缺乏一个统一的框架来建模外部刺激与内部神经活动之间的双向对应关系。

Method: 提出 BrainJanus 统一框架：(1) Unified Brain Tokenizer，将连续 fMRI 神经活动量化为离散 token，并与视觉和语言表征在共享的 Omni 空间中对齐；(2) All-in-One 自回归架构，基于 next-token prediction 实现 any-to-any 生成，包括 image→brain、text→brain（编码）以及 brain→image、brain→text（解码）。

Result: 在多个脑编码与脑解码基准上取得最优性能，展现出零样本泛化能力，并保持了可解释的生物学脑地形图结构。

Conclusion: BrainJanus 验证了将脑信号、视觉和语言统一建模的可行性，为通用脑建模范式提供了新方向，其零样本泛化与可解释性表明该方法具有作为通用脑模型基础的潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 BrainJanus，将脑信号、视觉和语言统一在一个框架中，实现 any-to-any 的理解与生成（脑编码与脑解码）。这属于统一多模态模型（理解与生成一体化、Any-to-Any 多模态模型）范畴，该 subtopic 在 multimodal_understanding_generation 和 multimodal_generation 方向下均有定义，且为 [CANONICAL] 条目。

Importance reason: Novel unified brain-vision-language model but outside user's core priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: unified multimodal model, brain encoding/decoding, autoregressive any-to-any generation, zero-shot generalization, interpretable topography

Abstract: Modeling the bidirectional correspondence between external sensory stimuli and internal neural activity has emerged as a critical frontier in neuroscience. However, existing approaches predominantly treat brain encoding and decoding as isolated tasks, relying heavily on unimodal alignment and external priors while overlooking the brain's intrinsic nature as a multimodal integration system. To address these limitations, we propose BrainJanus, the first unified brain model that integrates brain, vision, and language within a single framework. Specifically, we introduce a Unified Brain Tokenizer to quantize continuous neural dynamics into discrete tokens aligned with visual and linguistic representations in a shared Omni space. Building on this, we utilize an All-in-One autoregressive architecture that leverages next-token prediction to enable seamless any-to-any generation, which encompasses image-to-brain and text-to-brain encoding, and brain-to-image and brain-to-text decoding. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BrainJanus achieves superior performance across diverse benchmarks. Furthermore, our framework exhibits zero-shot generalization and preserves interpretable biological topography, highlighting its potential as a general-purpose brain modeling paradigm. The code is available at \href{ }{GitHub}.

</details>
