### [207] [Recursive Self-Evolving Agents via Held-Out Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28374)
*Michael Nguyen, Quoc Nguyen, Paul Vuong*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 RSEA（Recursive Self-Evolving Agent），一种递归自演化 agent 框架，通过维护三层自然语言状态（策略、技能、剧本）并在保留集上严格筛选候选更新，实现单调安全的自我进化。在 ALFWorld、GAIA、τ-bench 和 WebShop 四个基准上，RSEA 在 ALFWorld 上达到 69.3%（单次）和 79.4%（重试），且从不显著低于基础 agent。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM agent 的自我改进方法（如反射、工作流优化）通常只在单一基准上报告正面结果，缺乏公平对比；且无保护的上下文演化可能导致性能崩溃（如 Dynamic Cheatsheet 在 WebShop 上从 0.43 跌至 0.14）。作者希望系统性地研究这类方法，并提出一种单调安全的递归自演化机制。

Method: RSEA 维护三层自然语言状态：策略（strategy）、技能（skills）和剧本（playbook）。每轮迭代中，agent 从自身轨迹中重写所有三层，生成候选更新；通过严格保留集（held-out split）验证候选是否导致性能退化，仅当不退化时才提交更新（keep-better gate）。在四个基准（ALFWorld、GAIA、τ-bench、WebShop）上与 ReAct、Reflexion、GEPA、AWM、ACE、Dynamic Cheatsheet 六种基线进行公平对比，均使用同一本地 backbone。

Result: 1）没有一种工件（artifact）在所有任务上通用最优：RSEA 在 ALFWorld 上单次达 69.3%（vs ReAct 64.6%，p=0.015），重试达 79.4% 为最佳；AWM 在强 backbone 工具使用任务上最佳。2）无保护的上下文演化高方差且不安全：Dynamic Cheatsheet 在 ALFWorld 上接近最优（70.7%），但在 WebShop 上崩溃（0.14 vs ReAct 0.43）。3）RSEA 的严格保留集选择使递归自演化单调安全：在所有基准上从未显著低于基础 agent，在退化时回退到 vanilla ReAct。

Conclusion: RSEA 证明了通过保留集门控可以实现递归自演化的单调安全性，避免了无保护演化带来的性能崩溃风险。研究也揭示了不同工件类型在不同任务上的适用性差异，为 LLM agent 的自我改进提供了系统性的理解和实用框架。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 LLM agent 的递归自演化方法（通过自然语言状态迭代优化），属于多模态理解与生成方向下的通用 LLM agent 方法。虽然论文本身不直接涉及视觉模态，但其方法（RSEA）可应用于多模态 agent 场景，且与 VLM/MLLM agent 的自我改进范式高度相关，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM agent self-evolution, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: LLM agent evolution, held-out selection, monotone-safe improvement, ALFWorld benchmark, no weight update

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly improved without weight updates by evolving a natural-language artifact, such as reflections, workflows, playbooks, cheatsheets, or optimized prompts, that conditions a frozen policy. Such methods are typically reported as wins on the single benchmark where they help. We study them apples-to-apples and surface a sharper picture. We introduce RSEA, a Recursive Self-Evolving Agent that carries a compact three-layer natural-language state: an imperative strategy, reusable skills, and a procedural playbook. Across generations, RSEA rewrites all three layers from its own trajectories and commits a candidate only if it does not regress on a disjoint held-out split, using a strict keep-better gate. Across four diverse benchmarks, ALFWorld, GAIA, (\tau)-bench, and WebShop, and six faithful baselines, ReAct, Reflexion, GEPA, AWM, ACE, and Dynamic Cheatsheet, all evaluated on one shared local backbone, we find three main results. First, no artifact universally wins. RSEA is the strongest single-pass method on ALFWorld, reaching 69.3% compared with 64.6% for ReAct (McNemar (p=0.015)), and reaches 79.4% with retry, the best overall result. However, concrete-workflow induction, represented by AWM, is best on the strong-backbone tool-use tasks. Second, unguarded context evolution is high-variance and unsafe. Dynamic Cheatsheet, which curates context online without a held-out gate, is near-best on ALFWorld at 70.7%, yet collapses on WebShop, with a score of 0.14 compared with 0.43 for ReAct. Third, RSEA's strict held-out selection is what makes recursive self-evolution monotone-safe: it never significantly underperforms the base agent on any benchmark and falls back to vanilla ReAct when evolved context would hurt.

</details>


### [208] [Digitizing Coaching Intelligence: An Agentic Framework for Holistic Athlete Profiling using VLM and RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28570)
*Deep Ghosal, Ishani Sen, Wazib Ansar, Amlan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI, cs.MA

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于 VLM 和 RAG 的多智能体框架，用于自动化、全面的运动员评估。通过 LangGraph 编排双流水线架构，结合 MediaPipe 的运动学跟踪与 Llama-4-scout 的语义推理，引入 3×3 "智能网格" 时间分块策略降低 88% 计算开销，并采用 "LLM-as-a-Judge" 自校正循环和双持久化 RAG 管道，支持教练用自然语言进行复杂语义查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运动员评估依赖人工观察，主观性强且不可扩展；现有 CV 系统仅能进行定量重复计数，缺乏评估形态退化、脊柱关节活动度、疲劳等定性生理指标的"教练智能"。需要一种自动化、客观、可扩展的全面运动员画像方法。

Method: 提出基于 LangGraph 编排的双流水线混合智能体框架：(1) CV 流水线使用 MediaPipe 进行运动学跟踪，提取几何精确的关键点；(2) VLM 流水线使用 Llama-4-scout 进行语义推理，评估定性指标。引入 3×3 "智能网格" 时间分块策略将视频分割为 3 个时间阶段 × 3 个空间区域，降低 88% 计算开销。采用 "LLM-as-a-Judge" 自校正循环交叉验证定量与定性指标。最后通过双持久化 RAG 管道（ChromaDB）支持自然语言语义查询。

Result: 实验表明该多智能体方法显著缩小了原始生物特征跟踪与可操作的教练洞察之间的差距，提供了可扩展、客观的解决方案，适用于国家级人才识别。

Conclusion: 本文提出的混合智能体框架成功将 VLM 的语义推理能力与 CV 的几何精度相结合，实现了符合印度体育局评估协议的自动化运动员全面画像，为大规模人才选拔提供了客观、可扩展的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于 VLM (Llama-4-scout) 和 RAG 的运动员评估智能体框架，属于视觉语言模型在特定领域的应用，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: The paper's focus on sports coaching with VLM and RAG is outside the user's core priorities of continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, and world models. Signals: VLM and RAG for athlete profiling, Smart Grid temporal chunking reduces overhead, LLM-as-a-Judge self-correction loop, dual-persistence RAG for natural language queries, niche application with limited generalizability to priority subtopics

Abstract: Athlete assessment is a critical process for tracking physical progress and identifying elite talent. However, during mass recruitment drives, traditional methods rely on manual observation, which is inherently subjective and unscalable, or basic computer vision (CV) systems limited to quantitative repetition counting. These standard approaches lack the "coaching intelligence" required to evaluate qualitative physiological markers such as form degradation, spinal articulation, and fatigue. This paper presents a novel, LLM-based hybrid agentic framework for automated, holistic athlete profiling that strictly aligns with the Sports Authority of India (SAI) assessment protocols. Orchestrated via LangGraph, our dual-pipeline architecture synthesizes the geometric precision of CV (MediaPipe) for kinematic tracking with the semantic reasoning of Vision-Language Models (Llama-4-scout). To overcome the latency and token constraints associated with multimodal video processing, we introduce a 3 X 3 "Smart Grid" temporal chunking strategy, reducing computational overhead by over 88% while preserving critical temporal continuity. To ensure data integrity and mitigate hallucination, the framework pioneers an autonomous "LLM-as-a-Judge" self-correction loop that cross-references quantitative and qualitative metrics before persistence. Finally, we implement a dual-persistence Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipeline utilizing a vector search engine (ChromaDB). This enables coaches to bypass rigid SQL databases and perform complex semantic queries (e.g., "Identify athletes with high endurance but poor core rigidity") using natural language. Experimental results demonstrate that this multi-agent approach significantly bridges the gap between raw biometric tracking and actionable coaching insights, offering a scalable, objective solution for national talent identification.

</details>


### [209] [Fine-Tuning General-Purpose Large Language Models for Agricultural Applications:A Reproducible Framework and Evaluation Protocol Based on Qwen3-8B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28992)
*Zhaoyang Li, Ruijie Zhang, Jiaqi Liu, Zhaoji Sun*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 普通相关 (62.41) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgriTune-R，一个基于 Qwen3-8B 的农业 LLM 微调框架，包含数据治理、指令构建、LoRA/QLoRA 高效微调、检索增强生成、专家评估与安全控制，并设计了农业知识问答、病虫害咨询、栽培管理和政策解释的评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型在开放域任务中表现优异，但农业应用具有领域特定、区域依赖、时效性强和安全关键等特点，直接使用通用 LLM 可能产生不可靠的农业建议。现有研究缺乏可复现、可审计的农业 LLM 适配框架和标准化评估协议。

Method: 提出 AgriTune-R 框架，选择 Qwen3-8B 作为基座模型，集成农业数据治理、指令构建、LoRA/QLoRA 参数高效微调、检索增强生成（RAG）、专家评估和高风险问题安全控制。同时设计了包含农业知识 QA、病虫害咨询、栽培管理和政策解释四个维度的评估协议，以及结合事实性、安全性、证据一致性和不确定性表达的专家评审标准。

Result: 论文明确声明不报告未经实际训练和专家评估的模型性能数据，而是提供了一个可执行的基线框架和评估协议，为未来的实证研究奠定基础。

Conclusion: AgriTune-R 框架为农业领域 LLM 适配提供了结构化的工作流和标准化的评估协议，将协议设计与实证结论明确分离，为后续农业 LLM 研究提供了可复现、可审计的基线。

Classification reason: 该论文研究通用大语言模型在农业领域的微调适配，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的视觉语言模型与 MLLM 范畴（vlm_mllm_general），因为论文关注 LLM 的领域适配、指令微调、RAG 与安全控制，与通用 MLLM 微调范式高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on agricultural LLM fine-tuning, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks the core technical innovations (e.g., continuous models, video generation, world models) that the user values. Signals: domain-specific agriculture, no continuous language/multimodal model, no video/image generation, no world model, no autoregressive or diffusion generation

Abstract: General-purpose large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong abilities in opendomain question answering, information extraction, and text generation. Agricultural applications, however, are domain-specific, region-dependent, time-sensitive, and safety-critical. Without data governance, expert evaluation, and evidence constraints, an agricultural assistant mayproduce unreliable advice on crop diseases, pesticide use, fertilization, or policy avoid presenting unverified simulated numbers as real experimental findings, this paper doesnot report any model-performance claims that have not been produced by an actual training runand expert evaluation. Instead, we propose AgriTune-R, a reproducible and auditable frameworkfor adapting general-purpose LLMs to agricultural tasks. The framework selects the publiclyverifiable Qwen3-8B model as the recommended base model and integrates agricultural datagovernance, instruction construction, LoRA/QLoRA parameter-efficient fine-tuning, retrievalaugmented generation, expert evaluation, and safety control for high-risk questions. The contributions are: (1) a structured workflow for agricultural LLM adaptation; (2) an evaluationprotocol for agricultural knowledge QA, pest and disease consultation, cultivation management,and policy explanation; (3) an expert-review rubric combining factuality, safety, evidence consistency, and uncertainty expression; and (4) a clear separation between protocol design andempirical conclusions, providing an executable baseline for future empirical studies.

</details>


### [210] [Self-Evolving Agentic Image Restoration via Deliberate Planning and Intuitive Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28971)
*Shuang Cui, Fan Ji, Guanglong Sun, Yufei Guo, Xiongxin Tang, Jiangmeng Li, Fanjiang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (89.43) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SEAR 提出了一种自进化智能体图像恢复框架，结合直觉执行器（System 1）和审慎规划器（System 2），利用剪枝感知的蒙特卡洛树搜索进行长程推理，并通过退化感知状态指纹索引的自进化情景记忆来克服智能体系统的情景遗忘问题，在合成和真实图像恢复基准上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的图像恢复面临复杂耦合退化，现有基于大语言模型的智能体恢复框架存在两个关键局限：一是过度依赖贪婪策略，无法平衡探索与利用；二是信息利用不足，存在情景遗忘问题。

Method: 将图像恢复建模为序列决策问题，受双过程理论启发，设计直觉执行器（System 1，快速思维）和审慎规划器（System 2，慢速思维）。审慎规划器采用剪枝感知的蒙特卡洛树搜索进行长程推理，结合混合无参考奖励和基于 MLLM 的锦标赛机制防止指标利用。直觉执行器利用退化感知状态指纹索引的自进化情景记忆，将昂贵的搜索轨迹蒸馏为自适应经验，通过记忆复用逐步摊销冷启动探索成本。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准上的大量实验证明了 SEAR 在感知质量和定量指标上的强劲性能。

Conclusion: SEAR 通过双过程理论和自进化记忆机制有效解决了现有智能体图像恢复框架的探索-利用平衡问题和情景遗忘问题，为复杂退化场景下的图像恢复提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SEAR，利用 MLLM 进行图像恢复的智能体规划与决策，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的视觉语言模型与 MLLM 应用（vlm_mllm_general）。

Importance reason: Novel dual-process agentic framework with MCTS and episodic memory for image restoration, but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: agentic image restoration, dual-process theory, MLLM-based planning, Monte Carlo Tree Search, self-evolving episodic memory

Abstract: Real-world image restoration (IR) remains challenging due to complex and coupled degradations. While recent agentic IR frameworks leverage Large Language Models for flexible tool planning, they face two critical limitations. First, from a search scheme perspective, excessive reliance on greedy strategies fails to balance exploration and exploitation. Second, existing agentic systems underutilize information, exhibiting episodic amnesia. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{Self-Evolving Agentic Image Restoration (SEAR)}, which formulates restoration as a sequential decision-making problem. Inspired by the dual-process theory, SEAR comprises an Intuitive Executor and a Deliberate Planner, respectively following the fast-thinking \textit{System 1} and slow-thinking \textit{System 2} principles. The Deliberate Planner employs Pruning-Aware Monte Carlo Tree Search for long-horizon reasoning, utilizing a hybrid no-reference reward and a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM)-based tournament to prevent metric exploitation. Complementarily, the Intuitive Executor leverages a self-evolving episodic memory indexed by degradation-aware state fingerprints. This mechanism distills expensive search trajectories into adaptive expertise, overcoming episodic amnesia while progressively amortizing cold-start exploration costs through memory reuse. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks demonstrate its strong perceptual and quantitative performance.

</details>


### [211] [Dynamic Parsing and Updating Natural Language Specification using VLMs for Robust Vision-Language Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29357)
*Xiao Wang, Liye Jin, Dan Xu, Yuehang Li, Lan Chen, Yaowei Wang, Yonghong Tian, Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (72.66) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于 VLM 的动态解析与更新自然语言规范的方法，用于鲁棒的视觉语言跟踪。通过语言依赖解析机制提取目标、语义概念和背景上下文等核心跟踪成分，并利用 Qwen-VL 进行成分感知的自适应文本描述更新，在多个基准上取得一致优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言跟踪方法通过优化文本描述来缓解目标外观动态变化导致的语义-视觉不匹配，但主流方法直接生成文本存在错误更新、背景干扰和幻觉等问题。

Method: 提出语言依赖解析机制，精确提取目标对象、语义概念和背景上下文等核心跟踪成分；在此基础上，利用预训练 VLM Qwen-VL 进行成分感知的自适应文本描述更新。

Result: 在 TNL2K、LaSOT、TNLLT 和 OTB-LANG 等多个大规模视觉语言跟踪基准上取得一致且优越的跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的动态解析与更新自然语言规范的方法能有效缓解语义-视觉不匹配问题，提升视觉语言跟踪的准确性和鲁棒性。

Classification reason: 该论文利用预训练视觉语言模型 Qwen-VL 进行自然语言规范解析与更新以增强视觉语言跟踪，属于通用视觉语言模型的应用，映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "vlm_mllm_general"（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）。

Importance reason: Focuses on vision-language tracking with VLM-based text updating, which is a niche application not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or generation. Signals: vision-language tracking, VLM-based text update, language dependency parsing, Qwen-VL, tracking benchmark

Abstract: Vision-language tracking guided by natural language specifications leverages high-level semantic cues of target objects to substantially boost tracking accuracy and robustness. Existing studies have verified that adaptively optimizing textual descriptions throughout the tracking process can effectively mitigate the semantic-visual mismatch induced by dynamic variations in target appearance, position, and other inherent attributes. Nevertheless, mainstream methods that directly generate textual information via sequence models or large language models inevitably suffer from inherent defects, including erroneous target updating, excessive background distraction, and pervasive hallucination artifacts. To address the aforementioned limitations, this paper proposes a novel language dependency parsing mechanism to precisely distill core tracking principal components, encompassing target objects, semantic concepts, and background contextual information. On this basis, we perform component-aware adaptive textual description updates by exploiting the powerful cross-modal understanding capability of the pre-trained vision-language model Qwen-VL. By integrating the proposed elaborately designed modules into the baseline framework, our method achieves consistent and superior tracking performance on multiple large-scale vision-language tracking benchmarks, including TNL2K, LaSOT, TNLLT, and OTB-LANG. The source code and pre-trained models will be released at .

</details>


### [212] [Dynamo: Dynamic Skill-Tool Evolution for Vision-Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30185)
*Yutao Sun, Yanting Miao, Hao-Xuan Ma, Mengyu Zhou, Mingshuai Chen, Tiancheng Zhao, Dexin Wang, Lei Lv, Li Xu, Xiaoxi Jiang, Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (84.23) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Dynamo 是一个无需训练的框架，让冻结的 VLM 在少量标注数据上通过自我检查，自动演化出可复用的推理技能（解决认知瓶颈）和可执行的视觉工具（解决感知瓶颈），并积累到持久库中。在 4 个视觉推理基准和 5 个 VLM 骨干上，Dynamo 在所有 20 个设置上均提升直接推理性能（平均 +5.6 准确率），并能与任务特定 RL 方法互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在视觉推理任务上通常需要重训练或手工设计提示和工具，成本高且泛化性差。作者希望设计一种无需训练、无需手工设计的框架，让 VLM 能够自主地从自身错误中学习并演化出可复用的能力。

Method: Dynamo 包含两个核心演化模块：(1) 技能演化——分析正确/错误推理轨迹，提取可复用的推理技能（如"先定位物体再计数"）并存入技能库；(2) 工具演化——识别感知瓶颈，生成可执行的视觉工具（如边缘检测、颜色过滤），每个工具配有一个技能说明何时调用。在推理时，VLM 根据任务从库中检索相关技能和工具进行增强。

Result: 在 4 个视觉推理基准（如 GQA、VQA v2、NLVR2 等）和 5 种 VLM 骨干（LLaVA、BLIP-2 等）上，Dynamo 在所有 20 个设置上均提升性能（平均 +5.6 准确率）。与任务特定 RL 方法（VTool-R1、DeepEyes）相比，Dynamo 以极低计算成本缩小了 65-99% 的 RL 差距，并能与 RL 方法叠加使用。

Conclusion: Dynamo 证明了无需权重更新的训练自由框架也能有效提升 VLM 的视觉推理能力，通过让模型从自身错误中学习可复用的技能和工具，实现了高效、可扩展的性能提升，且与现有 RL 方法互补。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Dynamo，一种无需训练的框架，通过让冻结的 VLM 在少量标注数据上自我检查并演化出可复用的推理技能和视觉工具，从而提升视觉推理能力。这属于视觉语言模型（VLM）的通用能力增强方法，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a training-free skill-tool evolution framework for frozen VLMs, improving visual reasoning without retraining. Signals: training-free VLM adaptation, skill-tool evolution, visual reasoning benchmarks, complements task-specific RL, no weight updates

Abstract: Improving vision-language models (VLMs) on visual reasoning typically requires retraining or hand-designed prompts and tools. We present Dynamo, a training-free framework that adapts a frozen VLM without any weight updates. On a small labeled training subset, the agent inspects its own correct and incorrect attempts and evolves two complementary capabilities: reusable reasoning skills for cognitive bottlenecks, and executable visual tools for perceptual ones. Each generated tool is paired with a skill that specifies when to invoke it, and both capability types accumulate in a persistent library. Across four visual reasoning benchmarks and five VLM backbones, Dynamo improves direct inference on all 20 model--benchmark settings (avg. +5.6 acc). When the tool set is given in advance, the framework learns when to call each tool, and per-step tool choice improves on every tested backbone. Against task-specific RL (VTool-R1, DeepEyes), Dynamo closes 65--99% of the RL gap at a fraction of the compute, and combines additively with RL when available.

</details>


### [213] [ManimAgent: Self-Evolving Multimodal Agents for Visual Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30296)
*Wenjia Jiang, Zongyuan Cai, Yuanhang Shao, Chenru Wang, Boyan Han, Zhixue Song, Keyu Chen, Shengwei An, Xu Yang, Zhou Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (88.71) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ManimAgent 是一个自进化的多模态智能体，利用视觉语言模型对生成的数学动画关键帧进行评分，通过正负双通道情景记忆库跨任务积累反思经验，无需权重更新或人工种子，在 Manim 代码生成任务上显著提升 Pass@1 并减少反思轮数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的多轮反思智能体虽然能在单任务内从失败中恢复，但每个任务都是孤立的片段——一个任务中学到的经验在下一个任务开始前就被丢弃了。作者希望让智能体能够跨任务积累反思经验，实现持续自我进化。

Method: 提出 ManimAgent，包含一个双通道情景记忆库（Episodic Memory Bank）：正通道 M+ 存储成功推理作为软参考示例（Reference Examples），负通道 M- 存储已验证的失败模式作为硬已知陷阱（Known Pitfalls）。每次任务完成后，VLM 对渲染的关键帧进行评分，根据结果更新记忆库。智能体在后续任务中检索相关记忆来指导代码生成与反思。

Result: 在固定探针评测中，与无记忆、匹配预算的检索增强生成（RAG）和打乱记忆基线相比，盲人评估的 Pass@1 随记忆库增长而提升，同时反思轮数下降。

Conclusion: ManimAgent 证明了通过纯记忆机制（无需权重更新）跨任务传递反思经验的有效性，为构建持续自进化的多模态智能体提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ManimAgent，一个基于 VLM 的自进化多模态智能体，通过双通道记忆库跨任务积累反思经验，用于数学动画代码生成。核心是视觉语言模型驱动的多模态智能体系统，属于通用视觉语言模型与 MLLM 应用范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM"。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with user's priority on multimodal understanding/generation and MLLM, with self-evolving memory that boosts code generation for visual education. Signals: multimodal agent, visual language model, self-evolving memory, code generation, Manim animation

Abstract: Multi-round reflection lets agents built on large language models recover from failures within a single task, but each task remains an isolated episode: lessons learned across many reflection rounds on one task are discarded before the next begins. We study this gap on a code-generation task: from a scientific paper section, the agent writes Python in the open-source Manim library to render a mathematical animation. We present ManimAgent, a self-evolving multimodal agent that carries reflection experience across tasks through a dual-channel Episodic Memory Bank grown entirely from its own task stream, with no weight updates and no human seeds. After each animation converges, a vision-language model scores the rendered keyframes; the resulting signals populate a positive channel M+ that stores success rationales as soft Reference Examples, and a negative channel M- that stores validated failure patterns as hard Known Pitfalls. On a fixed-probe evaluation against no-memory, matched-budget retrieval-augmented generation, and shuffled-memory baselines, blind human Pass@1 rises and reflection rounds fall as memory size grows. We will release the code, frozen memory snapshots, and the task stream.

</details>


### [214] [GROW$^2$: Grounding Which and Where for Robot Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30632)
*Yuhong Deng, Yuyao Liu, David Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GROW² 提出一种层次化方法，利用 VLM 进行语义级工具选择与部件推理，结合视觉基础模型进行几何级 3D 区域定位，实现开放世界机器人工具使用的零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要创造性地使用工具（如用盘子切蛋糕），但现有方法难以处理开放世界中未见过的物体作为工具，缺乏对工具"哪个物体"和"哪个区域"的联合推理能力。

Method: 将工具使用接地（affordance grounding）分解为语义和几何两个层次：语义层利用 VLM 的常识推理解析任务指令、选择合适工具并识别工具和目标物体上的任务相关部件；几何层利用视觉基础模型（如 SAM、DINOv2）将选中的部件从单张 RGB-D 图像中定位到精确的 3D 区域。

Result: 在 affordance 预测基准上超越 SOTA 基线；在仿真和真实机器人实验中，对开放类别物体实现零样本泛化，性能优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 物体部件作为自然抽象层次，可以有效拆分语义与几何接地过程，避免端到端训练对大量数据的依赖，为开放世界机器人工具使用提供可行方案。

Classification reason: 论文利用 VLM 的常识推理进行工具选择与部件定位，属于视觉语言模型在机器人工具使用中的应用，映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM"。

Importance reason: Strong robotics grounding work but outside user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: VLM for tool selection, zero-shot affordance grounding, hierarchical semantic-geometric approach, outperforms baselines on benchmarks, real-world robot experiments

Abstract: Can the robot use a plate to cut a cake if no knife is available? Tool use greatly expands robot capabilities, but to use tools creatively beyond their intended functions, the robot faces the challenge of $\textit{open-world affordance grounding}$: select an open-category object to act as a tool and localize its specific region of action. To this end, we introduce GROW$^2$ (GROunding Which and Where), which leverages object parts as a natural abstraction to split the grounding process hierarchically into semantic and geometric levels, thus bypassing the need for data-heavy, end-to-end training. Semantically, GROW$^2$ harnesses the commonsense reasoning of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to parse a natural-language task instruction, select a suitable object as the tool, and identify task-relevant parts on the tool and the target object. Geometrically, vision foundation models then ground the selected parts into precise 3D regions from a single RGB-D image. Experiments on established benchmarks show that GROW$^2$ outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on affordance prediction benchmarks. Further, it achieves zero-shot generalization over open-category objects and outperforms baselines in both simulated and real-world robot tool use experiments.

</details>


### [215] [One Forward Beats Two: InnerZoom for Accurate and Efficient GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30084)
*Chen Liu, Ling Chen, Hanzhang Zhou, Liangyu Chen, Chenglin Cai, Xin Yu, Steven Hoi, Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (87.2) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: InnerZoom 提出一种单次前向传播的跨层证据桥接框架，通过将中间层的目标区域线索压缩为紧凑的跨层证据状态，并在后续解码层中保留、精炼和重新注入该状态来指导坐标预测，从而在不增加额外前向传播开销的情况下实现 ZoomIn 风格的两阶段定位精度。在 6 个 GUI grounding 基准上达到 SOTA，4B 模型平均提升 5.3 点，同时降低延迟 31.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 的 GUI grounding 方法将目标定位建模为自回归坐标生成，但诊断分析发现目标区域感知在中间解码层出现后未能保留并转化为最终的精确坐标预测。ZoomIn 风格的两阶段方法通过外部裁剪和二次推理提升精度，但增加了延迟和计算成本。因此，作者希望在不增加额外前向传播的情况下保留两阶段 zooming 的精度优势。

Method: InnerZoom 是一个单次前向传播框架，包含三个步骤：(1) 从原始前向传播中提取与目标相关的跨层线索，压缩为紧凑的跨层证据状态；(2) 在后续解码层中保留并精炼该状态；(3) 将该状态重新注入解码层以引导坐标预测。整个过程无需额外的 crop-and-rerun 操作。

Result: InnerZoom-4B 在 6 个 GUI grounding 基准上达到 SOTA：OSWorld-G 64.7、UI-Vision 40.2、OSWorld-GR 73.1、MMBench-GUI 87.6，分别超过此前最佳结果 4.1、3.2、2.9 和 2.3 点。在受控 4B 设置下，比 SFT+RL 基线平均提升 5.3 点，超过两阶段 ZoomIn 方法 1.3 点，同时端到端延迟降低 31.8%，TFLOPs 降低约 29%。

Conclusion: InnerZoom 通过跨层证据桥接，在单次前向传播中实现了两阶段 zooming 的精度，同时显著降低了计算开销和延迟，为 MLLM 在 GUI grounding 等需要空间精度的任务上提供了一种高效且准确的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 InnerZoom，一种改进 MLLM 进行 GUI 目标定位（坐标生成）的方法，属于视觉语言模型在 GUI grounding 任务上的架构改进，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a single-forward cross-layer evidence bridging method for GUI grounding that matches two-pass ZoomIn accuracy with lower latency, relevant to efficient inference and MLLM improvement. Signals: Efficient inference, MLLM GUI grounding, Cross-layer evidence bridging, Latency reduction, State-of-the-art on multiple benchmarks

Abstract: MLLM-based GUI grounding methods commonly formulate target localization as autoregressive coordinate generation, enabling models to leverage the strong instruction-following and semantic understanding capabilities of MLLMs. However, this formulation requires the model to retain region-level target evidence while decoding coordinate tokens with the spatial precision demanded by GUI clicking. Our diagnostic analysis reveals that target-region awareness emerges in intermediate decoder layers but is neither retained nor translated into the final coordinate prediction. Existing ZoomIn-style methods address this issue through an external crop-and-rerun pass, which improves localization but increases end-to-end latency and computational cost. To retain the accuracy benefits of two-pass zooming without this extra cost, we propose InnerZoom, a single-forward framework for cross-layer evidence bridging. InnerZoom transforms target-related cues from the original forward pass into a compact cross-layer evidence state, then preserves, refines, and reinjects this state throughout later decoding layers to guide coordinate prediction. Extensive experimental results suggest that InnerZoom-4B achieves state-of-the-art performance on all six GUI grounding benchmarks, obtaining 64.7 on OSWorld-G, 40.2 on UI-Vision, 73.1 on OSWorld-GR, and 87.6 on MMBench-GUI, surpassing the previous best results by 4.1, 3.2, 2.9, and 2.3 points, respectively. Under a controlled 4B setting, InnerZoom improves the same SFT+RL baseline by 5.3 points on average and outperforms two-pass ZoomIn by 1.3 points on average, while reducing end-to-end latency by up to 31.8% and TFLOPs by about 29%. Code and models will be publicly available.

</details>


### [216] [Few-Shot Domain Incremental Learning via Continual Vision-Language Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30190)
*Naeem Paeedeh, Mahardhika Pratama, Wolfgang Mayer, Mukesh Prasad, Weiping Ding, Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出少样本域增量学习（FSDIL）问题及 CVLC 算法，通过视觉-语言原型融合与双共轭投影（DCP）参数高效微调，在基域中预留潜空间以应对新域适应，在多个基准上超越先前方法最高达 16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有域增量学习（DIL）方法需要大量数据来适应新域，在数据稀缺场景下容易过拟合。本文提出少样本域增量学习（FSDIL）这一新问题，旨在解决极端数据短缺下的域增量学习挑战。

Method: 提出 Continual Vision-Language Consolidation (CVLC) 算法。核心包括：(1) 视觉原型校准与通过 LLM 生成多模板/同义词诱导语言原型，融合视觉与语言原型；(2) 双共轭投影（DCP）作为参数高效微调方法，在基域中预留潜空间以应对新域；(3) 共享与域特定组件分离的结构，结合通用知识与域特定细节。

Result: 在多个基准问题上进行实验，CVLC 相比先前方法性能提升最高达 16%。

Conclusion: CVLC 有效解决了少样本域增量学习问题，通过视觉-语言原型融合与潜空间预留策略，在数据稀缺条件下实现了对新域的良好适应。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CVLC 算法，利用视觉-语言原型融合与参数高效微调（DCP）解决少样本域增量学习问题，属于视觉语言模型在多模态理解与生成方向下的应用，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）。

Importance reason: Focuses on few-shot domain incremental learning, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key boost keywords. Signals: Few-shot domain incremental learning, Vision-language consolidation, Parameter-efficient fine-tuning, Latent space reservation, Outperforms prior arts up to 16%

Abstract: Existing domain-incremental learning (DIL) strategies call for massive amounts of data to adapt to new domains and suffer from the overfitting problem in the case of data scarcity. This paper puts forward a relatively uncharted problem, namely, few-shot domain incremental learning (FSDIL), taking into account the problem of extreme data shortages in the realm of DIL. A novel algorithm, namely Continual Vision-Language Consolidation (CVLC), is proposed to address the FSDIL problem, where the key idea lies in the concept of latent space reservation in the base domain coupled with dual coalescent projection (DCP) as a parameter-efficient fine-tuning method. First, the vision prototype is calibrated while multiple templates and synonyms are generated via LLMs to induce the language prototype. The vision and language prototypes are fused. Adaptation to never-ending arrivals of new domains is done by the DCP technique, fine-tuned in such a way to prepare the model to unseen domains via latent-space reservations committed in the base domain. CVLC is structured under shared and domain-specific components to combine general knowledge and domain-specific details. The advantage of our approach is demonstrated through a range of benchmark problems and comparisons with prior arts, in which CVLC outperforms them by up to a 16% gap. Our codes are shared publicly in .

</details>


### [217] [RiverONE: Generating Knowledge-Intensive VLM by Simulated Quantum Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29966)
*Xindian Ma, Xinyu Long, Yefei Zhang, Yanchen Liu, Xianghao Li, Yufu Wen, Yike Hu, Yuedong Zhu, Zeyang Ma, Wen Qin, Yikun Wang, Peng Yang, Monan Wang, Teng Yu*

Main category: cs.AI, quant-ph

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: RiverONE 是一个约 1.9B 参数的轻量级视觉语言模型，利用模拟量子计算在模型构建阶段生成结构化参数，用于量子校准图理解任务。在纯经典 GPU 上推理，以不到 NVIDIA Ising Calibration 1 模型 10% 的参数规模达到了其 95% 以上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子硬件尚不成熟，无法直接用于大规模 VLM 推理；同时科学领域（如量子校准图理解）需要轻量级但知识密集的 VLM。作者希望探索模拟量子计算能否在模型构建阶段帮助生成结构化参数，从而在保持紧凑模型规模的同时提升性能。

Method: RiverONE 采用专用视觉编码器 + InternVL 语言骨干的架构。核心创新在于引入"量子生成参数"（quantum-generated parameters）：在训练过程中使用模拟量子计算生成结构化参数，这些参数在训练后被物化为经典张量，推理时完全运行在经典 GPU 上，无需量子硬件或运行时量子模拟。

Result: RiverONE 以约 1.9B 参数（不到 NVIDIA Ising Calibration 1 模型 10% 的参数规模），在量子校准图理解任务上达到了后者 95% 以上的性能。

Conclusion: 模拟量子计算可以作为构建轻量级、知识密集型科学 VLM 的一种实用的构建阶段机制，在无需量子硬件的情况下提升紧凑模型的性能。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了一个轻量级视觉语言模型 RiverONE（基于 InternVL），用于量子校准图理解任务，属于通用视觉语言模型与 MLLM 架构范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "vlm_mllm_general"。

Importance reason: Uses simulated quantum computation for model construction, but the domain (quantum calibration plot understanding) is niche and not aligned with user's priority subtopics. Signals: simulated quantum computation, lightweight VLM, quantum calibration, knowledge-intensive, classical GPU inference

Abstract: Quantum computing provides a powerful paradigm for representing and transforming high-dimensional information through superposition, entanglement, and measurement-induced nonlinear features. While current quantum hardware is not yet practical for direct large-scale vision-language model (VLM) inference, simulated quantum computation can be used during model construction to generate structured parameters for compact classical AI systems. We build RiverONE, a lightweight vision-language model for quantum calibration plot understanding, using simulated quantum computation. It employs a specialized visual encoder and an InternVL-based language backbone. To compensate for compression-induced information loss, we introduce quantum-generated parameters, which are materialized as classical tensors after training. This allows RiverONE to run entirely on classical GPUs at inference time, with no quantum hardware or runtime quantum simulation. With approximately 1.9 billion parameters, RiverONE achieves at least 95\% of the performance of NVIDIA Ising Calibration 1 on quantum calibration plot understanding tasks while using less than 10\% of its parameter count. These results suggest that simulated quantum computation can serve as a practical construction-stage mechanism for building lightweight, knowledge-intensive scientific VLMs. Our code is available at .

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_vqa_doc'></div>

## 视觉问答与文档理解 [[Back]](#toc)

VQA、文档/图表/表格理解、OCR-free 文档智能。

### [218] [RSGPNet: Geometric Prompting for Remote Sensing Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28410)
*Shanwen Wang, Xin Sun, Sirui Wang, Xiao Xiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (58.75) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: RSGPNet提出了一种无需训练的几何提示框架，用于遥感开放词汇语义分割。通过文本引导粗掩码、几何重提示和一致性验证三个模块，利用物体几何区域和一致性约束来细化分割结果，在遥感数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇语义分割方法主要聚焦于修改CLIP注意力机制，但在遥感领域仍存在局部分割不稳定的问题。遥感图像中物体尺度变化大、边界复杂，需要更鲁棒的分割方法。

Method: RSGPNet包含三个核心模块：(1) 文本引导粗掩码模块（TCM），利用文本提示和输入图像构建初始粗分割掩码；(2) 几何重提示模块（GRP），将粗掩码转换为几何框提示，重新输入分割模型生成细化掩码；(3) 粗到细一致性验证机制（CVM），通过一致性计算防止提示强化错误区域。整体框架无需训练，直接利用预训练模型。

Result: 在多个遥感数据集上的实验表明，RSGPNet在定量和定性指标上均显著优于现有最先进方法，并在复杂区域（如类别边界）表现出良好的可解释性。

Conclusion: RSGPNet通过几何提示和一致性约束，有效提升了遥感开放词汇语义分割的准确性，是一种无需训练的轻量级增强框架。

Classification reason: 该论文研究遥感领域的开放词汇语义分割（open-vocabulary semantic segmentation），属于视觉语言模型在特定视觉理解任务上的应用。虽然遥感分割并非典型的VQA/文档理解，但"开放词汇语义分割"本质上是通过文本引导进行视觉分割，属于多模态视觉理解范畴。现有最接近的[CANONICAL]子主题是"视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc），该子主题涵盖VQA、文档/图表理解等视觉理解任务，开放词汇分割可视为其扩展。此外，该论文也涉及多模态理解与生成方向下的通用视觉语言模型能力。

Importance reason: Remote sensing segmentation is a negative keyword, and the paper lacks connection to priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: remote sensing, open-vocabulary segmentation, geometric prompting, training-free, CLIP adaptation

Abstract: Open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) enables text-guided segmentation of unseen objects, breaking fixed-class limitations to achieve open-world understanding. However, existing OVSS methods primarily focus on modifying the CLIP attention mechanism, which still suffers from unstable local segmentation for remote sensing (RS) domain. To address these limitations, we propose RSGPNet, a training-free geometric prompting framework for RS OVSS that refines segmentation by leveraging object geometric areas and consistency constraints. Specifically, RSGPNet comprises three core modules: a Text-guided Coarse Mask module (TCM), a Geometric Re-prompting Module (GRP), and a Coarse-to-fine Consistency Verification Mechanism (CVM). TCM utilizes text prompts and the input image to construct initial coarse segmentation masks. GRP then converts these coarse masks into geometric box prompts, feeding them back into the segmentation model to generate refined masks. Finally, CVM employs consistency computation to prevent prompting from reinforcing erroneous regions. They allow the model to improve segmentation accuracy in complex areas, such as category boundaries. Extensive experiments on RS datasets demonstrate that RSGPNet significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods across both quantitative and qualitative metrics while exhibiting excellent interpretability. The code is released at \href{ }{ }.

</details>


### [219] [$M^3 QuestionIng$: Multi-modal Multi-span Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28329)
*Anisha Saha, Vaibhav Rathore, Abhisek Tiwari, Akash Ghosh, Sai Ruthvik Edara, Sriparna Saha*

Main category: cs.IR, cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.MM

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (47.46) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 $M^3QAFrame$，一个多模态多跨度医学问答框架，利用视觉线索增强从文本和图像跨度中生成综合答案的能力，并构建了相应的 $M^3 QuestionIng$ 数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学问答系统主要处理纯文本的多跨度问答，但真实医疗文档常包含图像内容，现有方法无法有效利用视觉信息来生成更全面的答案。

Method: 提出 $M^3QAFrame$ 框架，以上下文、查询和图像为输入，使用基于 Transformer 的架构处理文本和图像嵌入，判断句子和图像的相关性，输出包含文本答案和相关图像的联合答案。同时构建了包含查询、医疗上下文、关联医学图像和抽取式答案的多模态多跨度医学问答数据集，并为每个查询-答案对标注了用户意图和查询类型。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在多种评估指标上持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 多模态信息（尤其是视觉线索）能显著提升医学问答系统的综合性和准确性，所提出的框架和数据集为多模态医学问答提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多模态多跨度医学问答框架，涉及视觉与文本联合的问答任务，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的视觉问答（VQA）范畴，与 canonical subtopic "multimodal_vqa_doc"（视觉问答与文档理解）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Medical QA with images is not a priority subtopic and uses negative keywords. Signals: medical diagnosis, clinical, graph neural network, recommender system, protein

Abstract: The growing adoption of AI in healthcare, particularly in preventive care, highlights the critical need for accessibility and precision in Medical Question Answering (MedQA). In recent years, significant efforts have been made to develop multi-span medical question-answering systems, where the answer to a query may span multiple sections or paragraphs of a source document. However, existing systems fall short of aligning with real-world scenarios, where source documents often include both textual and visual content, requiring answers to incorporate images for better comprehension. To address this gap, we propose $M^3QAFrame$, a multi-modal, multi-span medical question-answering framework that leverages visual cues to enhance the generation of comprehensive answers drawn from diverse textual and visual spans. The model takes the context, query, and images as input and outputs an answer containing both textual answers and relevant images. The text and image embeddings are processed using a transformer-based architecture to determine the sentence and image relevance. We curate a multi-modal, multi-span medical question-answering ($M^3 QuestionIng$) dataset containing queries, medical contexts, associated medical images, and extractive answers. Additionally, each query-answer pair is labeled with user intent and query type to enhance query and context comprehension. Extensive experiments show that our approach consistently outperforms existing methods across various evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [220] [CCRC: A Change-Aware Captioning and Reasoning Chain for Image Change Captioning and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28724)
*Jinhong Hu, Xiaoping Wang, Shuyin Huang, Guojin Zhong, Kaitai Liu, Kai Lu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出图像变化描述与分割（ICCS）新任务，并设计双链框架CCRC，通过变化感知注意力融合模块（CCC链）进行语义推理，再通过变化感知Token精炼器（CCS链）进行像素级分割，在合成和真实变化检测基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像变化描述（ICC）缺乏空间定位能力，无法精确指出变化发生的位置。作者希望将变化描述与像素级分割结合起来，提出ICCS新任务，实现结构化的变化描述与精确的空间定位。

Method: 提出CCRC双链框架：1) CCC链（变化描述链）在MLLM的视觉与语言组件之间插入基于多头变化感知注意力的视觉融合模块，增强细粒度变化感知，并判断变化是否可分割；2) 若可分割，激活CCS链（变化分割链），利用CCC的空间先验，通过变化感知Token精炼器进行精确边界定位。

Result: 在合成和真实变化检测基准上，CCRC在变化描述和分割任务上均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: CCRC通过解耦语义推理与空间分割的双链设计，有效解决了图像变化描述与分割的联合任务，验证了变化感知注意力机制在细粒度多模态理解中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出图像变化描述与分割（ICCS）新任务，属于视觉问答与细粒度视觉理解范畴。虽然任务新颖，但本质上是对图像对进行变化描述和定位，与VQA/文档理解中的细粒度视觉理解高度相关，可归入[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"。

Importance reason: Focuses on a niche multimodal task (ICCS) with limited overlap with user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: New task definition, MLLM-based dual-chain framework, Change-aware attention, SOTA on change detection benchmarks, No direct connection to continuous language/video generation

Abstract: Understanding and localizing subtle changes between paired images is critical for tasks such as surveillance and image editing. However, traditional Image Change Captioning (ICC) methods lack spatial grounding, limiting their precision. We introduce Image Change Captioning and Segmentation (ICCS), a new multimodal task that jointly requires structured change description and pixel-level localization. To address ICCS, we propose the Change-aware Captioning and Reasoning Chain (CCRC), a dual-chain framework that decouples semantic reasoning from spatial segmentation. The first chain, Chain-of-Change-Captioning (CCC), enhances fine-grained change perception via a visual fusion module based on Multi-Head Change-aware Attention inserted between the visual and language components of a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM). CCC also determines whether a change is segmentable. If not, it alone generates the caption. Otherwise, the second chain, Chain-of-Change-Segmenting (CCS), is activated, leveraging spatial priors from CCC and refining masks with a Change-aware Token Refiner for accurate boundary localization. We evaluate CCRC on both synthetic and real-world change detection benchmarks with pixel-level supervision. Experiments show CCRC achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [221] [CMTFormer: Marrying Transformer with Hierarchical Information Interaction for RGB-Event Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29136)
*Yu Li, Yuenan Hou, Yingmei Wei, Jiangming Chen, Yanming Guo*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (47.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出CMTFormer，一种用于RGB-Event双模态目标检测的跨模态信息交互Transformer，通过浅层对齐、中层增强和深层自适应融合的分层交互方案，有效处理RGB帧与事件流之间的异质性，在DSEC-Detection和PKU-DAVIS-SOD基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有高时间分辨率和高动态范围的优势，可弥补传统RGB帧的不足。然而，RGB帧与事件流之间存在固有的异质性，现有多模态融合方法在跨模态融合时容易放大噪声或引入冗余特征，导致检测性能受限。

Method: 提出CMTFormer，设计浅层到深层的分层信息交互方案：(1) 浅层对齐模块(SAM)实现RGB与事件低层特征的高效融合，缓解属性差异并抑制噪声；(2) 中层跨模态增强模块(CEM)利用纹理和边缘信息产生相互增强的中层特征；(3) 深层可学习深度融合模块(LDFM)通过可学习权重自适应融合高层RGB与事件线索；(4) 空间先验模块(SPM)利用全局空间信息提升定位精度。

Result: 在DSEC-Detection和PKU-DAVIS-SOD两个事件目标检测基准上，CMTFormer在单模态和多模态设置下均一致超越现有检测方法，验证了分层交互方案的有效性。

Conclusion: CMTFormer通过分层跨模态信息交互有效解决了RGB与事件流之间的异质性问题，为事件相机辅助下的目标检测提供了高效稳定的多模态融合范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究RGB-Event双模态目标检测，属于多模态视觉理解任务。虽然现有subtopic中无完全匹配的"RGB-Event目标检测"，但"视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）涵盖VQA及多模态视觉理解任务，且该论文核心是多模态融合与视觉检测，与多模态理解方向最为接近。考虑到论文主要贡献在跨模态融合方法而非生成或世界模型，归入多模态理解方向下的该subtopic。

Importance reason: Focuses on RGB-Event object detection, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: RGB-Event detection, hierarchical fusion, event camera, object detection, not autoregressive

Abstract: Event cameras capture sparse brightness changes with high temporal resolution and high dynamic range, compensating for the deficiencies of the conventional RGB frames. However, previous multi-modal fusion techniques typically fail to handle the inherent heterogeneity between RGB frames and event streams, thus easily leading to noise amplification or redundant feature integration during cross-modal fusion. In this paper, we propose a Cross-Modal information inTeraction transFormer, coined as CMTFormer, which hierarchically integrates RGB and event information to achieve efficient and stable multimodal collaboration. Specifically, we design a shallow-to-deep information interaction scheme. In the shallow stage, we present the Shallow Alignment Module (SAM) to achieve an efficient fusion of RGB and event low-level features, which mitigates attribute disparities and prevents noisy information. In the middle stage, we devise the Cross-modal Enhancement Module (CEM) that utilizes texture and edge information to produce mutually reinforced middle-level features. In the deep stage, we present the Learnable Deep Fusion Module (LDFM) which performs high-level information aggregation through learnable weights, thus enabling the network to adaptively fuse RGB and event clues. A Spatial Prior Module is further designed to utilize global spatial information to enhance localization accuracy. Extensive experiments are conducted on two prevalent event-based object detection benchmarks, i.e., DSEC-Detection and PKU-DAVIS-SOD. Our CMTFormer consistently surpasses the detection counterparts in both uni-modal and multi-modal settings, strongly demonstrating the effectiveness of our paradigm. Codes will be available upon publication.

</details>


### [222] [Enhancing Part-Level Point Grounding for Any Open-Source MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29267)
*Jin-Cheng Jhang, Fu-En Wang, Xin Yang, Nan Qiao, Lu Xia, Min Sun, Cheng-Hao Kuo*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 值得关注 (67.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通用方法，为任意开源多模态大模型（MLLM）赋予精确的2D部件级点定位能力。通过冻结原始MLLM参数，利用其注意力机制，设计Q-Synth模块合成文本条件化的定位感知查询，再通过轻量级Attention-to-Point解码器将注意力模式转换为点热图，实现部件级点定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在物体级视觉定位上表现良好，但在部件级（part-level）定位上仍存在困难，而部件级定位对于机器人操作等细粒度任务至关重要。现有方法通常需要重新训练或修改MLLM，缺乏一种通用、即插即用的解决方案。

Method: 提出Q-Synth Module，在MLLM中间层合成文本条件化、定位感知的查询向量，捕获目标相关的注意力模式；再通过轻量级Attention-to-Point Decoder将这些模式转换为点热图进行预测。所有原始MLLM参数保持冻结，仅训练新增的轻量模块。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，该方法一致地提升了部件级点定位精度，且可无缝集成到任意开源MLLM中，同时完整保留其预训练能力。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过冻结MLLM并利用其内部注意力机制，可以高效地为任意开源MLLM赋予部件级点定位能力，为细粒度视觉理解与机器人操作等应用提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉定位（visual grounding），特别是部件级（part-level）点定位，属于视觉问答与细粒度视觉理解范畴。虽然论文聚焦于部件级点定位而非传统VQA，但visual grounding是VQA/文档理解方向下的经典子任务，且[multimodal_vqa_doc]是现有CANONICAL subtopic中最为匹配的类别。

Importance reason: Focuses on part-level point grounding for MLLMs, which is not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Part-level grounding, MLLM enhancement, attention mechanism, frozen parameters, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Visual grounding aims to associate free-form textual queries with specific regions in an image. While recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated promising capabilities in this domain, they primarily excel at object-level grounding and often struggle with part-level grounding-an essential requirement for fine-grained tasks such as robotic manipulation. In this work, we introduce a general approach that equips any open-source MLLMs with accurate 2D part-level point grounding, offering a more direct alternative to conventional grounding representations. Our method leverages the attention mechanisms inherently present in MLLMs. By synthesizing text-conditioned, grounding-aware queries within intermediate layers via the proposed Q-Synth Module, we capture target-relevant attention patterns and refine them with a lightweight Attention-to-Point Decoder, which converts these patterns into a point-centric heatmap for final prediction. Notably, all original MLLM parameters are frozen, ensuring full preservation of their pre-trained capabilities. Experiments show that our design consistently improves part-level grounding accuracy across datasets and can be seamlessly integrated into any open-source MLLMs.

</details>


### [223] [SonoCLIP: Mask-Guided Region-Aware Vision-Language Pretraining for Fetal Ultrasound Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29586)
*Hang Su, Chao Sun, Zhaofan Li, Wei Hu, Juhua Liu, Bo Du*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (44.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SonoCLIP 是首个百万级区域可控的胎儿超声视觉语言基础模型，通过将分割掩码作为视觉提示嵌入视觉编码器，实现全局-局部对比学习，并引入基于 sigmoid 的成对对比损失以提升大规模监督下的稳定性。在包含 144 万图像、24 个标准切面的胎儿超声数据集上预训练后，跨中心零样本迁移性能显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CLIP 类模型在超声图像分析中仅依赖全局图像-文本对齐，对临床关键的局部结构（如胎儿器官边界）不敏感，且超声图像存在散斑噪声、采集变异大、解剖边界模糊等问题，导致观察者间差异大。因此需要一种能同时捕捉全局和局部语义的视觉语言基础模型。

Method: 1) 在视觉编码器中引入分割掩码作为 mask-channel visual prompt，将掩码叠加到图像通道上，使模型能关注特定区域；2) 提出基于 sigmoid 的成对对比损失（Sigmoid-based Pairwise Contrastive Loss），替代传统 InfoNCE 损失，提升大规模监督下的训练稳定性；3) 构建包含 144 万图像、覆盖 24 个标准胎儿超声切面的多模态数据集用于预训练。

Result: 在跨中心零样本迁移评估中，SonoCLIP 在全局推理和掩码引导推理两种模式下均取得优于现有方法的性能，验证了区域可控对比学习在超声分析中的有效性。

Conclusion: SonoCLIP 通过掩码引导的区域感知对比学习，为胎儿超声分析建立了一个可控且面向临床的基础模型，证明了将分割先验融入视觉语言预训练的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SonoCLIP，一个面向胎儿超声分析的视觉-语言基础模型，属于医学图像分析中的视觉语言模型。虽然具体应用是超声，但其核心是 CLIP 框架下的视觉-语言对比学习与零样本迁移，与多模态理解与生成方向下的"视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）子主题在方法范式上最为接近（CLIP-based VLM + 区域级对齐），且该子主题涵盖医学图像 VLM 分析。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical ultrasound, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: medical imaging, fetal ultrasound, CLIP adaptation, mask-guided learning, zero-shot transfer

Abstract: Vision-language foundation models have shown strong potential in medical image analysis. Although foundation models for ultrasound imaging have recently emerged, the domain remains particularly challenging due to severe speckle noise, acquisition variability, and subtle anatomical boundaries, leading to high inter-observer variability. Existing CLIP-based models rely primarily on global image-text alignment, limiting their sensitivity to clinically decisive local structures. We propose SonoCLIP, the first million-scale region-controllable fetal ultrasound vision-language foundation model that integrates segmentation masks as mask-channel visual prompts within the vision encoder, enabling joint global-local contrastive representation learning. To support scalable region-text alignment, we introduce a sigmoid-based pairwise contrastive loss that improves stability under large-scale supervision. We further curate a 1.44M-image multimodal fetal ultrasound dataset spanning 24 standard planes for large-scale pretraining. Extensive cross-center evaluations demonstrate that SonoCLIP achieves superior zero-shot transfer performance under both global and mask-guided inference, establishing a controllable and clinically oriented foundation model for fetal ultrasound analysis. Our code and data are available at .

</details>


### [224] [UniVAD v2: Unified Visual Anomaly Detection via Support-Conditioned Boundary Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29714)
*Zhaopeng Gu, Bingke Zhu, Zhaowen Li, Guibo Zhu, Yingying Chen, Ming Tang, Peng Su, Jinqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: UniVAD v2 提出了一种基于支持集条件边界构建的统一视觉异常检测框架，通过最优传输关系建模（OTRM）、检索与关系建模自适应协调（ACRRM）以及少样本异常参考模块（FAR），在正常侧和异常侧双面构建决策边界，在6个数据集上实现了跨域泛化性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一视觉异常检测方法在少样本迁移场景中主要依赖正常侧证据推断类别特定的决策边界，缺乏异常侧证据支持，且难以同时捕捉局部对应关系和全局支持-查询关联，导致对未见异常的边界估计不可靠。

Method: 基于UniVAD的组件-补丁分治框架，提出三模块改进：(1) OTRM（最优传输关系建模）通过传输式分配补充检索与支持-查询匹配；(2) ACRRM（检索与关系建模自适应协调）估计情节条件可靠性以融合两种证据；(3) FAR（少样本异常参考模块）将可选的异常参考转换为拒绝侧证据以调整边界。

Result: 在6个数据集（工业、逻辑、医学异常检测）上，1N-shot协议下图像级AUC从UniVAD的83.0%提升至84.5%，1N+1A-shot设置下达到85.7%。在MVTec-AD-SS上达到96.2%图像级AUC和96.9%像素级AUC。

Conclusion: UniVAD v2通过双面支持条件边界构建框架，有效提升了统一视觉异常检测的跨域泛化能力，且异常参考可在无需重新训练的情况下实现可控边界定制。

Classification reason: 该论文研究统一视觉异常检测（Unified Visual Anomaly Detection），属于视觉理解与推理任务，与多模态理解方向下的"视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）中的异常检测/视觉检测子领域相关。虽然异常检测并非传统VQA，但该论文关注跨类别、跨域的统一视觉检测，属于视觉理解范畴，且该[CANONICAL] subtopic是现有最接近的匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on visual anomaly detection, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks alignment with user's key directions like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: visual anomaly detection, few-shot transfer, support-conditioned boundary, cross-domain generalization, optimal transport

Abstract: Unified visual anomaly detection seeks to train a single detector that can be deployed across categories, domains, and application scenarios. In the few-shot transfer regime, the key challenge is to estimate an episode-specific boundary for an unseen target category from a small support set. Existing approaches mainly infer this boundary from normal-side evidence and provide limited abnormal-side evidence for deployment-specific tolerance. Within the normal side, they often struggle to jointly capture local correspondences and global support-query relations, making their boundaries less reliable for unseen anomalies. To address these issues, we propose UniVAD v2, a two-sided support-conditioned boundary construction framework for unified visual anomaly detection. Built on the component-patch divide-and-conquer framework of UniVAD, UniVAD v2 strengthens the normal side with an Optimal Transport-based Relational Modeling module (OTRM), which complements retrieval with support-query matching through transport-style allocation, and an Adaptive Coordination mechanism for Retrieval and Relational Modeling (ACRRM), which estimates episode-conditioned reliabilities to fuse the two sources of evidence. On the abnormal side, a Few-Shot Abnormal Reference module (FAR) converts optional abnormal references into rejection-side evidence for boundary adjustment. Experiments on six datasets spanning industrial, logical, and medical anomaly detection demonstrate strong cross-domain generalization. Under the 1N-shot protocol, UniVAD v2 improves the mean image-level AUC over UniVAD from 83.0\% to 84.5\%, and further reaches 85.7\% in the 1N+1A-shot setting. On the MVTec-AD Severity Split (MVTec-AD-SS), UniVAD v2 achieves 96.2\% image-level AUC and 96.9\% pixel-level AUC, showing that abnormal references enable controllable boundary customization without retraining.

</details>


### [225] [Efficient Visual Pointing for Embodied AI:Agent-Driven Data Synthesis, Cross-Block Attention, and Iterative Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29850)
*Zijian Hong, Qi Lv, Yuxiang Xie, Jianming Xing, Xiang Deng, Weili Guan, Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (54.46) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出PointArena 2026竞赛的解决方案，通过智能体驱动数据合成、跨块注意力机制（AttnRes）和迭代坐标校正（ABC）三个模块，在视觉指代定位任务上达到77.2%的整体准确率，排名第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉指代定位（visual pointing）是具身AI的核心技能，但现有方法在可操控性（steerability）、空间关系推理和坐标定位精度上存在三类失败模式，亟需系统性的解决方案。

Method: 1) 智能体驱动数据合成：构建大规模语义和锚点相对候选池（55,372个处理输出）；2) 确定性可操控数据流水线：利用掩码、模板和路径验证生成10,000样本验证集；3) AttnRes模块：添加门控跨块注意力增强可操控性；4) ABC校正模块：将扰动坐标与视觉特征编码用于通用坐标定位；5) 类别感知路由组合多个专家模型。

Result: 整体准确率77.2%，在PointArena 2026基准上排名第二。各子类别准确率：Affordance 93.9%、Spatial Relation 82.6%、Reasoning 78.2%、Counting 70.4%、Steerability 63.0%。

Conclusion: 通过数据合成、跨块注意力和迭代校正的组合策略，有效解决了视觉指代定位中的三类失败模式，验证了类别感知路由组合专家模型的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉指代定位（visual pointing），将语言指令映射到像素坐标，属于具身AI中的视觉问答与空间理解任务。虽然涉及具身AI，但核心是视觉语言定位（VQA类任务），因此映射到多模态理解与生成方向下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc），该subtopic涵盖VQA及视觉定位相关任务。

Importance reason: Focuses on embodied AI visual pointing with data synthesis and attention modules, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: embodied AI, visual pointing, data synthesis, cross-block attention, iterative correction

Abstract: Visual pointing maps a language instruction to pixel co ordinates, a core skill for embodied AI. We describe our PointArena 2026 solution, which achieves 77.2% overall accuracy and ranks second on the benchmark. The ap proach targets three failure modes. First, agent-driven syn thesis builds large semantic and anchor-relative candidate pools; the server inventory contains 55,372 processed out puts, 53,772 de-duplicated sample IDs, and 37,574 train able completed or accepted rows. Second, a determinis tic steerable-data pipeline creates a verified 10,000-sample main set, plus reserve samples, using masks, templates, and path verification. Third, two model-side modules address complementary errors: AttnRes adds gated cross-block at tention for steerability, while ABC correction encodes per turbed coordinates with visual features for general coordi nate grounding. Category-aware routing combines comple mentary specialists; local validation used to select experts records 93.9% Affordance, 82.6% Spatial Relation, 78.2% Reasoning, 70.4% Counting, and 63.0% Steerability.

</details>


### [226] [Multimodal Graph RAG for Long-range Visually Rich Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28780)
*Yi-Cheng Wang, Chu-Song Chen*

Main category: cs.IR, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出多模态图RAG方法（Multimodal Graph RAG），通过自动构建多模态知识图谱（MMKG）来增强MLLM对长文档的全局理解能力，并引入文档级VQA基准DLVQA，实验表明该方法在多跳QA/VQA基准上优于现有MMRAG或KG方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在处理长文档时受限于上下文窗口，多模态检索增强生成（MMRAG）虽能检索相关页面，但难以回答需要全局理解文档的VQA问题。而现有基于LLM的知识图谱构建方法仅处理语言模态，缺乏自动构建多模态知识图谱（MMKG）的方法。

Method: 提出多模态图RAG方法，自动从视觉丰富文档中构建多模态知识图谱（MMKG），将文档的全局知识以图结构进行总结和表示，并基于该图进行检索增强生成。同时构建了DLVQA基准，提供参考摘要和对应支持事实用于评估文档级VQA。

Result: 在多跳QA/VQA基准和DLVQA基准上，所提方法优于现有的MMRAG或基于KG的方法。

Conclusion: 多模态图RAG通过自动构建MMKG有效提升了MLLM对长文档的全局理解能力，DLVQA基准为文档级VQA提供了更全面的评估手段。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于长程视觉丰富文档理解，提出多模态图RAG方法用于文档级VQA，并构建了文档级VQA基准DLVQA。这与[多模态理解与生成]方向下的[视觉问答与文档理解]（multimodal_vqa_doc）规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on document-level VQA with graph RAG, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct alignment with user's key boost keywords. Signals: Multimodal Graph RAG, Long-range document understanding, DLVQA benchmark, Multimodal knowledge graph, Not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are widely applied to visual document understanding. However, comprehending long documents remains an issue by the limited context window. Though recent multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (MMRAG) can address this challenge by retrieving relevant pages. It still struggles with the visual question answering (VQA) requiring holistic comprehension of a document. To cope with this, knowledge graph (KG) that summarizes global knowledge of a document can provide an effective solution. However, most existing LLM-based KG construction methods handle only the language modality, leaving the automatic creation of multimodal KGs (MMKGs) for visually rich documents largely unexplored. In this paper, we introduce a multimodal graph-based RAG approach to tackle this problem. Existing LLM-based KG methods evaluate the QA performance relying on indirect evidence such as comprehensiveness, diversity, empowerment, and so on. The lack of annotated datasets for comprehensive document-level VQA poses a significant challenge to effective model evaluation. To overcome this limitation, we also introduce a new benchmark, DLVQA (document-level VQA), which provides reference summaries and corresponding supporting facts for global document-level questions. Experimental results show that our approach outperforms existing MMRAG or KG-based approaches on multi-hop QA/VQA benchmarks and DLVQA.

</details>


### [227] [Making Multimodal LLMs Reliable Chart Data Extractors: A Benchmark and Training Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29808)
*Yuchen He, Peizhi Ying, Liqi Cheng, Kuilin Peng, Yuan Tian, Dazhen Deng, Yingcai Wu*

Main category: cs.HC, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个面向无标签真实世界图表的基准来评估MLLM的图表数据提取能力，发现MLLM能可靠重建表格结构但数值精度不足。为此提出一个渐进式训练框架，显著提升了数值精度，7B模型达到SOTA，并通过用户研究验证了其在混合主动工作流中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式工具可靠但繁琐，混合主动系统高效但缺乏泛化性。MLLM提供了统一接口，但其从图表中精确提取数据表格（尤其是无可见标签时）的能力尚不明确，缺乏系统评估和有效方法。

Method: 构建了一个包含多样化真实世界无标签图表的基准；从人机交互视角出发，提出渐进式学习训练框架，模拟人类阅读图表的认知过程，分阶段训练MLLM进行图表数据提取。

Result: 7B参数模型在数值精度上达到SOTA性能；用户研究表明该模型能有效支持混合主动工作流，实现可靠的图表数据提取。

Conclusion: MLLM在图表数据提取中结构重建能力强但数值精度不足，所提出的渐进式训练框架能有效提升数值准确性，为可靠的数据提取提供了新方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从图表图像中提取数据表格，属于图表/文档理解任务，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on chart data extraction reliability, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks alignment with user's key directions like continuous models or video generation. Signals: MLLM chart extraction, numerical accuracy improvement, progressive training framework, mixed-initiative workflow, benchmark for real-world charts

Abstract: Chart data extraction, which reverse-engineers data tables from chart images, is essential for reproducibility, analysis, retrieval, and redesign. Existing interactive tools are reliable but tedious, and mixed-initiative systems, while more efficient, lack generalizability. Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) offer a unified interface for chart interpretation, yet their ability to extract accurate data tables, especially without visible labels, remains unclear. We build a benchmark featuring diverse real-world charts without data labels to evaluate this capability. Results show that, while current MLLMs reliably reconstruct table structures, they struggle with precise value recovery. To address this, we revisit chart data extraction from a human-centered perspective and argue that extraction should follow a progressive learning process similar to how people read charts. Our training framework substantially improves numerical accuracy, achieving state-of-the-art performance with a 7B-parameter model. A user study further shows that our model effectively supports mixed-initiative workflows for reliable chart data extraction.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-video_understanding_temporal'></div>

## 视频理解与时序推理 [[Back]](#toc)

视频 LLM、视频问答、时序定位、长视频理解、第一视角视频。

### [228] [Efficient Spatio-Temporal Grounding with Multimodal Large Models via Second-Level Tracking and RL Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29023)
*Tianshu Zhang, Yan Wang, Ji Qi, Lijie Wen*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 普通相关 (56.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出一种高效的长视频时空定位流水线，将逐帧推理降为秒级跟踪，结合跨秒平滑、CoT轨迹合成与基于t_IoU+mv_IoU的RL验证器，在效率与定位质量间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在长视频上逐帧推理计算昂贵且不稳定，需要一种兼顾效率与精度的时空定位方法。

Method: 1) 从逐帧推理降为秒级跟踪（second-level tracking），并做跨秒平滑保持连续性；2) 利用先进多模态模型合成CoT风格的轨迹用于时序定位与目标选择，并用真实标注替换生成坐标避免噪声；3) 使用基于t_IoU+mv_IoU的验证器进行强化学习策略优化。

Result: 在多种FPS设置下，该方法在效率与定位质量之间实现了良好的权衡。

Conclusion: 秒级跟踪结合RL验证是一种有效的长视频时空定位方案，可在降低计算开销的同时保持定位精度。

Classification reason: 该论文研究长视频中的时空定位（spatio-temporal grounding），涉及时序定位与目标跟踪，属于视频理解与时序推理范畴，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "视频理解与时序推理"。

Importance reason: Focuses on efficient video grounding with RL, but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous models or autoregressive generation. Signals: efficient spatio-temporal grounding, second-level tracking, RL verifier, chain-of-thought trajectories, long video understanding

Abstract: Spatio-temporal grounding in long videos requires precise temporal localization and robust object tracking conditioned on natural-language queries. While recent vision-language models (VLMs) show strong reasoning ability, directly applying frame-by-frame inference to long sequences is computationally expensive and unstable. We propose a practical pipeline that shifts from frame-level to second-level tracking and performs cross-second smoothing to preserve continuity while reducing sequence length. To improve reasoning supervision, we synthesize chain-of-thought style trajectories using advanced multimodal models for temporal localization and target selection, and replace generated spatio-temporal coordinates with ground-truth annotations to avoid noisy supervision. We further optimize the policy with reinforcement learning using a verifier based on $t\_\mathrm{IoU}+mv\_\mathrm{IoU}$. Experiments across multiple FPS settings show that our method achieves a strong trade-off between efficiency and localization quality.

</details>


### [229] [MotionAtlas: Detailed Region Captioning for Motion-Centric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29531)
*Weisong Liu, Haochen Wang, Kuan Gao, Yuhao Wang, Yikang Zhou, Zhongwei Ren, Jacky Mai, Anna Wang, Yanwei Li, Jason Li, Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MotionAtlas 提出了一个面向运动中心视频的细粒度区域描述系统，包含人工标注基准 MotionAtlas-Bench（2073道选择题）、自举精炼数据管道（159k高质量数据）以及训练策略，在 Molmo2 和 Qwen3-VL 等基线模型上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频描述方法通常生成全局描述，忽略了视频中不同区域的细粒度运动信息，且缺乏可量化评估的基准。作者希望实现区域感知的运动描述，以缓解视觉杂乱和运动纠缠问题。

Method: (1) 构建 MotionAtlas-Bench，包含2073道多项选择题，人工标注高质量运动中心视频的细粒度运动理解；(2) 设计可扩展的数据管道，利用自举精炼（self-bootstrap refinement）抑制细粒度幻觉，生成159k高质量运动描述数据；(3) 设计针对性的训练数据组合策略，在 Molmo2 和 Qwen3-VL 等 Video-MLLM 上进行微调。

Result: MotionAtlas-4B 在通用运动基准上平均超越 Qwen3-VL-4B 5.2个百分点，在多个基线模型上取得一致且显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 区域感知的运动描述能够有效提升视频MLLM的细粒度运动理解能力，自举精炼数据管道和针对性训练策略是提升性能的关键。

Classification reason: 该论文关注运动中心视频的细粒度区域描述，构建了视频MLLM和评测基准，属于视频理解范畴。现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "视频理解与时序推理"（video_understanding_temporal）与之高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on video understanding rather than generation or continuous multimodal models, which are the user's top priorities. Signals: motion-centric video captioning, region-aware spatiotemporal understanding, benchmark and data pipeline, not aligned with priority subtopics, no autoregressive or diffusion generation

Abstract: We propose MotionAtlas, a system for detailed captioning of motion-centric videos, comprising (1) a dedicated human-annotated benchmark, (2) a scalable, high-quality pipeline to construct training samples, and (3) a family of powerful Video-MLLMs. Unlike conventional global motion captioning datasets, we focus on region-aware motion captioning: given a video and a spatiotemporal mask, the model generates precise descriptions of motion within the target region, thereby alleviating visual clutter and motion entanglement and enabling reliable, quantifiable evaluation. Concretely, we first build MotionAtlas-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 2,073 multiple-choice questions, meticulously annotated for a curated set of high-quality, motion-centric videos, to evaluate fine-grained motion understanding of the objects in question. Second, we design a rigorous and scalable data pipeline that leverages self-bootstrap refinement to suppress fine-grained hallucinations, yielding 159k high-quality motion captioning data. Third, we design a tailored training data composition strategy, which achieves consistent and substantial performance gains across diverse baseline Video-MLLMs, including Molmo2 and Qwen3-VL. For instance, MotionAtlas-4B surpasses Qwen3-VL-4B by an average of 5.2 percentage points across general motion benchmarks. The benchmark, dataset, and code have been released.

</details>


### [230] [Tool-Augmented Spatiotemporal Reasoning for Streamlining Video Question Answering Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.10359)
*Sunqi Fan, Jiashuo Cui, Meng-Hao Guo, Shuojin Yang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 值得关注 (70.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出STAR框架，通过为MLLM配备可扩展的视频工具集（Video Toolkit）并设计时空推理调度策略，逐步定位视频关键区域，从而增强模型在复杂VideoQA任务中的时空推理能力。在GPT-4o上集成轻量工具后，在VideoMME和LongVideoBench上分别提升8.2%和4.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在复杂推理型VideoQA任务中难以同时建模视频帧内的空间关系与时序演变的因果动态，亟需一种系统性的工具增强与调度方法来提升时空推理能力。

Method: 提出STAR（Spatiotemporal Reasoning Framework），包含一个全面可扩展的Video Toolkit（涵盖时序和空间工具），以及一个策略性调度框架，通过逐步定位视频关键区域来控制工具调用顺序，避免工具链捷径问题。

Result: 在GPT-4o上集成轻量工具后，在VideoMME上提升8.2%，在LongVideoBench上提升4.6%。

Conclusion: 所提出的Video Toolkit和STAR框架有效增强了MLLM的时空推理能力，为构建自主智能的视频分析助手迈出了重要一步。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于视频问答（VideoQA）任务，通过工具增强和时空推理框架提升MLLM的视频理解能力，属于视频理解与时序推理这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on tool-augmented VideoQA, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: VideoQA, tool-augmented reasoning, spatiotemporal reasoning, MLLM enhancement, no generative or continuous modeling

Abstract: Video Question Answering (VideoQA) task serves as a critical playground for evaluating whether foundation models can effectively perceive, understand, and reason about dynamic real-world scenarios. However, existing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) struggle with simultaneously modeling spatial relationships within video frames and understanding the causal dynamics of temporal evolution on complex and reasoning-intensive VideoQA task. In this work, we equip MLLM with a comprehensive and extensible Video Toolkit, to enhance MLLM's spatiotemporal reasoning capabilities and ensure the harmony between the quantity and diversity of tools. To better control the tool invocation sequence and avoid toolchain shortcut issues, we propose a Spatiotemporal Reasoning Framework (STAR) that strategically schedules temporal and spatial tools, thereby progressively localizing the key area in the video. Our STAR framework enhances GPT-4o using lightweight tools, achieving an 8.2% gain on VideoMME and 4.6% on LongVideoBench. We believe that our proposed Video Toolkit and STAR framework make an important step towards building autonomous and intelligent video analysis assistants. The code is publicly available at .

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-continuous_multimodal_reasoning'></div>

## 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态模型在连续 latent 中进行 reasoning、planning、visual thought 或 chain-of-continuous-thought。

### [231] [VisReflect: Latent Visual Reflection for Fine-Grained Perception in Long Visual Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30288)
*Xiaoqian Shen, Mohamed Elhoseiny*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维

Importance: 值得关注 (68.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VisReflect 提出一种潜空间视觉反射机制，让 LVLM 在单次前向传播中生成连续视觉反射（而非离散 token）来引导注意力聚焦于相关视觉区域，从而缓解长视觉上下文中注意力沉没问题，提升细粒度感知能力，并在高分辨率图像和长视频理解任务上取得一致提升，同时推理速度比 zooming-based 方法快约 44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视觉上下文中（高分辨率图像、长视频），视觉 token 数量激增导致严重的注意力沉没现象（visual attention sink），无关 token 吸收了过多注意力。现有 zooming-based 方法依赖离散 token 空间中的数值定位（预测 bbox/时间跨度）并重新编码裁剪区域，不仅不可靠且计算开销大。

Method: VisReflect 框架在 LVLM 中引入一个额外的视觉反射 token（visual reflection token），该 token 在潜空间中生成连续视觉反射特征，表示与问题相关的视觉特征。这些连续反射特征通过交叉注意力机制引导模型关注输入中相关的视觉 token（空间区域或时间帧），整个过程在单次前向传播中完成，无需解码为离散 token 或额外的前向传播。

Result: 在 BLINK、V*、HRBench-4K/8K 等高分辨率图像基准上平均提升 4.1%，在 MVBench、VideoMME、MLVU 等视频理解基准上平均提升 1.8%。与 zooming-based 方法相比，在视频理解任务上推理时间减少约 44%，同时性能相当。

Conclusion: VisReflect 通过潜空间中的连续视觉反射机制，有效缓解了长视觉上下文中的注意力沉没问题，为细粒度感知提供了一种简单、高效且无需额外前向传播的解决方案，在图像和视频任务上均展现出良好的泛化能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 VisReflect 框架，通过生成连续视觉反射（continuous visual reflection）在潜空间中表示与问题相关的视觉特征，引导注意力聚焦于相关视觉 token，属于多模态模型在连续 latent 空间中进行推理的范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes latent visual reflection for fine-grained perception in long visual context, directly addressing continuous multimodal reasoning and attention sink. Signals: continuous visual reflection, latent space attention guidance, single forward pass, 44% faster inference, high-resolution image and long video understanding

Abstract: Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable success on vision-language tasks, yet fine-grained perception over high-resolution images and long-context videos remains challenging. As the number of visual tokens increases, the visual attention sink phenomenon becomes increasingly severe, causing irrelevant tokens to absorb a disproportionate amount of attention mass. Recent approaches attempt to mitigate this issue by explicitly predicting bounding boxes or temporal spans and re-encoding the cropped visual regions. Such methods depend on unreliable numeric localization in the discrete token space and incur significant computational overhead due to additional forward passes. In this work, we propose **VisReflect**, a simple yet effective framework that improves fine-grained perception in long visual contexts through latent visual reflection. Instead of decoding intermediate predictions into discrete tokens, the model generates continuous visual reflection that represents question-relevant visual features in the latent space. These reflections selectively emphasize salient regions or frames, guiding attention towards relevant visual tokens within a single forward pass. We conduct comprehensive evaluations on challenging high-resolution image benchmarks, including BLINK, V*, and HRBench-4K/8K, as well as video understanding benchmarks such as MVBench, VideoMME, and MLVU. Our method consistently improves over strong baselines, achieving gains of 4.1% on image benchmarks and 1.8% on video benchmarks. Compared with zooming-based methods, our model achieves comparable performance while reducing inference time by roughly 44% on video understanding.

</details>
